# 1. PROJECT VISION & OVERVIEW

```
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                         AETHER MEDIA ENGINE (AME)                            ║
║                                                                              ║
║     "Transcending Boundaries in Media Playback Technology"                   ║
║                                                                              ║
║  Version: 1.0.0-Enterprise                                                   ║
║  Codename: "Prometheus"                                                      ║
║  License: Dual (Commercial + Open Source LGPL v3)                            ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
```

## 1.1 Mission Statement

AETHER Media Engine is an enterprise-grade, AI-powered, cross-platform media playback solution designed to deliver unparalleled performance, extensibility, and user experience across all devices and operating systems.

## 1.2 Key Differentiators

```yaml
Core Philosophy:
  - Zero-Compromise Performance
  - AI-First Design Approach
  - Universal Platform Compatibility
  - Enterprise Security Standards
  - Modular Microkernel Architecture
  - Real-time Adaptive Processing
  - Future-Proof Extensibility
```

## 1.3 Feature Matrix

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                           FEATURE CAPABILITIES                              │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ ◆ PLAYBACK                    │ ◆ AI/ML FEATURES                           │
│   ├─ 8K/16K Video Support     │   ├─ Neural Video Upscaling               │
│   ├─ 384kHz/32-bit Audio      │   ├─ AI Audio Enhancement                 │
│   ├─ HDR10+/Dolby Vision      │   ├─ Smart Content Recognition            │
│   ├─ 360°/VR/AR Content       │   ├─ Predictive Buffering                 │
│   ├─ Multi-stream Sync        │   ├─ Emotion-Based Recommendations        │
│   └─ Gapless Playback         │   └─ Voice Command Control                │
│                               │                                            │
│ ◆ FORMATS (500+)              │ ◆ STREAMING                                │
│   ├─ Video: AV1,VP9,H.266     │   ├─ DASH/HLS/CMAF                        │
│   ├─ Audio: FLAC,DSD,MQA      │   ├─ WebRTC Live Streaming                │
│   ├─ Image: AVIF,HEIF,JXL     │   ├─ P2P Distribution                     │
│   ├─ Subtitle: ASS,VTT,TTML   │   ├─ CDN Integration                      │
│   └─ Container: MKV,MP4,WebM  │   └─ Adaptive Bitrate                     │
│                               │                                            │
│ ◆ PLATFORM SUPPORT            │ ◆ ENTERPRISE FEATURES                      │
│   ├─ Windows (7-11+)          │   ├─ DRM: Widevine,PlayReady,FairPlay     │
│   ├─ macOS (10.14+)           │   ├─ Analytics Dashboard                  │
│   ├─ Linux (All Distros)      │   ├─ Multi-tenant Support                 │
│   ├─ Android (5.0+)           │   ├─ LDAP/SSO Integration                 │
│   ├─ iOS/iPadOS (12.0+)       │   ├─ Audit Logging                        │
│   ├─ WebAssembly/Browser      │   ├─ Role-Based Access Control            │
│   ├─ Embedded Systems         │   └─ Compliance (GDPR,HIPAA,SOC2)         │
│   └─ Smart TV/Consoles        │                                            │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

# 2. ARCHITECTURE DESIGN

## 2.1 High-Level Architecture

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                              AETHER MEDIA ENGINE                                │
│                           SYSTEM ARCHITECTURE v1.0                              │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                              PRESENTATION LAYER                                 │
├─────────────┬─────────────┬─────────────┬─────────────┬─────────────────────────┤

│     GUI     │    Apps     │   Player    │    Apps     │       UI                │

└──────┬──────┴──────┬──────┴──────┬──────┴──────┬──────┴──────────┬──────────────┘
       │             │             │             │                 │
       └─────────────┴─────────────┴─────────────┴─────────────────┘

                     ┌─────────────▼─────────────┐

│  │  • Upscaling    │  │  • Enhancement  │  │  • Scene Det.   │                  │
│  │  • FPS Interp.  │  │  • Separation   │  │  • Face Recog.  │                  │
│  │  • Denoising    │  │  • Transcribe   │  │  • Object Det.  │                  │
│  │  • HDR Convert  │  │  • Translation  │  │  • OCR          │                  │
│  └─────────────────┘  └─────────────────┘  └─────────────────┘                  │
│                                                                                 │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │
│  │                    ML INFERENCE ENGINE (ONNX/TensorRT/CoreML)           │   │
│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                   │
┌──────��───────────────────────────▼──────────────────────────────────────────────┐

├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  ┌─────────┐ ┌─────────┐ ┌─────────┐ ┌─────────┐ ┌─────────┐ ┌─────────┐       │
│  │  GPU    │ │  CPU    │ │  NPU    │ │  DSP    │ │ DISPLAY │ │ STORAGE │       │
│  │ Driver  │ │Optimize │ │ Driver  │ │ Driver  │ │ Manager │ │ Manager │       │
│  └─────────┘ └─────────┘ └─────────┘ └─────────┘ └─────────┘ └─────────┘       │
│                                                                                 │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │
│  │     PLATFORM BACKENDS: Vulkan│Metal│D3D12│OpenGL│VAAPI│NVDEC│QSV       │   │
│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

└────────┴────────┴────────┴────────┴────────┴────────┴────────┴──────────────────┘
```

## 2.2 Component Interaction Diagram

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                         DATA FLOW ARCHITECTURE                                 │

    ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐

    ├─────────┬─────────┬─────────┬─────────┬─────────┬────────────────────┤

## 2.3 Microkernel Architecture

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                      MICROKERNEL DESIGN PATTERN                                │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

                         ┌─────────────────────────┐
                         │    EXTERNAL PLUGINS     │
                         │  ┌───────┐ ┌───────┐   │
                         │  │Custom │ │Third  │   │
                         │  │Plugin │ │Party  │   │
                         │  └───┬───┘ └───┬───┘   │
                         └─────│─────────│────────┘
                               │         │
                    ┌──────────▼─────────▼──────────┐
                    │      PLUGIN INTERFACE         │
                    │   ┌─────────────────────┐     │
                    │   │   Plugin Registry   │     │
                    │   │   Plugin Loader     │     │
                    │   │   Version Manager   │     │
                    │   │   Dependency Solver │     │
                    │   └─────────────────────┘     │
                    └──────────────┬────────────────┘
                                   │
┌──────────────────────────────────▼──────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                              MICROKERNEL CORE                                   │
│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │
│  │                         MESSAGE BUS / EVENT SYSTEM                       │  │
│  │    ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │  │
│  │    │  Pub/Sub Engine  │  Event Queue  │  Priority Scheduler          │   │  │
│  │    └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │  │
│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │
│                                                                                 │
│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │
│  │                         CORE SERVICES                                    │  │
│  │  ┌─────────┐ ┌─────────┐ ┌─────────┐ ┌─────────┐ ┌─────────┐            │  │
│  │  │ Memory  │ │ Thread  │ │  Timer  │ │Logging  │ │ Config  │            │  │
│  │  │ Manager │ │  Pool   │ │ Service │ │ Service │ │ Manager │            │  │
│  │  └─────────┘ └─────────┘ └─────────┘ └─────────┘ └─────────┘            │  │
│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │
│                                                                                 │
│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │
│  │                         INTERNAL PLUGINS (CORE)                          │  │
│  │  ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐       │  │
│  │  │ Demuxer  │ │ Decoder  │ │  Filter  │ │ Renderer │ │  Network │       │  │
│  │  │ Plugin   │ │ Plugin   │ │  Plugin  │ │  Plugin  │ │  Plugin  │       │  │
│  │  └──────────┘ └──────────┘ └──────────┘ └──────────┘ └──────────┘       │  │
│  │  ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐       │  │
│  │  │   AI     │ │   DRM    │ │Analytics │ │Subtitle  │ │Streaming │       │  │
│  │  │ Plugin   │ │ Plugin   │ │  Plugin  │ │  Plugin  │ │  Plugin  │       │  │
│  │  └──────────┘ └──────────┘ └──────────┘ └──────────┘ └──────────┘       │  │
│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

# 3. TECHNOLOGY STACK

```yaml
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#                           TECHNOLOGY STACK DEFINITION
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Core Languages:
  Primary:
    - C++20/23: "Core engine, performance-critical components"
    - C: "Low-level system interfaces, legacy codec integration"
  
  Secondary:
    - Rust: "Memory-safe modules, security-critical components"
    - Python: "AI/ML pipeline, scripting, automation, testing"
    - JavaScript/TypeScript: "Web player, Electron UI, Node.js services"
    - Swift: "iOS/macOS native UI, Metal rendering"
    - Kotlin: "Android native UI, JNI bridge"
    - Go: "Backend services, streaming servers"
    - Assembly: "SIMD optimizations (AVX-512, NEON, SVE)"
    
  Scripting/Config:
    - Lua: "Plugin scripting, runtime configuration"
    - YAML/JSON/TOML: "Configuration files"
    - GLSL/HLSL/MSL: "Shader programs"

Build & Development:
  Build Systems:
    - CMake 3.25+: "Primary build system"
    - Ninja: "Fast build execution"
    - Meson: "Alternative builds"
    - Bazel: "Large-scale builds, CI/CD"
    - Cargo: "Rust components"
    - Gradle: "Android builds"
    - Xcode: "iOS/macOS builds"
    
  Package Managers:
    - Conan 2.0: "C++ dependencies"
    - vcpkg: "C++ libraries"
    - pip/conda: "Python packages"
    - npm/yarn: "JavaScript packages"
    
  Compilers:
    - Clang/LLVM 16+: "Primary compiler"
    - GCC 13+: "Linux builds"
    - MSVC 2022+: "Windows builds"
    - Apple Clang: "macOS/iOS builds"
    - Emscripten: "WebAssembly builds"

Media Processing:
  Video Codecs:
    - FFmpeg 6.0+: "Codec framework"
    - libx264/x265: "H.264/H.265 encoding"
    - dav1d/libaom: "AV1 decoding/encoding"
    - libvpx: "VP8/VP9"
    - OpenH264: "H.264 baseline"
    - SVT-AV1/HEVC: "Scalable encoding"
    
  Audio Codecs:
    - libopus: "Opus codec"
    - FLAC: "Lossless audio"
    - libvorbis: "Vorbis codec"
    - lame: "MP3 encoding"
    - FDK-AAC: "AAC codec"
    
  Hardware Acceleration:
    - NVIDIA NVDEC/NVENC: "NVIDIA GPUs"
    - AMD AMF/VCE: "AMD GPUs"
    - Intel QSV: "Intel GPUs"
    - VAAPI: "Linux VA-API"
    - VDPAU: "Linux VDPAU"
    - VideoToolbox: "Apple platforms"
    - MediaCodec: "Android"

Graphics & Rendering:
  APIs:
    - Vulkan 1.3: "Primary graphics API"
    - Metal 3: "Apple platforms"
    - Direct3D 12: "Windows"
    - OpenGL 4.6/ES 3.2: "Compatibility"
    - WebGPU: "Web browser"
    
  Libraries:
    - SDL3: "Windowing, input"
    - GLFW: "OpenGL context"
    - Dear ImGui: "Debug UI"
    - Skia: "2D graphics"
    - Freetype/Harfbuzz: "Text rendering"

AI/ML Frameworks:
  Inference Engines:
    - ONNX Runtime: "Cross-platform inference"
    - TensorRT: "NVIDIA optimization"
    - OpenVINO: "Intel optimization"
    - CoreML: "Apple platforms"
    - NNAPI: "Android neural API"
    - DirectML: "Windows ML"
    
  Training Frameworks:
    - PyTorch 2.0+: "Model development"
    - TensorFlow 2.x: "Alternative training"
    - JAX: "Research models"
    
  Model Types:
    - ESRGAN/Real-ESRGAN: "Super resolution"
    - RIFE/FLAVR: "Frame interpolation"
    - Demucs: "Audio separation"
    - Whisper: "Speech recognition"
    - CLIP: "Content understanding"

Networking:
  Protocols:
    - libcurl: "HTTP/HTTPS"
    - libwebsockets: "WebSocket"
    - libsrt: "SRT streaming"
    - live555: "RTSP"
    - libnice: "ICE/STUN/TURN"
    - libquic: "QUIC protocol"
    
  Streaming:
    - dash.js (port): "DASH client"
    - hls.js (port): "HLS client"
    - libwebrtc: "WebRTC"
    - GStreamer: "Alternative pipeline"

UI Frameworks:
  Desktop:
    - Qt 6: "Cross-platform UI"
    - GTK 4: "Linux native"
    - wxWidgets: "Alternative"
    - Electron: "Web-based desktop"
    
  Mobile:
    - Jetpack Compose: "Android UI"
    - SwiftUI: "iOS UI"
    - Flutter: "Cross-platform mobile"
    - React Native: "Alternative"
    
  Web:
    - React 18: "Web UI"
    - WebAssembly: "Engine port"
    - WebGL/WebGPU: "Rendering"
    - Media Source Extensions: "Streaming"

Database & Storage:
  Embedded:
    - SQLite: "Local metadata"
    - RocksDB: "Cache storage"
    - LevelDB: "Key-value store"
    
  Distributed:
    - PostgreSQL: "Server backend"
    - Redis: "Caching layer"
    - Elasticsearch: "Search engine"
    - ClickHouse: "Analytics"

Security:
  Cryptography:
    - OpenSSL 3.0: "TLS/crypto"
    - libsodium: "Modern crypto"
    - mbedTLS: "Embedded"
    
  DRM:
    - Widevine CDM: "Google DRM"
    - PlayReady: "Microsoft DRM"
    - FairPlay: "Apple DRM"
    - ClearKey: "Open DRM"

Testing & QA:
  Unit Testing:
    - Google Test: "C++ testing"
    - Catch2: "Alternative"
    - pytest: "Python testing"
    - Jest: "JavaScript testing"
    
  Integration:
    - Docker: "Container testing"
    - Kubernetes: "Orchestration"
    
  Performance:
    - Google Benchmark: "Micro-benchmarks"
    - Valgrind: "Memory analysis"
    - perf: "Linux profiling"
    - Tracy: "Frame profiler"

Documentation:
  Tools:
    - Doxygen: "API documentation"
    - Sphinx: "User documentation"
    - MkDocs: "Markdown docs"
    - OpenAPI/Swagger: "REST API docs"
```

# 5. CORE MODULES

## 5.1 Core Header Files

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/aether.hpp
// DESCRIPTION: Main include header for AETHER Media Engine
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#ifndef AETHER_AETHER_HPP
#define AETHER_AETHER_HPP

// Version Information
#include "aether/version.hpp"

// Configuration
#include "aether/config.hpp"

// Export Macros
#include "aether/export.hpp"

// Core Components
#include "aether/core/engine.hpp"
#include "aether/core/player.hpp"
#include "aether/core/media_source.hpp"
#include "aether/core/event_system.hpp"
#include "aether/core/error_handling.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"

// Media Types
#include "aether/media/frame.hpp"
#include "aether/media/packet.hpp"
#include "aether/media/format.hpp"
#include "aether/media/codec.hpp"
#include "aether/media/metadata.hpp"

// Pipeline
#include "aether/pipeline/pipeline.hpp"
#include "aether/pipeline/demuxer.hpp"
#include "aether/pipeline/decoder.hpp"
#include "aether/pipeline/filter.hpp"
#include "aether/pipeline/renderer.hpp"

// Audio
#include "aether/audio/audio_device.hpp"
#include "aether/audio/audio_processor.hpp"

// Video
#include "aether/video/video_frame.hpp"
#include "aether/video/video_renderer.hpp"

// Network
#include "aether/network/streaming.hpp"
#include "aether/network/protocol.hpp"

// AI/ML
#include "aether/ai/inference_engine.hpp"
#include "aether/ai/upscaler.hpp"

// Plugin System
#include "aether/plugin/plugin.hpp"
#include "aether/plugin/plugin_manager.hpp"

// Utilities
#include "aether/utils/logging.hpp"
#include "aether/utils/memory.hpp"
#include "aether/utils/threading.hpp"

/**
 * @namespace aether
 * @brief Root namespace for AETHER Media Engine
 */
namespace aether {

/**
 * @brief Initialize the AETHER Media Engine
 * @param config Optional configuration object
 * @return true if initialization successful
 */
AETHER_API bool Initialize(const Config& config = Config::Default());

/**
 * @brief Shutdown the AETHER Media Engine
 */
AETHER_API void Shutdown();

/**
 * @brief Get the engine version string
 * @return Version string in format "major.minor.patch"
 */
AETHER_API const char* GetVersionString();

/**
 * @brief Get detailed build information
 * @return Build information structure
 */
AETHER_API BuildInfo GetBuildInfo();

} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_AETHER_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/version.hpp
// DESCRIPTION: Version information and build configuration
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#ifndef AETHER_VERSION_HPP
#define AETHER_VERSION_HPP

#include <cstdint>
#include <string_view>

namespace aether {

// Version numbers
constexpr uint32_t VERSION_MAJOR = 1;
constexpr uint32_t VERSION_MINOR = 0;
constexpr uint32_t VERSION_PATCH = 0;

// Version as single integer (major * 10000 + minor * 100 + patch)
constexpr uint32_t VERSION = VERSION_MAJOR * 10000 + VERSION_MINOR * 100 + VERSION_PATCH;

// Version string
constexpr std::string_view VERSION_STRING = "1.0.0";

// Build codename
constexpr std::string_view CODENAME = "Prometheus";

// Build type
#ifdef NDEBUG
    constexpr std::string_view BUILD_TYPE = "Release";
#else
    constexpr std::string_view BUILD_TYPE = "Debug";
#endif

// Git information (populated at build time)
extern const char* GIT_COMMIT_HASH;
extern const char* GIT_BRANCH;
extern const char* BUILD_DATE;
extern const char* BUILD_TIME;

/**
 * @struct BuildInfo
 * @brief Contains detailed build information
 */
struct BuildInfo {
    uint32_t version_major;
    uint32_t version_minor;
    uint32_t version_patch;
    const char* version_string;
    const char* codename;
    const char* build_type;
    const char* git_commit;
    const char* git_branch;
    const char* build_date;
    const char* build_time;
    const char* compiler;
    const char* compiler_version;
    const char* platform;
    const char* architecture;
    
    // Feature flags
    bool has_vulkan;
    bool has_metal;
    bool has_d3d12;
    bool has_opengl;
    bool has_cuda;
    bool has_opencl;
    bool has_tensorrt;
    bool has_coreml;
    bool has_onnxruntime;
    bool has_ffmpeg;
    bool has_drm_support;
};

/**
 * @brief Check if current version is at least the specified version
 */
constexpr bool IsVersionAtLeast(uint32_t major, uint32_t minor, uint32_t patch) {
    return VERSION >= (major * 10000 + minor * 100 + patch);
}

} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_VERSION_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/export.hpp
// DESCRIPTION: Export/import macros for shared library
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#ifndef AETHER_EXPORT_HPP
#define AETHER_EXPORT_HPP

// Platform detection
#if defined(_WIN32) || defined(_WIN64)
    #define AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
#elif defined(__APPLE__)
    #include <TargetConditionals.h>
    #if TARGET_OS_IPHONE || TARGET_IPHONE_SIMULATOR
        #define AETHER_PLATFORM_IOS
    #else
        #define AETHER_PLATFORM_MACOS
    #endif
    #define AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE
#elif defined(__ANDROID__)
    #define AETHER_PLATFORM_ANDROID
#elif defined(__linux__)
    #define AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX
#elif defined(__EMSCRIPTEN__)
    #define AETHER_PLATFORM_WEB
#else
    #define AETHER_PLATFORM_UNKNOWN
#endif

// Architecture detection
#if defined(__x86_64__) || defined(_M_X64)
    #define AETHER_ARCH_X64
#elif defined(__i386__) || defined(_M_IX86)
    #define AETHER_ARCH_X86
#elif defined(__aarch64__) || defined(_M_ARM64)
    #define AETHER_ARCH_ARM64
#elif defined(__arm__) || defined(_M_ARM)
    #define AETHER_ARCH_ARM
#elif defined(__wasm__)
    #define AETHER_ARCH_WASM
#endif

// Export/Import macros
#if defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS)
    #ifdef AETHER_BUILD_SHARED
        #ifdef AETHER_EXPORT
            #define AETHER_API __declspec(dllexport)
        #else
            #define AETHER_API __declspec(dllimport)
        #endif
    #else
        #define AETHER_API
    #endif
    #define AETHER_LOCAL
#else
    #if __GNUC__ >= 4 || defined(__clang__)
        #define AETHER_API __attribute__((visibility("default")))
        #define AETHER_LOCAL __attribute__((visibility("hidden")))
    #else
        #define AETHER_API
        #define AETHER_LOCAL
    #endif
#endif

// Calling convention
#if defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS)
    #define AETHER_CALL __cdecl
#else
    #define AETHER_CALL
#endif

// Deprecation macros
#if defined(__cplusplus) && __cplusplus >= 201402L
    #define AETHER_DEPRECATED [[deprecated]]
    #define AETHER_DEPRECATED_MSG(msg) [[deprecated(msg)]]
#elif defined(_MSC_VER)
    #define AETHER_DEPRECATED __declspec(deprecated)
    #define AETHER_DEPRECATED_MSG(msg) __declspec(deprecated(msg))
#elif defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
    #define AETHER_DEPRECATED __attribute__((deprecated))
    #define AETHER_DEPRECATED_MSG(msg) __attribute__((deprecated(msg)))
#else
    #define AETHER_DEPRECATED
    #define AETHER_DEPRECATED_MSG(msg)
#endif

// Nodiscard
#if defined(__cplusplus) && __cplusplus >= 201703L
    #define AETHER_NODISCARD [[nodiscard]]
#else
    #define AETHER_NODISCARD
#endif

// Force inline
#if defined(_MSC_VER)
    #define AETHER_FORCE_INLINE __forceinline
#elif defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
    #define AETHER_FORCE_INLINE __attribute__((always_inline)) inline
#else
    #define AETHER_FORCE_INLINE inline
#endif

// No inline
#if defined(_MSC_VER)
    #define AETHER_NO_INLINE __declspec(noinline)
#elif defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
    #define AETHER_NO_INLINE __attribute__((noinline))
#else
    #define AETHER_NO_INLINE
#endif

// Alignment
#define AETHER_ALIGN(x) alignas(x)
#define AETHER_CACHE_LINE_SIZE 64
#define AETHER_CACHE_ALIGNED AETHER_ALIGN(AETHER_CACHE_LINE_SIZE)

// Branch prediction hints
#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
    #define AETHER_LIKELY(x) __builtin_expect(!!(x), 1)
    #define AETHER_UNLIKELY(x) __builtin_expect(!!(x), 0)
#else
    #define AETHER_LIKELY(x) (x)
    #define AETHER_UNLIKELY(x) (x)
#endif

// Restrict keyword
#if defined(_MSC_VER)
    #define AETHER_RESTRICT __restrict
#elif defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
    #define AETHER_RESTRICT __restrict__
#else
    #define AETHER_RESTRICT
#endif

#endif // AETHER_EXPORT_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/core/types.hpp
// DESCRIPTION: Core type definitions for AETHER Media Engine
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#ifndef AETHER_CORE_TYPES_HPP
#define AETHER_CORE_TYPES_HPP

#include <cstdint>
#include <chrono>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <memory>
#include <optional>
#include <variant>
#include <functional>
#include <span>
#include <expected>

namespace aether {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Basic Types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

using i8  = std::int8_t;
using i16 = std::int16_t;
using i32 = std::int32_t;
using i64 = std::int64_t;

using u8  = std::uint8_t;
using u16 = std::uint16_t;
using u32 = std::uint32_t;
using u64 = std::uint64_t;

using f32 = float;
using f64 = double;

using usize = std::size_t;
using isize = std::ptrdiff_t;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Time Types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

using Clock = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock;
using TimePoint = Clock::time_point;
using Duration = Clock::duration;
using Nanoseconds = std::chrono::nanoseconds;
using Microseconds = std::chrono::microseconds;
using Milliseconds = std::chrono::milliseconds;
using Seconds = std::chrono::seconds;

/**
 * @struct Timestamp
 * @brief Represents a media timestamp with timebase
 */
struct Timestamp {
    i64 pts = 0;           // Presentation timestamp
    i64 dts = 0;           // Decoding timestamp
    i64 duration = 0;      // Duration
    
    // Timebase: pts = seconds * num / den
    i32 timebase_num = 1;
    i32 timebase_den = 1000000;  // Default: microseconds
    
    // Convert to seconds
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr f64 ToSeconds() const noexcept {
        return static_cast<f64>(pts) * timebase_num / timebase_den;
    }
    
    // Convert to milliseconds
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr i64 ToMilliseconds() const noexcept {
        return pts * timebase_num * 1000 / timebase_den;
    }
    
    // Create from seconds
    static constexpr Timestamp FromSeconds(f64 seconds) noexcept {
        Timestamp ts;
        ts.pts = static_cast<i64>(seconds * 1000000);
        ts.timebase_num = 1;
        ts.timebase_den = 1000000;
        return ts;
    }
    
    // Comparison operators
    auto operator<=>(const Timestamp& other) const {
        return ToSeconds() <=> other.ToSeconds();
    }
    bool operator==(const Timestamp& other) const {
        return ToSeconds() == other.ToSeconds();
    }
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Geometry Types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

template<typename T>
struct Size2D {
    T width = 0;
    T height = 0;
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr T Area() const noexcept { return width * height; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr f64 AspectRatio() const noexcept { 
        return height != 0 ? static_cast<f64>(width) / height : 0.0; 
    }
    
    bool operator==(const Size2D&) const = default;
    auto operator<=>(const Size2D&) const = default;
};

template<typename T>
struct Point2D {
    T x = 0;
    T y = 0;
    
    bool operator==(const Point2D&) const = default;
};

template<typename T>
struct Rect {
    T x = 0;
    T y = 0;
    T width = 0;
    T height = 0;
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr T Left() const noexcept { return x; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr T Top() const noexcept { return y; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr T Right() const noexcept { return x + width; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr T Bottom() const noexcept { return y + height; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr T Area() const noexcept { return width * height; }
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr Point2D<T> TopLeft() const noexcept { return {x, y}; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr Point2D<T> BottomRight() const noexcept { return {x + width, y + height}; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr Size2D<T> Size() const noexcept { return {width, height}; }
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool Contains(Point2D<T> point) const noexcept {
        return point.x >= x && point.x < Right() && point.y >= y && point.y < Bottom();
    }
    
    bool operator==(const Rect&) const = default;
};

using Size = Size2D<i32>;
using SizeU = Size2D<u32>;
using SizeF = Size2D<f32>;
using Point = Point2D<i32>;
using PointF = Point2D<f32>;
using Rectangle = Rect<i32>;
using RectangleF = Rect<f32>;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Color Types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct Color {
    f32 r = 0.0f;
    f32 g = 0.0f;
    f32 b = 0.0f;
    f32 a = 1.0f;
    
    static constexpr Color Black() { return {0, 0, 0, 1}; }
    static constexpr Color White() { return {1, 1, 1, 1}; }
    static constexpr Color Red() { return {1, 0, 0, 1}; }
    static constexpr Color Green() { return {0, 1, 0, 1}; }
    static constexpr Color Blue() { return {0, 0, 1, 1}; }
    static constexpr Color Transparent() { return {0, 0, 0, 0}; }
    
    [[nodiscard]] u32 ToRGBA8() const noexcept {
        return (static_cast<u32>(r * 255) << 24) |
               (static_cast<u32>(g * 255) << 16) |
               (static_cast<u32>(b * 255) << 8) |
               static_cast<u32>(a * 255);
    }
    
    static constexpr Color FromRGBA8(u32 rgba) noexcept {
        return {
            static_cast<f32>((rgba >> 24) & 0xFF) / 255.0f,
            static_cast<f32>((rgba >> 16) & 0xFF) / 255.0f,
            static_cast<f32>((rgba >> 8) & 0xFF) / 255.0f,
            static_cast<f32>(rgba & 0xFF) / 255.0f
        };
    }
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Media Types Enumerations
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @enum MediaType
 * @brief Type of media content
 */
enum class MediaType : u8 {
    Unknown = 0,
    Video,
    Audio,
    Subtitle,
    Data,
    Attachment
};

/**
 * @enum PixelFormat
 * @brief Video pixel formats
 */
enum class PixelFormat : u32 {
    Unknown = 0,
    
    // Packed RGB formats
    RGB24,
    BGR24,
    RGBA32,
    BGRA32,
    ARGB32,
    ABGR32,
    RGB565,
    RGB555,
    RGB48,
    RGBA64,
    
    // Planar YUV formats
    YUV420P,      // I420
    YUV422P,
    YUV444P,
    YUV420P10,    // 10-bit
    YUV420P12,    // 12-bit
    YUV420P16,    // 16-bit
    YUV422P10,
    YUV444P10,
    YUV444P16,
    
    // Semi-planar formats (NV12, P010, etc.)
    NV12,         // 4:2:0 semi-planar
    NV21,
    P010,         // 10-bit semi-planar
    P016,         // 16-bit semi-planar
    
    // Hardware formats
    D3D11,
    DXVA2,
    VAAPI,
    VDPAU,
    VideoToolbox,
    MediaCodec,
    CUDA,
    QSV,
    Vulkan,
    
    // HDR formats
    YUV420P10_HDR10,
    YUV420P10_HLG,
    YUV420P10_DolbyVision,
    
    // Other
    Gray8,
    Gray16,
    GrayF32
};

/**
 * @enum SampleFormat
 * @brief Audio sample formats
 */
enum class SampleFormat : u8 {
    Unknown = 0,
    
    // Integer formats
    U8,           // Unsigned 8-bit
    S16,          // Signed 16-bit
    S32,          // Signed 32-bit
    S64,          // Signed 64-bit
    
    // Floating point formats
    F32,          // 32-bit float
    F64,          // 64-bit double
    
    // Planar versions
    U8P,
    S16P,
    S32P,
    F32P,
    F64P
};

/**
 * @enum ChannelLayout
 * @brief Audio channel layouts
 */
enum class ChannelLayout : u32 {
    Unknown = 0,
    Mono = 1,
    Stereo = 2,
    Surround21 = 3,
    Surround30 = 4,
    Surround31 = 5,
    Quad = 6,
    Surround50 = 7,
    Surround51 = 8,
    Surround61 = 9,
    Surround71 = 10,
    Surround714 = 11,   // Dolby Atmos 7.1.4
    Surround916 = 12,   // 9.1.6 immersive
};

/**
 * @enum ColorSpace
 * @brief Video color spaces
 */
enum class ColorSpace : u8 {
    Unknown = 0,
    BT601,
    BT709,
    BT2020,
    SMPTE170M,
    SMPTE240M,
    BT2100_PQ,    // HDR10
    BT2100_HLG,   // HLG
    SRGB,
    AdobeRGB,
    DCI_P3,
    DisplayP3
};

/**
 * @enum ColorRange
 * @brief Video color range
 */
enum class ColorRange : u8 {
    Unknown = 0,
    Limited,      // 16-235 (TV)
    Full          // 0-255 (PC)
};

/**
 * @enum HDRType
 * @brief HDR format types
 */
enum class HDRType : u8 {
    None = 0,
    HDR10,
    HDR10Plus,
    DolbyVision,
    HLG,
    AdvancedHDR
};

/**
 * @enum CodecID
 * @brief Codec identifiers
 */
enum class CodecID : u32 {
    Unknown = 0,
    
    // Video codecs
    H264 = 0x1000,
    H265,
    H266,
    VP8,
    VP9,
    AV1,
    MPEG2,
    MPEG4,
    ProRes,
    DNxHD,
    MJPEG,
    Theora,
    WMV3,

    AAC = 0x2000,
    MP3,
    AC3,

    DTS,
    DTSHD,
    TrueHD,

    ALAC,

    Opus,

    // Subtitle codecs
    SRT = 0x3000,
    ASS,
    VTT,
    TTML,
    DVDSUB,
    DVBSUB,
    PGS,
    MOV_TEXT
};

/**
 * @enum ContainerFormat
 * @brief Media container formats
 */
enum class ContainerFormat : u16 {
    Unknown = 0,
    
    // Video containers
    MP4,
    MOV,
    MKV,
    WebM,
    AVI,
    WMV,
    FLV,
    TS,
    M2TS,

    DASH,
    HLS,
    SmoothStreaming
};

/**
 * @enum PlaybackState
 * @brief Player state enumeration
 */
enum class PlaybackState : u8 {
    Idle = 0,
    Opening,
    Buffering,
    Playing,
    Paused,
    Seeking,
    Stopped,
    EndOfMedia,
    Error
};

/**
 * @enum SeekMode

 */
enum class SeekMode : u8 {
    Precise = 0,      // Frame-accurate seek
    Keyframe,         // Seek to nearest keyframe
    Fast              // Fastest possible seek
};

/**
 * @enum LoopMode
 * @brief Playback loop modes
 */
enum class LoopMode : u8 {

    All,              // Loop entire playlist

};

 * @enum ScaleMode

 */

    Fit = 0,          // Fit within bounds (letterbox/pillarbox)
    Fill,             // Fill bounds (may crop)
    Stretch,          // Stretch to fill (may distort)
    Original,         // Original size
    Zoom,             // Zoom with crop
    Custom            // Custom scale factor
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Result Types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @enum ErrorCode
 * @brief Error codes for the engine
 */
enum class ErrorCode : u32 {
    Success = 0,
    
    // General errors
    Unknown = 1,
    InvalidArgument,
    InvalidState,
    NotInitialized,
    NotSupported,
    NotFound,
    AlreadyExists,
    OutOfMemory,
    PermissionDenied,
    Timeout,
    Cancelled,
    
    // File/IO errors
    FileNotFound = 100,
    FileAccessDenied,
    FileCorrupt,
    FileReadError,
    FileWriteError,
    
    // Media errors
    UnsupportedFormat = 200,
    UnsupportedCodec,
    DecoderError,
    EncoderError,
    DemuxerError,
    RenderError,
    
    // Network errors
    NetworkError = 300,
    ConnectionFailed,
    ConnectionTimeout,
    ProtocolError,
    AuthenticationFailed,
    
    // DRM errors
    DRMError = 400,
    LicenseExpired,
    LicenseNotFound,
    
    // Plugin errors
    PluginError = 500,
    PluginNotFound,
    PluginLoadFailed,
    
    // AI/ML errors
    InferenceError = 600,
    ModelNotFound,
    ModelLoadFailed
};

/**
 * @struct Error
 * @brief Error information structure
 */
struct Error {
    ErrorCode code = ErrorCode::Success;
    std::string message;
    std::string details;
    std::string source;  // File/function where error occurred
    i32 line = 0;
    
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsSuccess() const noexcept { return code == ErrorCode::Success; }
    [[nodiscard]] explicit operator bool() const noexcept { return !IsSuccess(); }
    
    static Error Success() { return {ErrorCode::Success, "", "", "", 0}; }
    static Error Make(ErrorCode code, std::string_view msg, std::string_view src = "", i32 ln = 0) {
        return {code, std::string(msg), "", std::string(src), ln};
    }
};

/**
 * @brief Result type for operations that can fail
 */
template<typename T>
using Result = std::expected<T, Error>;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Smart Pointers and Handles
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

template<typename T>
using Unique = std::unique_ptr<T>;

template<typename T>
using Shared = std::shared_ptr<T>;

template<typename T>
using Weak = std::weak_ptr<T>;

template<typename T>
using Ref = std::reference_wrapper<T>;

// Handle types (opaque pointers)
struct HandleBase {
    void* ptr = nullptr;
    u64 id = 0;
    u32 generation = 0;
    
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsValid() const noexcept { return ptr != nullptr; }
    [[nodiscard]] explicit operator bool() const noexcept { return IsValid(); }
};

using PlayerHandle = HandleBase;
using SourceHandle = HandleBase;
using TrackHandle = HandleBase;
using DecoderHandle = HandleBase;
using RendererHandle = HandleBase;
using PluginHandle = HandleBase;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Callback Types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

using ProgressCallback = std::function<void(f64 progress)>;
using CompletionCallback = std::function<void(const Error& error)>;
using DataCallback = std::function<void(std::span<const u8> data)>;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Utility Functions
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @brief Get string representation of MediaType
 */
constexpr std::string_view ToString(MediaType type) {
    switch (type) {
        case MediaType::Video: return "Video";
        case MediaType::Audio: return "Audio";
        case MediaType::Subtitle: return "Subtitle";
        case MediaType::Data: return "Data";
        case MediaType::Attachment: return "Attachment";
        default: return "Unknown";
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Get string representation of PlaybackState
 */
constexpr std::string_view ToString(PlaybackState state) {
    switch (state) {
        case PlaybackState::Idle: return "Idle";
        case PlaybackState::Opening: return "Opening";
        case PlaybackState::Buffering: return "Buffering";
        case PlaybackState::Playing: return "Playing";
        case PlaybackState::Paused: return "Paused";
        case PlaybackState::Seeking: return "Seeking";
        case PlaybackState::Stopped: return "Stopped";
        case PlaybackState::EndOfMedia: return "EndOfMedia";
        case PlaybackState::Error: return "Error";
        default: return "Unknown";
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Get bytes per sample for SampleFormat
 */
constexpr u32 GetBytesPerSample(SampleFormat format) {
    switch (format) {
        case SampleFormat::U8:
        case SampleFormat::U8P:
            return 1;
        case SampleFormat::S16:
        case SampleFormat::S16P:
            return 2;
        case SampleFormat::S32:
        case SampleFormat::S32P:
        case SampleFormat::F32:
        case SampleFormat::F32P:
            return 4;
        case SampleFormat::S64:
        case SampleFormat::F64:
        case SampleFormat::F64P:
            return 8;
        default:
            return 0;
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Get channel count for ChannelLayout
 */
constexpr u32 GetChannelCount(ChannelLayout layout) {
    switch (layout) {
        case ChannelLayout::Mono: return 1;
        case ChannelLayout::Stereo: return 2;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround21: return 3;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround30: return 3;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround31: return 4;
        case ChannelLayout::Quad: return 4;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround50: return 5;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround51: return 6;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround61: return 7;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround71: return 8;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround714: return 12;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround916: return 16;
        default: return 0;
    }
}

} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_CORE_TYPES_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/core/engine.hpp
// DESCRIPTION: Core engine interface
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#ifndef AETHER_CORE_ENGINE_HPP
#define AETHER_CORE_ENGINE_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"
#include "aether/config.hpp"

#include <memory>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>
#include <functional>

namespace aether {

// Forward declarations
class Player;
class PluginManager;
class AIEngine;
class Logger;

/**
 * @struct EngineCapabilities
 * @brief Describes engine capabilities
 */
struct EngineCapabilities {
    // Video capabilities
    bool hardware_decoding = false;
    bool hardware_encoding = false;
    std::vector<CodecID> supported_video_codecs;
    std::vector<PixelFormat> supported_pixel_formats;
    u32 max_video_width = 0;
    u32 max_video_height = 0;
    
    // Audio capabilities
    std::vector<CodecID> supported_audio_codecs;
    std::vector<SampleFormat> supported_sample_formats;
    u32 max_sample_rate = 0;
    u32 max_channels = 0;
    
    // Rendering capabilities
    bool vulkan_available = false;
    bool metal_available = false;
    bool d3d12_available = false;
    bool opengl_available = false;
    bool hdr_output = false;
    
    // AI capabilities
    bool ai_upscaling = false;
    bool ai_frame_interpolation = false;
    bool ai_audio_enhancement = false;
    std::vector<std::string> available_ai_backends;
    
    // Network capabilities
    bool streaming_support = false;
    std::vector<std::string> supported_protocols;
    
    // DRM capabilities
    bool drm_support = false;
    bool widevine_available = false;
    bool playready_available = false;
    bool fairplay_available = false;
};

/**
 * @struct EngineStatistics
 * @brief Runtime statistics
 */
struct EngineStatistics {
    // Memory
    usize total_memory_used = 0;
    usize video_memory_used = 0;
    usize audio_buffer_used = 0;
    usize cache_memory_used = 0;
    
    // Performance
    f64 average_decode_time_ms = 0;
    f64 average_render_time_ms = 0;
    f64 cpu_usage_percent = 0;
    f64 gpu_usage_percent = 0;
    
    // Counts
    u64 frames_decoded = 0;
    u64 frames_dropped = 0;
    u64 samples_processed = 0;
    u64 bytes_downloaded = 0;
    
    // Network
    f64 download_speed_mbps = 0;
    u32 active_connections = 0;
};

/**
 * @class Engine
 * @brief Core engine singleton managing all media operations
 */
class AETHER_API Engine {
public:
    /**
     * @brief Get the singleton engine instance
     * @return Reference to the engine
     */
    static Engine& Instance();
    
    /**
     * @brief Initialize the engine with configuration
     * @param config Engine configuration
     * @return Success or error
     */
    Result<void> Initialize(const Config& config = Config::Default());
    
    /**
     * @brief Shutdown the engine and release all resources
     */
    void Shutdown();
    
    /**
     * @brief Check if engine is initialized
     */
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsInitialized() const noexcept;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get current configuration
     */
    [[nodiscard]] const Config& GetConfig() const noexcept;
    
    /**
     * @brief Update configuration at runtime
     * @param config New configuration
     * @return Success or error
     */
    Result<void> UpdateConfig(const Config& config);
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Player Management
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Create a new player instance
     * @param name Optional player name for identification
     * @return Player instance or error
     */
    Result<Shared<Player>> CreatePlayer(std::string_view name = "");
    
    /**
     * @brief Destroy a player instance
     * @param player Player to destroy
     */
    void DestroyPlayer(Shared<Player>& player);
    
    /**
     * @brief Get all active players
     */
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<Shared<Player>> GetPlayers() const;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Subsystems
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Get plugin manager
     */
    [[nodiscard]] PluginManager& GetPluginManager();
    
    /**
     * @brief Get AI engine
     */
    [[nodiscard]] AIEngine& GetAIEngine();
    
    /**
     * @brief Get logger
     */
    [[nodiscard]] Logger& GetLogger();
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Capabilities & Statistics
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Get engine capabilities
     */
    [[nodiscard]] const EngineCapabilities& GetCapabilities() const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get runtime statistics
     */
    [[nodiscard]] EngineStatistics GetStatistics() const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Reset statistics counters
     */
    void ResetStatistics();
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Format Support
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Check if a codec is supported
     */
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsCodecSupported(CodecID codec) const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Check if a container format is supported
     */
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsContainerSupported(ContainerFormat format) const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Check if a protocol is supported
     */
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsProtocolSupported(std::string_view protocol) const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get all supported file extensions
     */
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::string> GetSupportedExtensions() const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get MIME types for supported formats
     */
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::string> GetSupportedMimeTypes() const;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Utility Functions
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Probe a media source and return its information
     * @param url URL or path to media
     * @return Media information or error
     */
    Result<struct MediaInfo> ProbeMedia(std::string_view url);
    
    /**
     * @brief Create a thumbnail from media
     * @param url URL or path to media
     * @param time_seconds Time position for thumbnail
     * @param size Desired thumbnail size
     * @return Thumbnail data or error
     */
    Result<std::vector<u8>> CreateThumbnail(
        std::string_view url,
        f64 time_seconds,
        Size size
    );
    
    /**
     * @brief Register a custom protocol handler
     * @param protocol Protocol name (e.g., "myproto")
     * @param handler Handler function
     */
    Result<void> RegisterProtocol(
        std::string_view protocol,
        std::function<Result<std::vector<u8>>(std::string_view url)> handler
    );
    
private:
    Engine();
    ~Engine();
    Engine(const Engine&) = delete;
    Engine& operator=(const Engine&) = delete;
    
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

/**
 * @brief Convenience function to get engine instance
 */
inline Engine& GetEngine() { return Engine::Instance(); }

} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_CORE_ENGINE_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/core/player.hpp
// DESCRIPTION: Main player interface
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#ifndef AETHER_CORE_PLAYER_HPP
#define AETHER_CORE_PLAYER_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"
#include "aether/core/media_source.hpp"
#include "aether/core/event_system.hpp"

#include <memory>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>
#include <optional>
#include <functional>

namespace aether {

// Forward declarations
class VideoRenderer;
class AudioRenderer;
class SubtitleRenderer;
class FilterChain;

/**
 * @struct PlayerOptions
 * @brief Configuration options for player
 */
struct PlayerOptions {
    // Video options
    bool enable_hardware_decoding = true;
    bool enable_hardware_rendering = true;
    PixelFormat preferred_pixel_format = PixelFormat::Unknown;  // Auto-select
    ScaleMode scale_mode = ScaleMode::Fit;
    bool enable_deinterlacing = true;
    bool enable_hdr_output = true;
    
    // Audio options
    SampleFormat preferred_sample_format = SampleFormat::F32;
    ChannelLayout preferred_channel_layout = ChannelLayout::Stereo;
    u32 preferred_sample_rate = 48000;
    f32 initial_volume = 1.0f;
    bool audio_exclusive_mode = false;
    
    // Playback options
    f32 initial_playback_speed = 1.0f;
    LoopMode loop_mode = LoopMode::None;
    SeekMode seek_mode = SeekMode::Precise;
    
    // Buffer options
    u32 video_buffer_frames = 8;
    u32 audio_buffer_ms = 200;
    u32 network_buffer_ms = 5000;
    
    // AI enhancement
    bool enable_ai_upscaling = false;
    bool enable_ai_frame_interpolation = false;
    bool enable_ai_audio_enhancement = false;
    
    // Subtitle options
    bool auto_select_subtitle = true;
    std::string preferred_subtitle_language;
    
    // Misc
    bool enable_analytics = false;
    std::string analytics_endpoint;
};

/**
 * @struct VideoTrackInfo
 * @brief Information about a video track
 */
struct VideoTrackInfo {
    i32 index = -1;
    CodecID codec = CodecID::Unknown;
    u32 width = 0;
    u32 height = 0;
    f64 frame_rate = 0;
    i64 bitrate = 0;
    PixelFormat pixel_format = PixelFormat::Unknown;
    ColorSpace color_space = ColorSpace::Unknown;
    ColorRange color_range = ColorRange::Unknown;
    HDRType hdr_type = HDRType::None;
    std::string language;
    std::string title;
    bool is_default = false;
};

/**
 * @struct AudioTrackInfo
 * @brief Information about an audio track
 */
struct AudioTrackInfo {
    i32 index = -1;
    CodecID codec = CodecID::Unknown;
    u32 sample_rate = 0;
    u32 channels = 0;
    ChannelLayout channel_layout = ChannelLayout::Unknown;
    SampleFormat sample_format = SampleFormat::Unknown;
    i64 bitrate = 0;
    std::string language;
    std::string title;
    bool is_default = false;
};

/**
 * @struct SubtitleTrackInfo
 * @brief Information about a subtitle track
 */
struct SubtitleTrackInfo {
    i32 index = -1;
    CodecID codec = CodecID::Unknown;
    std::string language;
    std::string title;
    bool is_forced = false;
    bool is_default = false;
    bool is_external = false;
    std::string external_path;
};

/**
 * @struct MediaInfo
 * @brief Complete information about a media source
 */
struct MediaInfo {
    std::string url;
    std::string title;
    std::string format_name;
    ContainerFormat container = ContainerFormat::Unknown;
    
    Duration duration{0};
    i64 file_size = 0;
    i64 total_bitrate = 0;
    
    std::vector<VideoTrackInfo> video_tracks;
    std::vector<AudioTrackInfo> audio_tracks;
    std::vector<SubtitleTrackInfo> subtitle_tracks;
    
    std::map<std::string, std::string> metadata;
    std::vector<struct Chapter> chapters;
    
    bool is_live = false;
    bool is_seekable = true;
    bool has_drm = false;
    std::string drm_system;
};

/**
 * @struct Chapter
 * @brief Chapter information
 */
struct Chapter {
    i32 index = 0;
    std::string title;
    Timestamp start;
    Timestamp end;
};

/**
 * @struct PlaybackStatus
 * @brief Current playback status
 */
struct PlaybackStatus {
    PlaybackState state = PlaybackState::Idle;
    
    Timestamp position;
    Timestamp duration;
    f64 buffered_percent = 0;
    
    f32 volume = 1.0f;
    bool is_muted = false;
    f32 playback_speed = 1.0f;
    
    // Currently selected tracks
    i32 current_video_track = -1;
    i32 current_audio_track = -1;
    i32 current_subtitle_track = -1;
    
    // Performance metrics
    f64 current_fps = 0;
    f64 decode_fps = 0;
    i64 dropped_frames = 0;
    
    // Network (for streaming)
    f64 download_speed_mbps = 0;
    i64 bytes_downloaded = 0;
    
    Error last_error;
};

/**
 * @class Player
 * @brief Main player class for media playback
 */
class AETHER_API Player : public std::enable_shared_from_this<Player> {
public:
    virtual ~Player() = default;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Playback Control
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Open a media source
     * @param url URL or file path
     * @param options Optional playback options
     * @return Success or error
     */
    virtual Result<void> Open(
        std::string_view url,
        const PlayerOptions& options = PlayerOptions{}
    ) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Open from a MediaSource object
     */
    virtual Result<void> Open(
        Shared<MediaSource> source,
        const PlayerOptions& options = PlayerOptions{}
    ) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Close current media
     */
    virtual void Close() = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Start or resume playback
     */
    virtual Result<void> Play() = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Pause playback
     */
    virtual Result<void> Pause() = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Toggle play/pause
     */
    virtual Result<void> TogglePlayPause() = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Stop playback and reset to beginning
     */
    virtual Result<void> Stop() = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Seek to position
     * @param timestamp Target position
     * @param mode Seek mode (precise, keyframe, fast)
     */
    virtual Result<void> Seek(Timestamp timestamp, SeekMode mode = SeekMode::Precise) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Seek to position in seconds
     */
    virtual Result<void> SeekToTime(f64 seconds, SeekMode mode = SeekMode::Precise) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Seek relative to current position
     */
    virtual Result<void> SeekRelative(f64 seconds) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Seek to specific frame number
     */
    virtual Result<void> SeekToFrame(i64 frame_number) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Step forward one frame (when paused)
     */
    virtual Result<void> StepForward() = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Step backward one frame (when paused)
     */
    virtual Result<void> StepBackward() = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // State & Info
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Get current playback state
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual PlaybackState GetState() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get detailed playback status
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual PlaybackStatus GetStatus() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get media information
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::optional<MediaInfo> GetMediaInfo() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get current position
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual Timestamp GetPosition() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get total duration
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual Timestamp GetDuration() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Check if media is currently playing
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool IsPlaying() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Check if media is paused
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool IsPaused() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Check if seeking is in progress
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool IsSeeking() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Check if buffering
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool IsBuffering() const = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Volume & Audio Control
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Set volume level
     * @param volume Volume level (0.0 to 1.0, can exceed for boost)
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetVolume(f32 volume) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get current volume
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual f32 GetVolume() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Mute/unmute audio
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetMuted(bool muted) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Check if muted
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool IsMuted() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Toggle mute state
     */
    virtual Result<void> ToggleMute() = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Set audio delay (sync adjustment)
     * @param delay_ms Audio delay in milliseconds (positive = delay audio)
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetAudioDelay(i32 delay_ms) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get current audio delay
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual i32 GetAudioDelay() const = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Playback Speed
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Set playback speed
     * @param speed Speed multiplier (e.g., 0.5 = half, 2.0 = double)
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetPlaybackSpeed(f32 speed) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get current playback speed
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual f32 GetPlaybackSpeed() const = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Track Selection
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Get video tracks
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::vector<VideoTrackInfo> GetVideoTracks() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get audio tracks
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::vector<AudioTrackInfo> GetAudioTracks() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get subtitle tracks
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::vector<SubtitleTrackInfo> GetSubtitleTracks() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Select video track
     * @param index Track index (-1 to disable)
     */
    virtual Result<void> SelectVideoTrack(i32 index) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Select audio track
     * @param index Track index (-1 to disable)
     */
    virtual Result<void> SelectAudioTrack(i32 index) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Select subtitle track
     * @param index Track index (-1 to disable)
     */
    virtual Result<void> SelectSubtitleTrack(i32 index) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Add external subtitle file
     */
    virtual Result<i32> AddSubtitleFile(std::string_view path) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Set subtitle delay
     * @param delay_ms Delay in milliseconds (positive = delay subtitles)
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetSubtitleDelay(i32 delay_ms) = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Video Output
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Set video output window/surface
     * @param window_handle Platform-specific window handle
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetVideoOutput(void* window_handle) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Set video renderer
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetVideoRenderer(Shared<VideoRenderer> renderer) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get current video size
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual Size GetVideoSize() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Set scale mode
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetScaleMode(ScaleMode mode) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Set custom aspect ratio (0 = original)
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetAspectRatio(f32 ratio) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Set video crop area
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetCrop(Rectangle crop) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Set video rotation (0, 90, 180, 270)
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetRotation(i32 degrees) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Enable/disable video
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetVideoEnabled(bool enabled) = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Filters & Effects
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Get video filter chain
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual Shared<FilterChain> GetVideoFilters() = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get audio filter chain
     */
    ```cpp
    [[nodiscard]] virtual Shared<FilterChain> GetAudioFilters() = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Set equalizer preset
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetEqualizerPreset(std::string_view preset) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Set custom equalizer bands
     * @param bands Array of gain values in dB for each frequency band
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetEqualizerBands(std::span<const f32> bands) = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // AI Enhancement
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Enable/disable AI video upscaling
     * @param enabled Whether to enable
     * @param scale Scale factor (2x, 4x)
     * @param model Model name (e.g., "esrgan", "real-esrgan")
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetAIUpscaling(bool enabled, f32 scale = 2.0f, 
                                        std::string_view model = "default") = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Enable/disable AI frame interpolation
     * @param enabled Whether to enable
     * @param target_fps Target frame rate
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetAIFrameInterpolation(bool enabled, f32 target_fps = 60.0f) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Enable/disable AI audio enhancement
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetAIAudioEnhancement(bool enabled) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Enable/disable AI noise reduction
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetAIDenoising(bool enabled, f32 strength = 0.5f) = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Chapters & Bookmarks
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Get chapters
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::vector<Chapter> GetChapters() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Go to specific chapter
     */
    virtual Result<void> GoToChapter(i32 index) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Go to next chapter
     */
    virtual Result<void> NextChapter() = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Go to previous chapter
     */
    virtual Result<void> PreviousChapter() = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Set A-B loop points
     * @param a Start point (nullopt to clear)
     * @param b End point (nullopt to clear)
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetABLoop(std::optional<Timestamp> a, 
                                   std::optional<Timestamp> b) = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Screenshots & Recording
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Take a screenshot
     * @param path Output file path (empty for auto-generated)
     * @param format Image format ("png", "jpg", "webp")
     * @return Path to saved screenshot
     */
    virtual Result<std::string> TakeScreenshot(std::string_view path = "",
                                                std::string_view format = "png") = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Start recording playback
     * @param path Output file path
     * @param options Recording options
     */
    virtual Result<void> StartRecording(std::string_view path,
                                        const struct RecordingOptions& options = {}) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Stop recording
     */
    virtual Result<void> StopRecording() = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Check if recording is active
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool IsRecording() const = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Events
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Subscribe to player events
     * @param callback Event callback function
     * @return Subscription handle (use to unsubscribe)
     */
    virtual u64 Subscribe(std::function<void(const PlayerEvent&)> callback) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Unsubscribe from events
     * @param handle Subscription handle
     */
    virtual void Unsubscribe(u64 handle) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Set callback for specific event type
     */
    template<typename EventType>
    u64 On(std::function<void(const EventType&)> callback);
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Playlist
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Add item to playlist
     */
    virtual Result<void> AddToPlaylist(std::string_view url) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Add multiple items to playlist
     */
    virtual Result<void> AddToPlaylist(std::span<const std::string> urls) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Clear playlist
     */
    virtual void ClearPlaylist() = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get playlist items
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::vector<std::string> GetPlaylist() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get current playlist index
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual i32 GetPlaylistIndex() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Play next item
     */
    virtual Result<void> PlayNext() = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Play previous item
     */
    virtual Result<void> PlayPrevious() = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Play specific playlist item
     */
    virtual Result<void> PlayAt(i32 index) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Set loop mode
     */
    virtual Result<void> SetLoopMode(LoopMode mode) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Shuffle playlist
     */
    virtual void ShufflePlaylist() = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Miscellaneous
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Get player name/identifier
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::string_view GetName() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get player options
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual const PlayerOptions& GetOptions() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get last error
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual Error GetLastError() const = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Execute custom command
     */
    virtual Result<std::string> ExecuteCommand(std::string_view command,
                                                std::span<const std::string> args = {}) = 0;

protected:
    Player() = default;
};

/**
 * @struct RecordingOptions
 * @brief Options for recording playback
 */
struct RecordingOptions {
    CodecID video_codec = CodecID::H264;
    CodecID audio_codec = CodecID::AAC;
    ContainerFormat container = ContainerFormat::MP4;
    
    i64 video_bitrate = 8000000;  // 8 Mbps
    i64 audio_bitrate = 192000;   // 192 kbps
    
    bool include_subtitles = true;
    bool include_ai_enhancements = true;
};

} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_CORE_PLAYER_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/core/event_system.hpp
// DESCRIPTION: Event system for player notifications
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#ifndef AETHER_CORE_EVENT_SYSTEM_HPP
#define AETHER_CORE_EVENT_SYSTEM_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"

#include <variant>
#include <string>
#include <functional>
#include <memory>
#include <vector>

namespace aether {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Event Types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @struct EventBase
 * @brief Base class for all events
 */
struct EventBase {
    TimePoint timestamp;
    std::string source;
    
    EventBase() : timestamp(Clock::now()) {}
};

/**
 * @struct StateChangedEvent
 * @brief Fired when playback state changes
 */
struct StateChangedEvent : EventBase {
    PlaybackState old_state;
    PlaybackState new_state;
};

/**
 * @struct MediaOpenedEvent
 * @brief Fired when media is successfully opened
 */
struct MediaOpenedEvent : EventBase {
    std::string url;
    MediaInfo media_info;
};

/**
 * @struct MediaClosedEvent
 * @brief Fired when media is closed
 */
struct MediaClosedEvent : EventBase {
    std::string url;
};

/**
 * @struct PositionChangedEvent
 * @brief Fired periodically during playback
 */
struct PositionChangedEvent : EventBase {
    Timestamp position;
    Timestamp duration;
    f64 progress_percent;
};

/**
 * @struct SeekStartedEvent
 * @brief Fired when seeking begins
 */
struct SeekStartedEvent : EventBase {
    Timestamp from_position;
    Timestamp to_position;
};

/**
 * @struct SeekCompletedEvent
 * @brief Fired when seeking completes
 */
struct SeekCompletedEvent : EventBase {
    Timestamp position;
    bool success;
};

/**
 * @struct BufferingEvent
 * @brief Fired during buffering
 */
struct BufferingEvent : EventBase {
    f64 percent;
    bool is_buffering;
    i64 bytes_buffered;
    Duration estimated_time;
};

/**
 * @struct VolumeChangedEvent
 * @brief Fired when volume changes
 */
struct VolumeChangedEvent : EventBase {
    f32 old_volume;
    f32 new_volume;
    bool is_muted;
};

/**
 * @struct TrackChangedEvent
 * @brief Fired when a track is changed
 */
struct TrackChangedEvent : EventBase {
    MediaType track_type;
    i32 old_track;
    i32 new_track;
};

/**
 * @struct ErrorEvent
 * @brief Fired when an error occurs
 */
struct ErrorEvent : EventBase {
    Error error;
    bool is_fatal;
};

/**
 * @struct EndOfMediaEvent
 * @brief Fired when media playback reaches the end
 */
struct EndOfMediaEvent : EventBase {
    std::string url;
};

/**
 * @struct ChapterChangedEvent
 * @brief Fired when chapter changes
 */
struct ChapterChangedEvent : EventBase {
    i32 old_chapter;
    i32 new_chapter;
    std::string chapter_title;
};

/**
 * @struct SubtitleEvent
 * @brief Fired when subtitle text changes
 */
struct SubtitleEvent : EventBase {
    std::string text;
    Timestamp start;
    Timestamp end;
    i32 track_index;
};

/**
 * @struct MetadataEvent
 * @brief Fired when metadata is updated
 */
struct MetadataEvent : EventBase {
    std::string key;
    std::string value;
};

/**
 * @struct VideoResizedEvent
 * @brief Fired when video size changes
 */
struct VideoResizedEvent : EventBase {
    Size old_size;
    Size new_size;
};

/**
 * @struct FrameDroppedEvent
 * @brief Fired when frames are dropped
 */
struct FrameDroppedEvent : EventBase {
    i64 frame_number;
    i64 total_dropped;
    std::string reason;
};

/**
 * @struct NetworkEvent
 * @brief Fired for network-related events
 */
struct NetworkEvent : EventBase {
    enum class Type {
        Connected,
        Disconnected,
        SpeedChanged,
        Error
    } type;
    
    std::string url;
    f64 download_speed_mbps;
    Error error;
};

/**
 * @struct AIProcessingEvent
 * @brief Fired for AI processing events
 */
struct AIProcessingEvent : EventBase {
    enum class Type {
        Started,
        Progress,
        Completed,
        Error
    } type;
    
    std::string processor_name;
    f64 progress_percent;
    Duration processing_time;
};

/**
 * @struct PlaylistEvent
 * @brief Fired for playlist changes
 */
struct PlaylistEvent : EventBase {
    enum class Type {
        ItemAdded,
        ItemRemoved,
        IndexChanged,
        Cleared,
        Shuffled
    } type;
    
    i32 index;
    std::string url;
};

/**
 * @struct RecordingEvent
 * @brief Fired for recording events
 */
struct RecordingEvent : EventBase {
    enum class Type {
        Started,
        Progress,
        Stopped,
        Error
    } type;
    
    std::string output_path;
    Duration recorded_duration;
    i64 file_size;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Event Variant
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @brief Variant containing all possible event types
 */
using PlayerEvent = std::variant<
    StateChangedEvent,
    MediaOpenedEvent,
    MediaClosedEvent,
    PositionChangedEvent,
    SeekStartedEvent,
    SeekCompletedEvent,
    BufferingEvent,
    VolumeChangedEvent,
    TrackChangedEvent,
    ErrorEvent,
    EndOfMediaEvent,
    ChapterChangedEvent,
    SubtitleEvent,
    MetadataEvent,
    VideoResizedEvent,
    FrameDroppedEvent,
    NetworkEvent,
    AIProcessingEvent,
    PlaylistEvent,
    RecordingEvent
>;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Event Dispatcher
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @class EventDispatcher
 * @brief Thread-safe event dispatcher
 */
class AETHER_API EventDispatcher {
public:
    using Callback = std::function<void(const PlayerEvent&)>;
    
    EventDispatcher();
    ~EventDispatcher();
    
    /**
     * @brief Subscribe to all events
     * @return Subscription ID
     */
    u64 Subscribe(Callback callback);
    
    /**
     * @brief Subscribe to specific event type
     */
    template<typename EventType>
    u64 Subscribe(std::function<void(const EventType&)> callback);
    
    /**
     * @brief Unsubscribe
     */
    void Unsubscribe(u64 id);
    
    /**
     * @brief Clear all subscriptions
     */
    void Clear();
    
    /**
     * @brief Dispatch an event
     */
    template<typename EventType>
    void Dispatch(const EventType& event);
    
    /**
     * @brief Dispatch event asynchronously
     */
    template<typename EventType>
    void DispatchAsync(EventType event);
    
    /**
     * @brief Process pending async events
     */
    void ProcessPending();
    
    /**
     * @brief Get number of subscribers
     */
    [[nodiscard]] usize GetSubscriberCount() const;

private:
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Event Bus (Global)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @class EventBus
 * @brief Global event bus for engine-wide events
 */
class AETHER_API EventBus {
public:
    static EventBus& Instance();
    
    /**
     * @brief Subscribe to engine events
     */
    u64 Subscribe(EventDispatcher::Callback callback);
    
    /**
     * @brief Unsubscribe
     */
    void Unsubscribe(u64 id);
    
    /**
     * @brief Publish event
     */
    template<typename EventType>
    void Publish(const EventType& event);
    
private:
    EventBus();
    ~EventBus();
    
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_CORE_EVENT_SYSTEM_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/config.hpp
// DESCRIPTION: Configuration system
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#ifndef AETHER_CONFIG_HPP
#define AETHER_CONFIG_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"

#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <map>

namespace aether {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Configuration Value Types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

using ConfigValue = std::variant<
    bool,
    i64,
    f64,
    std::string,
    std::vector<std::string>,
    std::map<std::string, std::string>
>;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Configuration Categories
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @struct VideoConfig
 * @brief Video-related configuration
 */
struct VideoConfig {
    // Decoding
    bool enable_hardware_decoding = true;
    std::vector<std::string> preferred_hw_decoders;  // e.g., ["nvdec", "vaapi", "qsv"]
    u32 decode_thread_count = 0;  // 0 = auto
    
    // Rendering
    std::string preferred_renderer;  // "vulkan", "metal", "d3d12", "opengl", "auto"
    bool enable_vsync = true;
    bool enable_triple_buffering = true;
    u32 render_queue_size = 3;
    
    // Output
    bool enable_hdr_output = true;
    bool enable_10bit_output = true;
    ColorSpace output_color_space = ColorSpace::Unknown;  // Auto
    
    // Quality
    ScaleMode default_scale_mode = ScaleMode::Fit;
    std::string scale_algorithm = "lanczos";  // "bilinear", "bicubic", "lanczos", "spline"
    bool enable_deinterlacing = true;
    std::string deinterlace_mode = "auto";  // "auto", "bob", "yadif", "bwdif"
    
    // Performance
    u32 max_video_width = 0;   // 0 = unlimited
    u32 max_video_height = 0;  // 0 = unlimited
    bool drop_frames_on_slow_decode = true;
};

/**
 * @struct AudioConfig
 * @brief Audio-related configuration
 */
struct AudioConfig {
    // Output
    std::string preferred_output_device;  // Empty = default device
    bool enable_exclusive_mode = false;
    u32 output_sample_rate = 0;  // 0 = device default
    ChannelLayout output_channel_layout = ChannelLayout::Unknown;  // Auto
    SampleFormat output_sample_format = SampleFormat::F32;
    
    // Buffering
    u32 buffer_size_ms = 50;
    u32 latency_target_ms = 20;
    
    // Processing
    bool enable_normalization = false;
    f32 normalization_target_lufs = -14.0f;
    bool enable_dynamic_range_compression = false;
    f32 drc_ratio = 4.0f;
    
    // Spatial audio
    bool enable_spatial_audio = true;
    bool enable_head_tracking = true;
    
    // Other
    f32 default_volume = 1.0f;
    bool remember_volume = true;
};

/**
 * @struct NetworkConfig
 * @brief Network-related configuration
 */
struct NetworkConfig {
    // Connections
    u32 max_connections = 8;
    u32 connection_timeout_ms = 30000;
    u32 read_timeout_ms = 15000;
    
    // Buffering
    u32 initial_buffer_ms = 5000;
    u32 rebuffer_ms = 2000;
    u32 max_buffer_ms = 60000;
    
    // Bandwidth
    bool adaptive_bitrate = true;
    i64 max_bitrate = 0;  // 0 = unlimited
    i64 min_bitrate = 0;

    std::string proxy_url;
    std::string proxy_username;
    std::string proxy_password;
    
    // User agent

    // Headers

    // Cache
    bool enable_disk_cache = true;
    std::string cache_directory;
    i64 max_cache_size_mb = 1024;
    u32 cache_expiry_days = 7;
    
    // Security
    bool verify_ssl_certificates = true;
    std::string ssl_ca_bundle_path;
};

/**
 * @struct AIConfig

 */
struct AIConfig {
    // General
    bool enable_ai_features = true;
    std::string preferred_backend;  // "onnx", "tensorrt", "coreml", "openvino", "auto"
    std::string models_directory;
    bool auto_download_models = true;
    
    // Upscaling
    bool enable_upscaling = false;
    std::string upscaling_model = "real-esrgan-x2";
    f32 upscaling_quality = 0.8f;  // 0-1, higher = better quality, slower
    
    // Frame interpolation
    bool enable_frame_interpolation = false;
    std::string interpolation_model = "rife-v4";
    f32 target_fps = 60.0f;
    
    // Audio enhancement
    bool enable_audio_enhancement = false;
    bool enable_noise_suppression = true;
