```cpp
    // Content analysis
    bool enable_scene_detection = false;
    bool enable_face_detection = false;
    bool enable_speech_recognition = false;
    std::string speech_recognition_language = "en";
    
    // Performance
    bool use_gpu_acceleration = true;
    u32 max_batch_size = 1;
    u32 inference_threads = 0;  // 0 = auto
    i64 max_gpu_memory_mb = 0;  // 0 = auto
};

/**
 * @struct SubtitleConfig
 * @brief Subtitle-related configuration
 */
struct SubtitleConfig {

    bool prefer_forced = false;

/**

    bool include_thread_id = false;
    bool include_source_location = false;

};

};

/**

    std::string api_key;
    bool collect_playback_metrics = true;
    bool collect_error_reports = true;
    bool collect_performance_metrics = true;
    u32 flush_interval_seconds = 30;
    bool respect_do_not_track = true;
};

/**
 * @struct DRMConfig
 * @brief DRM configuration
 */
struct DRMConfig {
    bool enable_drm = true;
    std::string license_server_url;
    std::map<std::string, std::string> license_headers;
    bool persist_licenses = true;
    std::string license_storage_path;
    u32 license_request_timeout_ms = 30000;
    bool hardware_security_required = false;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Main Configuration Class
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @class Config
 * @brief Main configuration container
 */
class AETHER_API Config {
public:
    Config();
    ~Config();
    Config(const Config& other);
    Config& operator=(const Config& other);
    Config(Config&& other) noexcept;
    Config& operator=(Config&& other) noexcept;
    
    // Subsystem configurations
    VideoConfig video;
    AudioConfig audio;
    NetworkConfig network;
    AIConfig ai;
    SubtitleConfig subtitle;
    CacheConfig cache;
    LogConfig log;
    PluginConfig plugin;
    AnalyticsConfig analytics;
    DRMConfig drm;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Factory Methods
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Get default configuration
     */
    static Config Default();
    
    /**
     * @brief Get configuration optimized for performance
     */
    static Config HighPerformance();
    
    /**
     * @brief Get configuration optimized for quality
     */
    static Config HighQuality();
    
    /**
     * @brief Get configuration for low-power devices
     */
    static Config LowPower();
    
    /**
     * @brief Get minimal configuration
     */
    static Config Minimal();
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Load/Save
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Load configuration from file
     * @param path Path to config file (JSON, YAML, or TOML)
     */
    static Result<Config> LoadFromFile(std::string_view path);
    
    /**
     * @brief Save configuration to file
     */
    Result<void> SaveToFile(std::string_view path) const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Load from JSON string
     */
    static Result<Config> LoadFromJSON(std::string_view json);
    
    /**
     * @brief Convert to JSON string
     */
    [[nodiscard]] std::string ToJSON(bool pretty = true) const;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Dynamic Configuration
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Set a configuration value by path
     * @param path Dot-separated path (e.g., "video.enable_hdr_output")
     * @param value Value to set
     */
    Result<void> Set(std::string_view path, const ConfigValue& value);
    
    /**
     * @brief Get a configuration value by path
     */
    [[nodiscard]] std::optional<ConfigValue> Get(std::string_view path) const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get value with default
     */
    template<typename T>
    [[nodiscard]] T GetOr(std::string_view path, T default_value) const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Check if path exists
     */
    [[nodiscard]] bool Has(std::string_view path) const;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Validation
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Validate configuration
     * @return List of validation errors (empty if valid)
     */
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::string> Validate() const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Check if configuration is valid
     */
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsValid() const;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Merge
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Merge another configuration into this one
     * @param other Configuration to merge
     * @param override_existing If true, other's values override existing
     */
    void Merge(const Config& other, bool override_existing = true);
    
private:
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_CONFIG_HPP
```

# 6. AI/ML FEATURES

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/ai/inference_engine.hpp
// DESCRIPTION: AI/ML inference engine interface
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#ifndef AETHER_AI_INFERENCE_ENGINE_HPP
#define AETHER_AI_INFERENCE_ENGINE_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"
#include "aether/ai/model.hpp"

#include <vector>
#include <memory>
#include <span>
#include <functional>

namespace aether {
namespace ai {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Backend Types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @enum InferenceBackend
 * @brief Supported inference backends
 */
enum class InferenceBackend : u8 {
    Auto = 0,       // Auto-select best available
    ONNX,           // ONNX Runtime (cross-platform)
    TensorRT,       // NVIDIA TensorRT (CUDA)
    CoreML,         // Apple CoreML (macOS/iOS)
    OpenVINO,       // Intel OpenVINO
    DirectML,       // Windows DirectML
    NNAPI,          // Android Neural Networks API
    CPU,            // CPU fallback (XNNPACK)
    Vulkan,         // Vulkan Compute
    Metal,          // Metal Performance Shaders
    CUDA,           // Direct CUDA
    WebNN           // Web Neural Network API
};

/**
 * @enum ExecutionProvider
 * @brief Execution providers for inference
 */
enum class ExecutionProvider : u16 {
    CPU = 0,
    CUDA,
    cuDNN,
    TensorRT,
    ROCm,
    DirectML,
    OpenVINO,
    CoreML,
    NNAPI,
    XNNPACK,
    WebNN,
    Vulkan,
    Metal,
    OpenCL
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Tensor Types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @enum TensorDataType
 * @brief Data types for tensors
 */
enum class TensorDataType : u8 {
    Float32,
    Float16,
    BFloat16,
    Int32,
    Int64,
    Int16,
    Int8,
    UInt8,
    Bool
};

/**
 * @struct TensorShape
 * @brief Shape of a tensor
 */
struct TensorShape {
    std::vector<i64> dims;
    
    [[nodiscard]] i64 TotalElements() const {
        i64 total = 1;
        for (auto d : dims) total *= d;
        return total;
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] usize Rank() const { return dims.size(); }
    
    [[nodiscard]] bool operator==(const TensorShape& other) const {
        return dims == other.dims;
    }
};

/**
 * @class Tensor
 * @brief Multi-dimensional array for inference
 */
class AETHER_API Tensor {
public:
    Tensor();
    Tensor(TensorShape shape, TensorDataType dtype);
    Tensor(TensorShape shape, TensorDataType dtype, void* external_data, bool owns_data = false);
    ~Tensor();
    
    Tensor(const Tensor& other);
    Tensor& operator=(const Tensor& other);
    Tensor(Tensor&& other) noexcept;
    Tensor& operator=(Tensor&& other) noexcept;
    
    // Shape and type
    [[nodiscard]] const TensorShape& Shape() const;
    [[nodiscard]] TensorDataType DataType() const;
    [[nodiscard]] i64 TotalElements() const;
    [[nodiscard]] usize ByteSize() const;
    
    // Data access
    [[nodiscard]] void* Data();
    [[nodiscard]] const void* Data() const;
    
    template<typename T>
    [[nodiscard]] T* DataAs() { return static_cast<T*>(Data()); }
    
    template<typename T>
    [[nodiscard]] const T* DataAs() const { return static_cast<const T*>(Data()); }
    
    template<typename T>
    [[nodiscard]] std::span<T> AsSpan() { 
        return std::span<T>(DataAs<T>(), TotalElements()); 
    }
    
    // Creation helpers
    static Tensor FromImage(const u8* data, u32 width, u32 height, u32 channels, bool normalize = true);
    static Tensor FromAudio(const f32* data, usize samples, u32 channels);
    static Tensor Zeros(TensorShape shape, TensorDataType dtype = TensorDataType::Float32);
    static Tensor Ones(TensorShape shape, TensorDataType dtype = TensorDataType::Float32);
    
    // Operations
    Tensor Reshape(TensorShape new_shape) const;
    Tensor Permute(std::span<const i64> order) const;
    Tensor ToDevice(ExecutionProvider device) const;
    Tensor ToCPU() const;
    
    // Validation
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsValid() const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsContiguous() const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsOnGPU() const;

private:
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Inference Session
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @struct SessionOptions
 * @brief Options for inference session
 */
struct SessionOptions {
    InferenceBackend backend = InferenceBackend::Auto;
    std::vector<ExecutionProvider> execution_providers;
    
    // Threading
    u32 intra_op_threads = 0;  // 0 = auto
    u32 inter_op_threads = 0;
    
    // Memory
    bool enable_memory_arena = true;
    i64 memory_limit_mb = 0;  // 0 = no limit
    
    // Optimization
    enum class OptimizationLevel {
        None = 0,
        Basic = 1,
        Extended = 2,
        All = 99
    } optimization_level = OptimizationLevel::All;
    
    bool enable_profiling = false;
    
    // GPU options
    i32 gpu_device_id = 0;
    bool enable_cuda_graph = true;
    bool enable_fp16 = true;
    bool enable_int8 = false;
    
    // Dynamic batching
    bool enable_dynamic_batching = false;
    u32 max_batch_size = 1;
    
    // Caching
    bool enable_model_caching = true;
    std::string cache_directory;
};

/**
 * @struct InferenceResult
 * @brief Result of an inference operation
 */
struct InferenceResult {
    std::vector<Tensor> outputs;
    Duration inference_time;
    Duration pre_process_time;
    Duration post_process_time;
    i64 memory_used;
    bool success = true;
    Error error;
};

/**
 * @class InferenceSession
 * @brief Manages a loaded model for inference
 */
class AETHER_API InferenceSession {
public:
    virtual ~InferenceSession() = default;
    
    /**
     * @brief Run inference
     * @param inputs Input tensors
     * @return Inference result with output tensors
     */
    virtual Result<InferenceResult> Run(std::span<const Tensor> inputs) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Run inference with named inputs/outputs
     */
    virtual Result<InferenceResult> Run(
        const std::map<std::string, Tensor>& inputs,
        const std::vector<std::string>& output_names = {}
    ) = 0;
    
    /**
     * @brief Run inference asynchronously
     */
    virtual void RunAsync(
        std::span<const Tensor> inputs,
        std::function<void(Result<InferenceResult>)> callback
    ) = 0;
    
    // Model information
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::vector<std::string> GetInputNames() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::vector<std::string> GetOutputNames() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::vector<TensorShape> GetInputShapes() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::vector<TensorShape> GetOutputShapes() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::vector<TensorDataType> GetInputTypes() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::vector<TensorDataType> GetOutputTypes() const = 0;
    
    // Session info
    [[nodiscard]] virtual InferenceBackend GetBackend() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::string GetModelPath() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual i64 GetMemoryUsage() const = 0;
    
    // Warm up
    virtual Result<void> WarmUp(u32 iterations = 3) = 0;

protected:
    InferenceSession() = default;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Inference Engine
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @class InferenceEngine
 * @brief Main interface for AI/ML inference
 */
class AETHER_API InferenceEngine {
public:
    InferenceEngine();
    ~InferenceEngine();
    
    /**
     * @brief Initialize the inference engine
     */
    Result<void> Initialize(const AIConfig& config = AIConfig{});
    
    /**
     * @brief Shutdown the engine
     */
    void Shutdown();
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Backend Management
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Get available backends
     */
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<InferenceBackend> GetAvailableBackends() const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get available execution providers
     */
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<ExecutionProvider> GetAvailableProviders() const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Check if backend is available
     */
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsBackendAvailable(InferenceBackend backend) const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get best available backend
     */
    [[nodiscard]] InferenceBackend GetBestBackend() const;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Model Management
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Load a model from file
     * @param path Path to model file (.onnx, .pt, .tflite, etc.)
     * @param options Session options
     * @return Inference session
     */
    Result<Shared<InferenceSession>> LoadModel(
        std::string_view path,
        const SessionOptions& options = SessionOptions{}
    );
    
    /**
     * @brief Load a model from memory
     */
    Result<Shared<InferenceSession>> LoadModelFromMemory(
        std::span<const u8> data,
        const SessionOptions& options = SessionOptions{}
    );
    
    /**
     * @brief Load a built-in model by name
     * @param name Model name (e.g., "esrgan-x2", "rife-v4", "whisper-small")
     */
    Result<Shared<InferenceSession>> LoadBuiltInModel(
        std::string_view name,
        const SessionOptions& options = SessionOptions{}
    );
    
    /**
     * @brief Download a model from repository
     */
    Result<std::string> DownloadModel(
        std::string_view name,
        ProgressCallback progress = nullptr
    );
    
    /**
     * @brief List available built-in models
     */
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<ModelInfo> ListBuiltInModels() const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Check if model is cached
     */
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsModelCached(std::string_view name) const;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Utility Functions
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    /**
     * @brief Convert model format
     */
    Result<void> ConvertModel(
        std::string_view input_path,
        std::string_view output_path,
        InferenceBackend target_backend
    );
    
    /**
     * @brief Optimize model for target backend
     */
    Result<void> OptimizeModel(
        std::string_view input_path,
        std::string_view output_path,
        InferenceBackend backend,
        const SessionOptions& options = SessionOptions{}
    );
    
    /**
     * @brief Benchmark a model
     */
    struct BenchmarkResult {
        f64 avg_inference_time_ms;
        f64 min_inference_time_ms;
        f64 max_inference_time_ms;
        f64 std_dev_ms;
        i64 memory_usage_bytes;
        f64 throughput_fps;
    };
    
    Result<BenchmarkResult> BenchmarkModel(
        Shared<InferenceSession> session,
        u32 iterations = 100,
        u32 warmup_iterations = 10
    );

private:
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

} // namespace ai
} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_AI_INFERENCE_ENGINE_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/ai/upscaler.hpp
// DESCRIPTION: AI-powered video upscaling
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#ifndef AETHER_AI_UPSCALER_HPP
#define AETHER_AI_UPSCALER_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"
#include "aether/video/video_frame.hpp"
#include "aether/ai/inference_engine.hpp"

namespace aether {
namespace ai {

/**
 * @enum UpscalingModel
 * @brief Available upscaling models
 */
enum class UpscalingModel {
    // Real-ESRGAN variants
    RealESRGAN_x2,
    RealESRGAN_x4,
    RealESRGAN_x4_Anime,
    
    // ESRGAN variants
    ESRGAN_x4,
    
    // Anime-specific
    Anime4K,
    Waifu2x,
    
    // Fast models
    FSRCNN_x2,
    FSRCNN_x4,
    
    // Other
    SwinIR_x2,
    SwinIR_x4,
    HAT_x4,
    
    // Custom
    Custom
};

/**
 * @struct UpscalerOptions
 * @brief Options for upscaling
 */
struct UpscalerOptions {
    UpscalingModel model = UpscalingModel::RealESRGAN_x2;
    std::string custom_model_path;
    
    f32 scale_factor = 2.0f;
    
    // Quality vs speed
    enum class Quality {
        Fast,
        Balanced,
        HighQuality
    } quality = Quality::Balanced;
    
    // Tile processing for large images
    bool enable_tiling = true;
    u32 tile_size = 512;
    u32 tile_overlap = 32;
    
    // Output format
    PixelFormat output_format = PixelFormat::Unknown;  // Auto
    bool preserve_alpha = true;
    
    // Performance
    bool enable_fp16 = true;
    u32 batch_size = 1;
};

/**
 * @class VideoUpscaler
 * @brief AI-powered video frame upscaler
 */
class AETHER_API VideoUpscaler {
public:
    VideoUpscaler();
    ~VideoUpscaler();
    
    /**
     * @brief Initialize with options
     */
    Result<void> Initialize(
        InferenceEngine& engine,
        const UpscalerOptions& options = UpscalerOptions{}
    );
    
    /**
     * @brief Upscale a single frame
     */
    Result<VideoFrame> Upscale(const VideoFrame& input);
    
    /**
     * @brief Upscale a batch of frames
     */
    Result<std::vector<VideoFrame>> UpscaleBatch(std::span<const VideoFrame> inputs);
    
    /**
     * @brief Upscale asynchronously
     */
    void UpscaleAsync(
        const VideoFrame& input,
        std::function<void(Result<VideoFrame>)> callback
    );
    
    /**
     * @brief Get output size for given input
     */
    [[nodiscard]] Size GetOutputSize(Size input_size) const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get current options
     */
    [[nodiscard]] const UpscalerOptions& GetOptions() const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Update options (may require reinitialization)
     */
    Result<void> UpdateOptions(const UpscalerOptions& options);
    
    /**
     * @brief Get performance statistics
     */
    struct Statistics {
        f64 avg_upscale_time_ms;
        i64 frames_processed;
        f64 throughput_fps;
        i64 memory_usage_bytes;
    };
    [[nodiscard]] Statistics GetStatistics() const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Reset statistics
     */
    void ResetStatistics();

private:
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

} // namespace ai
} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_AI_UPSCALER_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/ai/frame_interpolation.hpp
// DESCRIPTION: AI-powered frame interpolation for smooth playback
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#ifndef AETHER_AI_FRAME_INTERPOLATION_HPP
#define AETHER_AI_FRAME_INTERPOLATION_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"
#include "aether/video/video_frame.hpp"
#include "aether/ai/inference_engine.hpp"

namespace aether {
namespace ai {

/**
 * @enum InterpolationModel
 * @brief Available interpolation models
 */
enum class InterpolationModel {
    // RIFE variants (Real-Time Intermediate Flow Estimation)
    RIFE_v4,
    RIFE_v4_Lite,
    RIFE_v4_Anime,
    
    // FLAVR
    FLAVR,
    
    // FILM
    FILM,
    
    // Others
    DAIN,
    SuperSloMo,
    SepConv,
    
    // Custom
    Custom
};

/**
 * @struct InterpolationOptions
 * @brief Options for frame interpolation
 */
struct InterpolationOptions {
    InterpolationModel model = InterpolationModel::RIFE_v4;
    std::string custom_model_path;
    
    // Target frame rate
    f32 target_fps = 60.0f;
    
    // Multiplier mode (alternative to target_fps)
    enum class Mode {
        TargetFPS,      // Interpolate to reach target FPS
        Multiplier      // Multiply frame rate by factor
    } mode = Mode::TargetFPS;
    f32 multiplier = 2.0f;  // 2x, 4x, 8x
    
    // Quality
    enum class Quality {
        Fast,
        Balanced,
        HighQuality
    } quality = Quality::Balanced;
    
    // Scene change handling
    bool detect_scene_changes = true;
    f32 scene_change_threshold = 0.3f;
    
    // Performance
    bool enable_fp16 = true;
    u32 batch_size = 1;
    
    // Output
    PixelFormat output_format = PixelFormat::Unknown;  // Auto
};

/**
 * @class FrameInterpolator
 * @brief AI-powered frame interpolation
 */
class AETHER_API FrameInterpolator {
public:
    FrameInterpolator();
    ~FrameInterpolator();
    
    /**
     * @brief Initialize with options
     */
    Result<void> Initialize(
        InferenceEngine& engine,
        const InterpolationOptions& options = InterpolationOptions{}
    );
    
    /**
     * @brief Interpolate between two frames
     * @param frame1 First frame
     * @param frame2 Second frame
     * @param t Interpolation factor (0.0 = frame1, 1.0 = frame2)
     * @return Interpolated frame
     */
    Result<VideoFrame> Interpolate(
        const VideoFrame& frame1,
        const VideoFrame& frame2,
        f32 t
    );
    
    /**
     * @brief Generate multiple intermediate frames
     * @param frame1 First frame
     * @param frame2 Second frame
     * @param count Number of intermediate frames to generate
     * @return Vector of interpolated frames
     */
    Result<std::vector<VideoFrame>> InterpolateMultiple(
        const VideoFrame& frame1,
        const VideoFrame& frame2,
        u32 count
    );
    
    /**
     * @brief Check if scene change between frames
     */
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsSceneChange(
        const VideoFrame& frame1,
        const VideoFrame& frame2
    ) const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Calculate required interpolation count for target FPS
     */
    [[nodiscard]] u32 CalculateInterpolationCount(f32 source_fps, f32 target_fps) const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Get current options
     */
    [[nodiscard]] const InterpolationOptions& GetOptions() const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Update options
     */
    Result<void> UpdateOptions(const InterpolationOptions& options);
    
    /**
     * @brief Reset internal state (call when seeking)
     */
    void Reset();
    
    /**
     * @brief Get statistics
     */
    struct Statistics {
        f64 avg_interpolation_time_ms;
        i64 frames_generated;
        i64 scene_changes_detected;
        f64 throughput_fps;
    };
    [[nodiscard]] Statistics GetStatistics() const;

private:
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

} // namespace ai
} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_AI_FRAME_INTERPOLATION_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/ai/audio_enhancement.hpp
// DESCRIPTION: AI-powered audio enhancement
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#ifndef AETHER_AI_AUDIO_ENHANCEMENT_HPP
#define AETHER_AI_AUDIO_ENHANCEMENT_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"
#include "aether/ai/inference_engine.hpp"

namespace aether {
namespace ai {

/**
 * @enum AudioEnhancementType
 * @brief Types of audio enhancement
 */
enum class AudioEnhancementType {
    NoiseReduction,        // Remove background noise
    SpeechEnhancement,     // Enhance speech clarity
    MusicEnhancement,      // Enhance music quality
    DialogueIsolation,     // Isolate dialogue from mix
    Dereverberation,       // Remove reverb/echo
    SourceSeparation,      // Separate vocals/instruments
    Bandwidth Extension,   // Extend frequency range
    Declipping,            // Fix clipped audio
    Upsampling             // AI-based upsampling
};

/**
 * @struct AudioEnhancementOptions
 * @brief Options for audio enhancement
 */
struct AudioEnhancementOptions {
    // Enhancement types to apply
    std::vector<AudioEnhancementType> enhancements = {
        AudioEnhancementType::NoiseReduction
    };
    
    // Noise reduction
    f32 noise_reduction_strength = 0.5f;  // 0.0 - 1.0
    bool adaptive_noise_reduction = true;
    
    // Speech enhancement
    f32 speech_enhancement_strength = 0.5f;
    bool preserve_naturalness = true;
    
    // Source separation
    enum class SeparationTarget {
        Vocals,
        Drums,
        Bass,
        Other,
        All
    } separation_target = SeparationTarget::Vocals;
    
    // Bandwidth extension
    u32 target_sample_rate = 48000;
    
    // Performance
    u32 chunk_size_ms = 100;
    u32 overlap_ms = 25;
    bool realtime_mode = true;
    
    // Model selection
    std::string model_name;  // Empty = auto select
};

/**
 * @class AudioEnhancer
 * @brief AI-powered audio enhancement
 */
class AETHER_API AudioEnhancer {
public:
    AudioEnhancer();
    ~AudioEnhancer();
    
    /**
     * @brief Initialize with options
     */
    Result<void> Initialize(
        InferenceEngine& engine,
        const AudioEnhancementOptions& options = AudioEnhancementOptions{}
    );
    
    /**
     * @brief Process audio samples
     * @param input Input samples (interleaved)
     * @param channels Number of channels
     * @param sample_rate Sample rate
     * @return Processed samples
     */
    Result<std::vector<f32>> Process(
        std::span<const f32> input,
        u32 channels,
        u32 sample_rate
    );
    
    /**
     * @brief Process audio in-place
     */
    Result<void> ProcessInPlace(
        std::span<f32> audio,
        u32 channels,
        u32 sample_rate
    );
    
    /**
     * @brief Get latency introduced by processing
     */
    [[nodiscard]] u32 GetLatencyMs() const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Reset internal state
     */
    void Reset();
    
    /**
     * @brief Get current options
     */
    [[nodiscard]] const AudioEnhancementOptions& GetOptions() const;
    
    /**
     * @brief Update options
     */
    Result<void> UpdateOptions(const AudioEnhancementOptions& options);

private:
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

/**
 * @class SpeechRecognizer
 * @brief AI-powered speech recognition (using Whisper or similar)
 */
class AETHER_API SpeechRecognizer {
public:
    SpeechRecognizer();
    ~SpeechRecognizer();
    
    /**
     * @struct Options
     */
    struct Options {
        std::string model = "whisper-small";  // tiny, base, small, medium, large
        std::string language;  // Empty = auto-detect
        bool translate_to_english = false;
        bool output_timestamps = true;
        
        // Real-time options
        bool streaming_mode = false;
        u32 chunk_size_ms = 3000;
    };
    
    /**
     * @brief Initialize
     */
    Result<void> Initialize(InferenceEngine& engine, const Options& options = Options{});
    
    /**
     * @struct TranscriptionSegment
     */
    struct TranscriptionSegment {
        std::string text;
        Timestamp start;
        Timestamp end;
        f32 confidence;
        std::string language;
    };
    
    /**
     * @struct TranscriptionResult
     */
    struct TranscriptionResult {
        std::string full_text;
        std::vector<TranscriptionSegment> segments;
        std::string detected_language;
        Duration processing_time;
    };
    
    /**
     * @brief Transcribe audio
     */
    Result<TranscriptionResult> Transcribe(
        std::span<const f32> audio,
        u32 sample_rate
    );
    
    /**
     * @brief Transcribe from file
     */
    Result<TranscriptionResult> TranscribeFile(std::string_view path);
    
    /**
     * @brief Start streaming transcription
     */
    Result<void> StartStreaming(
        std::function<void(const TranscriptionSegment&)> callback
    );
    
    /**
     * @brief Feed audio to streaming recognizer
     */
    Result<void> FeedAudio(std::span<const f32> audio);
    
    /**
     * @brief Stop streaming
     */
    void StopStreaming();

private:
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

} // namespace ai
} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_AI_AUDIO_ENHANCEMENT_HPP
```

# 7. PLATFORM SUPPORT

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/platform/platform.hpp
// DESCRIPTION: Platform abstraction layer
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#ifndef AETHER_PLATFORM_PLATFORM_HPP
#define AETHER_PLATFORM_PLATFORM_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <memory>

namespace aether {
namespace platform {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Platform Detection
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @enum PlatformType
 * @brief Target platform types
 */
enum class PlatformType : u8 {
    Unknown = 0,
    Windows,
    macOS,
    Linux,
    Android,
    iOS,
    Web,
    Embedded,
    PlayStation,
    Xbox,
    Switch
};

/**
 * @enum Architecture
 * @brief CPU architectures
 */
enum class Architecture : u8 {
    Unknown = 0,
    x86,
    x64,
    ARM,
    ARM64,
    WASM,
    RISC_V
};

/**
 * @struct PlatformInfo
 * @brief Information about the current platform
 */
struct PlatformInfo {
    PlatformType type;
    Architecture arch;
    
    std::string os_name;
    std::string os_version;
    std::string device_model;
    std::string device_manufacturer;
    
    u32 cpu_cores;
    u64 total_memory;
    u64 available_memory;
    
    bool has_gpu;
    std::string gpu_name;
    u64 gpu_memory;
    
    // Display info
    u32 screen_width;
    u32 screen_height;
    f32 screen_dpi;
    f32 refresh_rate;
    bool hdr_supported;
    
    // Capabilities
    bool supports_hardware_decoding;
    bool supports_hardware_encoding;
    bool supports_vulkan;
    bool supports_metal;
    bool supports_d3d12;
    bool supports_opengl;
    bool supports_compute;
};

/**
 * @brief Get current platform information
 */
AETHER_API PlatformInfo GetPlatformInfo();

/**
 * @brief Get current platform type
 */
AETHER_API PlatformType GetPlatformType();

/**
 * @brief Get current architecture
 */
AETHER_API Architecture GetArchitecture();

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// File System
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @brief Get application data directory
 */

 * @brief Get cache directory

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Display
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @struct DisplayInfo
 * @brief Information about a display
 */
struct DisplayInfo {
    u32 index;
    std::string name;
    bool is_primary;
    
    i32 x, y;           // Position
    u32 width, height;  // Resolution
    f32 dpi;
    f32 scale_factor;
    
    f32 refresh_rate;
    std::vector<f32> available_refresh_rates;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Audio Devices
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @struct AudioDeviceInfo
 * @brief Information about an audio device
 */
struct AudioDeviceInfo {
    std::string id;
    std::string name;
    bool is_default;
    bool is_output;  // false = input device
    
    u32 max_channels;
    std::vector<u32> supported_sample_rates;
    std::vector<SampleFormat> supported_formats;
    
    u32 default_sample_rate;
    SampleFormat default_format;
    
    // Exclusive mode info
    bool supports_exclusive_mode;
    u32 exclusive_min_buffer_size;
    u32 exclusive_max_buffer_size;
};

/**
 * @brief Get audio output devices
 */
AETHER_API std::vector<AudioDeviceInfo> GetAudioOutputDevices();

 * @brief Get audio input devices
 */

/**
 * @brief Get default audio output device
 */
AETHER_API AudioDeviceInfo GetDefaultAudioOutput();

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// GPU Information
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @struct GPUInfo
 * @brief Information about a GPU
 */
struct GPUInfo {
    u32 index;
    std::string name;
    std::string vendor;
    std::string driver_version;
    
    enum class Type {
        Unknown,
        Integrated,
        Discrete,
        Virtual,
        CPU  // Software renderer
    } type;
    
    u64 total_memory;
    u64 available_memory;
    
    // API support
    bool supports_vulkan;
    u32 vulkan_version;
    
    bool supports_metal;
    u32 metal_version;
    
    bool supports_d3d12;
    u32 d3d_feature_level;
    
    bool supports_opengl;
    u32 opengl_version;
    
    bool supports_opencl;
    bool supports_cuda;
    u32 cuda_compute_capability;
    
    // Video capabilities
    std::vector<CodecID> hw_decode_codecs;
    std::vector<CodecID> hw_encode_codecs;
};

/**
 * @brief Get all GPUs
 */
AETHER_API std::vector<GPUInfo> GetGPUs();

/**
 * @brief Get preferred GPU for rendering
 */
AETHER_API GPUInfo GetPreferredGPU();

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Power Management
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @enum PowerState
 * @brief Device power state
 */
enum class PowerState {
    Unknown,
    OnBattery,
    Charging,
    Full,
    NoBattery
};

/**
 * @struct PowerInfo
 * @brief Power/battery information
 */
struct PowerInfo {
    PowerState state;
    f32 battery_percent;
    i32 time_remaining_minutes;  // -1 = unknown
    bool power_saver_enabled;
};

/**
 * @brief Get power information
 */
AETHER_API PowerInfo GetPowerInfo();

/**
 * @brief Prevent system sleep during playback
 */
AETHER_API void PreventSleep(bool prevent);

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// System Integration
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * @brief Register as default handler for media files
 */
AETHER_API Result<void> RegisterAsDefaultPlayer(const std::vector<std::string>& extensions);

/**
 * @brief Open URL in default browser
 */
AETHER_API Result<void> OpenURL(std::string_view url);

/**
 * @brief Show file in system file manager
 */
AETHER_API Result<void> ShowInFileManager(std::string_view path);

/**
 * @brief Show system notification
 */
AETHER_API Result<void> ShowNotification(
    std::string_view title,
    std::string_view message,
    std::string_view icon_path = ""
);

/**
 * @brief Get system locale
 */
AETHER_API std::string GetSystemLocale();

/**
 * @brief Get system time zone
 */
AETHER_API std::string GetSystemTimeZone();

} // namespace platform
} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_PLATFORM_PLATFORM_HPP
```

# 8. BUILD SYSTEM

```cmake
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# FILE: CMakeLists.txt
# DESCRIPTION: Root CMake build file for AETHER Media Engine
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.25)

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Project Definition
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

# Prevent in-source builds
if(CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR STREQUAL CMAKE_BINARY_DIR)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "In-source builds are not allowed. Please use a separate build directory.")
endif()

# Version from file
file(READ "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/VERSION" VERSION_CONTENT)
string(STRIP "${VERSION_CONTENT}" AETHER_VERSION)

project(AetherMediaEngine
    VERSION ${AETHER_VERSION}
    DESCRIPTION "Enterprise-grade AI-powered cross-platform media player"
    HOMEPAGE_URL "https://github.com/aether-engine/aether-media-engine"
    LANGUAGES C CXX
)

# C++ Standard
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
set(CMAKE_CXX_EXTENSIONS OFF)

# C Standard
set(CMAKE_C_STANDARD 17)
set(CMAKE_C_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

# Export compile commands for IDE support
set(CMAKE_EXPORT_COMPILE_COMMANDS ON)

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Build Options
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

# Library type
option(AETHER_BUILD_SHARED "Build as shared library" ON)

# Components
option(AETHER_BUILD_APPS "Build applications" ON)
option(AETHER_BUILD_EXAMPLES "Build examples" ON)
option(AETHER_BUILD_TESTS "Build tests" ON)
option(AETHER_BUILD_BENCHMARKS "Build benchmarks" OFF)
option(AETHER_BUILD_DOCS "Build documentation" OFF)
option(AETHER_BUILD_PYTHON_BINDINGS "Build Python bindings" OFF)

# Features
option(AETHER_ENABLE_AI "Enable AI/ML features" ON)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_DRM "Enable DRM support" ON)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_ANALYTICS "Enable analytics" OFF)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_PLUGINS "Enable plugin system" ON)

# Video backends
option(AETHER_ENABLE_VULKAN "Enable Vulkan renderer" ON)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_METAL "Enable Metal renderer (macOS/iOS)" ON)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_D3D12 "Enable Direct3D 12 renderer (Windows)" ON)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_OPENGL "Enable OpenGL renderer" ON)

# Hardware acceleration
option(AETHER_ENABLE_NVDEC "Enable NVIDIA decoder" ON)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_NVENC "Enable NVIDIA encoder" ON)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_VAAPI "Enable VA-API (Linux)" ON)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_QSV "Enable Intel Quick Sync" ON)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_VIDEOTOOLBOX "Enable VideoToolbox (Apple)" ON)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_MEDIACODEC "Enable MediaCodec (Android)" ON)

# AI backends
option(AETHER_ENABLE_ONNXRUNTIME "Enable ONNX Runtime" ON)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_TENSORRT "Enable TensorRT" OFF)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_COREML "Enable CoreML (Apple)" ON)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_OPENVINO "Enable OpenVINO" OFF)

# Debug options
option(AETHER_ENABLE_ASAN "Enable AddressSanitizer" OFF)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_UBSAN "Enable UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer" OFF)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_TSAN "Enable ThreadSanitizer" OFF)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_MSAN "Enable MemorySanitizer" OFF)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_COVERAGE "Enable code coverage" OFF)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_LTO "Enable Link-Time Optimization" OFF)

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Platform Detection
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

if(WIN32)
    set(AETHER_PLATFORM "Windows")
    set(AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS TRUE)
elseif(APPLE)
    if(IOS)
        set(AETHER_PLATFORM "iOS")
        set(AETHER_PLATFORM_IOS TRUE)
    else()
        set(AETHER_PLATFORM "macOS")
        set(AETHER_PLATFORM_MACOS TRUE)
    endif()
    set(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE TRUE)
elseif(ANDROID)
    set(AETHER_PLATFORM "Android")
    set(AETHER_PLATFORM_ANDROID TRUE)
elseif(EMSCRIPTEN)
    set(AETHER_PLATFORM "Web")
    set(AETHER_PLATFORM_WEB TRUE)
elseif(UNIX)
    set(AETHER_PLATFORM "Linux")
    set(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX TRUE)
else()
    set(AETHER_PLATFORM "Unknown")
endif()

message(STATUS "Building for platform: ${AETHER_PLATFORM}")
message(STATUS "Build type: ${CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE}")

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Include Custom CMake Modules
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

list(APPEND CMAKE_MODULE_PATH "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake/modules")

include(cmake/CompilerFlags.cmake)
include(cmake/Dependencies.cmake)
include(cmake/Version.cmake)
include(cmake/Packaging.cmake)

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Git Information
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

find_package(Git QUIET)
if(GIT_FOUND)
    execute_process(
        COMMAND ${GIT_EXECUTABLE} rev-parse --short HEAD
        WORKING_DIRECTORY ${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}
        OUTPUT_VARIABLE AETHER_GIT_COMMIT
        OUTPUT_STRIP_TRAILING_WHITESPACE
        ERROR_QUIET
    )
    execute_process(
        COMMAND ${GIT_EXECUTABLE} rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD
        WORKING_DIRECTORY ${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}
        OUTPUT_VARIABLE AETHER_GIT_BRANCH
        OUTPUT_STRIP_TRAILING_WHITESPACE
        ERROR_QUIET
    )
endif()

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Configure Files
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

configure_file(
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake/config.hpp.in"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/include/aether/generated/config.hpp"
    @ONLY
)

configure_file(
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake/version.cpp.in"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/src/version.cpp"
    @ONLY
)

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Core Library
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

# Collect source files
file(GLOB_RECURSE AETHER_CORE_SOURCES
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/core/*.cpp"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/media/*.cpp"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/pipeline/*.cpp"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/render/*.cpp"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/audio/*.cpp"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/network/*.cpp"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/utils/*.cpp"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/src/version.cpp"
)

# Platform-specific sources
if(AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS)
    file(GLOB_RECURSE AETHER_PLATFORM_SOURCES
        "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/platform/windows/*.cpp"
    )
elseif(AETHER_PLATFORM_MACOS)
    file(GLOB_RECURSE AETHER_PLATFORM_SOURCES
        "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/platform/macos/*.cpp"
        "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/platform/macos/*.mm"
    )
elseif(AETHER_PLATFORM_IOS)
    file(GLOB_RECURSE AETHER_PLATFORM_SOURCES
        "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/platform/ios/*.cpp"
        "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/platform/ios/*.mm"
    )
elseif(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
    file(GLOB_RECURSE AETHER_PLATFORM_SOURCES
        "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/platform/linux/*.cpp"
    )
elseif(AETHER_PLATFORM_ANDROID)
    file(GLOB_RECURSE AETHER_PLATFORM_SOURCES
        "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/platform/android/*.cpp"
    )
elseif(AETHER_PLATFORM_WEB)
    file(GLOB_RECURSE AETHER_PLATFORM_SOURCES
        "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/platform/web/*.cpp"
    )
endif()

list(APPEND AETHER_CORE_SOURCES ${AETHER_PLATFORM_SOURCES})

# AI sources (optional)
if(AETHER_ENABLE_AI)
    file(GLOB_RECURSE AETHER_AI_SOURCES
        "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/ai/*.cpp"
    )
    list(APPEND AETHER_CORE_SOURCES ${AETHER_AI_SOURCES})
endif()

# Plugin sources (optional)
if(AETHER_ENABLE_PLUGINS)
    file(GLOB_RECURSE AETHER_PLUGIN_SOURCES
        "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/plugin/*.cpp"
    )
    list(APPEND AETHER_CORE_SOURCES ${AETHER_PLUGIN_SOURCES})
endif()

# DRM sources (optional)
if(AETHER_ENABLE_DRM)
    file(GLOB_RECURSE AETHER_DRM_SOURCES
        "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src/drm/*.cpp"
    )
    list(APPEND AETHER_CORE_SOURCES ${AETHER_DRM_SOURCES})
endif()

# Create library
if(AETHER_BUILD_SHARED)
    add_library(aether SHARED ${AETHER_CORE_SOURCES})
    target_compile_definitions(aether PRIVATE AETHER_BUILD_SHARED AETHER_EXPORT)
else()
    add_library(aether STATIC ${AETHER_CORE_SOURCES})
    target_compile_definitions(aether PRIVATE AETHER_BUILD_STATIC)
endif()

add_library(Aether::aether ALIAS aether)

# Include directories
target_include_directories(aether
    PUBLIC
        $<BUILD_INTERFACE:${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/include>
        $<BUILD_INTERFACE:${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/include>
        $<INSTALL_INTERFACE:include>
    PRIVATE
        ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src
)

# Set library properties
set_target_properties(aether PROPERTIES
    VERSION ${PROJECT_VERSION}
    SOVERSION ${PROJECT_VERSION_MAJOR}
    OUTPUT_NAME "aether"
    DEBUG_POSTFIX "d"
    POSITION_INDEPENDENT_CODE ON
    CXX_VISIBILITY_PRESET hidden
    VISIBILITY_INLINES_HIDDEN ON
)

# Platform-specific settings
if(AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS)
    target_compile_definitions(aether PRIVATE
        WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
        NOMINMAX
        _CRT_SECURE_NO_WARNINGS
        UNICODE
        _UNICODE
    )
elseif(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE)
    target_compile_options(aether PRIVATE -fobjc-arc)
endif()

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Link Dependencies
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

# FFmpeg
find_package(FFmpeg REQUIRED COMPONENTS avcodec avformat avutil swscale swresample avfilter)
target_link_libraries(aether PRIVATE FFmpeg::avcodec FFmpeg::avformat FFmpeg::avutil
                                      FFmpeg::swscale FFmpeg::swresample FFmpeg::avfilter)

# SDL3 (windowing/input)
find_package(SDL3 REQUIRED)
target_link_libraries(aether PRIVATE SDL3::SDL3)

# spdlog (logging)
find_package(spdlog REQUIRED)
target_link_libraries(aether PRIVATE spdlog::spdlog)

# fmt (formatting)
find_package(fmt REQUIRED)
target_link_libraries(aether PRIVATE fmt::fmt)

# nlohmann_json (JSON parsing)
find_package(nlohmann_json REQUIRED)
target_link_libraries(aether PRIVATE nlohmann_json::nlohmann_json)

# Threading
find_package(Threads REQUIRED)
target_link_libraries(aether PUBLIC Threads::Threads)

# Vulkan (optional)
if(AETHER_ENABLE_VULKAN)
    find_package(Vulkan)
    if(Vulkan_FOUND)
        target_link_libraries(aether PRIVATE Vulkan::Vulkan)
        target_compile_definitions(aether PRIVATE AETHER_HAS_VULKAN)
    endif()
endif()

# ONNX Runtime (optional)
if(AETHER_ENABLE_ONNXRUNTIME)
    find_package(ONNXRuntime)
    if(ONNXRuntime_FOUND)
        target_link_libraries(aether PRIVATE ONNXRuntime::ONNXRuntime)
        target_compile_definitions(aether PRIVATE AETHER_HAS_ONNXRUNTIME)
    endif()
endif()

# Platform-specific libraries
if(AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS)
    target_link_libraries(aether PRIVATE
        d3d12 dxgi d3d11 dxguid
        mf mfplat mfuuid mfreadwrite
        windowscodecs
        ole32 oleaut32
        winmm
        ws2_32
        crypt32
    )
elseif(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE)
    find_library(FOUNDATION_FRAMEWORK Foundation REQUIRED)
    find_library(COREFOUNDATION_FRAMEWORK CoreFoundation REQUIRED)
    find_library(COREMEDIA_FRAMEWORK CoreMedia REQUIRED)
    find_library(COREVIDEO_FRAMEWORK CoreVideo REQUIRED)
    find_library(COREAUDIO_FRAMEWORK CoreAudio REQUIRED)
    find_library(AUDIOTOOLBOX_FRAMEWORK AudioToolbox REQUIRED)
    find_library(VIDEOTOOLBOX_FRAMEWORK VideoToolbox REQUIRED)
    find_library(METAL_FRAMEWORK Metal REQUIRED)
    find_library(METALKIT_FRAMEWORK MetalKit REQUIRED)
    find_library(QUARTZCORE_FRAMEWORK QuartzCore REQUIRED)
    
    target_link_libraries(aether PRIVATE
        ${FOUNDATION_FRAMEWORK}
        ${COREFOUNDATION_FRAMEWORK}
        ${COREMEDIA_FRAMEWORK}
        ${COREVIDEO_FRAMEWORK}
        ${COREAUDIO_FRAMEWORK}
        ${AUDIOTOOLBOX_FRAMEWORK}
        ${VIDEOTOOLBOX_FRAMEWORK}
        ${METAL_FRAMEWORK}
        ${METALKIT_FRAMEWORK}
        ${QUARTZCORE_FRAMEWORK}
    )
    
    if(AETHER_PLATFORM_MACOS)
        find_library(APPKIT_FRAMEWORK AppKit REQUIRED)
        target_link_libraries(aether PRIVATE ${APPKIT_FRAMEWORK})
    else()
        find_library(UIKIT_FRAMEWORK UIKit REQUIRED)
        target_link_libraries(aether PRIVATE ${UIKIT_FRAMEWORK})
    endif()
elseif(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
    find_package(PkgConfig REQUIRED)
    
    # ALSA
    pkg_check_modules(ALSA REQUIRED alsa)
    target_link_libraries(aether PRIVATE ${ALSA_LIBRARIES})
    target_include_directories(aether PRIVATE ${ALSA_INCLUDE_DIRS})
    
    # PulseAudio
    pkg_check_modules(PULSE REQUIRED libpulse)
    target_link_libraries(aether PRIVATE ${PULSE_LIBRARIES})
    target_include_directories(aether PRIVATE ${PULSE_INCLUDE_DIRS})
    
    # X11/Wayland
    find_package(X11)
    if(X11_FOUND)
        target_link_libraries(aether PRIVATE X11::X11)
        target_compile_definitions(aether PRIVATE AETHER_HAS_X11)
    endif()
    
    pkg_check_modules(WAYLAND wayland-client wayland-egl)
    if(WAYLAND_FOUND)
        target_link_libraries(aether PRIVATE ${WAYLAND_LIBRARIES})
        target_compile_definitions(aether PRIVATE AETHER_HAS_WAYLAND)
    endif()
    
    # VA-API
    if(AETHER_ENABLE_VAAPI)
        pkg_check_modules(VAAPI libva libva-drm libva-x11)
        if(VAAPI_FOUND)
            target_link_libraries(aether PRIVATE ${VAAPI_LIBRARIES})
            target_compile_definitions(aether PRIVATE AETHER_HAS_VAAPI)
        endif()
    endif()
elseif(AETHER_PLATFORM_ANDROID)
    target_link_libraries(aether PRIVATE
        log
        android
        aaudio
        mediandk
        nativewindow
        EGL
        GLESv3
    )
endif()

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Subdirectories
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

# Applications
if(AETHER_BUILD_APPS)
    add_subdirectory(apps)
endif()

# Examples
if(AETHER_BUILD_EXAMPLES)
    add_subdirectory(examples)
endif()

# Tests
if(AETHER_BUILD_TESTS)
    enable_testing()
    add_subdirectory(tests)
endif()

# Benchmarks
if(AETHER_BUILD_BENCHMARKS)
    add_subdirectory(benchmarks)
endif()

# Documentation
if(AETHER_BUILD_DOCS)
    add_subdirectory(docs)
endif()

# Python bindings
if(AETHER_BUILD_PYTHON_BINDINGS)
    add_subdirectory(bindings/python)
endif()

# Plugins
if(AETHER_ENABLE_PLUGINS)
    add_subdirectory(plugins)
endif()

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Installation
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

include(GNUInstallDirs)
include(CMakePackageConfigHelpers)

# Install library
install(TARGETS aether
    EXPORT AetherTargets
    LIBRARY DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}
    ARCHIVE DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}
    RUNTIME DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_BINDIR}
    INCLUDES DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_INCLUDEDIR}
)

# Install headers
install(DIRECTORY include/aether
    DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_INCLUDEDIR}
    FILES_MATCHING PATTERN "*.hpp" PATTERN "*.h"
)

# Install generated headers
install(DIRECTORY ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/include/aether
    DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_INCLUDEDIR}
    FILES_MATCHING PATTERN "*.hpp" PATTERN "*.h"
)

# Install CMake config
install(EXPORT AetherTargets
    FILE AetherTargets.cmake
    NAMESPACE Aether::
    DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}/cmake/Aether
)

configure_package_config_file(
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake/AetherConfig.cmake.in"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/AetherConfig.cmake"
    INSTALL_DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}/cmake/Aether
)

write_basic_package_version_file(
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/AetherConfigVersion.cmake"
    VERSION ${PROJECT_VERSION}
    COMPATIBILITY SameMajorVersion
)

install(FILES
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/AetherConfig.cmake"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/AetherConfigVersion.cmake"
    DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}/cmake/Aether
)

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# CPack Configuration
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

set(CPACK_PACKAGE_NAME "AetherMediaEngine")
set(CPACK_PACKAGE_VENDOR "Aether Engine Team")
set(CPACK_PACKAGE_DESCRIPTION_SUMMARY ${PROJECT_DESCRIPTION})
set(CPACK_PACKAGE_VERSION ${PROJECT_VERSION})
set(CPACK_PACKAGE_INSTALL_DIRECTORY "AetherMediaEngine")
set(CPACK_RESOURCE_FILE_LICENSE "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/LICENSE")
set(CPACK_RESOURCE_FILE_README "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/README.md")

if(AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS)
    set(CPACK_GENERATOR "NSIS;ZIP")
elseif(AETHER_PLATFORM_MACOS)
    set(CPACK_GENERATOR "DragNDrop;ZIP")
elseif(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
    set(CPACK_GENERATOR "DEB;RPM;TGZ")
endif()

include(CPack)

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Summary
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

message(STATUS "")
message(STATUS "╔═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗")
message(STATUS "║            AETHER MEDIA ENGINE - Configuration                ║")
message(STATUS "╠═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣")
message(STATUS "║ Version:        ${PROJECT_VERSION}")
message(STATUS "║ Platform:       ${AETHER_PLATFORM}")
message(STATUS "║ Build Type:     ${CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE}")
message(STATUS "║ Compiler:       ${CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER_ID} ${CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER_VERSION}")
message(STATUS "║ C++ Standard:   ${CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD}")
message(STATUS "╠═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣")
message(STATUS "║ Features:")
message(STATUS "║   AI/ML:        ${AETHER_ENABLE_AI}")
message(STATUS "║   DRM:          ${AETHER_ENABLE_DRM}")
message(STATUS "║   Plugins:      ${AETHER_ENABLE_PLUGINS}")
message(STATUS "║   Vulkan:       ${AETHER_ENABLE_VULKAN}")
message(STATUS "║   Metal:        ${AETHER_ENABLE_METAL}")
message(STATUS "║   D3D12:        ${AETHER_ENABLE_D3D12}")
message(STATUS "║   OpenGL:       ${AETHER_ENABLE_OPENGL}")
message(STATUS "╠═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣")
message(STATUS "║ Build:")
message(STATUS "║   Apps:         ${AETHER_BUILD_APPS}")
message(STATUS "║   Examples:     ${AETHER_BUILD_EXAMPLES}")
message(STATUS "║   Tests:        ${AETHER_BUILD_TESTS}")
message(STATUS "║   Docs:         ${AETHER_BUILD_DOCS}")
message(STATUS "╚═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝")
message(STATUS "")
```

# 9. IMPLEMENTATION

## 9.1 Core Engine Implementation

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: src/core/engine/engine.cpp
// DESCRIPTION: Core engine implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#include "aether/core/engine.hpp"
#include "engine_impl.hpp"

#include "aether/core/player.hpp"
#include "aether/plugin/plugin_manager.hpp"
#include "aether/ai/inference_engine.hpp"
#include "aether/utils/logging.hpp"

#include <mutex>
#include <atomic>
#include <unordered_map>

namespace aether {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Implementation Class
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class Engine::Impl {
public:
    Impl() = default;
    ~Impl() { Shutdown(); }
    
    Result<void> Initialize(const Config& config) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        if (initialized_.load()) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "Engine already initialized");
        }
        
        config_ = config;
        
        // Initialize logging
        if (auto result = InitializeLogging(); !result) {
            return result;
        }
        
        logger_->Info("AETHER Media Engine v{} initializing...", GetVersionString());
        logger_->Info("Platform: {}, Architecture: {}", 
                     ToString(platform::GetPlatformType()),
                     ToString(platform::GetArchitecture()));
        
        // Initialize subsystems
        if (auto result = InitializeSubsystems(); !result) {
            logger_->Error("Failed to initialize subsystems: {}", result.error().message);
            return result;
        }
        
        // Detect capabilities
        DetectCapabilities();
        
        initialized_.store(true);
        logger_->Info("AETHER Media Engine initialized successfully");
        
        return {};
    }
    
    void Shutdown() {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        if (!initialized_.load()) {
            return;
        }
        
        logger_->Info("AETHER Media Engine shutting down...");
        
        // Destroy all players
        for (auto& [id, player] : players_) {
            player.reset();
        }
        players_.clear();
        
        // Shutdown subsystems in reverse order
        ShutdownSubsystems();
        
        initialized_.store(false);
        logger_->Info("AETHER Media Engine shutdown complete");
    }
    
    bool IsInitialized() const {
        return initialized_.load();
    }
    
    const Config& GetConfig() const {
        return config_;
    }
    
    Result<void> UpdateConfig(const Config& config) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        auto errors = config.Validate();
        if (!errors.empty()) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidArgument, 
                             "Invalid configuration: " + errors[0]);
        }
        
        config_ = config;
        
        // Apply runtime-changeable settings
        ApplyConfig();
        
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<Shared<Player>> CreatePlayer(std::string_view name) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        if (!initialized_.load()) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::NotInitialized, "Engine not initialized");
        }
        
        auto player = std::make_shared<PlayerImpl>(name.empty() 
            ? "Player_" + std::to_string(next_player_id_++) 
            : std::string(name));
        
        if (auto result = player->Initialize(*this); !result) {
            return result.error();
        }
        
        u64 id = next_player_id_++;
        players_[id] = player;
        
        logger_->Debug("Created player '{}' (id: {})", player->GetName(), id);
        
        return player;
    }
    
    void DestroyPlayer(Shared<Player>& player) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        for (auto it = players_.begin(); it != players_.end(); ++it) {
            if (it->second == player) {
                logger_->Debug("Destroying player '{}'", player->GetName());
                players_.erase(it);
                player.reset();
                return;
            }
        }
    }
    
    std::vector<Shared<Player>> GetPlayers() const {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        std::vector<Shared<Player>> result;
        result.reserve(players_.size());
        for (const auto& [id, player] : players_) {
            result.push_back(player);
        }
        return result;
    }
    
    PluginManager& GetPluginManager() {
        return *plugin_manager_;
    }
    
    ai::InferenceEngine& GetAIEngine() {
        return *ai_engine_;
    }
    
    Logger& GetLogger() {
        return *logger_;
    }
    
    const EngineCapabilities& GetCapabilities() const {
        return capabilities_;
    }
    
    EngineStatistics GetStatistics() const {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(stats_mutex_);
        return statistics_;
    }
    
    void ResetStatistics() {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(stats_mutex_);
        statistics_ = EngineStatistics{};
    }
    
    bool IsCodecSupported(CodecID codec) const {
        return std::find(capabilities_.supported_video_codecs.begin(),
                        capabilities_.supported_video_codecs.end(),
                        codec) != capabilities_.supported_video_codecs.end() ||
               std::find(capabilities_.supported_audio_codecs.begin(),
                        capabilities_.supported_audio_codecs.end(),
                        codec) != capabilities_.supported_audio_codecs.end();
    }
    
    bool IsContainerSupported(ContainerFormat format) const {
        // Check FFmpeg demuxer availability
        return true; // Simplified
    }
    
    bool IsProtocolSupported(std::string_view protocol) const {
        return std::find(capabilities_.supported_protocols.begin(),
                        capabilities_.supported_protocols.end(),
                        std::string(protocol)) != capabilities_.supported_protocols.end();
    }
    
private:
    Result<void> InitializeLogging() {
        logger_ = std::make_unique<Logger>();
        return logger_->Initialize(config_.log);
    }
    
    Result<void> InitializeSubsystems() {
        // FFmpeg
        logger_->Debug("Initializing FFmpeg...");
        // av_log_set_callback(...);
        
        // Plugin manager
        if (config_.plugin.enable_plugins) {
            logger_->Debug("Initializing plugin system...");
            plugin_manager_ = std::make_unique<PluginManager>();
            if (auto result = plugin_manager_->Initialize(config_.plugin); !result) {
                logger_->Warn("Plugin system initialization failed: {}", 
                            result.error().message);
            }
        }
        
        // AI engine
        if (config_.ai.enable_ai_features) {
            logger_->Debug("Initializing AI engine...");
            ai_engine_ = std::make_unique<ai::InferenceEngine>();
            if (auto result = ai_engine_->Initialize(config_.ai); !result) {
                logger_->Warn("AI engine initialization failed: {}", 
                            result.error().message);
            }
        }
        
        // Network initialization
        logger_->Debug("Initializing network...");
        // curl_global_init(CURL_GLOBAL_ALL);
        
        return {};
    }
    
    void ShutdownSubsystems() {
        if (ai_engine_) {
            ai_engine_->Shutdown();
            ai_engine_.reset();
        }
        
        if (plugin_manager_) {
            plugin_manager_->Shutdown();
            plugin_manager_.reset();
        }
        
        // curl_global_cleanup();
    }
    
    void DetectCapabilities() {
        logger_->Debug("Detecting system capabilities...");
        
        // Detect video capabilities
        capabilities_.supported_video_codecs = {
            CodecID::H264, CodecID::H265, CodecID::VP9, CodecID::AV1,
            CodecID::MPEG2, CodecID::MPEG4, CodecID::VP8
        };
        
        capabilities_.supported_audio_codecs = {
            CodecID::AAC, CodecID::MP3, CodecID::FLAC, CodecID::Opus,
            CodecID::Vorbis, CodecID::AC3, CodecID::EAC3, CodecID::DTS
        };
        
        capabilities_.supported_protocols = {
            "file", "http", "https", "rtsp", "rtmp", "hls", "dash"
        };
        
        // Hardware decoding
        #ifdef AETHER_HAS_NVDEC
        capabilities_.hardware_decoding = true;
        #endif
        
        #ifdef AETHER_HAS_VAAPI
        capabilities_.hardware_decoding = true;
        #endif
        
        // Rendering capabilities
        #ifdef AETHER_HAS_VULKAN
        capabilities_.vulkan_available = true;
        #endif
        
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE
        capabilities_.metal_available = true;
        #endif
        
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
        capabilities_.d3d12_available = true;
        #endif
        
        // AI capabilities
        if (ai_engine_) {
            auto backends = ai_engine_->GetAvailableBackends();
            capabilities_.ai_upscaling = !backends.empty();
            capabilities_.ai_frame_interpolation = !backends.empty();
            capabilities_.ai_audio_enhancement = !backends.empty();
        }
        
        // Max resolution (based on hardware)
        capabilities_.max_video_width = 7680;   // 8K
        capabilities_.max_video_height = 4320;
        
        capabilities_.max_sample_rate = 384000;
        capabilities_.max_channels = 16;
        
        logger_->Info("Detected {} video codecs, {} audio codecs, {} protocols",
                     capabilities_.supported_video_codecs.size(),
                     capabilities_.supported_audio_codecs.size(),
                     capabilities_.supported_protocols.size());
    }
    
    void ApplyConfig() {
        if (logger_) {
            logger_->SetLevel(config_.log.level);
        }
        
        if (ai_engine_) {
            ai_engine_->UpdateConfig(config_.ai);
        }
    }
    
private:
    mutable std::mutex mutex_;
    mutable std::mutex stats_mutex_;
    
    std::atomic<bool> initialized_{false};
    
    Config config_;
    EngineCapabilities capabilities_;
    EngineStatistics statistics_;
    
    Unique<Logger> logger_;
    Unique<PluginManager> plugin_manager_;
    Unique<ai::InferenceEngine> ai_engine_;
    
    std::unordered_map<u64, Shared<Player>> players_;
    u64 next_player_id_ = 1;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Engine Singleton
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Engine& Engine::Instance() {
    static Engine instance;
    return instance;
}

Engine::Engine() : impl_(std::make_unique<Impl>()) {}
Engine::~Engine() = default;

Result<void> Engine::Initialize(const Config& config) {
    return impl_->Initialize(config);
}

void Engine::Shutdown() {
    impl_->Shutdown();
}

bool Engine::IsInitialized() const noexcept {
    return impl_->IsInitialized();
}

const Config& Engine::GetConfig() const noexcept {
    return impl_->GetConfig();
}

Result<void> Engine::UpdateConfig(const Config& config) {
    return impl_->UpdateConfig(config);
}

Result<Shared<Player>> Engine::CreatePlayer(std::string_view name) {
    return impl_->CreatePlayer(name);
}

void Engine::DestroyPlayer(Shared<Player>& player) {
    impl_->DestroyPlayer(player);
}

std::vector<Shared<Player>> Engine::GetPlayers() const {
    return impl_->GetPlayers();
}

PluginManager& Engine::GetPluginManager() {
    return impl_->GetPluginManager();
}

ai::InferenceEngine& Engine::GetAIEngine() {
    return impl_->GetAIEngine();
}

Logger& Engine::GetLogger() {
    return impl_->GetLogger();
}

const EngineCapabilities& Engine::GetCapabilities() const {
    return impl_->GetCapabilities();
}

EngineStatistics Engine::GetStatistics() const {
    return impl_->GetStatistics();
}

void Engine::ResetStatistics() {
    impl_->ResetStatistics();
}

bool Engine::IsCodecSupported(CodecID codec) const {
    return impl_->IsCodecSupported(codec);
}

bool Engine::IsContainerSupported(ContainerFormat format) const {
    return impl_->IsContainerSupported(format);
}

bool Engine::IsProtocolSupported(std::string_view protocol) const {
    return impl_->IsProtocolSupported(protocol);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Global Functions
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

bool Initialize(const Config& config) {
    return Engine::Instance().Initialize(config).has_value();
}

void Shutdown() {
    Engine::Instance().Shutdown();
}

const char* GetVersionString() {
    return VERSION_STRING.data();
}

BuildInfo GetBuildInfo() {
    return BuildInfo{
        .version_major = VERSION_MAJOR,
        .version_minor = VERSION_MINOR,
        .version_patch = VERSION_PATCH,
        .version_string = VERSION_STRING.data(),
        .codename = CODENAME.data(),
        .build_type = BUILD_TYPE.data(),
        .git_commit = GIT_COMMIT_HASH,
        .git_branch = GIT_BRANCH,
        .build_date = BUILD_DATE,
        .build_time = BUILD_TIME,
        
        #ifdef __clang__
        .compiler = "Clang",
        .compiler_version = __clang_version__,
        #elif defined(__GNUC__)
        .compiler = "GCC",
        .compiler_version = __VERSION__,
        #elif defined(_MSC_VER)
        .compiler = "MSVC",
        .compiler_version = std::to_string(_MSC_VER).c_str(),
        #else
        .compiler = "Unknown",
        .compiler_version = "Unknown",
        #endif
        
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
        .platform = "Windows",
        #elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_MACOS)
        .platform = "macOS",
        #elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
        .platform = "Linux",
        #elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_ANDROID)
        .platform = "Android",
        #elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_IOS)
        .platform = "iOS",
        #else
        .platform = "Unknown",
        #endif
        
        #ifdef AETHER_ARCH_X64
        .architecture = "x64",
        #elif defined(AETHER_ARCH_ARM64)
        .architecture = "ARM64",
        #elif defined(AETHER_ARCH_X86)
        .architecture = "x86",
        #elif defined(AETHER_ARCH_ARM)
        .architecture = "ARM",
        #else
        .architecture = "Unknown",
        #endif
        
        #ifdef AETHER_HAS_VULKAN
        .has_vulkan = true,
        #else
        .has_vulkan = false,
        #endif
        
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE
        .has_metal = true,
        #else
        .has_metal = false,
        #endif
        
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
        .has_d3d12 = true,
        #else
        .has_d3d12 = false,
        #endif
        
        .has_opengl = true,
        
        #ifdef AETHER_HAS_CUDA
        .has_cuda = true,
        #else
        .has_cuda = false,
        #endif
        
        .has_opencl = false,
        
        #ifdef AETHER_HAS_TENSORRT
        .has_tensorrt = true,
        #else
        .has_tensorrt = false,
        #endif
        
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE
        .has_coreml = true,
        #else
        .has_coreml = false,
        #endif
        
        #ifdef AETHER_HAS_ONNXRUNTIME
        .has_onnxruntime = true,
        #else
        .has_onnxruntime = false,
        #endif
        
        .has_ffmpeg = true,
        
        #ifdef AETHER_ENABLE_DRM
        .has_drm_support = true,
        #else
        .has_drm_support = false,
        #endif
    };
}

} // namespace aether
```
