```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Chapters & Bookmarks
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

std::vector<Chapter> PlayerImpl::GetChapters() const {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    return media_info_ ? media_info_->chapters : std::vector<Chapter>{};
}

Result<void> PlayerImpl::GoToChapter(i32 index) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    
    if (!media_info_) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "No media loaded");
    }
    
    if (index < 0 || static_cast<usize>(index) >= media_info_->chapters.size()) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidArgument, "Invalid chapter index");
    }
    
    const auto& chapter = media_info_->chapters[index];
    
    // Unlock before seeking
    mutex_.unlock();
    auto result = Seek(chapter.start, SeekMode::Keyframe);
    mutex_.lock();
    
    if (result) {
        DispatchEvent(ChapterChangedEvent{
            .old_chapter = -1,  // TODO: track current chapter
            .new_chapter = index,
            .chapter_title = chapter.title
        });
    }
    
    return result;
}

Result<void> PlayerImpl::NextChapter() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    
    if (!media_info_ || media_info_->chapters.empty()) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "No chapters available");
    }
    
    // Find current chapter
    i32 current = -1;
    for (usize i = 0; i < media_info_->chapters.size(); ++i) {
        if (position_ >= media_info_->chapters[i].start &&
            position_ < media_info_->chapters[i].end) {
            current = static_cast<i32>(i);
            break;
        }
    }
    
    i32 next = current + 1;
    if (static_cast<usize>(next) >= media_info_->chapters.size()) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "Already at last chapter");
    }
    
    mutex_.unlock();
    return GoToChapter(next);
}

Result<void> PlayerImpl::PreviousChapter() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    
    if (!media_info_ || media_info_->chapters.empty()) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "No chapters available");
    }
    
    // Find current chapter
    i32 current = 0;
    for (usize i = 0; i < media_info_->chapters.size(); ++i) {
        if (position_ >= media_info_->chapters[i].start &&
            position_ < media_info_->chapters[i].end) {
            current = static_cast<i32>(i);
            break;
        }
    }
    
    // If near start of chapter, go to previous; otherwise go to start of current
    const auto& chapter = media_info_->chapters[current];
    f64 time_in_chapter = position_.ToSeconds() - chapter.start.ToSeconds();
    
    i32 target = (time_in_chapter < 3.0 && current > 0) ? current - 1 : current;
    
    mutex_.unlock();
    return GoToChapter(target);
}

Result<void> PlayerImpl::SetABLoop(std::optional<Timestamp> a, std::optional<Timestamp> b) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    
    if (pipeline_) {
        return pipeline_->SetABLoop(a, b);
    }
    
    return {};
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Screenshots & Recording
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Result<std::string> PlayerImpl::TakeScreenshot(std::string_view path, std::string_view format) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    
    if (!pipeline_) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "No media loaded");
    }
    
    std::string output_path;
    if (path.empty()) {
        // Generate automatic filename
        auto now = std::chrono::system_clock::now();
        auto time_t = std::chrono::system_clock::to_time_t(now);
        std::stringstream ss;
        ss << "screenshot_" << std::put_time(std::localtime(&time_t), "%Y%m%d_%H%M%S");
        ss << "." << format;
        output_path = platform::GetDocumentsDirectory() + "/" + ss.str();
    } else {
        output_path = std::string(path);
    }
    
    if (auto result = pipeline_->CaptureFrame(output_path, format); !result) {
        return result.error();
    }
    
    engine_->GetLogger().Info("Screenshot saved: {}", output_path);
    
    return output_path;
}

Result<void> PlayerImpl::StartRecording(std::string_view path, const RecordingOptions& options) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    
    if (recording_.load()) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "Already recording");
    }
    
    if (!pipeline_) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "No media loaded");
    }
    
    if (auto result = pipeline_->StartRecording(path, options); !result) {
        return result;
    }
    
    recording_.store(true);
    
    DispatchEvent(RecordingEvent{
        .type = RecordingEvent::Type::Started,
        .output_path = std::string(path)
    });
    
    return {};
}

Result<void> PlayerImpl::StopRecording() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    
    if (!recording_.load()) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "Not recording");
    }
    
    std::string output_path;
    Duration recorded_duration;
    i64 file_size;
    
    if (pipeline_) {
        auto result = pipeline_->StopRecording();
        if (result) {
            output_path = result->path;
            recorded_duration = result->duration;
            file_size = result->file_size;
        }
    }
    
    recording_.store(false);
    
    DispatchEvent(RecordingEvent{
        .type = RecordingEvent::Type::Stopped,
        .output_path = output_path,
        .recorded_duration = recorded_duration,
        .file_size = file_size
    });
    
    return {};
}

bool PlayerImpl::IsRecording() const {
    return recording_.load();
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Events
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

u64 PlayerImpl::Subscribe(std::function<void(const PlayerEvent&)> callback) {
    return event_dispatcher_.Subscribe(std::move(callback));
}

void PlayerImpl::Unsubscribe(u64 handle) {
    event_dispatcher_.Unsubscribe(handle);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Playlist
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Result<void> PlayerImpl::AddToPlaylist(std::string_view url) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    
    playlist_.emplace_back(url);
    
    DispatchEvent(PlaylistEvent{
        .type = PlaylistEvent::Type::ItemAdded,
        .index = static_cast<i32>(playlist_.size() - 1),
        .url = std::string(url)
    });
    
    return {};
}

Result<void> PlayerImpl::AddToPlaylist(std::span<const std::string> urls) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    
    for (const auto& url : urls) {
        playlist_.push_back(url);
        
        DispatchEvent(PlaylistEvent{
            .type = PlaylistEvent::Type::ItemAdded,
            .index = static_cast<i32>(playlist_.size() - 1),
            .url = url
        });
    }
    
    return {};
}

void PlayerImpl::ClearPlaylist() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    
    playlist_.clear();
    playlist_index_ = -1;
    
    DispatchEvent(PlaylistEvent{
        .type = PlaylistEvent::Type::Cleared
    });
}

std::vector<std::string> PlayerImpl::GetPlaylist() const {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    return playlist_;
}

i32 PlayerImpl::GetPlaylistIndex() const {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    return playlist_index_;
}

Result<void> PlayerImpl::PlayNext() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    
    if (playlist_.empty()) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "Playlist is empty");
    }
    
    i32 next_index = playlist_index_ + 1;
    
    if (static_cast<usize>(next_index) >= playlist_.size()) {
        if (loop_mode_ == LoopMode::All) {
            next_index = 0;
        } else {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "End of playlist");
        }
    }
    
    mutex_.unlock();
    return PlayAt(next_index);
}

Result<void> PlayerImpl::PlayPrevious() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    
    if (playlist_.empty()) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "Playlist is empty");
    }
    
    i32 prev_index = playlist_index_ - 1;
    
    if (prev_index < 0) {
        if (loop_mode_ == LoopMode::All) {
            prev_index = static_cast<i32>(playlist_.size()) - 1;
        } else {
            // Go to start of current item
            mutex_.unlock();
            return SeekToTime(0, SeekMode::Keyframe);
        }
    }
    
    mutex_.unlock();
    return PlayAt(prev_index);
}

Result<void> PlayerImpl::PlayAt(i32 index) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    
    if (index < 0 || static_cast<usize>(index) >= playlist_.size()) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidArgument, "Invalid playlist index");
    }
    
    playlist_index_ = index;
    
    DispatchEvent(PlaylistEvent{
        .type = PlaylistEvent::Type::IndexChanged,
        .index = index,
        .url = playlist_[index]
    });
    
    mutex_.unlock();
    
    if (auto result = Open(playlist_[index], options_); !result) {
        return result;
    }
    
    return Play();
}

Result<void> PlayerImpl::SetLoopMode(LoopMode mode) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    loop_mode_ = mode;
    return {};
}

void PlayerImpl::ShufflePlaylist() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    
    if (playlist_.size() <= 1) {
        return;
    }
    
    // Fisher-Yates shuffle
    std::random_device rd;
    std::mt19937 gen(rd());
    
    for (usize i = playlist_.size() - 1; i > 0; --i) {
        std::uniform_int_distribution<usize> dis(0, i);
        std::swap(playlist_[i], playlist_[dis(gen)]);
    }
    
    DispatchEvent(PlaylistEvent{
        .type = PlaylistEvent::Type::Shuffled
    });
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Miscellaneous
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

std::string_view PlayerImpl::GetName() const {
    return name_;
}

const PlayerOptions& PlayerImpl::GetOptions() const {
    return options_;
}

Error PlayerImpl::GetLastError() const {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    return last_error_;
}

Result<std::string> PlayerImpl::ExecuteCommand(std::string_view command,
                                                std::span<const std::string> args) {
    // Custom command execution for advanced control
    
    if (command == "get-property") {
        if (args.empty()) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidArgument, "Property name required");
        }
        
        const auto& prop = args[0];
        
        if (prop == "volume") {
            return std::to_string(GetVolume());
        } else if (prop == "position") {
            return std::to_string(GetPosition().ToSeconds());
        } else if (prop == "duration") {
            return std::to_string(GetDuration().ToSeconds());
        } else if (prop == "state") {
            return std::string(ToString(GetState()));
        }
        
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidArgument, "Unknown property: " + std::string(prop));
    }
    
    if (command == "set-property") {
        if (args.size() < 2) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidArgument, "Property name and value required");
        }
        
        const auto& prop = args[0];
        const auto& value = args[1];
        
        if (prop == "volume") {
            SetVolume(std::stof(value));
            return "OK";
        }
        
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidArgument, "Unknown property: " + std::string(prop));
    }
    
    return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidArgument, "Unknown command: " + std::string(command));
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Private Methods
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

void PlayerImpl::SetState(PlaybackState new_state) {
    PlaybackState old_state = state_.exchange(new_state);
    
    if (old_state != new_state) {
        DispatchEvent(StateChangedEvent{
            .old_state = old_state,
            .new_state = new_state
        });
    }
}

void PlayerImpl::PlaybackLoop() {
    engine_->GetLogger().Debug("Playback thread started for '{}'", name_);
    
    while (running_.load()) {
        auto current_state = state_.load();
        
        if (current_state == PlaybackState::Playing) {
            ProcessFrame();
        } else if (current_state == PlaybackState::Paused ||
                   current_state == PlaybackState::Buffering) {
            // Wait for state change
            std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
            playback_cv_.wait_for(lock, std::chrono::milliseconds(50), [this] {
                return !running_.load() || state_.load() == PlaybackState::Playing;
            });
        } else {
            // Sleep briefly to avoid busy waiting
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(10));
        }
    }
    
    engine_->GetLogger().Debug("Playback thread stopped for '{}'", name_);
}

void PlayerImpl::ProcessFrame() {
    if (!pipeline_) {
        return;
    }
    
    // Get next frame from pipeline
    auto frame_result = pipeline_->GetNextFrame();
    
    if (!frame_result) {
        if (frame_result.error().code == ErrorCode::EndOfMedia) {
            // End of media
            DispatchEvent(EndOfMediaEvent{
                .url = media_info_ ? media_info_->url : ""
            });
            
            // Handle loop mode
            if (loop_mode_ == LoopMode::Single) {
                SeekToTime(0, SeekMode::Keyframe);
                Play();
            } else if (loop_mode_ == LoopMode::All && !playlist_.empty()) {
                PlayNext();
            } else {
                SetState(PlaybackState::EndOfMedia);
            }
        } else if (frame_result.error().code == ErrorCode::Buffering) {
            SetState(PlaybackState::Buffering);
            
            DispatchEvent(BufferingEvent{
                .percent = pipeline_->GetBufferPercent(),
                .is_buffering = true
            });
        }
        return;
    }
    
    // Update position
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        position_ = frame_result->timestamp;
    }
    
    // Dispatch position update (throttled)
    static auto last_position_event = Clock::now();
    auto now = Clock::now();
    if (now - last_position_event >= std::chrono::milliseconds(100)) {
        DispatchEvent(PositionChangedEvent{
            .position = position_,
            .duration = duration_,
            .progress_percent = duration_.pts > 0 
                ? (static_cast<f64>(position_.pts) / duration_.pts * 100.0) 
                : 0.0
        });
        last_position_event = now;
    }
}

template<typename EventType>
void PlayerImpl::DispatchEvent(const EventType& event) {
    event_dispatcher_.Dispatch(event);
}

} // namespace aether
```

## 9.3 Video Renderer (Vulkan)

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: src/render/video/renderer_vulkan.cpp
// DESCRIPTION: Vulkan video renderer implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#include "aether/video/video_renderer.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"
#include "aether/utils/logging.hpp"

#ifdef AETHER_HAS_VULKAN
#include <vulkan/vulkan.h>
#endif

#include <vector>
#include <array>

namespace aether {

#ifdef AETHER_HAS_VULKAN

class VulkanRenderer : public VideoRenderer {
public:
    VulkanRenderer() = default;
    ~VulkanRenderer() override { Shutdown(); }
    
    Result<void> Initialize(const VideoRendererConfig& config) override {
        config_ = config;
        
        // Create Vulkan instance
        if (auto result = CreateInstance(); !result) {
            return result;
        }
        
        // Setup debug messenger (debug builds)
        #ifndef NDEBUG
        SetupDebugMessenger();
        #endif
        
        // Create surface
        if (auto result = CreateSurface(config.window_handle); !result) {
            return result;
        }
        
        // Pick physical device
        if (auto result = PickPhysicalDevice(); !result) {
            return result;
        }
        
        // Create logical device
        if (auto result = CreateLogicalDevice(); !result) {
            return result;
        }
        
        // Create swap chain
        if (auto result = CreateSwapChain(); !result) {
            return result;
        }
        
        // Create image views
        if (auto result = CreateImageViews(); !result) {
            return result;
        }
        
        // Create render pass
        if (auto result = CreateRenderPass(); !result) {
            return result;
        }
        
        // Create graphics pipeline
        if (auto result = CreateGraphicsPipeline(); !result) {
            return result;
        }
        
        // Create framebuffers
        if (auto result = CreateFramebuffers(); !result) {
            return result;
        }
        
        // Create command pool and buffers
        if (auto result = CreateCommandBuffers(); !result) {
            return result;
        }
        
        // Create sync objects
        if (auto result = CreateSyncObjects(); !result) {
            return result;
        }
        
        // Create texture samplers
        if (auto result = CreateTextureSampler(); !result) {
            return result;
        }
        
        initialized_ = true;
        return {};
    }
    
    void Shutdown() override {
        if (!initialized_) {
            return;
        }
        
        vkDeviceWaitIdle(device_);
        
        CleanupSwapChain();
        
        vkDestroySampler(device_, texture_sampler_, nullptr);
        vkDestroyDescriptorPool(device_, descriptor_pool_, nullptr);
        vkDestroyDescriptorSetLayout(device_, descriptor_set_layout_, nullptr);
        
        for (usize i = 0; i < MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT; ++i) {
            vkDestroySemaphore(device_, render_finished_semaphores_[i], nullptr);
            vkDestroySemaphore(device_, image_available_semaphores_[i], nullptr);
            vkDestroyFence(device_, in_flight_fences_[i], nullptr);
        }
        
        vkDestroyCommandPool(device_, command_pool_, nullptr);
        vkDestroyPipeline(device_, graphics_pipeline_, nullptr);
        vkDestroyPipelineLayout(device_, pipeline_layout_, nullptr);
        vkDestroyRenderPass(device_, render_pass_, nullptr);
        vkDestroyDevice(device_, nullptr);
        
        #ifndef NDEBUG
        DestroyDebugMessenger();
        #endif
        
        vkDestroySurfaceKHR(instance_, surface_, nullptr);
        vkDestroyInstance(instance_, nullptr);
        
        initialized_ = false;
    }
    
    Result<void> RenderFrame(const VideoFrame& frame) override {
        // Wait for previous frame
        vkWaitForFences(device_, 1, &in_flight_fences_[current_frame_], 
                       VK_TRUE, UINT64_MAX);
        
        // Acquire next image
        u32 image_index;
        VkResult result = vkAcquireNextImageKHR(
            device_, swap_chain_, UINT64_MAX,
            image_available_semaphores_[current_frame_],
            VK_NULL_HANDLE, &image_index
        );
        
        if (result == VK_ERROR_OUT_OF_DATE_KHR) {
            return RecreateSwapChain();
        } else if (result != VK_SUCCESS && result != VK_SUBOPTIMAL_KHR) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::RenderError, "Failed to acquire swap chain image");
        }
        
        // Update texture with frame data
        if (auto update_result = UpdateTexture(frame); !update_result) {
            return update_result;
        }
        
        vkResetFences(device_, 1, &in_flight_fences_[current_frame_]);
        
        // Reset and record command buffer
        vkResetCommandBuffer(command_buffers_[current_frame_], 0);
        RecordCommandBuffer(command_buffers_[current_frame_], image_index);
        
        // Submit command buffer
        VkSubmitInfo submit_info{};
        submit_info.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_SUBMIT_INFO;
        
        VkSemaphore wait_semaphores[] = {image_available_semaphores_[current_frame_]};
        VkPipelineStageFlags wait_stages[] = {VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_COLOR_ATTACHMENT_OUTPUT_BIT};
        submit_info.waitSemaphoreCount = 1;
        submit_info.pWaitSemaphores = wait_semaphores;
        submit_info.pWaitDstStageMask = wait_stages;
        submit_info.commandBufferCount = 1;
        submit_info.pCommandBuffers = &command_buffers_[current_frame_];
        
        VkSemaphore signal_semaphores[] = {render_finished_semaphores_[current_frame_]};
        submit_info.signalSemaphoreCount = 1;
        submit_info.pSignalSemaphores = signal_semaphores;
        
        if (vkQueueSubmit(graphics_queue_, 1, &submit_info, 
                         in_flight_fences_[current_frame_]) != VK_SUCCESS) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::RenderError, "Failed to submit draw command buffer");
        }
        
        // Present
        VkPresentInfoKHR present_info{};
        present_info.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PRESENT_INFO_KHR;
        present_info.waitSemaphoreCount = 1;
        present_info.pWaitSemaphores = signal_semaphores;
        
        VkSwapchainKHR swap_chains[] = {swap_chain_};
        present_info.swapchainCount = 1;
        present_info.pSwapchains = swap_chains;
        present_info.pImageIndices = &image_index;
        
        result = vkQueuePresentKHR(present_queue_, &present_info);
        
        if (result == VK_ERROR_OUT_OF_DATE_KHR || result == VK_SUBOPTIMAL_KHR || 
            framebuffer_resized_) {
            framebuffer_resized_ = false;
            return RecreateSwapChain();
        } else if (result != VK_SUCCESS) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::RenderError, "Failed to present swap chain image");
        }
        
        current_frame_ = (current_frame_ + 1) % MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT;
        frame_count_++;
        
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<void> Resize(u32 width, u32 height) override {
        framebuffer_resized_ = true;
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<void> SetColorSpace(ColorSpace color_space) override {
        // Update color conversion matrix in shader
        color_space_ = color_space;
        return {};
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] RendererType GetType() const override {
        return RendererType::Vulkan;
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] std::string GetInfo() const override {
        if (!initialized_) {
            return "Vulkan Renderer (not initialized)";
        }
        
        VkPhysicalDeviceProperties props;
        vkGetPhysicalDeviceProperties(physical_device_, &props);
        
        return std::format("Vulkan Renderer: {} (Vulkan {}.{}.{})",
                          props.deviceName,
                          VK_VERSION_MAJOR(props.apiVersion),
                          VK_VERSION_MINOR(props.apiVersion),
                          VK_VERSION_PATCH(props.apiVersion));
    }

private:
    static constexpr usize MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT = 2;
    
    Result<void> CreateInstance() {
        VkApplicationInfo app_info{};
        app_info.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_APPLICATION_INFO;
        app_info.pApplicationName = "AETHER Media Engine";
        app_info.applicationVersion = VK_MAKE_VERSION(1, 0, 0);
        app_info.pEngineName = "AETHER";
        app_info.engineVersion = VK_MAKE_VERSION(1, 0, 0);
        app_info.apiVersion = VK_API_VERSION_1_3;
        
        VkInstanceCreateInfo create_info{};
        create_info.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_INSTANCE_CREATE_INFO;
        create_info.pApplicationInfo = &app_info;
        
        // Get required extensions
        std::vector<const char*> extensions = GetRequiredExtensions();
        create_info.enabledExtensionCount = static_cast<u32>(extensions.size());
        create_info.ppEnabledExtensionNames = extensions.data();
        
        #ifndef NDEBUG
        // Validation layers
        const std::vector<const char*> validation_layers = {
            "VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation"
        };
        
        if (CheckValidationLayerSupport(validation_layers)) {
            create_info.enabledLayerCount = static_cast<u32>(validation_layers.size());
            create_info.ppEnabledLayerNames = validation_layers.data();
        }
        #endif
        
        if (vkCreateInstance(&create_info, nullptr, &instance_) != VK_SUCCESS) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::RenderError, "Failed to create Vulkan instance");
        }
        
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<void> CreateSurface(void* window_handle) {
        // Platform-specific surface creation
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
        VkWin32SurfaceCreateInfoKHR create_info{};
        create_info.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_WIN32_SURFACE_CREATE_INFO_KHR;
        create_info.hwnd = static_cast<HWND>(window_handle);
        create_info.hinstance = GetModuleHandle(nullptr);
        
        if (vkCreateWin32SurfaceKHR(instance_, &create_info, nullptr, &surface_) != VK_SUCCESS) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::RenderError, "Failed to create window surface");
        }
        #elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
        // X11 or Wayland surface creation
        // ...
        #elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_MACOS)
        // Metal surface creation
        // ...
        #endif
        
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<void> PickPhysicalDevice() {
        u32 device_count = 0;
        vkEnumeratePhysicalDevices(instance_, &device_count, nullptr);
        
        if (device_count == 0) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::RenderError, "No Vulkan-capable GPU found");
        }
        
        std::vector<VkPhysicalDevice> devices(device_count);
        vkEnumeratePhysicalDevices(instance_, &device_count, devices.data());
        
        // Score and select best device
        i32 best_score = -1;
        for (const auto& device : devices) {
            i32 score = ScorePhysicalDevice(device);
            if (score > best_score && IsDeviceSuitable(device)) {
                best_score = score;
                physical_device_ = device;
            }
        }
        
        if (physical_device_ == VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::RenderError, "No suitable GPU found");
        }
        
        return {};
    }
    
    i32 ScorePhysicalDevice(VkPhysicalDevice device) {
        VkPhysicalDeviceProperties props;
        VkPhysicalDeviceFeatures features;
        vkGetPhysicalDeviceProperties(device, &props);
        vkGetPhysicalDeviceFeatures(device, &features);
        
        i32 score = 0;
        
        // Discrete GPUs are preferred
        if (props.deviceType == VK_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_TYPE_DISCRETE_GPU) {
            score += 1000;
        }
        
        // Maximum texture size
        score += props.limits.maxImageDimension2D;
        
        // Required features
        if (!features.samplerAnisotropy) {
            return 0;
        }
        
        return score;
    }
    
    bool IsDeviceSuitable(VkPhysicalDevice device) {
        // Check queue families
        auto indices = FindQueueFamilies(device);
        if (!indices.IsComplete()) {
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check extensions
        if (!CheckDeviceExtensionSupport(device)) {
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check swap chain support
        auto swap_chain_support = QuerySwapChainSupport(device);
        if (swap_chain_support.formats.empty() || 
            swap_chain_support.present_modes.empty()) {
            return false;
        }
        
        return true;
    }
    
    struct QueueFamilyIndices {
        std::optional<u32> graphics_family;
        std::optional<u32> present_family;
        
        [[nodiscard]] bool IsComplete() const {
            return graphics_family.has_value() && present_family.has_value();
        }
    };
    
    QueueFamilyIndices FindQueueFamilies(VkPhysicalDevice device) {
        QueueFamilyIndices indices;
        
        u32 queue_family_count = 0;
        vkGetPhysicalDeviceQueueFamilyProperties(device, &queue_family_count, nullptr);
        
        std::vector<VkQueueFamilyProperties> queue_families(queue_family_count);
        vkGetPhysicalDeviceQueueFamilyProperties(device, &queue_family_count, 
                                                  queue_families.data());
        
        for (u32 i = 0; i < queue_family_count; ++i) {
            if (queue_families[i].queueFlags & VK_QUEUE_GRAPHICS_BIT) {
                indices.graphics_family = i;
            }
            
            VkBool32 present_support = false;
            vkGetPhysicalDeviceSurfaceSupportKHR(device, i, surface_, &present_support);
            if (present_support) {
                indices.present_family = i;
            }
            
            if (indices.IsComplete()) {
                break;
            }
        }
        
        return indices;
    }
    
    Result<void> CreateLogicalDevice() {
        auto indices = FindQueueFamilies(physical_device_);
        
        std::vector<VkDeviceQueueCreateInfo> queue_create_infos;
        std::set<u32> unique_queue_families = {
            indices.graphics_family.value(),
            indices.present_family.value()
        };
        
        f32 queue_priority = 1.0f;
        for (u32 queue_family : unique_queue_families) {
            VkDeviceQueueCreateInfo queue_create_info{};
            queue_create_info.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DEVICE_QUEUE_CREATE_INFO;
            queue_create_info.queueFamilyIndex = queue_family;
            queue_create_info.queueCount = 1;
            queue_create_info.pQueuePriorities = &queue_priority;
            queue_create_infos.push_back(queue_create_info);
        }
        
        VkPhysicalDeviceFeatures device_features{};
        device_features.samplerAnisotropy = VK_TRUE;
        
        VkDeviceCreateInfo create_info{};
        create_info.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DEVICE_CREATE_INFO;
        create_info.queueCreateInfoCount = static_cast<u32>(queue_create_infos.size());
        create_info.pQueueCreateInfos = queue_create_infos.data();
        create_info.pEnabledFeatures = &device_features;
        
        const std::vector<const char*> device_extensions = {
            VK_KHR_SWAPCHAIN_EXTENSION_NAME
        };
        create_info.enabledExtensionCount = static_cast<u32>(device_extensions.size());
        create_info.ppEnabledExtensionNames = device_extensions.data();
        
        if (vkCreateDevice(physical_device_, &create_info, nullptr, &device_) != VK_SUCCESS) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::RenderError, "Failed to create logical device");
        }
        
        vkGetDeviceQueue(device_, indices.graphics_family.value(), 0, &graphics_queue_);
        vkGetDeviceQueue(device_, indices.present_family.value(), 0, &present_queue_);
        
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<void> CreateSwapChain() {
        auto swap_chain_support = QuerySwapChainSupport(physical_device_);
        
        auto surface_format = ChooseSwapSurfaceFormat(swap_chain_support.formats);
        auto present_mode = ChooseSwapPresentMode(swap_chain_support.present_modes);
        auto extent = ChooseSwapExtent(swap_chain_support.capabilities);
        
        u32 image_count = swap_chain_support.capabilities.minImageCount + 1;
        if (swap_chain_support.capabilities.maxImageCount > 0 &&
            image_count > swap_chain_support.capabilities.maxImageCount) {
            image_count = swap_chain_support.capabilities.maxImageCount;
        }
        
        VkSwapchainCreateInfoKHR create_info{};
        create_info.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_SWAPCHAIN_CREATE_INFO_KHR;
        create_info.surface = surface_;
        create_info.minImageCount = image_count;
        create_info.imageFormat = surface_format.format;
        create_info.imageColorSpace = surface_format.colorSpace;
        create_info.imageExtent = extent;
        create_info.imageArrayLayers = 1;
        create_info.imageUsage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_COLOR_ATTACHMENT_BIT;
        
        auto indices = FindQueueFamilies(physical_device_);
        u32 queue_family_indices[] = {
            indices.graphics_family.value(),
            indices.present_family.value()
        };
        
        if (indices.graphics_family != indices.present_family) {
            create_info.imageSharingMode = VK_SHARING_MODE_CONCURRENT;
            create_info.queueFamilyIndexCount = 2;
            create_info.pQueueFamilyIndices = queue_family_indices;
        } else {
            create_info.imageSharingMode = VK_SHARING_MODE_EXCLUSIVE;
        }
        
        create_info.preTransform = swap_chain_support.capabilities.currentTransform;
        create_info.compositeAlpha = VK_COMPOSITE_ALPHA_OPAQUE_BIT_KHR;
        create_info.presentMode = present_mode;
        create_info.clipped = VK_TRUE;
        create_info.oldSwapchain = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
        
        if (vkCreateSwapchainKHR(device_, &create_info, nullptr, &swap_chain_) != VK_SUCCESS) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::RenderError, "Failed to create swap chain");
        }
        
        vkGetSwapchainImagesKHR(device_, swap_chain_, &image_count, nullptr);
        swap_chain_images_.resize(image_count);
        vkGetSwapchainImagesKHR(device_, swap_chain_, &image_count, swap_chain_images_.data());
        
        swap_chain_image_format_ = surface_format.format;
        swap_chain_extent_ = extent;
        
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<void> UpdateTexture(const VideoFrame& frame) {
        // Convert frame data to texture
        // Handle different pixel formats (YUV420, NV12, etc.)
        
        VkDeviceSize image_size = frame.width * frame.height * 4;  // RGBA
        
        // Create staging buffer
        VkBuffer staging_buffer;
        VkDeviceMemory staging_memory;
        
        CreateBuffer(image_size, 
                    VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT,
                    VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_VISIBLE_BIT | VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_COHERENT_BIT,
                    staging_buffer, staging_memory);
        
        // Copy frame data to staging buffer
        void* data;
        vkMapMemory(device_, staging_memory, 0, image_size, 0, &data);
        
        // Convert pixel format if needed
        if (frame.format == PixelFormat::YUV420P) {
            ConvertYUV420ToRGBA(frame, static_cast<u8*>(data));
        } else if (frame.format == PixelFormat::NV12) {
            ConvertNV12ToRGBA(frame, static_cast<u8*>(data));
        } else {
            std::memcpy(data, frame.data[0], image_size);
        }
        
        vkUnmapMemory(device_, staging_memory);
        
        // Copy to GPU texture
        TransitionImageLayout(video_texture_, VK_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_SRGB,
                             VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED, 
                             VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL);
        
        CopyBufferToImage(staging_buffer, video_texture_, 
                         frame.width, frame.height);
        
        TransitionImageLayout(video_texture_, VK_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_SRGB,
                             VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL,
                             VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL);
        
        // Cleanup staging buffer
        vkDestroyBuffer(device_, staging_buffer, nullptr);
        vkFreeMemory(device_, staging_memory, nullptr);
        
        return {};
    }
    
    void ConvertYUV420ToRGBA(const VideoFrame& frame, u8* output) {
        // YUV to RGB conversion using BT.709 or BT.601 matrix
        const u8* y_plane = frame.data[0];
        const u8* u_plane = frame.data[1];
        const u8* v_plane = frame.data[2];
        
        i32 y_stride = frame.linesize[0];
        i32 u_stride = frame.linesize[1];
        i32 v_stride = frame.linesize[2];
        
        // BT.709 conversion matrix
        constexpr f32 kr = 0.2126f;
        constexpr f32 kb = 0.0722f;
        
        for (u32 j = 0; j < frame.height; ++j) {
            for (u32 i = 0; i < frame.width; ++i) {
                i32 y = y_plane[j * y_stride + i] - 16;
                i32 u = u_plane[(j / 2) * u_stride + (i / 2)] - 128;
                i32 v = v_plane[(j / 2) * v_stride + (i / 2)] - 128;
                
                // Convert to RGB
                i32 r = (298 * y + 409 * v + 128) >> 8;
                i32 g = (298 * y - 100 * u - 208 * v + 128) >> 8;
                i32 b = (298 * y + 516 * u + 128) >> 8;
                
                // Clamp
                r = std::clamp(r, 0, 255);
                g = std::clamp(g, 0, 255);
                b = std::clamp(b, 0, 255);
                
                u32 idx = (j * frame.width + i) * 4;
                output[idx + 0] = static_cast<u8>(r);
                output[idx + 1] = static_cast<u8>(g);
                output[idx + 2] = static_cast<u8>(b);
                output[idx + 3] = 255;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // ... Additional Vulkan helper methods ...
    
private:
    VideoRendererConfig config_;
    bool initialized_ = false;
    
    VkInstance instance_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkSurfaceKHR surface_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkPhysicalDevice physical_device_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkDevice device_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    
    VkQueue graphics_queue_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkQueue present_queue_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    
    VkSwapchainKHR swap_chain_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    std::vector<VkImage> swap_chain_images_;
    VkFormat swap_chain_image_format_;
    VkExtent2D swap_chain_extent_;
    std::vector<VkImageView> swap_chain_image_views_;
    std::vector<VkFramebuffer> swap_chain_framebuffers_;
    
    VkRenderPass render_pass_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkDescriptorSetLayout descriptor_set_layout_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkPipelineLayout pipeline_layout_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkPipeline graphics_pipeline_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    
    VkCommandPool command_pool_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    std::vector<VkCommandBuffer> command_buffers_;
    
    VkImage video_texture_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkDeviceMemory video_texture_memory_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkImageView video_texture_view_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkSampler texture_sampler_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    
    VkDescriptorPool descriptor_pool_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    std::vector<VkDescriptorSet> descriptor_sets_;
    
    std::vector<VkSemaphore> image_available_semaphores_;
    std::vector<VkSemaphore> render_finished_semaphores_;
    std::vector<VkFence> in_flight_fences_;
    
    usize current_frame_ = 0;
    u64 frame_count_ = 0;
    bool framebuffer_resized_ = false;
    
    ColorSpace color_space_ = ColorSpace::BT709;
    
    #ifndef NDEBUG
    VkDebugUtilsMessengerEXT debug_messenger_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    #endif
};

#endif // AETHER_HAS_VULKAN

// Factory function
Unique<VideoRenderer> CreateVideoRenderer(RendererType type) {
    switch (type) {
        #ifdef AETHER_HAS_VULKAN
        case RendererType::Vulkan:
            return std::make_unique<VulkanRenderer>();
        #endif
        
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE
        case RendererType::Metal:
            return CreateMetalRenderer();
        #endif
        
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
        case RendererType::D3D12:
            return CreateD3D12Renderer();
        #endif
        
        case RendererType::OpenGL:
            return CreateOpenGLRenderer();
        
        default:
            return nullptr;
    }
}

} // namespace aether
```

## 9.4 Audio Renderer (Cross-Platform)

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: src/audio/device/audio_renderer_base.cpp
// DESCRIPTION: Cross-platform audio renderer base
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#include "aether/audio/audio_device.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"

#include <atomic>
#include <mutex>
#include <queue>
#include <condition_variable>

namespace aether {

/**
 * @class AudioRendererBase
 * @brief Base class for audio renderers with common functionality
 */
class AudioRendererBase : public AudioRenderer {
public:
    AudioRendererBase() = default;
    ~AudioRendererBase() override = default;
    
    Result<void> SetFormat(const AudioFormat& format) override {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        format_ = format;
        return ConfigureFormat();
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] AudioFormat GetFormat() const override {
        return format_;
    }
    
    Result<void> SetVolume(f32 volume) override {
        volume_.store(std::clamp(volume, 0.0f, 2.0f));
        return {};
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] f32 GetVolume() const override {
        return volume_.load();
    }
    
    Result<void> SetMuted(bool muted) override {
        muted_.store(muted);
        return {};
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsMuted() const override {
        return muted_.load();
    }
    
    Result<void> Write(std::span<const f32> samples) override {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(queue_mutex_);
        
        // Apply volume and mute
        f32 volume = muted_.load() ? 0.0f : volume_.load();
        
        std::vector<f32> processed(samples.size());
        for (usize i = 0; i < samples.size(); ++i) {
            processed[i] = samples[i] * volume;
        }
        
        // Add to queue
        audio_queue_.push(std::move(processed));
        queue_cv_.notify_one();
        
        return {};
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] u32 GetLatency() const override {
        return latency_ms_.load();
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] u32 GetBufferedSamples() const override {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(queue_mutex_);
        u32 total = 0;
        // Note: This is a simplified implementation
        return total;
    }
    
    Result<void> Flush() override {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(queue_mutex_);
        while (!audio_queue_.empty()) {
            audio_queue_.pop();
        }
        return {};
    }
    
protected:
    virtual Result<void> ConfigureFormat() = 0;
    
    // Get next audio buffer for playback
    std::optional<std::vector<f32>> GetNextBuffer() {
        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(queue_mutex_);
        
        if (audio_queue_.empty()) {
            return std::nullopt;
        }
        
        auto buffer = std::move(audio_queue_.front());
        audio_queue_.pop();
        
        return buffer;
    }
    
protected:
    AudioFormat format_;
    std::atomic<f32> volume_{1.0f};
    std::atomic<bool> muted_{false};
    std::atomic<u32> latency_ms_{0};
    
    mutable std::mutex mutex_;
    mutable std::mutex queue_mutex_;
    std::condition_variable queue_cv_;
    std::queue<std::vector<f32>> audio_queue_;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Windows WASAPI Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS

#include <audioclient.h>
#include <mmdeviceapi.h>
#include <functiondiscoverykeys_devpkey.h>
#include <wrl/client.h>

using Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr;

class WASAPIAudioRenderer : public AudioRendererBase {
public:
    WASAPIAudioRenderer() {
        CoInitializeEx(nullptr, COINIT_MULTITHREADED);
    }
    
    ~WASAPIAudioRenderer() override {
        Stop();
        CoUninitialize();
    }
    
    Result<void> Initialize(const AudioDeviceConfig& config) override {
        config_ = config;
        
        // Get device enumerator
        ComPtr<IMMDeviceEnumerator> enumerator;
        HRESULT hr = CoCreateInstance(
            __uuidof(MMDeviceEnumerator),
            nullptr,
            CLSCTX_ALL,
            __uuidof(IMMDeviceEnumerator),
            reinterpret_cast<void**>(enumerator.GetAddressOf())
        );
        
        if (FAILED(hr)) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError, "Failed to create device enumerator");
        }
        
        // Get default device or specified device
        if (config.device_id.empty()) {
            hr = enumerator->GetDefaultAudioEndpoint(
                eRender, eConsole, device_.GetAddressOf()
            );
        } else {
            hr = enumerator->GetDevice(
                std::wstring(config.device_id.begin(), config.device_id.end()).c_str(),
                device_.GetAddressOf()
            );
        }
        
        if (FAILED(hr)) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError, "Failed to get audio device");
        }
        
        // Activate audio client
        hr = device_->Activate(
            __uuidof(IAudioClient),
            CLSCTX_ALL,
            nullptr,
            reinterpret_cast<void**>(audio_client_.GetAddressOf())
        );
        
        if (FAILED(hr)) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError, "Failed to activate audio client");
        }
        
        // Get mix format
        WAVEFORMATEX* mix_format;
        hr = audio_client_->GetMixFormat(&mix_format);
        if (FAILED(hr)) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError, "Failed to get mix format");
        }
        
        // Initialize audio client
        DWORD flags = 0;
        if (config.exclusive_mode) {
            flags |= AUDCLNT_STREAMFLAGS_EXCLUSIVE;
        }
        
        hr = audio_client_->Initialize(
            config.exclusive_mode ? AUDCLNT_SHAREMODE_EXCLUSIVE : AUDCLNT_SHAREMODE_SHARED,
            flags | AUDCLNT_STREAMFLAGS_EVENTCALLBACK,
            config.buffer_size_frames * 10000000 / mix_format->nSamplesPerSec,
            0,
            mix_format,
            nullptr
        );
        
        CoTaskMemFree(mix_format);
        
        if (FAILED(hr)) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError, "Failed to initialize audio client");
        }
        
        // Create event for buffer notifications
        buffer_event_ = CreateEvent(nullptr, FALSE, FALSE, nullptr);
        if (!buffer_event_) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError, "Failed to create event");
        }
        
        hr = audio_client_->SetEventHandle(buffer_event_);
        if (FAILED(hr)) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError, "Failed to set event handle");
        }
        
        // Get render client
        hr = audio_client_->GetService(
            __uuidof(IAudioRenderClient),
            reinterpret_cast<void**>(render_client_.GetAddressOf())
        );
        
        if (FAILED(hr)) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError, "Failed to get render client");
        }
        
        // Get buffer size
        hr = audio_client_->GetBufferSize(&buffer_frames_);
        if (FAILED(hr)) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError, "Failed to get buffer size");
        }
        
        initialized_ = true;
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<void> Start() override {
        if (!initialized_) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "Not initialized");
        }
        
        running_.store(true);
        render_thread_ = std::thread(&WASAPIAudioRenderer::RenderLoop, this);
        
        HRESULT hr = audio_client_->Start();
        if (FAILED(hr)) {
            running_.store(false);
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError, "Failed to start audio client");
        }
        
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<void> Stop() override {
        running_.store(false);
        
        if (render_thread_.joinable()) {
            SetEvent(buffer_event_);
            render_thread_.join();
        }
        
        if (audio_client_) {
            audio_client_->Stop();
        }
        
        return {};
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsRunning() const override {
        return running_.load();
    }

protected:
    Result<void> ConfigureFormat() override {
        // WASAPI handles format conversion automatically in shared mode
        return {};
    }

private:
    void RenderLoop() {
        while (running_.load()) {
            DWORD wait_result = WaitForSingleObject(buffer_event_, 100);
            
            if (wait_result != WAIT_OBJECT_0) {
                continue;
            }
            
            // Get padding (how much is already in buffer)
            UINT32 padding;
            HRESULT hr = audio_client_->GetCurrentPadding(&padding);
            if (FAILED(hr)) {
                continue;
            }
            
            UINT32 frames_available = buffer_frames_ - padding;
            if (frames_available == 0) {
                continue;
            }
            
            // Get buffer
            BYTE* buffer;
            hr = render_client_->GetBuffer(frames_available, &buffer);
            if (FAILED(hr)) {
                continue;
            }
            
            // Fill buffer with audio data
            auto audio_data = GetNextBuffer();
            DWORD flags = 0;
            
            if (audio_data) {
                // Convert to output format and copy
                usize samples_to_copy = std::min(
                    audio_data->size(),
                    static_cast<usize>(frames_available * format_.channels)
                );
                
                if (format_.sample_format == SampleFormat::F32) {
                    std::memcpy(buffer, audio_data->data(), 
                               samples_to_copy * sizeof(f32));
                } else {
                    // Convert to target format
                    ConvertSamples(*audio_data, buffer, format_.sample_format);
                }
            } else {
                flags = AUDCLNT_BUFFERFLAGS_SILENT;
            }
            
            render_client_->ReleaseBuffer(frames_available, flags);
        }
    }
    
    void ConvertSamples(const std::vector<f32>& input, BYTE* output, 
                        SampleFormat format) {
        switch (format) {
            case SampleFormat::S16: {
                i16* out = reinterpret_cast<i16*>(output);
                for (usize i = 0; i < input.size(); ++i) {
                    out[i] = static_cast<i16>(std::clamp(input[i], -1.0f, 1.0f) * 32767);
                }
                break;
            }
            case SampleFormat::S32: {
                i32* out = reinterpret_cast<i32*>(output);
                for (usize i = 0; i < input.size(); ++i) {
                    out[i] = static_cast<i32>(std::clamp(input[i], -1.0f, 1.0f) * 2147483647);
                }
                break;
            }
            default:
                // F32 - direct copy
                std::memcpy(output, input.data(), input.size() * sizeof(f32));
                break;
        }
    }

private:
    AudioDeviceConfig config_;
    bool initialized_ = false;
    std::atomic<bool> running_{false};
    
    ComPtr<IMMDevice> device_;
    ComPtr<IAudioClient> audio_client_;
    ComPtr<IAudioRenderClient> render_client_;
    
    HANDLE buffer_event_ = nullptr;
    UINT32 buffer_frames_ = 0;
    
    std::thread render_thread_;
};

#endif // AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// macOS/iOS CoreAudio Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE

#include <AudioToolbox/AudioToolbox.h>

class CoreAudioRenderer : public AudioRendererBase {
public:
    CoreAudioRenderer() = default;
    ~CoreAudioRenderer() override {
        Stop();
    }
    
    Result<void> Initialize(const AudioDeviceConfig& config) override {
        config_ = config;
        
        // Create audio component description
        AudioComponentDescription desc;
        desc.componentType = kAudioUnitType_Output;
        #if TARGET_OS_IPHONE
        desc.componentSubType = kAudioUnitSubType_RemoteIO;
        #else
        desc.componentSubType = kAudioUnitSubType_DefaultOutput;
        #endif
        desc.componentManufacturer = kAudioUnitManufacturer_Apple;
        desc.componentFlags = 0;
        desc.componentFlagsMask = 0;
        
        // Find component
        AudioComponent component = AudioComponentFindNext(nullptr, &desc);
        if (!component) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError, "Failed to find audio component");
        }
        
        // Create audio unit
        OSStatus status = AudioComponentInstanceNew(component, &audio_unit_);
        if (status != noErr) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError, "Failed to create audio unit");
        }
        
        // Set audio format
        AudioStreamBasicDescription format;
        format.mSampleRate = config.sample_rate > 0 ? config.sample_rate : 48000;
        format.mFormatID = kAudioFormatLinearPCM;
        format.mFormatFlags = kAudioFormatFlagIsFloat | kAudioFormatFlagIsPacked;
        format.mBitsPerChannel = 32;
        format.mChannelsPerFrame = config.channels > 0 ? config.channels : 2;
        format.mFramesPerPacket = 1;
        format.mBytesPerFrame = format.mChannelsPerFrame * sizeof(f32);
        format.mBytesPerPacket = format.mBytesPerFrame;
        
        status = AudioUnitSetProperty(
            audio_unit_,
            kAudioUnitProperty_StreamFormat,
            kAudioUnitScope_Input,
            0,
            &format,
            sizeof(format)
        );
        
        if (status != noErr) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError, "Failed to set audio format");
        }
        
        // Set render callback
        AURenderCallbackStruct callback;
        callback.inputProc = RenderCallback;
        callback.inputProcRefCon = this;
        
        status = AudioUnitSetProperty(
            audio_unit_,
            kAudioUnitProperty_SetRenderCallback,
            kAudioUnitScope_Input,
            0,
            &callback,
            sizeof(callback)
        );
        
        if (status != noErr) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError, "Failed to set render callback");
        }
        
        // Initialize
        status = AudioUnitInitialize(audio_unit_);
        if (status != noErr) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError, "Failed to initialize audio unit");
        }
        
        format_.sample_rate = static_cast<u32>(format.mSampleRate);
        format_.channels = format.mChannelsPerFrame;
        format_.sample_format = SampleFormat::F32;
        
        initialized_ = true;
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<void> Start() override {
        if (!initialized_) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "Not initialized");
        }
        
        running_.store(true);
        OSStatus status = AudioOutputUnitStart(audio_unit_);
        
        if (status != noErr) {
            running_.store(false);
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError, "Failed to start audio unit");
        }
        
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<void> Stop() override {
        running_.store(false);
        
        if (audio_unit_) {
            AudioOutputUnitStop(audio_unit_);
            AudioComponentInstanceDispose(audio_unit_);
            audio_unit_ = nullptr;
        }
        
        return {};
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsRunning() const override {
        return running_.load();
    }

protected:
    Result<void> ConfigureFormat() override {
        return {};
    }

private:
    static OSStatus RenderCallback(
        void* inRefCon,
        AudioUnitRenderActionFlags* ioActionFlags,
        const AudioTimeStamp* inTimeStamp,
        UInt32 inBusNumber,
        UInt32 inNumberFrames,
        AudioBufferList* ioData
    ) {
        auto* self = static_cast<CoreAudioRenderer*>(inRefCon);
        
        for (UInt32 i = 0; i < ioData->mNumberBuffers; ++i) {
            auto& buffer = ioData->mBuffers[i];
            f32* output = static_cast<f32*>(buffer.mData);
            
            auto audio_data = self->GetNextBuffer();
            
            if (audio_data && !audio_data->empty()) {
                usize samples_to_copy = std::min(
                    audio_data->size(),
                    static_cast<usize>(inNumberFrames * buffer.mNumberChannels)
                );
                std::memcpy(output, audio_data->data(), samples_to_copy * sizeof(f32));
                
                // Zero remaining
                if (samples_to_copy < inNumberFrames * buffer.mNumberChannels) {
                    std::memset(output + samples_to_copy, 0,
                               (inNumberFrames * buffer.mNumberChannels - samples_to_copy) * sizeof(f32));
                }
            } else {
                // Silence
                std::memset(output, 0, buffer.mDataByteSize);
            }
        }
        
        return noErr;
    }

private:
    AudioDeviceConfig config_;
    bool initialized_ = false;
    std::atomic<bool> running_{false};
    
    AudioUnit audio_unit_ = nullptr;
};

#endif // AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Linux ALSA/PulseAudio Implementation  
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX

#include <alsa/asoundlib.h>
#include <pulse/pulseaudio.h>

class ALSAAudioRenderer : public AudioRendererBase {
public:
    ALSAAudioRenderer() = default;
    ~ALSAAudioRenderer() override {
        Stop();
    }
    
    Result<void> Initialize(const AudioDeviceConfig& config) override {
        config_ = config;
        
        const char* device = config.device_id.empty() ? "default" : config.device_id.c_str();
        
        int err = snd_pcm_open(&pcm_handle_, device, SND_PCM_STREAM_PLAYBACK, 0);
        if (err < 0) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError, 
                             std::string("Failed to open ALSA device: ") + snd_strerror(err));
        }
        
        // Configure hardware parameters
        snd_pcm_hw_params_t* hw_params;
        snd_pcm_hw_params_alloca(&hw_params);
        snd_pcm_hw_params_any(pcm_handle_, hw_params);
        
        // Set access type
        snd_pcm_hw_params_set_access(pcm_handle_, hw_params, SND_PCM_ACCESS_RW_INTERLEAVED);
        
        // Set format
        snd_pcm_hw_params_set_format(pcm_handle_, hw_params, SND_PCM_FORMAT_FLOAT_LE);
        
        // Set sample rate
        unsigned int rate = config.sample_rate > 0 ? config.sample_rate : 48000;
        snd_pcm_hw_params_set_rate_near(pcm_handle_, hw_params, &rate, nullptr);
        
        // Set channels
        unsigned int channels = config.channels > 0 ? config.channels : 2;
        snd_pcm_hw_params_set_channels(pcm_handle_, hw_params, channels);
        
        // Set buffer size
        snd_pcm_uframes_t buffer_size = config.buffer_size_frames > 0 
            ? config.buffer_size_frames 
            : 4096;
        snd_pcm_hw_params_set_buffer_size_near(pcm_handle_, hw_params, &buffer_size);
        
        // Set period size
        snd_pcm_uframes_t period_size = buffer_size / 4;
        snd_pcm_hw_params_set_period_size_near(pcm_handle_, hw_params, &period_size, nullptr);
        
        // Apply parameters
        err = snd_pcm_hw_params(pcm_handle_, hw_params);
        if (err < 0) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AudioError,
                             std::string("Failed to set ALSA params: ") + snd_strerror(err));
        }
        
        // Get actual format
        format_.sample_rate = rate;
        format_.channels = channels;
        format_.sample_format = SampleFormat::F32;
        
        period_size_ = period_size;
        
        initialized_ = true;
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<void> Start() override {
        if (!initialized_) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "Not initialized");
        }
        
        running_.store(true);
        render_thread_ = std::thread(&ALSAAudioRenderer::RenderLoop, this);
        
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<void> Stop() override {
        running_.store(false);
        
        if (render_thread_.joinable()) {
            queue_cv_.notify_all();
            render_thread_.join();
        }
        
        if (pcm_handle_) {
            snd_pcm_drain(pcm_handle_);
            snd_pcm_close(pcm_handle_);
            pcm_handle_ = nullptr;
        }
        
        return {};
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsRunning() const override {
        return running_.load();
    }

protected:
    Result<void> ConfigureFormat() override {
        return {};
    }

private:
    void RenderLoop() {
        std::vector<f32> silence(period_size_ * format_.channels, 0.0f);
        
        while (running_.load()) {
            auto audio_data = GetNextBuffer();
            
            const f32* buffer;
            snd_pcm_uframes_t frames;
            
            if (audio_data && !audio_data->empty()) {
                buffer = audio_data->data();
                frames = audio_data->size() / format_.channels;
            } else {
                buffer = silence.data();
                frames = period_size_;
            }
            
            int err = snd_pcm_writei(pcm_handle_, buffer, frames);
            
            if (err < 0) {
                err = snd_pcm_recover(pcm_handle_, err, 0);
                if (err < 0) {
                    // Error recovery failed
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    }

private:
    AudioDeviceConfig config_;
    bool initialized_ = false;
    std::atomic<bool> running_{false};
    
    snd_pcm_t* pcm_handle_ = nullptr;
    snd_pcm_uframes_t period_size_ = 0;
    
    std::thread render_thread_;
};

#endif // AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Factory Function
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Unique<AudioRenderer> CreateAudioRenderer(AudioBackend backend) {
    switch (backend) {
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
        case AudioBackend::WASAPI:
            return std::make_unique<WASAPIAudioRenderer>();
        #endif
        
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE
        case AudioBackend::CoreAudio:
            return std::make_unique<CoreAudioRenderer>();
        #endif
        
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX
        case AudioBackend::ALSA:
            return std::make_unique<ALSAAudioRenderer>();
        #endif
        
        case AudioBackend::Auto:
        default:
            #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
            return std::make_unique<WASAPIAudioRenderer>();
            #elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE)
            return std::make_unique<CoreAudioRenderer>();
            #elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
            return std::make_unique<ALSAAudioRenderer>();
            #else
            return nullptr;
            #endif
    }
}

} // namespace aether
```

```bash
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# AETHER MEDIA ENGINE - Quick Start
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

# Clone repository
git clone --recursive https://github.com/aether-engine/aether-media-engine.git
cd aether-media-engine

# Install dependencies (using Conan)
pip install conan
conan profile detect
conan install . --build=missing -of build

# Configure and build
cmake --preset release
cmake --build build/release --parallel

# Run tests
ctest --test-dir build/release --output-on-failure

# Run desktop player
./build/release/bin/aether-player

# Build documentation
cmake --build build/release --target docs
```

---

This documentation and implementation provides a comprehensive foundation for the AETHER Media Engine. The architecture is designed to be:

- **Modular**: Each component can be developed and tested independently
- **Extensible**: Plugin system allows third-party extensions
- **Cross-Platform**: Abstractions for all major platforms
- **AI-Powered**: Integrated ML inference for enhancement features
- **Enterprise-Ready**: DRM, analytics, and security features

```cpp
#pragma once
```

```cpp
#define AETHER_VERSION_HPP

#include <cstdint>
#include <string_view>

namespace aether {

inline constexpr uint32_t VERSION_MAJOR = 1;
inline constexpr uint32_t VERSION_MINOR = 0;
inline constexpr uint32_t VERSION_PATCH = 0;
inline constexpr uint32_t VERSION = VERSION_MAJOR * 10000 + VERSION_MINOR * 100 + VERSION_PATCH;
inline constexpr std::string_view VERSION_STRING = "1.0.0";
inline constexpr std::string_view CODENAME = "Prometheus";

#ifdef NDEBUG
inline constexpr std::string_view BUILD_TYPE = "Release";
#else
inline constexpr std::string_view BUILD_TYPE = "Debug";
#endif

extern const char* GIT_COMMIT_HASH;
extern const char* GIT_BRANCH;
extern const char* BUILD_DATE;
extern const char* BUILD_TIME;

struct BuildInfo {
    uint32_t version_major;
    uint32_t version_minor;
    uint32_t version_patch;
    const char* version_string;
    const char* codename;
    const char* build_type;
    const char* git_commit;
    const char* git_branch;
    const char* build_date;
    const char* build_time;
    const char* compiler;
    const char* compiler_version;
    const char* platform;
    const char* architecture;
    
    bool has_vulkan;
    bool has_metal;
    bool has_d3d12;
    bool has_opengl;
    bool has_cuda;
    bool has_opencl;
    bool has_tensorrt;
    bool has_coreml;
    bool has_onnxruntime;
    bool has_ffmpeg;
    bool has_drm_support;
};

constexpr bool IsVersionAtLeast(uint32_t major, uint32_t minor, uint32_t patch) {
    return VERSION >= (major * 10000 + minor * 100 + patch);
}

} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_VERSION_HPP
```

```cpp
    #define AETHER_ARCH_NAME "WebAssembly"

    #define AETHER_ARCH_NAME "Unknown"

// Export macros

    #ifdef AETHER_BUILD_SHARED
        #ifdef AETHER_EXPORT
            #define AETHER_API __declspec(dllexport)
        #else
            #define AETHER_API __declspec(dllimport)
        #endif
    #else
        #define AETHER_API
    #endif
    #define AETHER_LOCAL
#else
    #if __GNUC__ >= 4 || defined(__clang__)
        #define AETHER_API __attribute__((visibility("default")))
        #define AETHER_LOCAL __attribute__((visibility("hidden")))
    #else
        #define AETHER_API
        #define AETHER_LOCAL
    #endif

    #define AETHER_FALLTHROUGH [[fallthrough]]
#else

    #define AETHER_MAYBE_UNUSED
    #define AETHER_FALLTHROUGH
#endif

    #define AETHER_UNLIKELY [[unlikely]]
#else
    #define AETHER_LIKELY
    #define AETHER_UNLIKELY
#endif

    #define AETHER_DEPRECATED [[deprecated]]
    #define AETHER_DEPRECATED_MSG(msg) [[deprecated(msg)]]

    #define AETHER_DEPRECATED_MSG(msg) __declspec(deprecated(msg))

    #define AETHER_DEPRECATED_MSG(msg) __attribute__((deprecated(msg)))

    #define AETHER_DEPRECATED_MSG(msg)
#endif

    #define AETHER_FORCE_INLINE __forceinline
#elif defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
    #define AETHER_FORCE_INLINE __attribute__((always_inline)) inline
#else
    #define AETHER_FORCE_INLINE inline
#endif

    #define AETHER_NO_INLINE __declspec(noinline)

    #define AETHER_NO_INLINE __attribute__((noinline))
#else
    #define AETHER_NO_INLINE
#endif

#define AETHER_CACHE_LINE_SIZE 64
#define AETHER_CACHE_ALIGNED AETHER_ALIGN(AETHER_CACHE_LINE_SIZE)

// Branch hints
#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
    #define AETHER_EXPECT(x, v) __builtin_expect(!!(x), v)
    #define AETHER_LIKELY_COND(x) __builtin_expect(!!(x), 1)
    #define AETHER_UNLIKELY_COND(x) __builtin_expect(!!(x), 0)
#else
    #define AETHER_EXPECT(x, v) (x)
    #define AETHER_LIKELY_COND(x) (x)
    #define AETHER_UNLIKELY_COND(x) (x)
#endif

// Restrict

    #define AETHER_RESTRICT __restrict
#elif defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
    #define AETHER_RESTRICT __restrict__
#else
    #define AETHER_RESTRICT
#endif

// Debug break
#if defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS)
    #define AETHER_DEBUG_BREAK() __debugbreak()
#elif defined(__clang__) || defined(__GNUC__)
    #define AETHER_DEBUG_BREAK() __builtin_trap()
#else
    #define AETHER_DEBUG_BREAK() ((void)0)
#endif

// Assert
#ifdef NDEBUG
    #define AETHER_ASSERT(expr) ((void)0)
    #define AETHER_ASSERT_MSG(expr, msg) ((void)0)
#else
    #define AETHER_ASSERT(expr) \
        do { \
            if (!(expr)) { \
                AETHER_DEBUG_BREAK(); \
            } \
        } while (0)
    #define AETHER_ASSERT_MSG(expr, msg) \
        do { \
            if (!(expr)) { \
                AETHER_DEBUG_BREAK(); \
            } \
        } while (0)
#endif

#endif // AETHER_EXPORT_HPP
```
