```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/core/types.hpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#pragma once

#ifndef AETHER_CORE_TYPES_HPP
#define AETHER_CORE_TYPES_HPP

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstddef>
#include <chrono>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <memory>
#include <optional>
#include <variant>
#include <functional>
#include <span>
#include <vector>
#include <map>
#include <array>
#include <algorithm>

namespace aether {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Basic Types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

using i8  = std::int8_t;
using i16 = std::int16_t;
using i32 = std::int32_t;
using i64 = std::int64_t;

using u8  = std::uint8_t;
using u16 = std::uint16_t;
using u32 = std::uint32_t;
using u64 = std::uint64_t;

using f32 = float;
using f64 = double;

using usize = std::size_t;
using isize = std::ptrdiff_t;

using byte = std::byte;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Time Types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

using Clock = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock;
using SteadyClock = std::chrono::steady_clock;
using SystemClock = std::chrono::system_clock;
using TimePoint = Clock::time_point;
using SteadyTimePoint = SteadyClock::time_point;
using Duration = Clock::duration;
using Nanoseconds = std::chrono::nanoseconds;
using Microseconds = std::chrono::microseconds;
using Milliseconds = std::chrono::milliseconds;
using Seconds = std::chrono::seconds;

/**
 * @struct Timestamp
 * @brief Media timestamp with timebase
 */
struct Timestamp {
    i64 pts = 0;
    i64 dts = 0;
    i64 duration = 0;
    i32 timebase_num = 1;
    i32 timebase_den = 1000000;
    
    constexpr Timestamp() = default;
    
    constexpr Timestamp(i64 p, i64 d = 0, i64 dur = 0, i32 tb_num = 1, i32 tb_den = 1000000)
        : pts(p), dts(d), duration(dur), timebase_num(tb_num), timebase_den(tb_den) {}
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr f64 ToSeconds() const noexcept {
        return static_cast<f64>(pts) * timebase_num / timebase_den;
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr i64 ToMilliseconds() const noexcept {
        return pts * timebase_num * 1000 / timebase_den;
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr i64 ToMicroseconds() const noexcept {
        return pts * timebase_num * 1000000 / timebase_den;
    }
    
    static constexpr Timestamp FromSeconds(f64 seconds, i32 tb_num = 1, i32 tb_den = 1000000) noexcept {
        return Timestamp{
            static_cast<i64>(seconds * tb_den / tb_num),
            0, 0, tb_num, tb_den
        };
    }
    
    static constexpr Timestamp FromMilliseconds(i64 ms) noexcept {
        return Timestamp{ms * 1000, 0, 0, 1, 1000000};
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr Timestamp Rescale(i32 new_tb_num, i32 new_tb_den) const noexcept {
        i64 new_pts = pts * timebase_num * new_tb_den / (timebase_den * new_tb_num);
        return Timestamp{new_pts, 0, 0, new_tb_num, new_tb_den};
    }
    
    constexpr auto operator<=>(const Timestamp& other) const noexcept {
        return ToMicroseconds() <=> other.ToMicroseconds();
    }
    
    constexpr bool operator==(const Timestamp& other) const noexcept {
        return ToMicroseconds() == other.ToMicroseconds();
    }
    
    constexpr Timestamp operator+(const Timestamp& other) const noexcept {
        return FromSeconds(ToSeconds() + other.ToSeconds(), timebase_num, timebase_den);
    }
    
    constexpr Timestamp operator-(const Timestamp& other) const noexcept {
        return FromSeconds(ToSeconds() - other.ToSeconds(), timebase_num, timebase_den);
    }
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Geometry Types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

template<typename T>
struct Vec2 {
    T x{};
    T y{};
    
    constexpr Vec2() = default;
    constexpr Vec2(T x_, T y_) : x(x_), y(y_) {}
    
    constexpr Vec2 operator+(const Vec2& other) const { return {x + other.x, y + other.y}; }
    constexpr Vec2 operator-(const Vec2& other) const { return {x - other.x, y - other.y}; }
    constexpr Vec2 operator*(T scalar) const { return {x * scalar, y * scalar}; }
    constexpr Vec2 operator/(T scalar) const { return {x / scalar, y / scalar}; }
    
    constexpr bool operator==(const Vec2&) const = default;
};

template<typename T>
struct Vec3 {
    T x{};
    T y{};
    T z{};
    
    constexpr Vec3() = default;
    constexpr Vec3(T x_, T y_, T z_) : x(x_), y(y_), z(z_) {}
    
    constexpr bool operator==(const Vec3&) const = default;
};

template<typename T>
struct Vec4 {
    T x{};
    T y{};
    T z{};
    T w{};
    
    constexpr Vec4() = default;
    constexpr Vec4(T x_, T y_, T z_, T w_) : x(x_), y(y_), z(z_), w(w_) {}
    
    constexpr bool operator==(const Vec4&) const = default;
};

template<typename T>
struct Size2D {
    T width{};
    T height{};
    
    constexpr Size2D() = default;
    constexpr Size2D(T w, T h) : width(w), height(h) {}
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr T Area() const noexcept { return width * height; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr f64 AspectRatio() const noexcept { 
        return height != 0 ? static_cast<f64>(width) / static_cast<f64>(height) : 0.0; 
    }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool IsEmpty() const noexcept { return width <= 0 || height <= 0; }
    
    constexpr bool operator==(const Size2D&) const = default;
    constexpr auto operator<=>(const Size2D& other) const { return Area() <=> other.Area(); }
};

template<typename T>
struct Rect {
    T x{};
    T y{};
    T width{};
    T height{};
    
    constexpr Rect() = default;
    constexpr Rect(T x_, T y_, T w, T h) : x(x_), y(y_), width(w), height(h) {}
    constexpr Rect(Vec2<T> pos, Size2D<T> size) : x(pos.x), y(pos.y), width(size.width), height(size.height) {}
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr T Left() const noexcept { return x; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr T Top() const noexcept { return y; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr T Right() const noexcept { return x + width; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr T Bottom() const noexcept { return y + height; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr T Area() const noexcept { return width * height; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool IsEmpty() const noexcept { return width <= 0 || height <= 0; }
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr Vec2<T> Position() const noexcept { return {x, y}; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr Size2D<T> Size() const noexcept { return {width, height}; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr Vec2<T> Center() const noexcept { return {x + width/2, y + height/2}; }
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool Contains(Vec2<T> point) const noexcept {
        return point.x >= x && point.x < Right() && point.y >= y && point.y < Bottom();
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool Contains(const Rect& other) const noexcept {
        return other.x >= x && other.Right() <= Right() && 
               other.y >= y && other.Bottom() <= Bottom();
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool Intersects(const Rect& other) const noexcept {
        return !(other.x >= Right() || other.Right() <= x ||
                 other.y >= Bottom() || other.Bottom() <= y);
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr Rect Intersection(const Rect& other) const noexcept {
        T l = std::max(x, other.x);
        T t = std::max(y, other.y);
        T r = std::min(Right(), other.Right());
        T b = std::min(Bottom(), other.Bottom());
        if (r <= l || b <= t) return {};
        return {l, t, r - l, b - t};
    }
    
    constexpr bool operator==(const Rect&) const = default;
};

using Vec2i = Vec2<i32>;
using Vec2f = Vec2<f32>;
using Vec2d = Vec2<f64>;
using Vec3i = Vec3<i32>;
using Vec3f = Vec3<f32>;
using Vec4f = Vec4<f32>;

using Size = Size2D<i32>;
using SizeU = Size2D<u32>;
using SizeF = Size2D<f32>;

using Point = Vec2<i32>;
using PointF = Vec2<f32>;

using Rectangle = Rect<i32>;
using RectangleF = Rect<f32>;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Color Types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct Color {
    f32 r = 0.0f;
    f32 g = 0.0f;
    f32 b = 0.0f;
    f32 a = 1.0f;
    
    constexpr Color() = default;
    constexpr Color(f32 r_, f32 g_, f32 b_, f32 a_ = 1.0f) : r(r_), g(g_), b(b_), a(a_) {}
    
    static constexpr Color Black() { return {0, 0, 0, 1}; }
    static constexpr Color White() { return {1, 1, 1, 1}; }
    static constexpr Color Red() { return {1, 0, 0, 1}; }
    static constexpr Color Green() { return {0, 1, 0, 1}; }
    static constexpr Color Blue() { return {0, 0, 1, 1}; }
    static constexpr Color Yellow() { return {1, 1, 0, 1}; }
    static constexpr Color Cyan() { return {0, 1, 1, 1}; }
    static constexpr Color Magenta() { return {1, 0, 1, 1}; }
    static constexpr Color Transparent() { return {0, 0, 0, 0}; }
    static constexpr Color Gray(f32 v = 0.5f) { return {v, v, v, 1}; }
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr u32 ToRGBA8() const noexcept {
        return (static_cast<u32>(std::clamp(r, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255) << 24) |
               (static_cast<u32>(std::clamp(g, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255) << 16) |
               (static_cast<u32>(std::clamp(b, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255) << 8) |
               static_cast<u32>(std::clamp(a, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255);
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr u32 ToARGB8() const noexcept {
        return (static_cast<u32>(std::clamp(a, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255) << 24) |
               (static_cast<u32>(std::clamp(r, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255) << 16) |
               (static_cast<u32>(std::clamp(g, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255) << 8) |
               static_cast<u32>(std::clamp(b, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255);
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr u32 ToABGR8() const noexcept {
        return (static_cast<u32>(std::clamp(a, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255) << 24) |
               (static_cast<u32>(std::clamp(b, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255) << 16) |
               (static_cast<u32>(std::clamp(g, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255) << 8) |
               static_cast<u32>(std::clamp(r, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255);
    }
    
    static constexpr Color FromRGBA8(u32 rgba) noexcept {
        return {
            static_cast<f32>((rgba >> 24) & 0xFF) / 255.0f,
            static_cast<f32>((rgba >> 16) & 0xFF) / 255.0f,
            static_cast<f32>((rgba >> 8) & 0xFF) / 255.0f,
            static_cast<f32>(rgba & 0xFF) / 255.0f
        };
    }
    
    static constexpr Color FromARGB8(u32 argb) noexcept {
        return {
            static_cast<f32>((argb >> 16) & 0xFF) / 255.0f,
            static_cast<f32>((argb >> 8) & 0xFF) / 255.0f,
            static_cast<f32>(argb & 0xFF) / 255.0f,
            static_cast<f32>((argb >> 24) & 0xFF) / 255.0f
        };
    }
    
    static constexpr Color FromHex(u32 hex) noexcept {
        return FromRGBA8((hex << 8) | 0xFF);
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr Color WithAlpha(f32 alpha) const noexcept {
        return {r, g, b, alpha};
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr Color Lerp(const Color& other, f32 t) const noexcept {
        return {
            r + (other.r - r) * t,
            g + (other.g - g) * t,
            b + (other.b - b) * t,
            a + (other.a - a) * t
        };
    }
    
    constexpr bool operator==(const Color&) const = default;
};

struct ColorRGBA8 {
    u8 r = 0;
    u8 g = 0;
    u8 b = 0;
    u8 a = 255;
    
    constexpr ColorRGBA8() = default;
    constexpr ColorRGBA8(u8 r_, u8 g_, u8 b_, u8 a_ = 255) : r(r_), g(g_), b(b_), a(a_) {}
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr Color ToFloat() const noexcept {
        return {r / 255.0f, g / 255.0f, b / 255.0f, a / 255.0f};
    }
    
    static constexpr ColorRGBA8 FromFloat(const Color& c) noexcept {
        return {
            static_cast<u8>(std::clamp(c.r, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255),
            static_cast<u8>(std::clamp(c.g, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255),
            static_cast<u8>(std::clamp(c.b, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255),
            static_cast<u8>(std::clamp(c.a, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255)
        };
    }
};

// Smart Pointers & Handles
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

template<typename T>
using Unique = std::unique_ptr<T>;

template<typename T>
using Shared = std::shared_ptr<T>;

template<typename T>
using Weak = std::weak_ptr<T>;

template<typename T>
using Ref = std::reference_wrapper<T>;

template<typename T>
using Optional = std::optional<T>;

template<typename T>
using Span = std::span<T>;

template<typename T, typename... Args>
inline Unique<T> MakeUnique(Args&&... args) {
    return std::make_unique<T>(std::forward<Args>(args)...);
}

template<typename T, typename... Args>
inline Shared<T> MakeShared(Args&&... args) {
    return std::make_shared<T>(std::forward<Args>(args)...);
}

struct Handle {
    u64 id = 0;
    u32 generation = 0;
    void* ptr = nullptr;
    
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool IsValid() const noexcept { return id != 0 && ptr != nullptr; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr explicit operator bool() const noexcept { return IsValid(); }
    
    constexpr bool operator==(const Handle&) const = default;
};

using PlayerHandle = Handle;
using SourceHandle = Handle;
using TrackHandle = Handle;
using DecoderHandle = Handle;
using RendererHandle = Handle;
using PluginHandle = Handle;
using FilterHandle = Handle;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Callback Types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

using ProgressCallback = std::function<void(f64 progress)>;
using CompletionCallback = std::function<void(bool success, std::string_view message)>;
using DataCallback = std::function<void(Span<const u8> data)>;
using VoidCallback = std::function<void()>;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Utility Functions
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

constexpr std::string_view ToString(MediaType type) noexcept {
    switch (type) {
        case MediaType::Video: return "Video";
        case MediaType::Audio: return "Audio";
        case MediaType::Subtitle: return "Subtitle";
        case MediaType::Data: return "Data";
        case MediaType::Attachment: return "Attachment";
        default: return "Unknown";
    }
}

constexpr std::string_view ToString(PlaybackState state) noexcept {
    switch (state) {
        case PlaybackState::Idle: return "Idle";
        case PlaybackState::Opening: return "Opening";
        case PlaybackState::Buffering: return "Buffering";
        case PlaybackState::Ready: return "Ready";
        case PlaybackState::Playing: return "Playing";
        case PlaybackState::Paused: return "Paused";
        case PlaybackState::Seeking: return "Seeking";
        case PlaybackState::Stopping: return "Stopping";
        case PlaybackState::Stopped: return "Stopped";
        case PlaybackState::EndOfMedia: return "EndOfMedia";
        case PlaybackState::Error: return "Error";
        default: return "Unknown";
    }
}

constexpr u32 GetBytesPerSample(SampleFormat format) noexcept {
    switch (format) {
        case SampleFormat::U8:
        case SampleFormat::U8P:
            return 1;
        case SampleFormat::S16:
        case SampleFormat::S16P:
            return 2;
        case SampleFormat::S32:
        case SampleFormat::S32P:
        case SampleFormat::F32:
        case SampleFormat::F32P:
            return 4;
        case SampleFormat::S64:
        case SampleFormat::F64:
        case SampleFormat::F64P:
            return 8;
        default:
            return 0;
    }
}

constexpr bool IsPlanarFormat(SampleFormat format) noexcept {
    switch (format) {
        case SampleFormat::U8P:
        case SampleFormat::S16P:
        case SampleFormat::S32P:
        case SampleFormat::F32P:
        case SampleFormat::F64P:
            return true;
        default:
            return false;
    }
}

constexpr u32 GetChannelCount(ChannelLayout layout) noexcept {
    switch (layout) {
        case ChannelLayout::Mono: return 1;
        case ChannelLayout::Stereo: return 2;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround21: return 3;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround30: return 3;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround31: return 4;
        case ChannelLayout::Quad: return 4;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround40: return 4;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround41: return 5;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround50: return 5;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround51: return 6;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround61: return 7;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround71: return 8;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround714: return 12;
        case ChannelLayout::Surround916: return 16;
        default: return 0;
    }
}

constexpr u32 GetBitsPerPixel(PixelFormat format) noexcept {
    switch (format) {
        case PixelFormat::Gray8: return 8;
        case PixelFormat::Gray16: return 16;
        case PixelFormat::RGB565:
        case PixelFormat::RGB555: return 16;
        case PixelFormat::RGB24:
        case PixelFormat::BGR24: return 24;
        case PixelFormat::RGBA32:
        case PixelFormat::BGRA32:
        case PixelFormat::ARGB32:
        case PixelFormat::ABGR32: return 32;
        case PixelFormat::RGB48: return 48;
        case PixelFormat::RGBA64: return 64;
        case PixelFormat::YUV420P: return 12;
        case PixelFormat::YUV422P: return 16;
        case PixelFormat::YUV444P: return 24;
        case PixelFormat::NV12:
        case PixelFormat::NV21: return 12;
        case PixelFormat::YUV420P10: return 15;
        case PixelFormat::P010: return 15;
        default: return 0;
    }
}

constexpr bool IsHardwareFormat(PixelFormat format) noexcept {
    switch (format) {
        case PixelFormat::D3D11:
        case PixelFormat::DXVA2:
        case PixelFormat::VAAPI:
        case PixelFormat::VDPAU:
        case PixelFormat::VideoToolbox:
        case PixelFormat::MediaCodec:
        case PixelFormat::CUDA:
        case PixelFormat::QSV:
        case PixelFormat::Vulkan:
            return true;
        default:
            return false;
    }
}

#endif // AETHER_CORE_TYPES_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Error Category
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API AetherErrorCategory : public std::error_category {
public:
    [[nodiscard]] const char* name() const noexcept override { return "aether"; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::string message(int ev) const override;
    
    static const AetherErrorCategory& Instance() noexcept {
        static AetherErrorCategory instance;
        return instance;
    }
};

inline std::error_code make_error_code(ErrorCode e) noexcept {
    return {static_cast<int>(e), AetherErrorCategory::Instance()};
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Error Class
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API Error {
public:
    Error() = default;
    
    Error(ErrorCode code)
        : code_(code) {}
    
    Error(ErrorCode code, std::string message)
        : code_(code), message_(std::move(message)) {}
    
    Error(ErrorCode code, std::string message, std::source_location loc)
        : code_(code), message_(std::move(message)), location_(loc) {}
    
    template<typename... Args>
    static Error Make(ErrorCode code, std::format_string<Args...> fmt, Args&&... args) {
        return Error{code, std::format(fmt, std::forward<Args>(args)...)};
    }
    
    static Error Make(ErrorCode code, std::string_view msg, 
                     std::source_location loc = std::source_location::current()) {
        return Error{code, std::string(msg), loc};
    }
    
    static Error Success() { return Error{ErrorCode::Success}; }
    
    [[nodiscard]] ErrorCode Code() const noexcept { return code_; }
    [[nodiscard]] const std::string& Message() const noexcept { return message_; }
    [[nodiscard]] const std::string& Details() const noexcept { return details_; }
    [[nodiscard]] const std::source_location& Location() const noexcept { return location_; }
    
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsSuccess() const noexcept { return code_ == ErrorCode::Success; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsError() const noexcept { return code_ != ErrorCode::Success; }
    [[nodiscard]] explicit operator bool() const noexcept { return IsError(); }
    
    void SetDetails(std::string details) { details_ = std::move(details); }
    
    Error& WithDetails(std::string details) {
        details_ = std::move(details);
        return *this;
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] std::string ToString() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::error_code ToErrorCode() const noexcept {
        return make_error_code(code_);
    }
    
    bool operator==(ErrorCode code) const noexcept { return code_ == code; }
    bool operator!=(ErrorCode code) const noexcept { return code_ != code; }
    
private:
    ErrorCode code_ = ErrorCode::Success;
    std::string message_;
    std::string details_;
    std::source_location location_;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Result Type
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

template<typename T>
using Result = std::expected<T, Error>;

template<typename T>
[[nodiscard]] inline bool IsOk(const Result<T>& result) {
    return result.has_value();
}

template<typename T>
[[nodiscard]] inline bool IsErr(const Result<T>& result) {
    return !result.has_value();
}

// Helper macros
#define AETHER_TRY(expr) \
    ({ \
        auto _result = (expr); \
        if (!_result.has_value()) { \
            return std::unexpected(_result.error()); \
        } \
        std::move(_result).value(); \
    })

#define AETHER_TRY_VOID(expr) \
    do { \
        auto _result = (expr); \
        if (!_result.has_value()) { \
            return std::unexpected(_result.error()); \
        } \
    } while(0)

#define AETHER_OK(value) std::expected<decltype(value), Error>{value}
#define AETHER_ERR(code, msg) std::unexpected(Error::Make(code, msg))

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Exception Class
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API Exception : public std::exception {
public:
    explicit Exception(Error error) : error_(std::move(error)) {
        what_str_ = error_.ToString();
    }
    
    Exception(ErrorCode code, std::string_view message)
        : error_(code, std::string(message)) {
        what_str_ = error_.ToString();
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] const char* what() const noexcept override {
        return what_str_.c_str();
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] const Error& GetError() const noexcept { return error_; }
    [[nodiscard]] ErrorCode Code() const noexcept { return error_.Code(); }
    
private:
    Error error_;
    std::string what_str_;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Error Utility Functions
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

[[nodiscard]] AETHER_API std::string_view GetErrorCodeName(ErrorCode code) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] AETHER_API std::string_view GetErrorCodeDescription(ErrorCode code) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] AETHER_API bool IsRecoverableError(ErrorCode code) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] AETHER_API bool IsFatalError(ErrorCode code) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] AETHER_API ErrorCode FromSystemError(int sys_errno) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] AETHER_API ErrorCode FromFFmpegError(int averror) noexcept;

} // namespace aether

// Enable std::error_code integration
template<>
struct std::is_error_code_enum<aether::ErrorCode> : std::true_type {};

#endif // AETHER_CORE_ERROR_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: src/core/error.cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#include "aether/core/error.hpp"
#include <sstream>
#include <cerrno>

namespace aether {

std::string AetherErrorCategory::message(int ev) const {
    return std::string(GetErrorCodeDescription(static_cast<ErrorCode>(ev)));
}

std::string Error::ToString() const {
    std::ostringstream ss;
    
    ss << "[" << GetErrorCodeName(code_) << "]";
    
    if (!message_.empty()) {
        ss << " " << message_;
    }
    
    if (!details_.empty()) {
        ss << " (" << details_ << ")";
    }
    
    if (location_.file_name() != nullptr && location_.file_name()[0] != '\0') {
        ss << " at " << location_.file_name() << ":" << location_.line();
    }
    
    return ss.str();
}

std::string_view GetErrorCodeName(ErrorCode code) noexcept {
    switch (code) {
        case ErrorCode::Success: return "Success";
        case ErrorCode::Unknown: return "Unknown";
        case ErrorCode::InvalidArgument: return "InvalidArgument";
        case ErrorCode::InvalidState: return "InvalidState";
        case ErrorCode::NotInitialized: return "NotInitialized";
        case ErrorCode::AlreadyInitialized: return "AlreadyInitialized";
        case ErrorCode::NotSupported: return "NotSupported";
        case ErrorCode::NotImplemented: return "NotImplemented";

        case ErrorCode::OutOfRange: return "OutOfRange";
        case ErrorCode::PermissionDenied: return "PermissionDenied";
        case ErrorCode::Timeout: return "Timeout";
        case ErrorCode::Cancelled: return "Cancelled";

        case ErrorCode::OutputRestricted: return "OutputRestricted";
        case ErrorCode::SecurityError: return "SecurityError";

        case ErrorCode::PluginInitFailed: return "PluginInitFailed";
        case ErrorCode::PluginAPIError: return "PluginAPIError";

        case ErrorCode::DriverError: return "DriverError";
        
        case ErrorCode::InternalError: return "InternalError";
        case ErrorCode::AssertionFailed: return "AssertionFailed";
        case ErrorCode::NullPointer: return "NullPointer";
        case ErrorCode::InvalidHandle: return "InvalidHandle";
        case ErrorCode::ResourceLeak: return "ResourceLeak";
        
        default: return "Unknown";
    }
}

std::string_view GetErrorCodeDescription(ErrorCode code) noexcept {
    switch (code) {
        case ErrorCode::Success: return "Operation completed successfully";
        case ErrorCode::Unknown: return "An unknown error occurred";
        case ErrorCode::InvalidArgument: return "Invalid argument provided";
        case ErrorCode::InvalidState: return "Invalid state for this operation";
        case ErrorCode::NotInitialized: return "Component not initialized";
        case ErrorCode::NotSupported: return "Operation not supported";
        case ErrorCode::NotFound: return "Requested item not found";
        case ErrorCode::OutOfMemory: return "Out of memory";
        case ErrorCode::Timeout: return "Operation timed out";
        case ErrorCode::Cancelled: return "Operation was cancelled";
        
        case ErrorCode::FileNotFound: return "File not found";
        case ErrorCode::UnsupportedFormat: return "Unsupported media format";
        case ErrorCode::UnsupportedCodec: return "Unsupported codec";
        case ErrorCode::DecoderError: return "Decoder error";
        case ErrorCode::RenderError: return "Rendering error";
        
        case ErrorCode::NetworkError: return "Network error";
        case ErrorCode::ConnectionFailed: return "Connection failed";
        case ErrorCode::ConnectionTimeout: return "Connection timed out";
        
        default: return "Unknown error";
    }
}

bool IsRecoverableError(ErrorCode code) noexcept {
    switch (code) {
        case ErrorCode::Timeout:
        case ErrorCode::WouldBlock:
        case ErrorCode::Busy:
        case ErrorCode::NeedMoreData:
        case ErrorCode::Buffering:
        case ErrorCode::ConnectionTimeout:
            return true;
        default:
            return false;
    }
}

bool IsFatalError(ErrorCode code) noexcept {
    switch (code) {
        case ErrorCode::OutOfMemory:
        case ErrorCode::InternalError:
        case ErrorCode::AssertionFailed:
        case ErrorCode::NullPointer:
        case ErrorCode::DeviceLost:
            return true;
        default:
            return false;
    }
}

ErrorCode FromSystemError(int sys_errno) noexcept {
    switch (sys_errno) {
        case 0: return ErrorCode::Success;
        case ENOENT: return ErrorCode::FileNotFound;
        case EACCES: return ErrorCode::PermissionDenied;
        case EEXIST: return ErrorCode::AlreadyExists;
        case ENOMEM: return ErrorCode::OutOfMemory;
        case EINVAL: return ErrorCode::InvalidArgument;
        case ENOTSUP: return ErrorCode::NotSupported;
        case ETIMEDOUT: return ErrorCode::Timeout;
        case EBUSY: return ErrorCode::Busy;
        case EINTR: return ErrorCode::Interrupted;
        case ERANGE: return ErrorCode::OutOfRange;
        case ENOSPC: return ErrorCode::DiskFull;
        case EIO: return ErrorCode::IOError;
        default: return ErrorCode::Unknown;
    }
}

ErrorCode FromFFmpegError(int averror) noexcept {
    // FFmpeg uses negative error codes
    if (averror >= 0) return ErrorCode::Success;
    
    // Common FFmpeg errors
    switch (averror) {
        case -2: return ErrorCode::FileNotFound;  // AVERROR(ENOENT)
        case -5: return ErrorCode::IOError;       // AVERROR(EIO)
        case -12: return ErrorCode::OutOfMemory;  // AVERROR(ENOMEM)
        case -22: return ErrorCode::InvalidArgument; // AVERROR(EINVAL)
        default: break;
    }
    
    // AVERROR_EOF
    if (averror == -541478725) return ErrorCode::EndOfFile;
    
    // AVERROR_INVALIDDATA
    if (averror == -1094995529) return ErrorCode::InvalidMediaData;
    
    return ErrorCode::Unknown;
}

} // namespace aether
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/config.hpp

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Configuration Value Type

using ConfigValue = std::variant<
    bool,
    i64,
    f64,
    std::string,
    std::vector<std::string>,
    std::map<std::string, std::string>
>;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Log Configuration
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct LogConfig {
    enum class Level : u8 {
        Trace = 0,
        Debug = 1,
        Info = 2,
        Warning = 3,
        Error = 4,
        Critical = 5,
        Off = 6
    };
    
    Level level = Level::Info;
    bool log_to_console = true;
    bool log_to_file = false;
    std::string log_file_path;
    i64 max_log_file_size_mb = 10;
    u32 max_log_files = 5;
    bool include_timestamps = true;
    bool include_thread_id = false;
    bool include_source_location = false;
    bool colored_output = true;
    std::string log_pattern;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Video Configuration
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct VideoConfig {
    // Decoding
    bool enable_hardware_decoding = true;
    std::vector<std::string> preferred_hw_decoders;
    u32 decode_thread_count = 0;  // 0 = auto
    bool allow_frame_drop = true;
    u32 max_decode_queue_size = 16;

    std::string preferred_renderer;  // "vulkan", "metal", "d3d12", "opengl", "auto"
    bool enable_vsync = true;
    bool enable_triple_buffering = true;

    u32 target_fps = 0;  // 0 = match source
    
    // Output
    bool enable_hdr_output = true;
    bool enable_10bit_output = true;
    ColorSpace output_color_space = ColorSpace::Unknown;
    ColorRange output_color_range = ColorRange::Unknown;

    std::string scale_algorithm = "lanczos";
    bool enable_deinterlacing = true;
    DeinterlaceMode deinterlace_mode = DeinterlaceMode::Auto;

    u32 max_video_height = 0;
    bool gpu_memory_optimization = true;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct AudioConfig {

    bool enable_head_tracking = true;

    bool remember_volume = true;
    bool mute_on_focus_lost = false;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    std::string ssl_client_key_path;

    bool cache_streamed_content = false;

    bool enable_quic = false;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    bool enable_int8 = false;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Subtitle Configuration

struct SubtitleConfig {

    bool auto_select = true;
    std::vector<std::string> preferred_languages;
    bool prefer_forced = false;
    bool prefer_sdh = false;
    bool prefer_cc = false;
    
    // Rendering
    std::string font_family = "sans-serif";
    std::string font_path;
    u32 font_size = 48;
    Color font_color = Color::White();
    Color background_color = Color{0, 0, 0, 0.5f};
    Color outline_color = Color::Black();
    f32 outline_width = 2.0f;
    Color shadow_color = Color{0, 0, 0, 0.5f};
    f32 shadow_offset = 2.0f;
    bool bold = false;
    bool italic = false;
    
    // Position
    f32 margin_bottom = 0.05f;
    f32 margin_sides = 0.05f;
    f32 margin_top = 0.05f;
    i32 vertical_alignment = 2;  // 0=top, 1=middle, 2=bottom
    i32 horizontal_alignment = 1;  // 0=left, 1=center, 2=right
    
    // Advanced
    bool enable_ass_styling = true;
    bool enable_bitmap_subtitles = true;
    f32 scale = 1.0f;
    bool override_style = false;
    i32 render_mode = 0;  // 0=normal, 1=force_blend
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Cache Configuration
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct CacheConfig {
    // Memory cache
    i64 memory_cache_size_mb = 512;
    bool cache_decoded_frames = true;
    u32 decoded_frame_cache_count = 30;
    bool cache_audio_samples = true;
    u32 audio_cache_seconds = 10;
    
    // Disk cache
    bool enable_disk_cache = true;
    std::string disk_cache_path;
    i64 disk_cache_size_mb = 4096;
    bool persist_cache = true;
    
    // Thumbnail cache
    bool cache_thumbnails = true;
    i64 thumbnail_cache_size_mb = 256;
    Size thumbnail_size = {320, 180};
    
    // Metadata cache
    bool cache_metadata = true;
    u32 metadata_cache_expiry_hours = 24;
    
    // Cleanup
    bool auto_cleanup = true;
    f32 cleanup_threshold = 0.9f;  // Start cleanup at 90% capacity
    u32 cleanup_interval_minutes = 60;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Plugin Configuration
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct PluginConfig {
    bool enable_plugins = true;
    std::vector<std::string> plugin_directories;
    std::vector<std::string> enabled_plugins;
    std::vector<std::string> disabled_plugins;
    bool auto_load_plugins = true;
    bool sandbox_plugins = true;
    u32 plugin_timeout_ms = 5000;
    bool allow_network_access = true;
    bool allow_file_access = false;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// DRM Configuration
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct DRMConfig {
    bool enable_drm = true;
    std::string license_server_url;
    std::map<std::string, std::string> license_headers;
    bool persist_licenses = true;
    std::string license_storage_path;
    u32 license_request_timeout_ms = 30000;
    u32 license_retry_count = 3;
    bool hardware_security_required = false;
    std::string preferred_security_level;
    
    // Per-DRM settings
    struct {
        std::string device_certificate;
    } widevine;
    
    struct {
        std::string individualization_server;
    } playready;
    
    struct {
        std::string certificate_url;
    } fairplay;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Analytics Configuration
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct AnalyticsConfig {
    bool enable_analytics = false;
    std::string analytics_endpoint;
    std::string api_key;
    
    // Data collection
    bool collect_playback_metrics = true;
    bool collect_quality_metrics = true;
    bool collect_error_reports = true;
    bool collect_performance_metrics = true;
    bool collect_user_interactions = false;
    
    // Privacy
    bool anonymize_data = true;
    bool respect_do_not_track = true;
    std::vector<std::string> excluded_domains;
    
    // Batching
    u32 flush_interval_seconds = 30;
    u32 max_batch_size = 100;
    u32 max_queue_size = 1000;
    bool flush_on_exit = true;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Main Configuration
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API Config {
public:
    Config();
    ~Config();
    Config(const Config& other);
    Config& operator=(const Config& other);
    Config(Config&& other) noexcept;
    Config& operator=(Config&& other) noexcept;
    
    // Subsystem configurations
    LogConfig log;
    VideoConfig video;
    AudioConfig audio;
    NetworkConfig network;
    AIConfig ai;
    SubtitleConfig subtitle;
    CacheConfig cache;
    PluginConfig plugin;
    DRMConfig drm;
    AnalyticsConfig analytics;
    
    // Application settings
    std::string app_name = "AetherPlayer";
    std::string app_version = "1.0.0";
    std::string data_directory;
    std::string temp_directory;
    std::string locale = "en";
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Factory Methods
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    static Config Default();
    static Config HighPerformance();
    static Config HighQuality();
    static Config LowPower();
    static Config Minimal();
    static Config Headless();
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Load/Save
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    static Result<Config> LoadFromFile(std::string_view path);
    static Result<Config> LoadFromJSON(std::string_view json);
    static Result<Config> LoadFromTOML(std::string_view toml);
    
    Result<void> SaveToFile(std::string_view path) const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::string ToJSON(bool pretty = true) const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::string ToTOML() const;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Dynamic Access
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    Result<void> Set(std::string_view path, const ConfigValue& value);
    [[nodiscard]] std::optional<ConfigValue> Get(std::string_view path) const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool Has(std::string_view path) const;
    
    template<typename T>
    [[nodiscard]] T GetOr(std::string_view path, T default_value) const {
        auto value = Get(path);
        if (!value) return default_value;
        
        if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, bool>) {
            if (auto* v = std::get_if<bool>(&*value)) return *v;
        } else if constexpr (std::is_integral_v<T>) {
            if (auto* v = std::get_if<i64>(&*value)) return static_cast<T>(*v);
        } else if constexpr (std::is_floating_point_v<T>) {
            if (auto* v = std::get_if<f64>(&*value)) return static_cast<T>(*v);
        } else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, std::string>) {
            if (auto* v = std::get_if<std::string>(&*value)) return *v;
        }
        
        return default_value;
    }
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Validation
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::string> Validate() const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsValid() const;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Merge
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    void Merge(const Config& other, bool override_existing = true);
    Config Merged(const Config& other, bool override_existing = true) const;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Reset
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    void Reset();
    void ResetSection(std::string_view section);

private:
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_CONFIG_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: src/config.cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#include "aether/config.hpp"
#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>
#include <filesystem>
#include <regex>

// Using nlohmann/json for JSON parsing
#include <nlohmann/json.hpp>

namespace aether {

namespace fs = std::filesystem;
using json = nlohmann::json;

class Config::Impl {
public:
    std::map<std::string, ConfigValue> dynamic_values;
};

Config::Config() : impl_(std::make_unique<Impl>()) {
    // Initialize with default paths
    #if defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS)
    const char* appdata = std::getenv("APPDATA");
    if (appdata) {
        data_directory = std::string(appdata) + "\\AetherMediaEngine";
    }
    const char* temp = std::getenv("TEMP");
    if (temp) {
        temp_directory = std::string(temp) + "\\AetherMediaEngine";
    }
    #elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE)
    const char* home = std::getenv("HOME");
    if (home) {
        data_directory = std::string(home) + "/Library/Application Support/AetherMediaEngine";
        temp_directory = "/tmp/AetherMediaEngine";
    }
    #elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
    const char* xdg_data = std::getenv("XDG_DATA_HOME");
    const char* home = std::getenv("HOME");
    if (xdg_data) {
        data_directory = std::string(xdg_data) + "/AetherMediaEngine";
    } else if (home) {
        data_directory = std::string(home) + "/.local/share/AetherMediaEngine";
    }
    temp_directory = "/tmp/AetherMediaEngine";
    #elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_ANDROID)
    data_directory = "/data/data/com.aether.mediaplayer/files";
    temp_directory = "/data/data/com.aether.mediaplayer/cache";
    #endif
    
    // Set default directories
    if (!data_directory.empty()) {
        cache.disk_cache_path = data_directory + "/cache";
        ai.models_directory = data_directory + "/models";
        drm.license_storage_path = data_directory + "/licenses";
    }
}

Config::~Config() = default;

Config::Config(const Config& other) 
    : impl_(std::make_unique<Impl>(*other.impl_))
    , log(other.log)
    , video(other.video)
    , audio(other.audio)
    , network(other.network)
    , ai(other.ai)
    , subtitle(other.subtitle)
    , cache(other.cache)
    , plugin(other.plugin)
    , drm(other.drm)
    , analytics(other.analytics)
    , app_name(other.app_name)
    , app_version(other.app_version)
    , data_directory(other.data_directory)
    , temp_directory(other.temp_directory)
    , locale(other.locale) {
}

Config& Config::operator=(const Config& other) {
    if (this != &other) {
        impl_ = std::make_unique<Impl>(*other.impl_);
        log = other.log;
        video = other.video;
        audio = other.audio;
        network = other.network;
        ai = other.ai;
        subtitle = other.subtitle;
        cache = other.cache;
        plugin = other.plugin;
        drm = other.drm;
        analytics = other.analytics;
        app_name = other.app_name;
        app_version = other.app_version;
        data_directory = other.data_directory;
        temp_directory = other.temp_directory;
        locale = other.locale;
    }
    return *this;
}

    return Config{};
}

    config.cache.decoded_frame_cache_count = 60;

}

Result<Config> Config::LoadFromFile(std::string_view path) {
    std::ifstream file(std::string(path));
    if (!file.is_open()) {
        return std::unexpected(Error::Make(ErrorCode::FileNotFound, 
            "Config file not found: {}", path));
    }
    
    std::stringstream buffer;
    buffer << file.rdbuf();
    std::string content = buffer.str();
    
    fs::path filepath(path);
    std::string ext = filepath.extension().string();
    
    if (ext == ".json") {
        return LoadFromJSON(content);
    } else if (ext == ".toml") {
        return LoadFromTOML(content);
    } else {
        // Try JSON first
        auto result = LoadFromJSON(content);
        if (result) return result;
        
        return LoadFromTOML(content);
    }
}

Result<Config> Config::LoadFromJSON(std::string_view json_str) {
    try {
        json j = json::parse(json_str);
        Config config;
        
        // Log config
        if (j.contains("log")) {
            auto& log_j = j["log"];
            if (log_j.contains("level")) {
                std::string level = log_j["level"];
                if (level == "trace") config.log.level = LogConfig::Level::Trace;
                else if (level == "debug") config.log.level = LogConfig::Level::Debug;
                else if (level == "info") config.log.level = LogConfig::Level::Info;
                else if (level == "warning") config.log.level = LogConfig::Level::Warning;
                else if (level == "error") config.log.level = LogConfig::Level::Error;
                else if (level == "critical") config.log.level = LogConfig::Level::Critical;
                else if (level == "off") config.log.level = LogConfig::Level::Off;
            }
            if (log_j.contains("log_to_console")) config.log.log_to_console = log_j["log_to_console"];
            if (log_j.contains("log_to_file")) config.log.log_to_file = log_j["log_to_file"];
            if (log_j.contains("log_file_path")) config.log.log_file_path = log_j["log_file_path"];
        }
        
        // Video config
        if (j.contains("video")) {
            auto& video_j = j["video"];
            if (video_j.contains("enable_hardware_decoding")) 
                config.video.enable_hardware_decoding = video_j["enable_hardware_decoding"];
            if (video_j.contains("preferred_renderer")) 
                config.video.preferred_renderer = video_j["preferred_renderer"];
            if (video_j.contains("enable_vsync")) 
                config.video.enable_vsync = video_j["enable_vsync"];
            if (video_j.contains("enable_hdr_output")) 
                config.video.enable_hdr_output = video_j["enable_hdr_output"];
            if (video_j.contains("scale_algorithm")) 
                config.video.scale_algorithm = video_j["scale_algorithm"];
        }
        
        // Audio config
        if (j.contains("audio")) {
            auto& audio_j = j["audio"];
            if (audio_j.contains("preferred_output_device")) 
                config.audio.preferred_output_device = audio_j["preferred_output_device"];
            if (audio_j.contains("enable_exclusive_mode")) 
                config.audio.enable_exclusive_mode = audio_j["enable_exclusive_mode"];
            if (audio_j.contains("buffer_size_ms")) 
                config.audio.buffer_size_ms = audio_j["buffer_size_ms"];
            if (audio_j.contains("default_volume")) 
                config.audio.default_volume = audio_j["default_volume"];
        }
        
        // Network config
        if (j.contains("network")) {
            auto& net_j = j["network"];
            if (net_j.contains("max_connections")) 
                config.network.max_connections = net_j["max_connections"];
            if (net_j.contains("user_agent")) 
                config.network.user_agent = net_j["user_agent"];
            if (net_j.contains("proxy_url")) 
                config.network.proxy_url = net_j["proxy_url"];
            if (net_j.contains("enable_disk_cache")) 
                config.network.enable_disk_cache = net_j["enable_disk_cache"];
            if (net_j.contains("max_cache_size_mb")) 
                config.network.max_cache_size_mb = net_j["max_cache_size_mb"];
        }
        
        // AI config
        if (j.contains("ai")) {
            auto& ai_j = j["ai"];
            if (ai_j.contains("enable_ai_features")) 
                config.ai.enable_ai_features = ai_j["enable_ai_features"];
            if (ai_j.contains("preferred_backend")) 
                config.ai.preferred_backend = ai_j["preferred_backend"];
            if (ai_j.contains("enable_upscaling")) 
                config.ai.enable_upscaling = ai_j["enable_upscaling"];
            if (ai_j.contains("upscaling_model")) 
                config.ai.upscaling_model = ai_j["upscaling_model"];
            if (ai_j.contains("enable_frame_interpolation")) 
                config.ai.enable_frame_interpolation = ai_j["enable_frame_interpolation"];
            if (ai_j.contains("target_fps")) 
                config.ai.target_fps = ai_j["target_fps"];
        }
        
        // Subtitle config
        if (j.contains("subtitle")) {
            auto& sub_j = j["subtitle"];
            if (sub_j.contains("font_family")) 
                config.subtitle.font_family = sub_j["font_family"];
            if (sub_j.contains("font_size")) 
                config.subtitle.font_size = sub_j["font_size"];
            if (sub_j.contains("preferred_languages") && sub_j["preferred_languages"].is_array()) {
                config.subtitle.preferred_languages.clear();
                for (const auto& lang : sub_j["preferred_languages"]) {
                    config.subtitle.preferred_languages.push_back(lang);
                }
            }
        }
        
        // App settings
        if (j.contains("app_name")) config.app_name = j["app_name"];
        if (j.contains("app_version")) config.app_version = j["app_version"];
        if (j.contains("data_directory")) config.data_directory = j["data_directory"];
        if (j.contains("locale")) config.locale = j["locale"];
        
        return config;
        
    } catch (const json::exception& e) {
        return std::unexpected(Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidArgument,
            "Invalid JSON configuration: {}", e.what()));
    }
}

Result<Config> Config::LoadFromTOML(std::string_view toml_str) {
    // Basic TOML parsing (simplified)
    // In production, use a proper TOML library like toml++
    
    Config config;
    
    std::istringstream stream(std::string(toml_str));
    std::string line;
    std::string current_section;
    
    std::regex section_regex(R"(\[([^\]]+)\])");
    std::regex key_value_regex(R"((\w+)\s*=\s*(.+))");
    
    while (std::getline(stream, line)) {
        // Trim whitespace
        size_t start = line.find_first_not_of(" \t");
        if (start == std::string::npos) continue;
        line = line.substr(start);
        
        // Skip comments
        if (line[0] == '#') continue;
        
        std::smatch match;
        
        // Check for section header
        if (std::regex_match(line, match, section_regex)) {
            current_section = match[1].str();
            continue;
        }
        
        // Check for key=value
        if (std::regex_match(line, match, key_value_regex)) {
            std::string key = match[1].str();
            std::string value = match[2].str();
            
            // Remove quotes from string values
            if (!value.empty() && value.front() == '"') {
                value = value.substr(1);
                if (!value.empty() && value.back() == '"') {
                    value.pop_back();
                }
            }
            
            // Apply to config based on section
            std::string full_key = current_section + "." + key;
            
            // Parse boolean
            if (value == "true" || value == "false") {
                config.Set(full_key, value == "true");
            }
            // Parse number
            else if (std::regex_match(value, std::regex(R"(-?\d+\.?\d*)"))) {
                if (value.find('.') != std::string::npos) {
                    config.Set(full_key, std::stod(value));
                } else {
                    config.Set(full_key, static_cast<i64>(std::stoll(value)));
                }
            }
            // String
            else {
                config.Set(full_key, value);
            }
        }
    }
    
    return config;
}

Result<void> Config::SaveToFile(std::string_view path) const {
    fs::path filepath(path);
    std::string ext = filepath.extension().string();
    
    std::string content;
    if (ext == ".toml") {
        content = ToTOML();
    } else {
        content = ToJSON(true);
    }
    
    // Create directory if needed
    auto parent = filepath.parent_path();
    if (!parent.empty() && !fs::exists(parent)) {
        std::error_code ec;
        fs::create_directories(parent, ec);
        if (ec) {
            return std::unexpected(Error::Make(ErrorCode::FileWriteError,
                "Failed to create directory: {}", parent.string()));
        }
    }
    
    std::ofstream file(filepath);
    if (!file.is_open()) {
        return std::unexpected(Error::Make(ErrorCode::FileWriteError,
            "Failed to open file for writing: {}", path));
    }
    
    file << content;
    
    if (!file.good()) {
        return std::unexpected(Error::Make(ErrorCode::FileWriteError,
            "Failed to write config file: {}", path));
    }
    
    return {};
}

std::string Config::ToJSON(bool pretty) const {
    json j;
    
    // Log
    j["log"]["level"] = [this]() {
        switch (log.level) {
            case LogConfig::Level::Trace: return "trace";
            case LogConfig::Level::Debug: return "debug";
            case LogConfig::Level::Info: return "info";
            case LogConfig::Level::Warning: return "warning";
            case LogConfig::Level::Error: return "error";
            case LogConfig::Level::Critical: return "critical";
            case LogConfig::Level::Off: return "off";
            default: return "info";
        }
    }();
    j["log"]["log_to_console"] = log.log_to_console;
    j["log"]["log_to_file"] = log.log_to_file;
    j["log"]["log_file_path"] = log.log_file_path;
    
    // Video
    j["video"]["enable_hardware_decoding"] = video.enable_hardware_decoding;
    j["video"]["preferred_renderer"] = video.preferred_renderer;
    j["video"]["enable_vsync"] = video.enable_vsync;
    j["video"]["enable_hdr_output"] = video.enable_hdr_output;
    j["video"]["scale_algorithm"] = video.scale_algorithm;
    
    // Audio
    j["audio"]["preferred_output_device"] = audio.preferred_output_device;
    j["audio"]["enable_exclusive_mode"] = audio.enable_exclusive_mode;
    j["audio"]["buffer_size_ms"] = audio.buffer_size_ms;
    j["audio"]["default_volume"] = audio.default_volume;
    
    // Network
    j["network"]["max_connections"] = network.max_connections;
    j["network"]["user_agent"] = network.user_agent;
    j["network"]["enable_disk_cache"] = network.enable_disk_cache;
    j["network"]["max_cache_size_mb"] = network.max_cache_size_mb;
    
    // AI
    j["ai"]["enable_ai_features"] = ai.enable_ai_features;
    j["ai"]["preferred_backend"] = ai.preferred_backend;
    j["ai"]["enable_upscaling"] = ai.enable_upscaling;
    j["ai"]["upscaling_model"] = ai.upscaling_model;
    j["ai"]["enable_frame_interpolation"] = ai.enable_frame_interpolation;
    j["ai"]["target_fps"] = ai.target_fps;
    
    // Subtitle
    j["subtitle"]["font_family"] = subtitle.font_family;
    j["subtitle"]["font_size"] = subtitle.font_size;
    j["subtitle"]["preferred_languages"] = subtitle.preferred_languages;
    
    // App
    j["app_name"] = app_name;
    j["app_version"] = app_version;
    j["data_directory"] = data_directory;
    j["locale"] = locale;
    
    return pretty ? j.dump(2) : j.dump();
}

std::string Config::ToTOML() const {
    std::ostringstream ss;
    
    ss << "# AETHER Media Engine Configuration\n\n";
    
    ss << "[log]\n";
    ss << "level = \"" << [this]() {
        switch (log.level) {
            case LogConfig::Level::Trace: return "trace";
            case LogConfig::Level::Debug: return "debug";
            case LogConfig::Level::Info: return "info";
            case LogConfig::Level::Warning: return "warning";
            case LogConfig::Level::Error: return "error";
            case LogConfig::Level::Critical: return "critical";
            case LogConfig::Level::Off: return "off";
            default: return "info";
        }
    }() << "\"\n";
    ss << "log_to_console = " << (log.log_to_console ? "true" : "false") << "\n";
    ss << "log_to_file = " << (log.log_to_file ? "true" : "false") << "\n\n";
    
    ss << "[video]\n";
    ss << "enable_hardware_decoding = " << (video.enable_hardware_decoding ? "true" : "false") << "\n";
    ss << "preferred_renderer = \"" << video.preferred_renderer << "\"\n";
    ss << "enable_vsync = " << (video.enable_vsync ? "true" : "false") << "\n";
    ss << "enable_hdr_output = " << (video.enable_hdr_output ? "true" : "false") << "\n\n";
    
    ss << "[audio]\n";
    ss << "preferred_output_device = \"" << audio.preferred_output_device << "\"\n";
    ss << "enable_exclusive_mode = " << (audio.enable_exclusive_mode ? "true" : "false") << "\n";
    ss << "buffer_size_ms = " << audio.buffer_size_ms << "\n";
    ss << "default_volume = " << audio.default_volume << "\n\n";
    
    ss << "[network]\n";
    ss << "max_connections = " << network.max_connections << "\n";
    ss << "user_agent = \"" << network.user_agent << "\"\n";
    ss << "enable_disk_cache = " << (network.enable_disk_cache ? "true" : "false") << "\n\n";
    
    ss << "[ai]\n";
    ss << "enable_ai_features = " << (ai.enable_ai_features ? "true" : "false") << "\n";
    ss << "preferred_backend = \"" << ai.preferred_backend << "\"\n";
    ss << "enable_upscaling = " << (ai.enable_upscaling ? "true" : "false") << "\n\n";
    
    return ss.str();
}

Result<void> Config::Set(std::string_view path, const ConfigValue& value) {
    impl_->dynamic_values[std::string(path)] = value;
    
    // Also update the actual config fields
    // This is a simplified implementation - a full implementation would
    // parse the path and update the appropriate field
    
    return {};
}

std::optional<ConfigValue> Config::Get(std::string_view path) const {
    auto it = impl_->dynamic_values.find(std::string(path));
    if (it != impl_->dynamic_values.end()) {
        return it->second;
    }
    return std::nullopt;
}

bool Config::Has(std::string_view path) const {
    return impl_->dynamic_values.contains(std::string(path));
}

std::vector<std::string> Config::Validate() const {
    std::vector<std::string> errors;
    
    // Video validation
    if (video.decode_thread_count > 64) {
        errors.push_back("video.decode_thread_count is too high (max 64)");
    }
    
    if (video.max_video_width > 0 && video.max_video_width < 64) {
        errors.push_back("video.max_video_width is too low (min 64)");
    }
    
    // Audio validation
    if (audio.buffer_size_ms < 5 || audio.buffer_size_ms > 1000) {
        errors.push_back("audio.buffer_size_ms must be between 5 and 1000");
    }
    
    if (audio.default_volume < 0.0f || audio.default_volume > 2.0f) {
        errors.push_back("audio.default_volume must be between 0.0 and 2.0");
    }
    
    // Network validation
    if (network.max_connections < 1 || network.max_connections > 64) {
        errors.push_back("network.max_connections must be between 1 and 64");
    }
    
    if (network.connection_timeout_ms < 1000) {
        errors.push_back("network.connection_timeout_ms must be at least 1000ms");
    }
    
    // AI validation
    if (ai.target_fps < 1.0f || ai.target_fps > 480.0f) {
        errors.push_back("ai.target_fps must be between 1 and 480");
    }
    
    if (ai.upscaling_quality < 0.0f || ai.upscaling_quality > 1.0f) {
        errors.push_back("ai.upscaling_quality must be between 0.0 and 1.0");
    }
    
    // Subtitle validation
    if (subtitle.font_size < 8 || subtitle.font_size > 200) {
        errors.push_back("subtitle.font_size must be between 8 and 200");
    }
    
    if (subtitle.scale < 0.1f || subtitle.scale > 10.0f) {
        errors.push_back("subtitle.scale must be between 0.1 and 10.0");
    }
    
    // Cache validation
    if (cache.memory_cache_size_mb < 16) {
        errors.push_back("cache.memory_cache_size_mb must be at least 16MB");
    }
    
    return errors;
}

bool Config::IsValid() const {
    return Validate().empty();
}

void Config::Merge(const Config& other, bool override_existing) {
    // This is a simplified merge - a full implementation would
    // iterate through all fields
    
    if (override_existing) {
        *this = other;
    }
    
    // Merge dynamic values
    for (const auto& [key, value] : other.impl_->dynamic_values) {
        if (override_existing || !impl_->dynamic_values.contains(key)) {
            impl_->dynamic_values[key] = value;
        }
    }
}

Config Config::Merged(const Config& other, bool override_existing) const {
    Config result = *this;
    result.Merge(other, override_existing);
    return result;
}

void Config::Reset() {
    *this = Default();
}

void Config::ResetSection(std::string_view section) {
    Config defaults = Default();
    
    if (section == "log") log = defaults.log;
    else if (section == "video") video = defaults.video;
    else if (section == "audio") audio = defaults.audio;
    else if (section == "network") network = defaults.network;
    else if (section == "ai") ai = defaults.ai;
    else if (section == "subtitle") subtitle = defaults.subtitle;
    else if (section == "cache") cache = defaults.cache;
    else if (section == "plugin") plugin = defaults.plugin;
    else if (section == "drm") drm = defaults.drm;
    else if (section == "analytics") analytics = defaults.analytics;
}

} // namespace aether
```

## Part 3: Logging System

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/utils/logging.hpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#pragma once

#ifndef AETHER_UTILS_LOGGING_HPP
#define AETHER_UTILS_LOGGING_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"
#include "aether/core/error.hpp"
#include "aether/config.hpp"

#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <format>
#include <source_location>
#include <memory>
#include <functional>

namespace aether {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Log Level
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

using LogLevel = LogConfig::Level;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Log Entry
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct LogEntry {
    LogLevel level;
    std::string message;
    std::string logger_name;
    TimePoint timestamp;
    std::thread::id thread_id;
    std::source_location location;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Log Sink Interface
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API ILogSink {
public:
    virtual ~ILogSink() = default;
    virtual void Write(const LogEntry& entry) = 0;
    virtual void Flush() = 0;
    virtual void SetLevel(LogLevel level) = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual LogLevel GetLevel() const = 0;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Logger Class
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API Logger {
public:
    Logger();
    explicit Logger(std::string name);
    ~Logger();
    
    Logger(const Logger&) = delete;
    Logger& operator=(const Logger&) = delete;
    Logger(Logger&&) noexcept;
    Logger& operator=(Logger&&) noexcept;
    
    // Initialization
    Result<void> Initialize(const LogConfig& config);
    void Shutdown();
    
    // Level control
    void SetLevel(LogLevel level);
    [[nodiscard]] LogLevel GetLevel() const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsLevelEnabled(LogLevel level) const;
    
    // Sinks
    void AddSink(Shared<ILogSink> sink);
    void RemoveSink(Shared<ILogSink> sink);
    void ClearSinks();
    
    // Logging methods
    template<typename... Args>
    void Log(LogLevel level, std::format_string<Args...> fmt, Args&&... args) {
        if (!IsLevelEnabled(level)) return;
        LogImpl(level, std::format(fmt, std::forward<Args>(args)...), 
               std::source_location::current());
    }
    
    template<typename... Args>
    void Trace(std::format_string<Args...> fmt, Args&&... args) {
        Log(LogLevel::Trace, fmt, std::forward<Args>(args)...);
    }
    
    template<typename... Args>
    void Debug(std::format_string<Args...> fmt, Args&&... args) {
        Log(LogLevel::Debug, fmt, std::forward<Args>(args)...);
    }
    
    template<typename... Args>
    void Info(std::format_string<Args...> fmt, Args&&... args) {
        Log(LogLevel::Info, fmt, std::forward<Args>(args)...);
    }
    
    template<typename... Args>
    void Warning(std::format_string<Args...> fmt, Args&&... args) {
        Log(LogLevel::Warning, fmt, std::forward<Args>(args)...);
    }
    
    template<typename... Args>
    void Warn(std::format_string<Args...> fmt, Args&&... args) {
        Warning(fmt, std::forward<Args>(args)...);
    }
    
    template<typename... Args>
    void Error(std::format_string<Args...> fmt, Args&&... args) {
        Log(LogLevel::Error, fmt, std::forward<Args>(args)...);
    }
    
    template<typename... Args>
    void Critical(std::format_string<Args...> fmt, Args&&... args) {
        Log(LogLevel::Critical, fmt, std::forward<Args>(args)...);
    }
    
    // Log with source location
    void LogImpl(LogLevel level, std::string message, std::source_location loc);
    
    // Flush all sinks
    void Flush();
    
    // Get logger name
    [[nodiscard]] const std::string& GetName() const;
    
    // Static access
    static Logger& GetDefault();
    static Logger& Get(std::string_view name);
    static void SetDefaultLevel(LogLevel level);
    
private:
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Built-in Sinks
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API ConsoleSink : public ILogSink {
public:
    ConsoleSink();
    ~ConsoleSink() override;
    
    void Write(const LogEntry& entry) override;
    void Flush() override;
    void SetLevel(LogLevel level) override;
    [[nodiscard]] LogLevel GetLevel() const override;
    
    void SetColored(bool colored);
    void SetPattern(std::string_view pattern);
    
private:
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

class AETHER_API FileSink : public ILogSink {
public:
    FileSink(std::string_view path, bool truncate = false);
    ~FileSink() override;
    
    void Write(const LogEntry& entry) override;
    void Flush() override;
    void SetLevel(LogLevel level) override;
    [[nodiscard]] LogLevel GetLevel() const override;
    
    void SetMaxFileSize(i64 bytes);
    void SetMaxFiles(u32 count);
    void Rotate();
    
private:
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

class AETHER_API CallbackSink : public ILogSink {
public:
    using Callback = std::function<void(const LogEntry&)>;
    
    explicit CallbackSink(Callback callback);
    ~CallbackSink() override;
    
    void Write(const LogEntry& entry) override;
    void Flush() override;
    void SetLevel(LogLevel level) override;
    [[nodiscard]] LogLevel GetLevel() const override;
    
private:
    Callback callback_;
    LogLevel level_ = LogLevel::Trace;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Convenience Macros

#define AETHER_LOG_TRACE(...) ::aether::Logger::GetDefault().Trace(__VA_ARGS__)
#define AETHER_LOG_DEBUG(...) ::aether::Logger::GetDefault().Debug(__VA_ARGS__)

#else
    #define AETHER_LOG_DEBUG_ONLY(...) AETHER_LOG_DEBUG(__VA_ARGS__)
#endif
```
