```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: src/utils/logging.cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#include "aether/utils/logging.hpp"

#include <iostream>
#include <fstream>
#include <mutex>
#include <vector>
#include <map>
#include <chrono>
#include <iomanip>
#include <sstream>
#include <filesystem>
#include <thread>

#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    #include <windows.h>
#endif

namespace aether {

namespace fs = std::filesystem;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Logger Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class Logger::Impl {
public:
    std::string name;
    LogLevel level = LogLevel::Info;
    std::vector<Shared<ILogSink>> sinks;
    mutable std::mutex mutex;
    bool initialized = false;
    
    static std::mutex global_mutex;
    static std::map<std::string, Unique<Logger>> loggers;
    static LogLevel default_level;
};

std::mutex Logger::Impl::global_mutex;
std::map<std::string, Unique<Logger>> Logger::Impl::loggers;
LogLevel Logger::Impl::default_level = LogLevel::Info;

Logger::Logger() : impl_(std::make_unique<Impl>()) {
    impl_->name = "default";
}

Logger::Logger(std::string name) : impl_(std::make_unique<Impl>()) {
    impl_->name = std::move(name);
}

Logger::~Logger() {
    Shutdown();
}

Logger::Logger(Logger&&) noexcept = default;
Logger& Logger::operator=(Logger&&) noexcept = default;

Result<void> Logger::Initialize(const LogConfig& config) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    
    impl_->level = config.level;
    impl_->sinks.clear();
    
    // Add console sink
    if (config.log_to_console) {
        auto console_sink = MakeShared<ConsoleSink>();
        console_sink->SetLevel(config.level);
        console_sink->SetColored(config.colored_output);
        if (!config.log_pattern.empty()) {
            console_sink->SetPattern(config.log_pattern);
        }
        impl_->sinks.push_back(console_sink);
    }
    
    // Add file sink
    if (config.log_to_file && !config.log_file_path.empty()) {
        try {
            auto file_sink = MakeShared<FileSink>(config.log_file_path);
            file_sink->SetLevel(config.level);
            file_sink->SetMaxFileSize(config.max_log_file_size_mb * 1024 * 1024);
            file_sink->SetMaxFiles(config.max_log_files);
            impl_->sinks.push_back(file_sink);
        } catch (const std::exception& e) {
            return std::unexpected(Error::Make(ErrorCode::FileWriteError,
                "Failed to create log file: {}", e.what()));
        }
    }
    
    impl_->initialized = true;
    
    Info("Logger initialized: {} (level: {})", impl_->name, GetLogLevelName(impl_->level));
    
    return {};
}

void Logger::Shutdown() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    
    if (impl_->initialized) {
        Flush();
        impl_->sinks.clear();
        impl_->initialized = false;
    }
}

void Logger::SetLevel(LogLevel level) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    impl_->level = level;
    
    for (auto& sink : impl_->sinks) {
        sink->SetLevel(level);
    }
}

LogLevel Logger::GetLevel() const {
    return impl_->level;
}

bool Logger::IsLevelEnabled(LogLevel level) const {
    return static_cast<u8>(level) >= static_cast<u8>(impl_->level);
}

void Logger::AddSink(Shared<ILogSink> sink) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    impl_->sinks.push_back(std::move(sink));
}

void Logger::RemoveSink(Shared<ILogSink> sink) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    impl_->sinks.erase(
        std::remove(impl_->sinks.begin(), impl_->sinks.end(), sink),
        impl_->sinks.end()
    );
}

void Logger::ClearSinks() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    impl_->sinks.clear();
}

void Logger::LogImpl(LogLevel level, std::string message, std::source_location loc) {
    if (!IsLevelEnabled(level)) return;
    
    LogEntry entry{
        .level = level,
        .message = std::move(message),
        .logger_name = impl_->name,
        .timestamp = Clock::now(),
        .thread_id = std::this_thread::get_id(),
        .location = loc
    };
    
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    
    for (auto& sink : impl_->sinks) {
        if (static_cast<u8>(level) >= static_cast<u8>(sink->GetLevel())) {
            sink->Write(entry);
        }
    }
}

void Logger::Flush() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    
    for (auto& sink : impl_->sinks) {
        sink->Flush();
    }
}

const std::string& Logger::GetName() const {
    return impl_->name;
}

Logger& Logger::GetDefault() {
    static Logger default_logger;
    static std::once_flag init_flag;
    
    std::call_once(init_flag, []() {
        LogConfig config;
        config.level = Impl::default_level;
        config.log_to_console = true;
        config.colored_output = true;
        default_logger.Initialize(config);
    });
    
    return default_logger;
}

Logger& Logger::Get(std::string_view name) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(Impl::global_mutex);
    
    std::string name_str(name);
    auto it = Impl::loggers.find(name_str);
    
    if (it == Impl::loggers.end()) {
        auto logger = std::make_unique<Logger>(name_str);
        
        LogConfig config;
        config.level = Impl::default_level;
        config.log_to_console = true;
        config.colored_output = true;
        logger->Initialize(config);
        
        it = Impl::loggers.emplace(name_str, std::move(logger)).first;
    }
    
    return *it->second;
}

void Logger::SetDefaultLevel(LogLevel level) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(Impl::global_mutex);
    Impl::default_level = level;
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Console Sink Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class ConsoleSink::Impl {
public:
    LogLevel level = LogLevel::Trace;
    bool colored = true;
    std::string pattern;
    std::mutex mutex;
    
    #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    HANDLE console_handle = INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE;
    #endif
};

ConsoleSink::ConsoleSink() : impl_(std::make_unique<Impl>()) {
    #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    impl_->console_handle = GetStdHandle(STD_OUTPUT_HANDLE);
    // Enable ANSI escape codes on Windows 10+
    DWORD mode;
    if (GetConsoleMode(impl_->console_handle, &mode)) {
        SetConsoleMode(impl_->console_handle, mode | ENABLE_VIRTUAL_TERMINAL_PROCESSING);
    }
    #endif
}

ConsoleSink::~ConsoleSink() = default;

void ConsoleSink::Write(const LogEntry& entry) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    
    std::string formatted = FormatLogEntry(entry, impl_->pattern);
    
    if (impl_->colored) {
        const char* color_code = "";
        const char* reset_code = "\033[0m";
        
        switch (entry.level) {
            case LogLevel::Trace:    color_code = "\033[90m"; break;  // Gray
            case LogLevel::Debug:    color_code = "\033[36m"; break;  // Cyan
            case LogLevel::Info:     color_code = "\033[32m"; break;  // Green
            case LogLevel::Warning:  color_code = "\033[33m"; break;  // Yellow
            case LogLevel::Error:    color_code = "\033[31m"; break;  // Red
            case LogLevel::Critical: color_code = "\033[35;1m"; break; // Bold Magenta
            default: break;
        }
        
        std::cout << color_code << formatted << reset_code << "\n";
    } else {
        std::cout << formatted << "\n";
    }
}

void ConsoleSink::Flush() {
    std::cout.flush();
}

void ConsoleSink::SetLevel(LogLevel level) {
    impl_->level = level;
}

LogLevel ConsoleSink::GetLevel() const {
    return impl_->level;
}

void ConsoleSink::SetColored(bool colored) {
    impl_->colored = colored;
}

void ConsoleSink::SetPattern(std::string_view pattern) {
    impl_->pattern = std::string(pattern);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// File Sink Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class FileSink::Impl {
public:
    std::string path;
    std::ofstream file;
    LogLevel level = LogLevel::Trace;
    i64 max_file_size = 10 * 1024 * 1024;  // 10MB
    u32 max_files = 5;
    i64 current_size = 0;
    std::mutex mutex;
    
    void OpenFile(bool truncate = false) {
        // Create directory if needed
        fs::path filepath(path);
        auto parent = filepath.parent_path();
        if (!parent.empty() && !fs::exists(parent)) {
            fs::```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: src/utils/logging.cpp (continued)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

            fs::create_directories(parent);
        }
        
        auto mode = std::ios::out;
        if (!truncate && fs::exists(path)) {
            mode |= std::ios::app;
            current_size = fs::file_size(path);
        } else {
            current_size = 0;
        }
        
        file.open(path, mode);
    }
    
    void RotateFiles() {
        file.close();
        
        // Delete oldest file if we have too many
        for (i32 i = static_cast<i32>(max_files) - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
            std::string old_path = path + (i > 0 ? "." + std::to_string(i) : "");
            std::string new_path = path + "." + std::to_string(i + 1);
            
            if (fs::exists(old_path)) {
                if (i == static_cast<i32>(max_files) - 1) {
                    fs::remove(old_path);
                } else {
                    fs::rename(old_path, new_path);
                }
            }
        }
        
        OpenFile(true);
    }
};

FileSink::FileSink(std::string_view path, bool truncate) 
    : impl_(std::make_unique<Impl>()) {
    impl_->path = std::string(path);
    impl_->OpenFile(truncate);
}

FileSink::~FileSink() {
    if (impl_->file.is_open()) {
        impl_->file.flush();
        impl_->file.close();
    }
}

void FileSink::Write(const LogEntry& entry) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    
    if (!impl_->file.is_open()) {
        impl_->OpenFile();
    }
    
    std::string formatted = FormatLogEntry(entry, "");
    formatted += "\n";
    
    impl_->file << formatted;
    impl_->current_size += formatted.size();
    
    // Check if rotation is needed
    if (impl_->max_file_size > 0 && impl_->current_size >= impl_->max_file_size) {
        impl_->RotateFiles();
    }
}

void FileSink::Flush() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    if (impl_->file.is_open()) {
        impl_->file.flush();
    }
}

void FileSink::SetLevel(LogLevel level) {
    impl_->level = level;
}

LogLevel FileSink::GetLevel() const {
    return impl_->level;
}

void FileSink::SetMaxFileSize(i64 bytes) {
    impl_->max_file_size = bytes;
}

void FileSink::SetMaxFiles(u32 count) {
    impl_->max_files = count;
}

void FileSink::Rotate() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    impl_->RotateFiles();
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Callback Sink Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

CallbackSink::CallbackSink(Callback callback) 
    : callback_(std::move(callback)) {}

CallbackSink::~CallbackSink() = default;

void CallbackSink::Write(const LogEntry& entry) {
    if (callback_) {
        callback_(entry);
    }
}

void CallbackSink::Flush() {
    // Nothing to flush for callback sink
}

void CallbackSink::SetLevel(LogLevel level) {
    level_ = level;
}

LogLevel CallbackSink::GetLevel() const {
    return level_;
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Utility Functions
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

std::string_view GetLogLevelName(LogLevel level) noexcept {
    switch (level) {
        case LogLevel::Trace:    return "TRACE";
        case LogLevel::Debug:    return "DEBUG";
        case LogLevel::Info:     return "INFO";
        case LogLevel::Warning:  return "WARNING";
        case LogLevel::Error:    return "ERROR";
        case LogLevel::Critical: return "CRITICAL";
        case LogLevel::Off:      return "OFF";
        default:                 return "UNKNOWN";
    }
}

std::string_view GetLogLevelShortName(LogLevel level) noexcept {
    switch (level) {
        case LogLevel::Trace:    return "TRC";
        case LogLevel::Debug:    return "DBG";
        case LogLevel::Info:     return "INF";
        case LogLevel::Warning:  return "WRN";
        case LogLevel::Error:    return "ERR";
        case LogLevel::Critical: return "CRT";
        case LogLevel::Off:      return "OFF";
        default:                 return "UNK";
    }
}

LogLevel ParseLogLevel(std::string_view name) noexcept {
    std::string lower;
    lower.reserve(name.size());
    for (char c : name) {
        lower += static_cast<char>(std::tolower(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)));
    }
    
    if (lower == "trace" || lower == "trc") return LogLevel::Trace;
    if (lower == "debug" || lower == "dbg") return LogLevel::Debug;
    if (lower == "info" || lower == "inf") return LogLevel::Info;
    if (lower == "warning" || lower == "warn" || lower == "wrn") return LogLevel::Warning;
    if (lower == "error" || lower == "err") return LogLevel::Error;
    if (lower == "critical" || lower == "crit" || lower == "crt") return LogLevel::Critical;
    if (lower == "off" || lower == "none") return LogLevel::Off;
    
    return LogLevel::Info;
}

std::string FormatLogEntry(const LogEntry& entry, std::string_view pattern) {
    std::ostringstream ss;
    
    // Format timestamp
    auto time_t = std::chrono::system_clock::to_time_t(
        std::chrono::system_clock::now() + 
        std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::system_clock::duration>(
            entry.timestamp.time_since_epoch() - Clock::now().time_since_epoch()
        )
    );
    auto ms = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        entry.timestamp.time_since_epoch()
    ).count() % 1000;
    
    ss << std::put_time(std::localtime(&time_t), "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S");
    ss << "." << std::setfill('0') << std::setw(3) << ms;
    
    // Level
    ss << " [" << GetLogLevelShortName(entry.level) << "]";
    
    // Logger name
    if (!entry.logger_name.empty() && entry.logger_name != "default") {
        ss << " [" << entry.logger_name << "]";
    }
    
    // Message
    ss << " " << entry.message;
    
    return ss.str();
}

} // namespace aether
```

## Part 4: Memory Management

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/utils/memory.hpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#pragma once

#ifndef AETHER_UTILS_MEMORY_HPP
#define AETHER_UTILS_MEMORY_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"

#include <memory>
#include <atomic>
#include <vector>
#include <mutex>
#include <span>

namespace aether {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Aligned Memory Allocation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

AETHER_API void* AlignedAlloc(usize size, usize alignment = 64);
AETHER_API void AlignedFree(void* ptr);
AETHER_API void* AlignedRealloc(void* ptr, usize old_size, usize new_size, usize alignment = 64);

template<typename T>
struct AlignedDeleter {
    void operator()(T* ptr) const {
        if (ptr) {
            ptr->~T();
            AlignedFree(ptr);
        }
    }
};

template<typename T>
using AlignedPtr = std::unique_ptr<T, AlignedDeleter<T>>;

template<typename T, typename... Args>
AlignedPtr<T> MakeAligned(usize alignment, Args&&... args) {
    void* mem = AlignedAlloc(sizeof(T), alignment);
    return AlignedPtr<T>(new (mem) T(std::forward<Args>(args)...));
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Buffer Class
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API Buffer {
public:
    Buffer();
    explicit Buffer(usize size, usize alignment = 64);
    Buffer(const void* data, usize size, usize alignment = 64);
    Buffer(std::span<const u8> data, usize alignment = 64);
    ~Buffer();
    
    Buffer(const Buffer& other);
    Buffer& operator=(const Buffer& other);
    Buffer(Buffer&& other) noexcept;
    Buffer& operator=(Buffer&& other) noexcept;
    
    // Access
    [[nodiscard]] u8* Data() noexcept { return data_; }
    [[nodiscard]] const u8* Data() const noexcept { return data_; }
    [[nodiscard]] usize Size() const noexcept { return size_; }
    [[nodiscard]] usize Capacity() const noexcept { return capacity_; }
    [[nodiscard]] usize Alignment() const noexcept { return alignment_; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsEmpty() const noexcept { return size_ == 0; }
    
    template<typename T>
    [[nodiscard]] T* DataAs() noexcept { return reinterpret_cast<T*>(data_); }
    
    template<typename T>
    [[nodiscard]] const T* DataAs() const noexcept { return reinterpret_cast<const T*>(data_); }
    
    [[nodiscard]] std::span<u8> Span() noexcept { return {data_, size_}; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::span<const u8> Span() const noexcept { return {data_, size_}; }
    
    // Operators
    u8& operator[](usize index) { return data_[index]; }
    const u8& operator[](usize index) const { return data_[index]; }
    
    // Modification
    void Resize(usize new_size);
    void Reserve(usize new_capacity);
    void Clear();
    void Shrink();
    void Fill(u8 value);
    void Zero() { Fill(0); }
    
    void Assign(const void* data, usize size);
    void Assign(std::span<const u8> data);
    void Append(const void* data, usize size);
    void Append(std::span<const u8> data);
    
    // Factory
    static Buffer Zeroed(usize size, usize alignment = 64);
    static Buffer FromString(std::string_view str);
    
private:
    u8* data_ = nullptr;
    usize size_ = 0;
    usize capacity_ = 0;
    usize alignment_ = 64;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Ring Buffer
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API RingBuffer {
public:
    explicit RingBuffer(usize capacity);
    ~RingBuffer();
    
    RingBuffer(const RingBuffer&) = delete;
    RingBuffer& operator=(const RingBuffer&) = delete;
    RingBuffer(RingBuffer&&) noexcept;
    RingBuffer& operator=(RingBuffer&&) noexcept;
    
    // Write data
    usize Write(const void* data, usize size);
    usize Write(std::span<const u8> data);
    
    // Read data
    usize Read(void* data, usize size);
    usize Peek(void* data, usize size) const;
    usize Skip(usize size);
    
    // State
    [[nodiscard]] usize Available() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] usize Free() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] usize Capacity() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsEmpty() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsFull() const noexcept;
    
    void Clear();
    
private:
    u8* buffer_ = nullptr;
    usize capacity_ = 0;
    std::atomic<usize> read_pos_{0};
    std::atomic<usize> write_pos_{0};
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Memory Pool
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API MemoryPool {
public:
    MemoryPool(usize block_size, usize initial_blocks = 16, usize max_blocks = 0);
    ~MemoryPool();
    
    MemoryPool(const MemoryPool&) = delete;
    MemoryPool& operator=(const MemoryPool&) = delete;
    
    void* Allocate();
    void Deallocate(void* ptr);
    
    [[nodiscard]] usize BlockSize() const noexcept { return block_size_; }
    [[nodiscard]] usize TotalBlocks() const noexcept { return total_blocks_; }
    [[nodiscard]] usize FreeBlocks() const noexcept { return free_blocks_; }
    [[nodiscard]] usize UsedBlocks() const noexcept { return total_blocks_ - free_blocks_; }
    
    void Shrink();
    void Clear();
    
private:
    void GrowPool();
    
    usize block_size_;
    usize max_blocks_;
    std::atomic<usize> total_blocks_{0};
    std::atomic<usize> free_blocks_{0};
    
    struct Block {
        Block* next;
    };
    std::atomic<Block*> free_list_{nullptr};
    
    std::vector<void*> chunks_;
    std::mutex mutex_;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Object Pool
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

template<typename T>
class ObjectPool {
public:
    explicit ObjectPool(usize initial_size = 16, usize max_size = 0)
        : pool_(sizeof(T), initial_size, max_size) {}
    
    template<typename... Args>
    T* Acquire(Args&&... args) {
        void* ptr = pool_.Allocate();
        if (!ptr) return nullptr;
        return new (ptr) T(std::forward<Args>(args)...);
    }
    
    void Release(T* obj) {
        if (obj) {
            obj->~T();
            pool_.Deallocate(obj);
        }
    }
    
    // RAII wrapper
    struct Deleter {
        ObjectPool* pool;
        void operator()(T* ptr) const { pool->Release(ptr); }
    };
    
    using Ptr = std::unique_ptr<T, Deleter>;
    
    template<typename... Args>
    Ptr AcquireUnique(Args&&... args) {
        return Ptr(Acquire(std::forward<Args>(args)...), Deleter{this});
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] usize FreeCount() const noexcept { return pool_.FreeBlocks(); }
    [[nodiscard]] usize UsedCount() const noexcept { return pool_.UsedBlocks(); }
    
private:
    MemoryPool pool_;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Frame Allocator (Linear Allocator)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API FrameAllocator {
public:
    explicit FrameAllocator(usize size);
    ~FrameAllocator();
    
    FrameAllocator(const FrameAllocator&) = delete;
    FrameAllocator& operator=(const FrameAllocator&) = delete;
    
    void* Allocate(usize size, usize alignment = 16);
    
    template<typename T, typename... Args>
    T* Allocate(Args&&... args) {
        void* ptr = Allocate(sizeof(T), alignof(T));
        if (!ptr) return nullptr;
        return new (ptr) T(std::forward<Args>(args)...);
    }
    
    template<typename T>
    T* AllocateArray(usize count) {
        return static_cast<T*>(Allocate(sizeof(T) * count, alignof(T)));
    }
    
    void Reset();
    
    [[nodiscard]] usize Used() const noexcept { return offset_; }
    [[nodiscard]] usize Free() const noexcept { return size_ - offset_; }
    [[nodiscard]] usize Size() const noexcept { return size_; }
    
private:
    u8* buffer_;
    usize size_;
    usize offset_ = 0;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Reference Counted Buffer
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API RefBuffer {
public:
    RefBuffer();
    explicit RefBuffer(usize size);
    RefBuffer(const void* data, usize size);
    
    RefBuffer(const RefBuffer& other);
    RefBuffer& operator=(const RefBuffer& other);
    RefBuffer(RefBuffer&& other) noexcept;
    RefBuffer& operator=(RefBuffer&& other) noexcept;
    
    ~RefBuffer();
    
    [[nodiscard]] u8* Data() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] usize Size() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsEmpty() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] u32 RefCount() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsUnique() const noexcept;
    
    // Copy-on-write: ensure exclusive ownership before modification
    void MakeUnique();
    
    void Reset();
    
private:
    struct ControlBlock {
        std::atomic<u32> ref_count{1};
        usize size;
        u8 data[];
    };
    
    ControlBlock* block_ = nullptr;
};

```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: src/utils/memory.cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#include "aether/utils/memory.hpp"

#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <new>
#include <algorithm>

#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    #include <windows.h>
    #include <psapi.h>
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE)
    #include <mach/mach.h>
    #include <sys/sysctl.h>
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
    #include <unistd.h>
    #include <sys/resource.h>
    #include <fstream>
#endif

namespace aether {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Global Memory Statistics
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

static std::atomic<usize> g_total_allocated{0};
static std::atomic<usize> g_total_freed{0};
static std::atomic<usize> g_current_usage{0};
static std::atomic<usize> g_peak_usage{0};
static std::atomic<usize> g_allocation_count{0};
static std::atomic<usize> g_deallocation_count{0};

static void TrackAllocation(usize size) {
    g_total_allocated += size;
    g_allocation_count++;
    usize current = (g_current_usage += size);
    
    usize peak = g_peak_usage.load();
    while (current > peak && !g_peak_usage.compare_exchange_weak(peak, current)) {}
}

static void TrackDeallocation(usize size) {
    g_total_freed += size;
    g_deallocation_count++;
    g_current_usage -= size;
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Aligned Memory Allocation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

void* AlignedAlloc(usize size, usize alignment) {
    if (size == 0) return nullptr;
    
    // Ensure alignment is power of 2 and at least sizeof(void*)
    alignment = std::max(alignment, sizeof(void*));
    if ((alignment & (alignment - 1)) != 0) {
        // Round up to next power of 2
        alignment--;
        alignment |= alignment >> 1;
        alignment |= alignment >> 2;
        alignment |= alignment >> 4;
        alignment |= alignment >> 8;
        alignment |= alignment >> 16;
        alignment++;
    }
    
    void* ptr = nullptr;
    
#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    ptr = _aligned_malloc(size, alignment);
#elif defined(_ISOC11_SOURCE) || (defined(__STDC_VERSION__) && __STDC_VERSION__ >= 201112L)
    ptr = aligned_alloc(alignment, ((size + alignment - 1) / alignment) * alignment);
#else
    if (posix_memalign(&ptr, alignment, size) != 0) {
        ptr = nullptr;
    }
#endif
    
    if (ptr) {
        TrackAllocation(size);
    }
    
    return ptr;
}

void AlignedFree(void* ptr) {
    if (!ptr) return;
    
#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    _aligned_free(ptr);
#else
    free(ptr);
#endif
}

void* AlignedRealloc(void* ptr, usize old_size, usize new_size, usize alignment) {
    if (new_size == 0) {
        AlignedFree(ptr);
        TrackDeallocation(old_size);
        return nullptr;
    }
    
    if (!ptr) {
        return AlignedAlloc(new_size, alignment);
    }
    
#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    void* new_ptr = _aligned_realloc(ptr, new_size, alignment);
    if (new_ptr) {
        TrackDeallocation(old_size);
        TrackAllocation(new_size);
    }
    return new_ptr;
#else
    void* new_ptr = AlignedAlloc(new_size, alignment);
    if (new_ptr) {
        std::memcpy(new_ptr, ptr, std::min(old_size, new_size));
        AlignedFree(ptr);
        TrackDeallocation(old_size);
    }
    return new_ptr;
#endif
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Buffer Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Buffer::Buffer() = default;

Buffer::Buffer(usize size, usize alignment) 
    : alignment_(alignment) {
    if (size > 0) {
        data_ = static_cast<u8*>(AlignedAlloc(size, alignment_));
        if (data_) {
            size_ = size;
            capacity_ = size;
        }
    }
}

Buffer::Buffer(const void* data, usize size, usize alignment)
    : alignment_(alignment) {
    if (size > 0 && data) {
        data_ = static_cast<u8*>(AlignedAlloc(size, alignment_));
        if (data_) {
            std::memcpy(data_, data, size);
            size_ = size;
            capacity_ = size;
        }
    }
}

Buffer::Buffer(std::span<const u8> data, usize alignment)
    : Buffer(data.data(), data.size(), alignment) {}

Buffer::~Buffer() {
    if (data_) {
        TrackDeallocation(capacity_);
        AlignedFree(data_);
    }
}

Buffer::Buffer(const Buffer& other)
    : alignment_(other.alignment_) {
    if (other.size_ > 0) {
        data_ = static_cast<u8*>(AlignedAlloc(other.size_, alignment_));
        if (data_) {
            std::memcpy(data_, other.data_, other.size_);
            size_ = other.size_;
            capacity_ = other.size_;
        }
    }
}

Buffer& Buffer::operator=(const Buffer& other) {
    if (this != &other) {
        if (data_) {
            TrackDeallocation(capacity_);
            AlignedFree(data_);
            data_ = nullptr;
            size_ = 0;
            capacity_ = 0;
        }
        
        alignment_ = other.alignment_;
        
        if (other.size_ > 0) {
            data_ = static_cast<u8*>(AlignedAlloc(other.size_, alignment_));
            if (data_) {
                std::memcpy(data_, other.data_, other.size_);
                size_ = other.size_;
                capacity_ = other.size_;
            }
        }
    }
    return *this;
}

Buffer::Buffer(Buffer&& other) noexcept
    : data_(other.data_)
    , size_(other.size_)
    , capacity_(other.capacity_)
    , alignment_(other.alignment_) {
    other.data_ = nullptr;
    other.size_ = 0;
    other.capacity_ = 0;
}

Buffer& Buffer::operator=(Buffer&& other) noexcept {
    if (this != &other) {
        if (data_) {
            TrackDeallocation(capacity_);
            AlignedFree(data_);
        }
        
        data_ = other.data_;
        size_ = other.size_;
        capacity_ = other.capacity_;
        alignment_ = other.alignment_;
        
        other.data_ = nullptr;
        other.size_ = 0;
        other.capacity_ = 0;
    }
    return *this;
}

void Buffer::Resize(usize new_size) {
    if (new_size <= capacity_) {
        size_ = new_size;
        return;
    }
    
    Reserve(new_size);
    size_ = new_size;
}

void Buffer::Reserve(usize new_capacity) {
    if (new_capacity <= capacity_) return;
    
    // Grow by at least 50%
    usize grow_capacity = capacity_ + capacity_ / 2;
    new_capacity = std::max(new_capacity, grow_capacity);
    
    u8* new_data = static_cast<u8*>(AlignedAlloc(new_capacity, alignment_));
    if (!new_data) return;
    
    if (data_) {
        std::memcpy(new_data, data_, size_);
        TrackDeallocation(capacity_);
        AlignedFree(data_);
    }
    
    data_ = new_data;
    capacity_ = new_capacity;
}

void Buffer::Clear() {
    size_ = 0;
}

void Buffer::Shrink() {
    if (size_ == capacity_ || size_ == 0) return;
    
    if (size_ == 0) {
        TrackDeallocation(capacity_);
        AlignedFree(data_);
        data_ = nullptr;
        capacity_ = 0;
        return;
    }
    
    u8* new_data = static_cast<u8*>(AlignedAlloc(size_, alignment_));
    if (new_data) {
        std::memcpy(new_data, data_, size_);
        TrackDeallocation(capacity_);
        AlignedFree(data_);
        data_ = new_data;
        capacity_ = size_;
    }
}

void Buffer::Fill(u8 value) {
    if (data_ && size_ > 0) {
        std::memset(data_, value, size_);
    }
}

void Buffer::Assign(const void* data, usize size) {
    if (size == 0) {
        Clear();
        return;
    }
    
    Resize(size);
    if (data_ && data) {
        std::memcpy(data_, data, size);
    }
}

void Buffer::Assign(std::span<const u8> data) {
    Assign(data.data(), data.size());
}

void Buffer::Append(const void* data, usize size) {
    if (size == 0 || !data) return;
    
    usize old_size = size_;
    Resize(size_ + size);
    if (data_) {
        std::memcpy(data_ + old_size, data, size);
    }
}

void Buffer::Append(std::span<const u8> data) {
    Append(data.data(), data.size());
}

Buffer Buffer::Zeroed(usize size, usize alignment) {
    Buffer buf(size, alignment);
    buf.Zero();
    return buf;
}

Buffer Buffer::FromString(std::string_view str) {
    return Buffer(str.data(), str.size());
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Ring Buffer Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

RingBuffer::RingBuffer(usize capacity)
    : capacity_(capacity) {
    buffer_ = static_cast<u8*>(AlignedAlloc(capacity_, 64));
}

RingBuffer::~RingBuffer() {
    if (buffer_) {
        TrackDeallocation(capacity_);
        AlignedFree(buffer_);
    }
}

RingBuffer::RingBuffer(RingBuffer&& other) noexcept
    : buffer_(other.buffer_)
    , capacity_(other.capacity_)
    , read_pos_(other.read_pos_.load())
    , write_pos_(other.write_pos_.load()) {
    other.buffer_ = nullptr;
    other.capacity_ = 0;
    other.read_pos_ = 0;
    other.write_pos_ = 0;
}

RingBuffer& RingBuffer::operator=(RingBuffer&& other) noexcept {
    if (this != &other) {
        if (buffer_) {
            TrackDeallocation(capacity_);
            AlignedFree(buffer_);
        }
        
        buffer_ = other.buffer_;
        capacity_ = other.capacity_;
        read_pos_.store(other.read_pos_.load());
        write_pos_.store(other.write_pos_.load());
        
        other.buffer_ = nullptr;
        other.capacity_ = 0;
        other.read_pos_ = 0;
        other.write_pos_ = 0;
    }
    return *this;
}

usize RingBuffer::Write(const void* data, usize size) {
    if (!buffer_ || !data || size == 0) return 0;
    
    usize free_space = Free();
    usize write_size = std::min(size, free_space);
    if (write_size == 0) return 0;
    
    usize write_pos = write_pos_.load();
    const u8* src = static_cast<const u8*>(data);
    
    usize first_part = std::min(write_size, capacity_ - write_pos);
    std::memcpy(buffer_ + write_pos, src, first_part);
    
    if (first_part < write_size) {
        std::memcpy(buffer_, src + first_part, write_size - first_part);
    }
    
    write_pos_.store((write_pos + write_size) % capacity_);
    
    return write_size;
}

usize RingBuffer::Write(std::span<const u8> data) {
    return Write(data.data(), data.size());
}

usize RingBuffer::Read(void* data, usize size) {
    if (!buffer_ || !data || size == 0) return 0;
    
    usize available = Available();
    usize read_size = std::min(size, available);
    if (read_size == 0) return 0;
    
    usize read_pos = read_pos_.load();
    u8* dst = static_cast<u8*>(data);
    
    usize first_part = std::min(read_size, capacity_ - read_pos);
    std::memcpy(dst, buffer_ + read_pos, first_part);
    
    if (first_part < read_size) {
        std::memcpy(dst + first_part, buffer_, read_size - first_part);
    }
    
    read_pos_.store((read_pos + read_size) % capacity_);
    
    return read_size;
}

usize RingBuffer::Peek(void* data, usize size) const {
    if (!buffer_ || !data || size == 0) return 0;
    
    usize available = Available();
    usize read_size = std::min(size, available);
    if (read_size == 0) return 0;
    
    usize read_pos = read_pos_.load();
    u8* dst = static_cast<u8*>(data);
    
    usize first_part = std::min(read_size, capacity_ - read_pos);
    std::memcpy(dst, buffer_ + read_pos, first_part);
    
    if (first_part < read_size) {
        std::memcpy(dst + first_part, buffer_, read_size - first_part);
    }
    
    return read_size;
}

usize RingBuffer::Skip(usize size) {
    usize available = Available();
    usize skip_size = std::min(size, available);
    
    usize read_pos = read_pos_.load();
    read_pos_.store((read_pos + skip_size) % capacity_);
    
    return skip_size;
}

usize RingBuffer::Available() const noexcept {
    usize write_pos = write_pos_.load();
    usize read_pos = read_pos_.load();
    
    if (write_pos >= read_pos) {
        return write_pos - read_pos;
    }
    return capacity_ - read_pos + write_pos;
}

usize RingBuffer::Free() const noexcept {
    return capacity_ - Available() - 1;  // -1 to distinguish full from empty
}

usize RingBuffer::Capacity() const noexcept {
    return capacity_;
}

bool RingBuffer::IsEmpty() const noexcept {
    return Available() == 0;
}

bool RingBuffer::IsFull() const noexcept {
    return Free() == 0;
}

void RingBuffer::Clear() {
    read_pos_.store(0);
    write_pos_.store(0);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Memory Pool Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

MemoryPool::MemoryPool(usize block_size, usize initial_blocks, usize max_blocks)
    : block_size_(std::max(block_size, sizeof(Block)))
    , max_blocks_(max_blocks) {
    // Pre-allocate initial blocks
    for (usize i = 0; i < initial_blocks; ++i) {
        GrowPool();
    }
}

MemoryPool::~MemoryPool() {
    for (void* chunk : chunks_) {
        AlignedFree(chunk);
    }
}

void* MemoryPool::Allocate() {
    Block* block = free_list_.load();
    
    while (block) {
        if (free_list_.compare_exchange_weak(block, block->next)) {
            --free_blocks_;
            return block;
        }
    }
    
    // No free blocks, grow pool
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        GrowPool();
    }
    
    // Try again
    block = free_list_.load();
    while (block) {
        if (free_list_.compare_exchange_weak(block, block->next)) {
            --free_blocks_;
            return block;
        }
    }
    
    return nullptr;
}

void MemoryPool::Deallocate(void* ptr) {
    if (!ptr) return;
    
    Block* block = static_cast<Block*>(ptr);
    Block* expected = free_list_.load();
    
    do {
        block->next = expected;
    } while (!free_list_.compare_exchange_weak(expected, block));
    
    ++free_blocks_;
}

void MemoryPool::GrowPool() {
    if (max_blocks_ > 0 && total_blocks_ >= max_blocks_) {
        return;
    }
    
    // Allocate chunk of blocks
    constexpr usize BLOCKS_PER_CHUNK = 32;
    usize chunk_size = block_size_ * BLOCKS_PER_CHUNK;
    
    void* chunk = AlignedAlloc(chunk_size, 64);
    if (!chunk) return;
    
    chunks_.push_back(chunk);
    
    // Add blocks to free list
    u8* ptr = static_cast<u8*>(chunk);
    for (usize i = 0; i < BLOCKS_PER_CHUNK; ++i) {
        if (max_blocks_ > 0 && total_blocks_ >= max_blocks_) {
            break;
        }
        
        Block* block = reinterpret_cast<Block*>(ptr + i * block_size_);
        Block* expected = free_list_.load();
        
        do {
            block->next = expected;
        } while (!free_list_.compare_exchange_weak(expected, block));
        
        ++total_blocks_;
        ++free_blocks_;
    }
}

void MemoryPool::Shrink() {
    // Not implemented for thread-safe pool
}

void MemoryPool::Clear() {
    free_list_.store(nullptr);
    free_blocks_.store(0);
    
    // Rebuild free list from all chunks
    for (void* chunk : chunks_) {
        constexpr usize BLOCKS_PER_CHUNK = 32;
        u8* ptr = static_cast<u8*>(chunk);
        
        for (usize i = 0; i < BLOCKS_PER_CHUNK; ++i) {
            Block* block = reinterpret_cast<Block*>(ptr + i * block_size_);
            Block* expected = free_list_.load();
            
            do {
                block->next = expected;
            } while (!free_list_.compare_exchange_weak(expected, block));
            
            ++free_blocks_;
        }
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Frame Allocator Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

FrameAllocator::FrameAllocator(usize size)
    : size_(size) {
    buffer_ = static_cast<u8*>(AlignedAlloc(size_, 64));
}

FrameAllocator::~FrameAllocator() {
    if (buffer_) {
        TrackDeallocation(size_);
        AlignedFree(buffer_);
    }
}

void* FrameAllocator::Allocate(usize size, usize alignment) {
    // Align offset
    usize aligned_offset = (offset_ + alignment - 1) & ~(alignment - 1);
    
    if (aligned_offset + size > size_) {
        return nullptr;
    }
    
    void* ptr = buffer_ + aligned_offset;
    offset_ = aligned_offset + size;
    
    return ptr;
}

void FrameAllocator::Reset() {
    offset_ = 0;
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Reference Counted Buffer Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

RefBuffer::RefBuffer() = default;

RefBuffer::RefBuffer(usize size) {
    if (size > 0) {
        usize total_size = sizeof(ControlBlock) + size;
        block_ = static_cast<ControlBlock*>(AlignedAlloc(total_size, 64));
        if (block_) {
            block_->ref_count.store(1);
            block_->size = size;
        }
    }
}

RefBuffer::RefBuffer(const void* data, usize size) : RefBuffer(size) {
    if (block_ && data) {
        std::memcpy(block_->data, data, size);
    }
}

RefBuffer::RefBuffer(const RefBuffer& other) : block_(other.block_) {
    if (block_) {
        block_->ref_count.fetch_add(1);
    }
}

RefBuffer& RefBuffer::operator=(const RefBuffer& other) {
    if (this != &other) {
        Reset();
        block_ = other.block_;
        if (block_) {
            block_->ref_count.fetch_add(1);
        }
    }
    return *this;
}

RefBuffer::RefBuffer(RefBuffer&& other) noexcept : block_(other.block_) {
    other.block_ = nullptr;
}

RefBuffer& RefBuffer::operator=(RefBuffer&& other) noexcept {
    if (this != &other) {
        Reset();
        block_ = other.block_;
        other.block_ = nullptr;
    }
    return *this;
}

RefBuffer::~RefBuffer() {
    Reset();
}

u8* RefBuffer::Data() const noexcept {
    return block_ ? block_->data : nullptr;
}

usize RefBuffer::Size() const noexcept {
    return block_ ? block_->size : 0;
}

bool RefBuffer::IsEmpty() const noexcept {
    return !block_ || block_->size == 0;
}

u32 RefBuffer::RefCount() const noexcept {
    return block_ ? block_->ref_count.load() : 0;
}

bool RefBuffer::IsUnique() const noexcept {
    return block_ && block_->ref_count.load() == 1;
}

void RefBuffer::MakeUnique() {
    if (!block_ || IsUnique()) return;
    
    usize size = block_->size;
    usize total_size = sizeof(ControlBlock) + size;
    
    ControlBlock* new_block = static_cast<ControlBlock*>(AlignedAlloc(total_size, 64));
    if (new_block) {
        new_block->ref_count.store(1);
        new_block->size = size;
        std::memcpy(new_block->data, block_->data, size);
        
        Reset();
        block_ = new_block;
    }
}

void RefBuffer::Reset() {
    if (block_) {
        if (block_->ref_count.fetch_sub(1) == 1) {
            usize total_size = sizeof(ControlBlock) + block_->size;
            TrackDeallocation(total_size);
            AlignedFree(block_);
        }
        block_ = nullptr;
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Memory Statistics
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

MemoryStats GetMemoryStats() {
    return MemoryStats{
        .total_allocated = g_total_allocated.load(),
        .total_freed = g_total_freed.load(),
        .current_usage = g_current_usage.load(),
        .peak_usage = g_peak_usage.load(),
        .allocation_count = g_allocation_count.load(),
        .deallocation_count = g_deallocation_count.load()
    };
}

void ResetMemoryStats() {
    g_total_allocated.store(0);
    g_total_freed.store(0);
    g_current_usage.store(0);
    g_peak_usage.store(0);
    g_allocation_count.store(0);
    g_deallocation_count.store(0);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Memory Utilities
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

usize GetPageSize() {
#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    SYSTEM_INFO si;
    GetSystemInfo(&si);
    return si.dwPageSize;
#else
    return static_cast<usize>(sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE));
#endif
}

usize GetTotalPhysicalMemory() {
#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    MEMORYSTATUSEX status;
    status.dwLength = sizeof(status);
    GlobalMemoryStatusEx(&status);
    return static_cast<usize>(status.ullTotalPhys);
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE)
    int mib[2] = {CTL_HW, HW_MEMSIZE};
    u64 size;
    size_t len = sizeof(size);
    sysctl(mib, 2, &size, &len, nullptr, 0);
    return static_cast<usize>(size);
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
    long pages = sysconf(_SC_PHYS_PAGES);
    long page_size = sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE);
    return static_cast<usize>(pages * page_size);
#else
    return 0;
#endif
}

usize GetAvailablePhysicalMemory() {
#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    MEMORYSTATUSEX status;
    status.dwLength = sizeof(status);
    GlobalMemoryStatusEx(&status);
    return static_cast<usize>(status.ullAvailPhys);
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE)
    vm_statistics64_data_t stats;
    mach_msg_type_number_t count = HOST_VM_INFO64_COUNT;
    if (host_statistics64(mach_host_self(), HOST_VM_INFO64, 
                         reinterpret_cast<host_info64_t>(&stats), &count) == KERN_SUCCESS) {
        return static_cast<usize>(stats.free_count) * GetPageSize();
    }
    return 0;
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
    std::ifstream meminfo("/proc/meminfo");
    std::string line;
    while (std::getline(meminfo, line)) {
        if (line.find("MemAvailable:") == 0) {
            usize available;
            sscanf(line.c_str(), "MemAvailable: %zu kB", &available);
            return available * 1024;
        }
    }
    return 0;
#else
    return 0;
#endif
}

usize GetProcessMemoryUsage() {
#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS pmc;
    if (GetProcessMemoryInfo(GetCurrentProcess(), &pmc, sizeof(pmc))) {
        return static_cast<usize>(pmc.WorkingSetSize);
    }
    return 0;
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE)
    struct mach_task_basic_info info;
    mach_msg_type_number_t count = MACH_TASK_BASIC_INFO_COUNT;
    if (task_info(mach_task_self(), MACH_TASK_BASIC_INFO,
                  reinterpret_cast<task_info_t>(&info), &count) == KERN_SUCCESS) {
        return static_cast<usize>(info.resident_size);
    }
    return 0;
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
    std::ifstream statm("/proc/self/statm");
    usize resident;
    statm >> resident >> resident;  // Skip first value, get RSS
    return resident * GetPageSize();
#else
    return 0;
#endif
}

} // namespace aether
```

## Part 5: Threading Utilities

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/utils/threading.hpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#pragma once

#ifndef AETHER_UTILS_THREADING_HPP
#define AETHER_UTILS_THREADING_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"
#include "aether/core/error.hpp"

#include <thread>
#include <mutex>
#include <shared_mutex>
#include <condition_variable>
#include <atomic>
#include <functional>
#include <queue>
#include <future>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <optional>

namespace aether {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Thread Utilities
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

AETHER_API void SetThreadName(const std::string& name);
AETHER_API void SetThreadName(std::thread& thread, const std::string& name);
AETHER_API std::string GetThreadName();
AETHER_API u32 GetHardwareConcurrency();
AETHER_API void SetThreadPriority(std::thread& thread, i32 priority);
AETHER_API void SetThreadAffinity(std::thread& thread, u32 core);

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Spinlock
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API Spinlock {
public:
    void lock() noexcept {
        while (flag_.test_and_set(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
            // Spin with pause instruction for efficiency
            #if defined(__x86_64__) || defined(_M_X64)
            __builtin_ia32_pause();
            #elif defined(__aarch64__)
            __asm__ volatile("yield");
            #endif
        }
    }
    
    bool try_lock() noexcept {
        return !flag_.test_and_set(std::memory_order_acquire);
    }
    
    void unlock() noexcept {
        flag_.clear(std::memory_order_release);
    }
    
private:
    std::atomic_flag flag_ = ATOMIC_FLAG_INIT;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Semaphore (C++20 has std::counting_semaphore, but we provide our own)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API Semaphore {
public:
    explicit Semaphore(u32 initial_count = 0);
    
    void Acquire();
    bool TryAcquire();
    bool TryAcquireFor(std::chrono::milliseconds timeout);
    void Release(u32 count = 1);
    
    [[nodiscard]] u32 Count() const noexcept;
    
private:
    std::mutex mutex_;
    std::condition_variable cv_;
    u32 count_;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Event (Manual/Auto Reset)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API Event {
public:
    explicit Event(bool manual_reset = false, bool initial_state = false);
    
    void Set();
    void Reset();
    void Wait();
    bool Wait(std::chrono::milliseconds timeout);
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsSet() const noexcept;
    
private:
    std::mutex mutex_;
    std::condition_variable cv_;
    bool signaled_;
    bool manual_reset_;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Thread-Safe Queue
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

template<typename T>
class ThreadSafeQueue {
public:
    ThreadSafeQueue() = default;
    explicit ThreadSafeQueue(usize max_size) : max_size_(max_size) {}
    
    void Push(T item) {
        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        if (max_size_ > 0) {
            not_full_.wait(lock, [this] { return queue_.size() < max_size_ || closed_; });
        }
        
        if (closed_) return;
        
        queue_.push(std::move(item));
        lock.unlock();
        not_empty_.notify_one();
    }
    
    bool TryPush(T item) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        if (closed_) return false;
        if (max_size_ > 0 && queue_.size() >= max_size_) return false;
        
        queue_.push(std::move(item));
        not_empty_.notify_one();
        return true;
    }
    
    bool Pop(T& item) {
        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        not_empty_.wait(lock, [this] { return !queue_.empty() || closed_; });
        
        if (queue_.empty()) return false;
        
        item = std::move(queue_.front());
        queue_.pop();
        
        if (max_size_ > 0) {
            lock.unlock();
            not_full_.notify_one();
        }
        
        return true;
    }
    
    bool TryPop(T& item) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        if (queue_.empty()) return false;
        
        item = std::move(queue_.front());
        queue_.pop();
        
        not_full_.notify_one();
        return true;
    }
    
    std::optional<T> Pop() {
        T item;
        if (Pop(item)) {
            return std::move(item);
        }
        return std::nullopt;
    }
    
    std::optional<T> TryPop() {
        T item;
        if (TryPop(item)) {
            return std::move(item);
        }
        return std::nullopt;
    }
    
    void Close() {
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
            closed_ = true;
        }
        not_empty_.notify_all();
        not_full_.notify_all();
    }
    
    void Clear() {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        while (!queue_.empty()) {
            queue_.pop();
        }
        not_full_.notify_all();
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] usize Size() const {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        return queue_.size();
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsEmpty() const {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        return queue_.empty();
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsClosed() const {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        return closed_;
    }
    
private:
    mutable std::mutex mutex_;
    std::condition_variable not_empty_;
    std::condition_variable not_full_;
    std::queue<T> queue_;
    usize max_size_ = 0;
    bool closed_ = false;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Thread Pool
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API ThreadPool {
public:
    explicit ThreadPool(u32 num_threads = 0, std::string_view name = "Worker");
    ~ThreadPool();
    
    ThreadPool(const ThreadPool&) = delete;
    ThreadPool& operator=(const ThreadPool&) = delete;
    
    // Submit task and get future
    template<typename F, typename... Args>
    auto Submit(F&& f, Args&&... args) -> std::future<std::invoke_result_t<F, Args...>> {
        using ReturnType = std::invoke_result_t<F, Args...>;
        
        auto task = std::make_shared<std::packaged_task<ReturnType()>>(
            std::bind(std::forward<F>(f), std::forward<Args>(args)...)
        );
        
        std::future<ReturnType> future = task->get_future();
        
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
            if (stopped_) {
                throw Exception(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "ThreadPool is stopped");
            }
            tasks_.emplace([task]() { (*task)(); });
        }
        
        condition_.notify_one();
        return future;
    }
    
    // Submit task without caring about result
    void Execute(std::function<void()> task);
    
    // Wait for all tasks to complete
    void Wait();
    
    // Stop the pool (waits for current tasks to finish)
    void Stop();
    
    // Shutdown immediately (cancels pending tasks)
    void Shutdown();
    
    [[nodiscard]] u32 ThreadCount() const noexcept { return thread_count_; }
    [[nodiscard]] usize PendingTasks() const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsStopped() const noexcept { return stopped_; }
    
private:
    void WorkerThread(u32 id);
    
    std::vector<std::thread> workers_;
    std::queue<std::function<void()>> tasks_;
    
    mutable std::mutex mutex_;
    std::condition_variable condition_;
    std::condition_variable done_condition_;
    
    std::atomic<u32> active_tasks_{0};
    u32 thread_count_;
    std::string name_;
    std::atomic<bool> stopped_{false};
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Worker Thread
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API WorkerThread {
public:
    using Task = std::function<void()>;
    
    explicit WorkerThread(std::string_view name = "Worker");
    ~WorkerThread();
    
    WorkerThread(const WorkerThread&) = delete;
    WorkerThread& operator=(const WorkerThread&) = delete;
    
    void Start();
    void Stop();
    void Join();
    
    void Post(Task task);
    void PostDelayed(Task task, std::chrono::milliseconds delay);
    
    template<typename F, typename... Args>
    auto Submit(F&& f, Args&&... args) -> std::future<std::invoke_result_t<F, Args...>> {
        using ReturnType = std::invoke_result_t<F, Args...>;
        
        auto task = std::make_shared<std::packaged_task<ReturnType()>>(
            std::bind(std::forward<F>(f), std::forward<Args>(args)...)
        );
        
        std::future<ReturnType> future = task->get_future();
        Post([task]() { (*task)(); });
        return future;
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsRunning() const noexcept { return running_; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::thread::id GetThreadId() const noexcept;
    
private:
    void Run();
    
    std::thread thread_;
    std::string name_;
    
    std::mutex mutex_;
    std::condition_variable cv_;
    std::queue<Task> tasks_;
    
    std::atomic<bool> running_{false};
    std::atomic<bool> stop_requested_{false};
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Read-Write Lock Wrapper
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

template<typename T>
class RWLocked {
public:
    template<typename... Args>
    RWLocked(Args&&... args) : data_(std::forward<Args>(args)...) {}
    
    class ReadLock {
    public:
        ReadLock(const RWLocked& parent) : lock_(parent.mutex_), data_(parent.data_) {}
        const T* operator->() const { return &data_; }
        const T& operator*() const { return data_; }
    private:
        std::shared_lock<std::shared_mutex> lock_;
        const T& data_;
    };
    
    class WriteLock {
    public:
        WriteLock(RWLocked& parent) : lock_(parent.mutex_), data_(parent.data_) {}
        T* operator->() { return &data_; }
        T& operator*() { return data_; }
    private:
        std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> lock_;
        T& data_;
    };
    
    ReadLock Read() const { return ReadLock(*this); }
    WriteLock Write() { return WriteLock(*this); }
    
    template<typename F>
    auto WithRead(F&& f) const -> decltype(f(std::declval<const T&>())) {
        std::shared_lock<std::shared_mutex> lock(mutex_);
        return f(data_);
    }
    
    template<typename F>
    auto WithWrite(F&& f) -> decltype(f(std::declval<T&>())) {
        std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> lock(mutex_);
        return f(data_);
    }
    
private:
    mutable std::shared_mutex mutex_;
    T data_;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Once Flag with State
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API OnceFlag {
public:
    OnceFlag() = default;
    
    template<typename F, typename... Args>
    void Call(F&& f, Args&&... args) {
        std::call_once(flag_, std::forward<F>(f), std::forward<Args>(args)...);
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] bool WasCalled() const noexcept {
        return called_.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    }
    
    void Reset() {
        // Note: This is not fully thread-safe, use with care
        flag_ = std::once_flag{};
        called_.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
    }
    
private:
    std::once_flag flag_;
    std::atomic<bool> called_{false};
};

} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_UTILS_THREADING_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: src/utils/threading.cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#include "aether/utils/threading.hpp"

#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    #include <windows.h>
    #include <processthreadsapi.h>
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE)
    #include <pthread.h>
    #include <mach/mach.h>
    #include <mach/thread_policy.h>
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
    #include <pthread.h>
    #include <sched.h>
    #include <sys/prctl.h>
#endif

namespace aether {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Thread Utilities Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

void SetThreadName(const std::string& name) {
#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    // Windows 10 version 1607+ API
    using SetThreadDescriptionFunc = HRESULT(WINAPI*)(HANDLE, PCWSTR);
    static auto set_thread_description = reinterpret_cast<SetThreadDescriptionFunc>(
        GetProcAddress(GetModuleHandleA("kernel32.dll"), "SetThreadDescription")
    );
    
    if (set_thread_description) {
        std::wstring wname(name.begin(), name.end());
        set_thread_description(GetCurrentThread(), wname.c_str());
    }
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE)
    pthread_setname_np(name.c_str());
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
    prctl(PR_SET_NAME, name.c_str(), 0, 0, 0);
#endif
}

void SetThreadName(std::thread& thread, const std::string& name) {
#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    using SetThreadDescriptionFunc = HRESULT(WINAPI*)(HANDLE, PCWSTR);
    static auto set_thread_description = reinterpret_cast<SetThreadDescriptionFunc>(
        GetProcAddress(GetModuleHandleA("kernel32.dll"), "SetThreadDescription")
    );
    
    if (set_thread_description) {
        std::wstring wname(name.begin(), name.end());
        set_thread_description(thread.native_handle(), wname.c_str());
    }
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE)
    // macOS only supports setting name for current thread
    (void)thread;
    (void)name;
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
    pthread_setname_np(thread.native_handle(), name.c_str());
#endif
}

std::string GetThreadName() {
#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    using GetThreadDescriptionFunc = HRESULT(WINAPI*)(HANDLE, PWSTR*);
    static auto get_thread_description = reinterpret_cast<GetThreadDescriptionFunc>(
        GetProcAddress(GetModuleHandleA("kernel32.dll"), "GetThreadDescription")
    );
    
    if (get_thread_description) {
        PWSTR name;
        if (SUCCEEDED(get_thread_description(GetCurrentThread(), &name))) {
            std::wstring wname(name);
            LocalFree(name);
            return std::string(wname.begin(), wname.end());
        }
    }
    return "";
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE)
    char name[64];
    pthread_getname_np(pthread_self(), name, sizeof(name));
    return name;
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
    char name[16];
    pthread_getname_np(pthread_self(), name, sizeof(name));
    return name;
#else
    return "";
#endif
}

u32 GetHardwareConcurrency() {
    u32 count = std::thread::hardware_concurrency();
    return count > 0 ? count : 1;
}

void SetThreadPriority(std::thread& thread, i32 priority) {
#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    int win_priority;
    if (priority < -1) win_priority = THREAD_PRIORITY_LOWEST;
    else if (priority == -1) win_priority = THREAD_PRIORITY_BELOW_NORMAL;
    else if (priority == 0) win_priority = THREAD_PRIORITY_NORMAL;
    else if (priority == 1) win_priority = THREAD_PRIORITY_ABOVE_NORMAL;
    else win_priority = THREAD_PRIORITY_HIGHEST;
    
    SetThreadPriority(thread.native_handle(), win_priority);
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE) || defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
    sched_param param;
    int policy;
    pthread_getschedparam(thread.native_handle(), &policy, &param);
    
    int min_priority = sched_get_priority_min(policy);
    int max_priority = sched_get_priority_max(policy);
    
    // Map priority (-2 to 2) to system range
    param.sched_priority = min_priority + 
        (priority + 2) * (max_priority - min_priority) / 4;
    
    pthread_setschedparam(thread.native_handle(), policy, &param);
#endif
}

void SetThreadAffinity(std::thread& thread, u32 core) {
#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    DWORD_PTR mask = 1ULL << core;
    SetThreadAffinityMask(thread.native_handle(), mask);
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
    cpu_set_t cpuset;
    CPU_ZERO(&cpuset);
    CPU_SET(core, &cpuset);
    pthread_setaffinity_np(thread.native_handle(), sizeof(cpu_set_t), &cpuset);
#elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE)
    thread_affinity_policy_data_t policy = { static_cast<integer_t>(core) };
    thread_policy_set(pthread_mach_thread_np(thread.native_handle()),
                     THREAD_AFFINITY_POLICY,
                     reinterpret_cast<thread_policy_t>(&policy),
                     THREAD_AFFINITY_POLICY_COUNT);
#endif
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Semaphore Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Semaphore::Semaphore(u32 initial_count) : count_(initial_count) {}

void Semaphore::Acquire() {
    std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    cv_.wait(lock, [this] { return count_ > 0; });
    --count_;
}

bool Semaphore::TryAcquire() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    if (count_ > 0) {
        --count_;
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}

bool Semaphore::TryAcquireFor(std::chrono::milliseconds timeout) {
    std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    if (!cv_.wait_for(lock, timeout, [this] { return count_ > 0; })) {
        return false;
    }
    --count_;
    return true;
}

void Semaphore::Release(u32 count) {
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        count_ += count;
    }
    for (u32 i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        cv_.notify_one();
    }
}

u32 Semaphore::Count() const noexcept {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(const_cast<std::mutex&>(mutex_));
    return count_;
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Event Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Event::Event(bool manual_reset, bool initial_state)
    : signaled_(initial_state)
    , manual_reset_(manual_reset) {}

void Event::Set() {
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        signaled_ = true;
    }
    if (manual_reset_) {
        cv_.notify_all();
    } else {
        cv_.notify_one();
    }
}

void Event::Reset() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    signaled_ = false;
}

void Event::Wait() {
    std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    cv_.wait(lock, [this] { return signaled_; });
    if (!manual_reset_) {
        signaled_ = false;
    }
}

bool Event::Wait(std::chrono::milliseconds timeout) {
    std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    bool result = cv_.wait_for(lock, timeout, [this] { return signaled_; });
    if (result && !manual_reset_) {
        signaled_ = false;
    }
    return result;
}

bool Event::IsSet() const noexcept {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(const_cast<std::mutex&>(mutex_));
    return signaled_;
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Thread Pool Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

ThreadPool::ThreadPool(u32 num_threads, std::string_view name)
    : thread_count_(num_threads > 0 ? num_threads : GetHardwareConcurrency())
    , name_(name) {
    
    workers_.reserve(thread_count_);
    
    for (u32 i = 0; i < thread_count_; ++i) {
        workers_.emplace_back(&ThreadPool::WorkerThread, this, i);
    }
}

ThreadPool::~ThreadPool() {
    Shutdown();
}

void ThreadPool::Execute(std::function<void()> task) {
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        if (stopped_) return;
        tasks_.emplace(std::move(task));
    }
    condition_.notify_one();
}

void ThreadPool::Wait() {
    std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    done_condition_.wait(lock, [this] {
        return tasks_.empty() && active_tasks_ == 0;
    });
}

void ThreadPool::Stop() {
    Wait();
    Shutdown();
}

void ThreadPool::Shutdown() {
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        stopped_ = true;
    }
    
    condition_.notify_all();
    
    for (auto& worker : workers_) {
        if (worker.joinable()) {
            worker.join();
        }
    }
    
    workers_.clear();
}

usize ThreadPool::PendingTasks() const {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    return tasks_.size();
}

void ThreadPool::WorkerThread(u32 id) {
    SetThreadName(name_ + "_" + std::to_string(id));
    
    while (true) {
        std::function<void()> task;
        
        {
            std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
            
            condition_.wait(lock, [this] {
                return stopped_ || !tasks_.empty();
            });
            
            if (stopped_ && tasks_.empty()) {
                return;
            }
            
            task = std::move(tasks_.front());
            tasks_.pop();
            ++active_tasks_;
        }
        
        task();
        
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
            --active_tasks_;
        }
        done_condition_.notify_all();
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Worker Thread Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

WorkerThread::WorkerThread(std::string_view name) : name_(name) {}

WorkerThread::~WorkerThread() {
    Stop();
    Join();
}

void WorkerThread::Start() {
    if (running_) return;
    
    running_ = true;
    stop_requested_ = false;
    thread_ = std::thread(&WorkerThread::Run, this);
}

void WorkerThread::Stop() {
    if (!running_) return;
    
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        stop_requested_ = true;
    }
    cv_.notify_one();
}

void WorkerThread::Join() {
    if (thread_.joinable()) {
        thread_.join();
    }
    running_ = false;
}

void WorkerThread::Post(Task task) {
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        tasks_.push(std::move(task));
    }
    cv_.notify_one();
}

void WorkerThread::PostDelayed(Task task, std::chrono::milliseconds delay) {
    // Simple implementation: post a wrapper that sleeps
    Post([task = std::move(task), delay]() {
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(delay);
        task();
    });
}

std::thread::id WorkerThread::GetThreadId() const noexcept {
    return thread_.get_id();
}

void WorkerThread::Run() {
    SetThreadName(name_);
    
    while (true) {
        Task task;
        
        {
            std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
            
            cv_.wait(lock, [this] {
                return stop_requested_ || !tasks_.empty();
            });
            
            if (stop_requested_ && tasks_.empty()) {
                break;
            }
            
            task = std::move(tasks_.front());
            tasks_.pop();
        }
        
        if (task) {
            task();
        }
    }
    
    running_ = false;
}

} // namespace aether
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/core/engine.hpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#pragma once

#ifndef AETHER_CORE_ENGINE_HPP
#define AETHER_CORE_ENGINE_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"

#include <string_view>

namespace aether {

// Forward declarations
class Player;
class PluginManager;
class Logger;

namespace ai {
    class InferenceEngine;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Engine Capabilities
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct EngineCapabilities {
    // Video
    bool hardware_decoding = false;
    bool hardware_encoding = false;
    std::vector<CodecID> supported_video_codecs;
    std::vector<PixelFormat> supported_pixel_formats;
    u32 max_video_width = 0;
    u32 max_video_height = 0;
    
    // Audio
    std::vector<CodecID> supported_audio_codecs;
    std::vector<SampleFormat> supported_sample_formats;
    u32 max_sample_rate = 0;
    u32 max_channels = 0;
    
    // Rendering
    bool vulkan_available = false;
    bool metal_available = false;
    bool d3d12_available = false;
    bool d3d11_available = false;
    bool opengl_available = false;
    bool hdr_output = false;
    
    // AI

    bool ai_frame_interpolation = false;
    bool ai_audio_enhancement = false;
    std::vector<std::string> available_ai_backends;

    // DRM

    bool widevine_available = false;
    bool playready_available = false;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Engine Statistics

struct EngineStatistics {
    // Memory
    usize total_memory_used = 0;
    usize video_memory_used = 0;
    usize audio_buffer_used = 0;
    usize cache_memory_used = 0;
    usize peak_memory_used = 0;

    f64 average_render_time_ms = 0;
    f64 average_audio_process_time_ms = 0;

    u64 frames_rendered = 0;
    u64 frames_dropped = 0;
    u64 samples_processed = 0;
