```cpp
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Media Information
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct VideoTrackInfo {
    i32 index = -1;
    CodecID codec = CodecID::Unknown;
    std::string codec_name;
    u32 width = 0;
    u32 height = 0;
    f64 frame_rate = 0;
    f64 avg_frame_rate = 0;
    i64 bitrate = 0;
    PixelFormat pixel_format = PixelFormat::Unknown;
    ColorSpace color_space = ColorSpace::Unknown;
    ColorRange color_range = ColorRange::Unknown;
    ColorPrimaries color_primaries = ColorPrimaries::Unknown;
    TransferCharacteristics transfer = TransferCharacteristics::Unknown;
    HDRType hdr_type = HDRType::None;
    std::string language;
    std::string title;
    bool is_default = false;
    bool is_forced = false;
    i32 rotation = 0;
    f64 sar_num = 1;  // Sample aspect ratio
    f64 sar_den = 1;
    std::map<std::string, std::string> metadata;
};

struct AudioTrackInfo {
    i32 index = -1;
    CodecID codec = CodecID::Unknown;
    std::string codec_name;
    u32 sample_rate = 0;
    u32 channels = 0;
    ChannelLayout channel_layout = ChannelLayout::Unknown;
    SampleFormat sample_format = SampleFormat::Unknown;
    i64 bitrate = 0;
    u32 bits_per_sample = 0;
    std::string language;
    std::string title;
    bool is_default = false;
    bool is_original = false;
    bool is_commentary = false;
    bool is_visual_impaired = false;
    bool is_hearing_impaired = false;
    std::map<std::string, std::string> metadata;
};

struct SubtitleTrackInfo {
    i32 index = -1;
    CodecID codec = CodecID::Unknown;
    std::string codec_name;
    std::string language;
    std::string title;
    bool is_default = false;
    bool is_forced = false;
    bool is_sdh = false;
    bool is_cc = false;
    bool is_external = false;
    std::string external_path;
    std::map<std::string, std::string> metadata;
};

struct ChapterInfo {
    i32 index = 0;
    std::string title;
    Timestamp start;
    Timestamp end;
    std::map<std::string, std::string> metadata;
};

struct AttachmentInfo {
    i32 index = 0;
    std::string filename;
    std::string mimetype;
    usize size = 0;
    std::vector<u8> data;
};

struct MediaInfo {
    // Basic info
    std::string url;
    std::string format_name;
    std::string format_long_name;
    ContainerFormat container = ContainerFormat::Unknown;
    
    // Timing
    Timestamp duration;
    Timestamp start_time;
    i64 bitrate = 0;
    i64 file_size = 0;
    
    // Tracks
    std::vector<VideoTrackInfo> video_tracks;
    std::vector<AudioTrackInfo> audio_tracks;
    std::vector<SubtitleTrackInfo> subtitle_tracks;
    std::vector<ChapterInfo> chapters;
    std::vector<AttachmentInfo> attachments;
    
    // Metadata
    std::string title;
    std::string artist;
    std::string album;
    std::string album_artist;
    std::string composer;
    std::string genre;
    std::string date;
    std::string comment;
    std::string copyright;
    std::string encoder;
    i32 track_number = 0;
    i32 disc_number = 0;
    std::map<std::string, std::string> metadata;
    
    // Cover art
    std::vector<u8> cover_art;
    std::string cover_art_mimetype;
    
    // Flags
    bool is_live = false;
    bool is_seekable = true;
    bool has_video = false;
    bool has_audio = false;
    bool has_subtitles = false;
    bool has_drm = false;
    std::string drm_system;
    
    // Helpers
    [[nodiscard]] bool HasVideo() const { return !video_tracks.empty(); }
    [[nodiscard]] bool HasAudio() const { return !audio_tracks.empty(); }
    [[nodiscard]] bool HasSubtitles() const { return !subtitle_tracks.empty(); }
    [[nodiscard]] bool HasChapters() const { return !chapters.empty(); }
    
    [[nodiscard]] f64 DurationSeconds() const { return duration.ToSeconds(); }
    
    [[nodiscard]] const VideoTrackInfo* GetDefaultVideoTrack() const {
        for (const auto& t : video_tracks) {
            if (t.is_default) return &t;
        }
        return video_tracks.empty() ? nullptr : &video_tracks[0];
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] const AudioTrackInfo* GetDefaultAudioTrack() const {
        for (const auto& t : audio_tracks) {
            if (t.is_default) return &t;
        }
        return audio_tracks.empty() ? nullptr : &audio_tracks[0];
    }
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Engine Class
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API Engine {
public:
    // Singleton access
    static Engine& Instance();
    
    // Initialization
    Result<void> Initialize(const Config& config = Config::Default());
    void Shutdown();
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsInitialized() const noexcept;
    
    // Configuration
    [[nodiscard]] const Config& GetConfig() const noexcept;
    Result<void> UpdateConfig(const Config& config);
    
    // Player management
    Result<Shared<Player>> CreatePlayer(std::string_view name = "");
    void DestroyPlayer(Shared<Player>& player);
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<Shared<Player>> GetPlayers() const;
    [[nodiscard]] Shared<Player> GetPlayer(std::string_view name) const;
    
    // Subsystems
    [[nodiscard]] PluginManager& GetPluginManager();
    [[nodiscard]] ai::InferenceEngine& GetAIEngine();
    [[nodiscard]] Logger& GetLogger();
    
    // Capabilities & statistics
    [[nodiscard]] const EngineCapabilities& GetCapabilities() const;
    [[nodiscard]] EngineStatistics GetStatistics() const;
    void ResetStatistics();
    
    // Format support
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsCodecSupported(CodecID codec) const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsContainerSupported(ContainerFormat format) const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsProtocolSupported(std::string_view protocol) const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::string> GetSupportedExtensions() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::string> GetSupportedMimeTypes() const;
    
    // Media probing
    Result<MediaInfo> ProbeMedia(std::string_view url);
    Result<MediaInfo> ProbeMediaAsync(std::string_view url, 
                                       std::function<void(Result<MediaInfo>)> callback);
    
    // Thumbnail generation
    Result<std::vector<u8>> CreateThumbnail(std::string_view url, 
                                            f64 time_seconds,
                                            Size size,
                                            std::string_view format = "png");
    
    // Protocol registration
    using ProtocolHandler = std::function<Result<std::vector<u8>>(std::string_view url, i64 offset, i64 size)>;
    Result<void> RegisterProtocol(std::string_view protocol, ProtocolHandler handler);
    void UnregisterProtocol(std::string_view protocol);
    
    // Global event callback
    using GlobalEventCallback = std::function<void(const std::string& event, const std::string& data)>;
    u64 AddGlobalEventListener(GlobalEventCallback callback);
    void RemoveGlobalEventListener(u64 id);
    
    // Version info
    [[nodiscard]] static const char* GetVersion();
    [[nodiscard]] static BuildInfo GetBuildInfo();
    
private:
    Engine();
    ~Engine();
    Engine(const Engine&) = delete;
    Engine& operator=(const Engine&) = delete;
    
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

// Convenience functions
inline Engine& GetEngine() { return Engine::Instance(); }
inline Logger& GetLogger() { return Engine::Instance().GetLogger(); }

} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_CORE_ENGINE_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: src/core/engine/engine.cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#include "aether/core/engine.hpp"
#include "aether/core/player.hpp"
#include "aether/utils/logging.hpp"
#include "aether/utils/memory.hpp"
#include "aether/utils/threading.hpp"

#include <mutex>
#include <atomic>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <chrono>

// FFmpeg headers
extern "C" {
#include <libavcodec/avcodec.h>
#include <libavformat/avformat.h>
#include <libavutil/avutil.h>
#include <libswscale/swscale.h>
#include <libswresample/swresample.h>
#include <libavfilter/avfilter.h>
}

namespace aether {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Engine Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class Engine::Impl {
public:
    Impl() : start_time_(SteadyClock::now()) {}
    
    ~Impl() {
        Shutdown();
    }
    
    Result<void> Initialize(const Config& config) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        if (initialized_.load()) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AlreadyInitialized, "Engine already initialized");
        }
        
        config_ = config;
        
        // Initialize logging first
        if (auto result = InitializeLogging(); !result) {
            return result;
        }
        
        logger_->Info("═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════");
        logger_->Info("  AETHER Media Engine v{} \"{}\"", VERSION_STRING, CODENAME);
        logger_->Info("  Platform: {} | Arch: {}", AETHER_PLATFORM_NAME, AETHER_ARCH_NAME);
        logger_->Info("  Build: {} | {}", BUILD_TYPE, __DATE__);
        logger_->Info("═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════");
        
        // Initialize FFmpeg
        if (auto result = InitializeFFmpeg(); !result) {
            return result;
        }
        
        // Initialize networking
        if (auto result = InitializeNetwork(); !result) {
            logger_->Warn("Network initialization failed: {}", result.error().Message());
        }
        
        // Detect capabilities
        DetectCapabilities();
        
        // Initialize plugin system
        if (config_.plugin.enable_plugins) {
            if (auto result = InitializePlugins(); !result) {
                logger_->Warn("Plugin system initialization failed: {}", result.error().Message());
            }
        }
        
        // Initialize AI engine
        if (config_.ai.enable_ai_features) {
            if (auto result = InitializeAI(); !result) {
                logger_->Warn("AI engine initialization failed: {}", result.error().Message());
            }
        }
        
        // Create thread pool
        thread_pool_ = MakeUnique<ThreadPool>(
            config_.video.decode_thread_count > 0 ? config_.video.decode_thread_count : GetHardwareConcurrency(),
            "AetherWorker"
        );
        
        initialized_.store(true);
        
        logger_->Info("Engine initialized successfully");
        logger_->Info("  Video codecs: {}", capabilities_.supported_video_codecs.size());
        logger_->Info("  Audio codecs: {}", capabilities_.supported_audio_codecs.size());
        logger_->Info("  Protocols: {}", capabilities_.supported_protocols.size());
        logger_->Info("  Hardware decode: {}", capabilities_.hardware_decoding ? "Yes" : "No");
        logger_->Info("  AI features: {}", capabilities_.ai_upscaling ? "Available" : "Disabled");
        
        return {};
    }
    
    void Shutdown() {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        if (!initialized_.load()) {
            return;
        }
        
        if (logger_) {
            logger_->Info("Shutting down AETHER Media Engine...");
        }
        
        // Destroy all players
        for (auto& [id, player] : players_) {
            if (player) {
                player->Stop();
            }
        }
        players_.clear();
        
        // Shutdown thread pool
        if (thread_pool_) {
            thread_pool_->Stop();
            thread_pool_.reset();
        }
        
        // Shutdown subsystems
        ShutdownAI();
        ShutdownPlugins();
        ShutdownNetwork();
        ShutdownFFmpeg();
        
        if (logger_) {
            logger_->Info("Engine shutdown complete");
            logger_->Shutdown();
        }
        
        initialized_.store(false);
    }
    
    bool IsInitialized() const noexcept {
        return initialized_.load();
    }
    
    const Config& GetConfig() const noexcept {
        return config_;
    }
    
    Result<void> UpdateConfig(const Config& config) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        auto errors = config.Validate();
        if (!errors.empty()) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidArgument, 
                "Invalid configuration: {}", errors[0]);
        }
        
        // Update log level
        if (logger_ && config.log.level != config_.log.level) {
            logger_->SetLevel(config.log.level);
        }
        
        config_ = config;
        
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<Shared<Player>> CreatePlayer(std::string_view name) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        if (!initialized_.load()) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::NotInitialized, "Engine not initialized");
        }
        
        std::string player_name;
        if (name.empty()) {
            player_name = "Player_" + std::to_string(next_player_id_);
        } else {
            player_name = std::string(name);
        }
        
        // Check for duplicate name
        for (const auto& [id, p] : players_) {
            if (p->GetName() == player_name) {
                return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AlreadyExists, 
                    "Player with name '{}' already exists", player_name);
            }
        }
        
        auto player = MakeShared<Player>(player_name);
        
        PlayerOptions options;
        options.enable_hardware_decoding = config_.video.enable_hardware_decoding;
        options.preferred_renderer = config_.video.preferred_renderer;
        options.enable_ai_upscaling = config_.ai.enable_upscaling;
        options.enable_ai_frame_interpolation = config_.ai.enable_frame_interpolation;
        options.initial_volume = config_.audio.default_volume;
        
        if (auto result = player->Initialize(options); !result) {
            return result.error();
        }
        
        u64 id = next_player_id_++;
        players_[id] = player;
        
        logger_->Debug("Created player '{}' (id: {})", player_name, id);
        
        return player;
    }
    
    void DestroyPlayer(Shared<Player>& player) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        for (auto it = players_.begin(); it != players_.end(); ++it) {
            if (it->second == player) {
                logger_->Debug("Destroying player '{}'", player->GetName());
                it->second->Stop();
                players_.erase(it);
                player.reset();
                return;
            }
        }
    }
    
    std::vector<Shared<Player>> GetPlayers() const {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        std::vector<Shared<Player>> result;
        result.reserve(players_.size());
        for (const auto& [id, player] : players_) {
            result.push_back(player);
        }
        return result;
    }
    
    Shared<Player> GetPlayer(std::string_view name) const {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        for (const auto& [id, player] : players_) {
            if (player->GetName() == name) {
                return player;
            }
        }
        return nullptr;
    }
    
    Logger& GetLogger() {
        return *logger_;
    }
    
    const EngineCapabilities& GetCapabilities() const {
        return capabilities_;
    }
    
    EngineStatistics GetStatistics() const {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(stats_mutex_);
        
        auto stats = statistics_;
        
        // Update uptime
        auto now = SteadyClock::now();
        stats.uptime_seconds = std::chrono::duration<f64>(now - start_time_).count();
        
        // Aggregate player statistics
        for (const auto& [id, player] : players_) {
            auto player_stats = player->GetStatistics();
            stats.frames_decoded += player_stats.frames_decoded;
            stats.frames_rendered += player_stats.frames_rendered;
            stats.frames_dropped += player_stats.frames_dropped;
        }
        
        // Memory stats
        auto mem_stats = GetMemoryStats();
        stats.total_memory_used = mem_stats.current_usage;
        stats.peak_memory_used = mem_stats.peak_usage;
        
        return stats;
    }
    
    void ResetStatistics() {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(stats_mutex_);
        statistics_ = EngineStatistics{};
        ResetMemoryStats();
    }
    
    bool IsCodecSupported(CodecID codec) const {
        auto& video_codecs = capabilities_.supported_video_codecs;
        auto& audio_codecs = capabilities_.supported_audio_codecs;
        
        return std::find(video_codecs.begin(), video_codecs.end(), codec) != video_codecs.end() ||
               std::find(audio_codecs.begin(), audio_codecs.end(), codec) != audio_codecs.end();
    }
    
    bool IsContainerSupported(ContainerFormat format) const {
        // FFmpeg supports most containers
        switch (format) {
            case ContainerFormat::MP4:
            case ContainerFormat::MKV:
            case ContainerFormat::WebM:
            case ContainerFormat::AVI:
            case ContainerFormat::MOV:
            case ContainerFormat::FLV:
            case ContainerFormat::TS:
            case ContainerFormat::OGG:
            case ContainerFormat::WAV:
            case ContainerFormat::FLAC_File:
            case ContainerFormat::MP3_File:
                return true;
            default:
                return false;
        }
    }
    
    bool IsProtocolSupported(std::string_view protocol) const {
        auto& protocols = capabilities_.supported_protocols;
        return std::find(protocols.begin(), protocols.end(), std::string(protocol)) != protocols.end();
    }
    
    std::vector<std::string> GetSupportedExtensions() const {
        return {
            // Video
            "mp4", "mkv", "webm", "avi", "mov", "wmv", "flv", "m4v",
            "ts", "m2ts", "mts", "mpg", "mpeg", "vob", "ogv", "3gp",
            // Audio
            "mp3", "flac", "wav", "aac", "m4a", "ogg", "oga", "opus",
            "wma", "aiff", "ape", "wv", "mka",
            // Subtitles
            "srt", "ass", "ssa", "vtt", "sub", "idx"
        };
    }
    
    std::vector<std::string> GetSupportedMimeTypes() const {
        return {
            "video/mp4", "video/x-matroska", "video/webm", "video/x-msvideo",
            "video/quicktime", "video/x-flv", "video/mp2t",
            "audio/mpeg", "audio/flac", "audio/wav", "audio/aac",
            "audio/mp4", "audio/ogg", "audio/opus"
        };
    }
    
    Result<MediaInfo> ProbeMedia(std::string_view url) {
        AVFormatContext* format_ctx = nullptr;
        
        int ret = avformat_open_input(&format_ctx, std::string(url).c_str(), nullptr, nullptr);
        if (ret < 0) {
            char err_buf[AV_ERROR_MAX_STRING_SIZE];
            av_strerror(ret, err_buf, sizeof(err_buf));
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::DemuxerError, "Failed to open media: {}", err_buf);
        }
        
        ret = avformat_find_stream_info(format_ctx, nullptr);
        if (ret < 0) {
            avformat_close_input(&format_ctx);
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::DemuxerError, "Failed to find stream info");
        }
        
        MediaInfo info;
        info.url = std::string(url);
        
        if (format_ctx->iformat) {
            info.format_name = format_ctx->iformat->name ? format_ctx->iformat->name : "";
            info.format_long_name = format_ctx->iformat->long_name ? format_ctx->iformat->long_name : "";
        }
        
        if (format_ctx->duration != AV_NOPTS_VALUE) {
            info.duration = Timestamp::FromSeconds(
                static_cast<f64>(format_ctx->duration) / AV_TIME_BASE
            );
        }
        
        if (format_ctx->start_time != AV_NOPTS_VALUE) {
            info.start_time = Timestamp::FromSeconds(
                static_cast<f64>(format_ctx->start_time) / AV_TIME_BASE
            );
        }
        
        info.bitrate = format_ctx->bit_rate;
        
        // Parse metadata
        AVDictionaryEntry* tag = nullptr;
        while ((tag = av_dict_get(format_ctx->metadata, "", tag, AV_DICT_IGNORE_SUFFIX))) {
            info.metadata[tag->key] = tag->value;
            
            if (std::string(tag->key) == "title") info.title = tag->value;
            else if (std::string(tag->key) == "artist") info.artist = tag->value;
            else if (std::string(tag->key) == "album") info.album = tag->value;
            else if (std::string(tag->key) == "genre") info.genre = tag->value;
            else if (std::string(tag->key) == "date") info.date = tag->value;
        }
        
        // Parse streams
        for (u32 i = 0; i < format_ctx->nb_streams; ++i) {
            AVStream* stream = format_ctx->streams[i];
            AVCodecParameters* codec_par = stream->codecpar;
            
            switch (codec_par->codec_type) {
                case AVMEDIA_TYPE_VIDEO: {
                    VideoTrackInfo track;
                    track.index = static_cast<i32>(i);
                    track.codec = MapFFmpegCodec(codec_par->codec_id);
                    
                    const AVCodecDescriptor* desc = avcodec_descriptor_get(codec_par->codec_id);
                    if (desc) {
                        track.codec_name = desc->name;
                    }
                    
                    track.width = codec_par->width;
                    track.height = codec_par->height;
                    track.bitrate = codec_par->bit_rate;
                    track.pixel_format = MapFFmpegPixelFormat(static_cast<AVPixelFormat>(codec_par->format));
                    
                    if (stream->avg_frame_rate.den > 0) {
                        track.avg_frame_rate = av_q2d(stream->avg_frame_rate);
                    }
                    if (stream->r_frame_rate.den > 0) {
                        track.frame_rate = av_q2d(stream->r_frame_rate);
                    }
                    
                    track.color_space = MapFFmpegColorSpace(codec_par->color_space);
                    track.color_range = codec_par->color_range == AVCOL_RANGE_JPEG 
                        ? ColorRange::Full : ColorRange::Limited;
                    
                    // Check for rotation
                    AVDictionaryEntry* rotate = av_dict_get(stream->metadata, "rotate", nullptr, 0);
                    if (rotate) {
                        track.rotation = std::atoi(rotate->value);
                    }
                    
                    // Check disposition
                    if (stream->disposition & AV_DISPOSITION_DEFAULT) {
                        track.is_default = true;
                    }
                    
                    info.video_tracks.push_back(track);
                    info.has_video = true;
                    break;
                }
                
                case AVMEDIA_TYPE_AUDIO: {
                    AudioTrackInfo track;
                    track.index = static_cast<i32>(i);
                    track.codec = MapFFmpegCodec(codec_par->codec_id);
                    
                    const AVCodecDescriptor* desc = avcodec_descriptor_get(codec_par->codec_id);
                    if (desc) {
                        track.codec_name = desc->name;
                    }
                    
                    track.sample_rate = codec_par->sample_rate;
                    track.channels = codec_par->ch_layout.nb_channels;
                    track.bitrate = codec_par->bit_rate;
                    track.bits_per_sample = codec_par->bits_per_raw_sample;
                    track.sample_format = MapFFmpegSampleFormat(static_cast<AVSampleFormat>(codec_par->format));
                    
                    // Parse language
                    AVDictionaryEntry* lang = av_dict_get(stream->metadata, "language", nullptr, 0);
                    if (lang) {
                        track.language = lang->value;
                    }
                    
                    AVDictionaryEntry* title = av_dict_get(stream->metadata, "title", nullptr, 0);
                    if (title) {
                        track.title = title->value;
                    }
                    
                    if (stream->disposition & AV_DISPOSITION_DEFAULT) {
                        track.is_default = true;
                    }
                    
                    info.audio_tracks.push_back(track);
                    info.has_audio = true;
                    break;
                }
                
                case AVMEDIA_TYPE_SUBTITLE: {
                    SubtitleTrackInfo track;
                    track.index = static_cast<i32>(i);
                    track.codec = MapFFmpegCodec(codec_par->codec_id);
                    
                    const AVCodecDescriptor* desc = avcodec_descriptor_get(codec_par->codec_id);
                    if (desc) {
                        track.codec_name = desc->name;
                    }
                    
                    AVDictionaryEntry* lang = av_dict_get(stream->metadata, "language", nullptr, 0);
                    if (lang) {
                        track.language = lang->value;
                    }
                    
                    AVDictionaryEntry* title = av_dict_get(stream->metadata, "title", nullptr, 0);
                    if (title) {
                        track.title = title->value;
                    }
                    
                    if (stream->disposition & AV_DISPOSITION_DEFAULT) {
                        track.is_default = true;
                    }
                    if (stream->disposition & AV_DISPOSITION_FORCED) {
                        track.is_forced = true;
                    }
                    if (stream->disposition & AV_DISPOSITION_HEARING_IMPAIRED) {
                        track.is_sdh = true;
                    }
                    
                    info.subtitle_tracks.push_back(track);
                    info.has_subtitles = true;
                    break;
                }
                
                case AVMEDIA_TYPE_ATTACHMENT: {
                    AttachmentInfo attachment;
                    attachment.index = static_cast<i32>(i);
                    
                    AVDictionaryEntry* filename = av_dict_get(stream->metadata, "filename", nullptr, 0);
                    if (filename) {
                        attachment.filename = filename->value;
                    }
                    
                    AVDictionaryEntry* mimetype = av_dict_get(stream->metadata, "mimetype", nullptr, 0);
                    if (mimetype) {
                        attachment.mimetype = mimetype->value;
                    }
                    
                    if (codec_par->extradata_size > 0) {
                        attachment.size = codec_par->extradata_size;
                        attachment.data.assign(codec_par->extradata, 
                            codec_par->extradata + codec_par->extradata_size);
                        
                        // Check if it's cover art
                        if (attachment.mimetype.find("image/") == 0) {
                            info.cover_art = attachment.data;
                            info.cover_art_mimetype = attachment.mimetype;
                        }
                    }
                    
                    info.attachments.push_back(attachment);
                    break;
                }
                
                default:
                    break;
            }
        }
        
        // Parse chapters
        for (u32 i = 0; i < format_ctx->nb_chapters; ++i) {
            AVChapter* chapter = format_ctx->chapters[i];
            
            ChapterInfo ch;
            ch.index = static_cast<i32>(i);
            
            f64 time_base = av_q2d(chapter->time_base);
            ch.start = Timestamp::FromSeconds(chapter->start * time_base);
            ch.end = Timestamp::FromSeconds(chapter->end * time_base);
            
            AVDictionaryEntry* title = av_dict_get(chapter->metadata, "title", nullptr, 0);
            if (title) {
                ch.title = title->value;
            }
            
            info.chapters.push_back(ch);
        }
        
        // Check if live stream
        if (format_ctx->duration == AV_NOPTS_VALUE || 
            (format_ctx->pb && !format_ctx->pb->seekable)) {
            info.is_live = true;
            info.is_seekable = false;
        }
        
        avformat_close_input(&format_ctx);
        
        return info;
    }
    
private:
    Result<void> InitializeLogging() {
        logger_ = MakeUnique<Logger>("Engine");
        return logger_->Initialize(config_.log);
    }
    
    Result<void> InitializeFFmpeg() {
        // Register all codecs, demuxers, filters, etc.
        // Note: avcodec_register_all() etc. are deprecated in FFmpeg 4.0+
        // as registration is now automatic
        
        // Set log callback
        av_log_set_callback([](void* ptr, int level, const char* fmt, va_list vl) {
            if (level > AV_LOG_WARNING) return;
            
            char buf[1024];
            vsnprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), fmt, vl);
            
            // Remove trailing newline
            size_t len = strlen(buf);
            if (len > 0 && buf[len - 1] == '\n') {
                buf[len - 1] = '\0';
            }
            
            auto& engine = Engine::Instance();
            if (engine.IsInitialized()) {
                switch (level) {
                    case AV_LOG_ERROR:
                    case AV_LOG_FATAL:
                    case AV_LOG_PANIC:
                        engine.GetLogger().Error("[FFmpeg] {}", buf);
                        break;
                    case AV_LOG_WARNING:
                        engine.GetLogger().Warning("[FFmpeg] {}", buf);
                        break;
                    default:
                        engine.GetLogger().Debug("[FFmpeg] {}", buf);
                        break;
                }
            }
        });
        
        // Set log level
        av_log_set_level(config_.log.level <= LogConfig::Level::Debug ? AV_LOG_VERBOSE : AV_LOG_WARNING);
        
        logger_->Debug("FFmpeg version: {}", av_version_info());
        logger_->Debug("  libavcodec: {}.{}.{}", 
            LIBAVCODEC_VERSION_MAJOR, LIBAVCODEC_VERSION_MINOR, LIBAVCODEC_VERSION_MICRO);
        logger_->Debug("  libavformat: {}.{}.{}", 
            LIBAVFORMAT_VERSION_MAJOR, LIBAVFORMAT_VERSION_MINOR, LIBAVFORMAT_VERSION_MICRO);
        
        return {};
    }
    
    void ShutdownFFmpeg() {
        // Nothing specific needed for FFmpeg 4.0+
    }
    
    Result<void> InitializeNetwork() {
        // Network initialization is handled by FFmpeg for protocols
        // Additional initialization for curl, etc. would go here
        
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
        WSADATA wsa_data;
        if (WSAStartup(MAKEWORD(2, 2), &wsa_data) != 0) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::NetworkError, "WSAStartup failed");
        }
        #endif
        
        return {};
    }
    
    void ShutdownNetwork() {
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
        WSACleanup();
        #endif
    }
    
    Result<void> InitializePlugins() {
        // Plugin initialization
        logger_->Debug("Initializing plugin system...");
        return {};
    }
    
    void ShutdownPlugins() {
        // Plugin shutdown
    }
    
    Result<void> InitializeAI() {
        logger_->Debug("Initializing AI engine...");
        // AI initialization
        return {};
    }
    
    void ShutdownAI() {
        // AI shutdown
    }
    
    void DetectCapabilities() {
        logger_->Debug("Detecting system capabilities...");
        
        // Detect available codecs
        void* iter = nullptr;
        const AVCodec* codec;
        
        while ((codec = av_codec_iterate(&iter))) {
            CodecID id = MapFFmpegCodec(codec->id);
            if (id == CodecID::Unknown) continue;
            
            if (codec->type == AVMEDIA_TYPE_VIDEO) {
                if (av_codec_is_decoder(codec)) {
                    if (std::find(capabilities_.supported_video_codecs.begin(),
                                 capabilities_.supported_video_codecs.end(), id) == 
                        capabilities_.supported_video_codecs.end()) {
                        capabilities_.supported_video_codecs.push_back(id);
                    }
                }
            } else if (codec->type == AVMEDIA_TYPE_AUDIO) {
                if (av_codec_is_decoder(codec)) {
                    if (std::find(capabilities_.supported_audio_codecs.begin(),
                                 capabilities_.supported_audio_codecs.end(), id) == 
                        capabilities_.supported_audio_codecs.end()) {
                        capabilities_.supported_audio_codecs.push_back(id);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        
        // Detect hardware acceleration
        AVHWDeviceType hw_type = AV_HWDEVICE_TYPE_NONE;
        while ((hw_type = av_hwdevice_iterate_types(hw_type)) != AV_HWDEVICE_TYPE_NONE) {
            capabilities_.hardware_decoding = true;
            logger_->Debug("  HW decoder available: {}", av_hwdevice_get_type_name(hw_type));
        }
        
        // Detect protocols
        void* opaque = nullptr;
        const char* name;
        while ((name = avio_enum_protocols(&opaque, 0))) {
            capabilities_.supported_protocols.emplace_back(name);
        }
        opaque = nullptr;
        while ((name = avio_enum_protocols(&opaque, 1))) {
            if (std::find(capabilities_.supported_protocols.begin(),
                         capabilities_.supported_protocols.end(), name) == 
                capabilities_.supported_protocols.end()) {
                capabilities_.supported_protocols.emplace_back(name);
            }
        }
        
        // Detect rendering capabilities
        #ifdef AETHER_HAS_VULKAN
        capabilities_.vulkan_available = true;
        #endif
        
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE
        capabilities_.metal_available = true;
        #endif
        
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
        capabilities_.d3d11_available = true;
        capabilities_.d3d12_available = true;
        #endif
        
        capabilities_.opengl_available = true;
        
        // Set limits
        capabilities_.max_video_width = 16384;
        capabilities_.max_video_height = 16384;
        capabilities_.max_sample_rate = 384000;
        capabilities_.max_channels = 32;
    }
    
    static CodecID MapFFmpegCodec(AVCodecID id) {
        switch (id) {
            case AV_CODEC_ID_H264: return CodecID::H264;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_HEVC: return CodecID::H265;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_VP8: return CodecID::VP8;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_VP9: return CodecID::VP9;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_AV1: return CodecID::AV1;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_MPEG2VIDEO: return CodecID::MPEG2;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_MPEG4: return CodecID::MPEG4;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_MJPEG: return CodecID::MJPEG;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_PRORES: return CodecID::ProRes;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_THEORA: return CodecID::Theora;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_VC1: return CodecID::VC1;
            
            case AV_CODEC_ID_AAC: return CodecID::AAC;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_MP3: return CodecID::MP3;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_AC3: return CodecID::AC3;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_EAC3: return CodecID::EAC3;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_DTS: return CodecID::DTS;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_TRUEHD: return CodecID::TrueHD;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_FLAC: return CodecID::FLAC;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_ALAC: return CodecID::ALAC;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_VORBIS: return CodecID::Vorbis;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_OPUS: return CodecID::Opus;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_PCM_S16LE: return CodecID::PCM_S16LE;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_PCM_S24LE: return CodecID::PCM_S24LE;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_PCM_S32LE: return CodecID::PCM_S32LE;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_PCM_F32LE: return CodecID::PCM_F32LE;
            
            case AV_CODEC_ID_SUBRIP: return CodecID::SRT;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_ASS: return CodecID::ASS;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_WEBVTT: return CodecID::VTT;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_DVD_SUBTITLE: return CodecID::DVDSUB;
            case AV_CODEC_ID_HDMV_PGS_SUBTITLE: return CodecID::PGS;
            
            default: return CodecID::Unknown;
        }
    }
    
    static PixelFormat MapFFmpegPixelFormat(AVPixelFormat fmt) {
        switch (fmt) {
            case AV_PIX_FMT_YUV420P: return PixelFormat::YUV420P;
            case AV_PIX_FMT_YUV422P: return PixelFormat::YUV422P;
            case AV_PIX_FMT_YUV444P: return PixelFormat::YUV444P;
            case AV_PIX_FMT_YUV420P10LE: return PixelFormat::YUV420P10;
            case AV_PIX_FMT_NV12: return PixelFormat::NV12;
            case AV_PIX_FMT_NV21: return PixelFormat::NV21;
            case AV_PIX_FMT_P010LE: return PixelFormat::P010;
            case AV_PIX_FMT_RGB24: return PixelFormat::RGB24;
            case AV_PIX_FMT_BGR24: return PixelFormat::BGR24;
            case AV_PIX_FMT_RGBA: return PixelFormat::RGBA32;
            case AV_PIX_FMT_BGRA: return PixelFormat::BGRA32;
            case AV_PIX_FMT_GRAY8: return PixelFormat::Gray8;
            case AV_PIX_FMT_GRAY16LE: return PixelFormat::Gray16;
            default: return PixelFormat::Unknown;
        }
    }
    
    static SampleFormat MapFFmpegSampleFormat(AVSampleFormat fmt) {
        switch (fmt) {
            case AV_SAMPLE_FMT_U8: return SampleFormat::U8;
            case AV_SAMPLE_FMT_S16: return SampleFormat::S16;
            case AV_SAMPLE_FMT_S32: return SampleFormat::S32;
            case AV_SAMPLE_FMT_FLT: return SampleFormat::F32;
            case AV_SAMPLE_FMT_DBL: return SampleFormat::F64;
            case AV_SAMPLE_FMT_U8P: return SampleFormat::U8P;
            case AV_SAMPLE_FMT_S16P: return SampleFormat::S16P;
            case AV_SAMPLE_FMT_S32P: return SampleFormat::S32P;
            case AV_SAMPLE_FMT_FLTP: return SampleFormat::F32P;
            case AV_SAMPLE_FMT_DBLP: return SampleFormat::F64P;
            default: return SampleFormat::Unknown;
        }
    }
    
    static ColorSpace MapFFmpegColorSpace(AVColorSpace cs) {
        switch (cs) {
            case AVCOL_SPC_BT709: return ColorSpace::BT709;
            case AVCOL_SPC_BT470BG: return ColorSpace::BT601;
            case AVCOL_SPC_SMPTE170M: return ColorSpace::SMPTE170M;
            case AVCOL_SPC_SMPTE240M: return ColorSpace::SMPTE240M;
            case AVCOL_SPC_BT2020_NCL: return ColorSpace::BT2020_NCL;
            case AVCOL_SPC_BT2020_CL: return ColorSpace::BT2020_CL;
            default: return ColorSpace::Unknown;
        }
    }
    
private:
    mutable std::mutex mutex_;
    mutable std::mutex stats_mutex_;
    
    std::atomic<bool> initialized_{false};
    
    Config config_;
    EngineCapabilities capabilities_;
    EngineStatistics statistics_;
    
    Unique<Logger> logger_;
    Unique<ThreadPool> thread_pool_;
    
    std::unordered_map<u64, Shared<Player>> players_;
    u64 next_player_id_ = 1;
    
    SteadyTimePoint start_time_;
    
    std::unordered_map<std::string, ProtocolHandler> custom_protocols_;
    std::vector<std::pair<u64, GlobalEventCallback>> global_listeners_;
    u64 next_listener_id_ = 1;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Engine Implementation Wrappers
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Engine& Engine::Instance() {
    static Engine instance;
    return instance;
}

Engine::Engine() : impl_(MakeUnique<Impl>()) {}
Engine::~Engine() = default;

Result<void> Engine::Initialize(const Config& config) {
    return impl_->Initialize(config);
}

void Engine::Shutdown() {
    impl_->Shutdown();
}

bool Engine::IsInitialized() const noexcept {
    return impl_->IsInitialized();
}

const Config& Engine::GetConfig() const noexcept {
    return impl_->GetConfig();
}

Result<void> Engine::UpdateConfig(const Config& config) {
    return impl_->UpdateConfig(config);
}

Result<Shared<Player>> Engine::CreatePlayer(std::string_view name) {
    return impl_->CreatePlayer(name);
}

void Engine::DestroyPlayer(Shared<Player>& player) {
    impl_->DestroyPlayer(player);
}

std::vector<Shared<Player>> Engine::GetPlayers() const {
    return impl_->GetPlayers();
}

Shared<Player> Engine::GetPlayer(std::string_view name) const {
    return impl_->GetPlayer(name);
}

Logger& Engine::GetLogger() {
    return impl_->GetLogger();
}

const EngineCapabilities& Engine::GetCapabilities() const {
    return impl_->GetCapabilities();
}

EngineStatistics Engine::GetStatistics() const {
    return impl_->GetStatistics();
}

void Engine::ResetStatistics() {
    impl_->ResetStatistics();
}

bool Engine::IsCodecSupported(CodecID codec) const {
    return impl_->IsCodecSupported(codec);
}

bool Engine::IsContainerSupported(ContainerFormat format) const {
    return impl_->IsContainerSupported(format);
}

bool Engine::IsProtocolSupported(std::string_view protocol) const {
    return impl_->IsProtocolSupported(protocol);
}

std::vector<std::string> Engine::GetSupportedExtensions() const {
    return impl_->GetSupportedExtensions();
}

std::vector<std::string> Engine::GetSupportedMimeTypes() const {
    return impl_->GetSupportedMimeTypes();
}

Result<MediaInfo> Engine::ProbeMedia(std::string_view url) {
    return impl_->ProbeMedia(url);
}

const char* Engine::GetVersion() {
    return VERSION_STRING.data();
}

BuildInfo Engine::GetBuildInfo() {
    return BuildInfo{
        .version_major = VERSION_MAJOR,
        .version_minor = VERSION_MINOR,
        .version_patch = VERSION_PATCH,
        .version_string = VERSION_STRING.data(),
        .codename = CODENAME.data(),
        .build_type = BUILD_TYPE.data(),
        .git_commit = GIT_COMMIT_HASH,
        .git_branch = GIT_BRANCH,
        .build_date = BUILD_DATE,
        .build_time = BUILD_TIME,
        .compiler = 
            #if defined(__clang__)
            "Clang",
            #elif defined(__GNUC__)
            "GCC",
            #elif defined(_MSC_VER)
            "MSVC",
            #else
            "Unknown",
            #endif
        .compiler_version = 
            #if defined(__clang__)
            __clang_version__,
            #elif defined(__GNUC__)
            __VERSION__,
            #elif defined(_MSC_VER)
            "19.x",
            #else
            "Unknown",
            #endif
        .platform = AETHER_PLATFORM_NAME,
        .architecture = AETHER_ARCH_NAME,
        #ifdef AETHER_HAS_VULKAN
        .has_vulkan = true,
        #else
        .has_vulkan = false,
        #endif
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE
        .has_metal = true,
        #else
        .has_metal = false,
        #endif
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
        .has_d3d12 = true,
        #else
        .has_d3d12 = false,
        #endif
        .has_opengl = true,
        .has_cuda = false,
        .has_opencl = false,
        .has_tensorrt = false,
        .has_coreml = false,
        .has_onnxruntime = false,
        .has_ffmpeg = true,
        .has_drm_support = false
    };
}

// Global initialization functions
bool Initialize(const Config& config) {
    return Engine::Instance().Initialize(config).has_value();
}

void Shutdown() {
    Engine::Instance().Shutdown();
}

const char* GetVersionString() {
    return Engine::GetVersion();
}

BuildInfo GetBuildInfo() {
    return Engine::GetBuildInfo();
}

} // namespace aether
```

## Part 7: Player Implementation

```cpp
#pragma once

// Player Options

struct PlayerOptions {
    // Video
    bool enable_hardware_decoding = true;
    std::string preferred_hw_decoder;
    std::string preferred_renderer = "auto";
    PixelFormat preferred_output_format = PixelFormat::Unknown;
    ScaleMode scale_mode = ScaleMode::Fit;
    bool enable_deinterlacing = true;
    DeinterlaceMode deinterlace_mode = DeinterlaceMode::Auto;
    bool enable_hdr = true;
    
    // Audio
    SampleFormat preferred_sample_format = SampleFormat::F32;
    ChannelLayout preferred_channel_layout = ChannelLayout::Unknown;
    u32 preferred_sample_rate = 0;
    f32 initial_volume = 1.0f;
    bool audio_exclusive_mode = false;
    std::string preferred_audio_device;
    
    // Playback
    f32 initial_speed = 1.0f;
    LoopMode loop_mode = LoopMode::None;
    SeekMode seek_mode = SeekMode::Precise;
    bool pause_on_buffer = true;
    bool auto_play = false;
    
    // Buffering
    u32 video_buffer_size = 8;
    u32 audio_buffer_ms = 200;
    u32 network_buffer_ms = 5000;
    u32 seek_buffer_ms = 2000;
    
    // AI
    bool enable_ai_upscaling = false;
    f32 ai_upscale_factor = 2.0f;
    std::string ai_upscale_model = "default";
    bool enable_ai_frame_interpolation = false;
    f32 ai_target_fps = 60.0f;
    bool enable_ai_audio_enhancement = false;
    
    // Subtitles
    bool auto_select_subtitle = true;
    std::string preferred_subtitle_language;
    bool enable_subtitle_rendering = true;
    
    // Network
    std::string user_agent;
    std::map<std::string, std::string> http_headers;
    std::string proxy_url;
    
    // Misc
    bool enable_analytics = false;
    std::map<std::string, std::string> custom_options;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Playback Status
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct PlayerStatistics {
    // Decoding
    u64 frames_decoded = 0;
    u64 frames_rendered = 0;
    u64 frames_dropped = 0;
    f64 decode_fps = 0;
    f64 render_fps = 0;
    f64 avg_decode_time_ms = 0;
    f64 avg_render_time_ms = 0;
    
    // Audio
    u64 samples_decoded = 0;
    u64 samples_rendered = 0;
    f64 audio_latency_ms = 0;
    
    // Sync
    f64 av_sync_diff_ms = 0;
    u64 sync_corrections = 0;
    
    // Network
    u64 bytes_downloaded = 0;
    f64 download_speed_mbps = 0;
    f64 buffer_health = 0;
    
    // AI processing
    u64 frames_upscaled = 0;
    u64 frames_interpolated = 0;
    f64 ai_processing_time_ms = 0;
};

struct PlaybackStatus {
    PlaybackState state = PlaybackState::Idle;
    
    Timestamp position;
    Timestamp duration;
    f64 progress = 0;  // 0.0 to 1.0
    
    f32 volume = 1.0f;
    bool is_muted = false;
    f32 speed = 1.0f;
    
    i32 video_track = -1;
    i32 audio_track = -1;
    i32 subtitle_track = -1;
    
    f64 buffer_percent = 0;
    bool is_buffering = false;
    
    Size video_size;
    f64 video_fps = 0;
    u32 audio_sample_rate = 0;
    u32 audio_channels = 0;
    
    PlayerStatistics statistics;
    Error last_error;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Player Class
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API Player : public std::enable_shared_from_this<Player> {
public:
    explicit Player(std::string name);
    ~Player();
    
    Player(const Player&) = delete;
    Player& operator=(const Player&) = delete;
    
    // Initialization
    Result<void> Initialize(const PlayerOptions& options = PlayerOptions{});
    void Shutdown();
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Playback Control

    Result<void> Open(std::string_view url);
    Result<void> Open(std::string_view url, const PlayerOptions& options);
    void Close();
    
    Result<void> Play();

    Result<void> TogglePlayPause();

    Result<void> StepBackward();
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // State

    [[nodiscard]] PlaybackState GetState() const;
    [[nodiscard]] PlaybackStatus GetStatus() const;
    [[nodiscard]] PlayerStatistics GetStatistics() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::optional<MediaInfo> GetMediaInfo() const;

    [[nodiscard]] Timestamp GetDuration() const;
    [[nodiscard]] f64 GetPositionSeconds() const;
    [[nodiscard]] f64 GetDurationSeconds() const;
    [[nodiscard]] f64 GetProgress() const;

    [[nodiscard]] bool IsStopped() const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsSeeking() const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsBuffering() const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsLive() const;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Volume & Audio
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    Result<void> SetVolume(f32 volume);
    [[nodiscard]] f32 GetVolume() const;

    [[nodiscard]] i32 GetAudioDelay() const;
    
    Result<void> SetAudioDevice(std::string_view device_id);
    [[nodiscard]] std::string GetAudioDevice() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> GetAudioDevices() const;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    // Track Selection

    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<AudioTrackInfo> GetAudioTracks() const;

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Video Output
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    Result<void> SetVideoOutput(void* window_handle);
    Result<void> SetVideoRenderer(Shared<VideoRenderer> renderer);
    Result<void> DetachVideoOutput();
    
    [[nodiscard]] Size GetVideoSize() const;

    Result<void> SetHue(f32 value);

    Result<void> ResetVideoAdjustments();
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Filters
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    Result<void> SetAudioFilter(std::string_view filter);

    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<f32> GetEqualizerBands() const;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // AI Enhancement
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    Result<void> SetAITargetFPS(f32 fps);
    
    Result<void> EnableAIAudioEnhancement(bool enable);
    Result<void> EnableAIDenoising(bool enable);
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Loop & Chapters
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```
