    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Screenshots & Recording
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    Result<std::vector<u8>> CaptureFrame(std::string_view format = "png");
    Result<std::string> SaveScreenshot(std::string_view path = "");
    
    Result<void> StartRecording(std::string_view path);
    Result<void> StopRecording();
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsRecording() const;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Playlist
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    Result<void> AddToPlaylist(std::string_view url);
    Result<void> AddToPlaylist(const std::vector<std::string>& urls);
    Result<void> InsertInPlaylist(i32 index, std::string_view url);
    Result<void> RemoveFromPlaylist(i32 index);
    void ClearPlaylist();
    void ShufflePlaylist();
    
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::string> GetPlaylist() const;
    [[nodiscard]] i32 GetPlaylistIndex() const;
    [[nodiscard]] i32 GetPlaylistCount() const;
    
    Result<void> PlaylistNext();
    Result<void> PlaylistPrevious();
    Result<void> PlaylistPlayAt(i32 index);
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Events
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    using EventCallback = std::function<void(const PlayerEvent&)>;
    u64 AddEventListener(EventCallback callback);
    void RemoveEventListener(u64 id);
    
    template<typename E>
    u64 On(std::function<void(const E&)> callback) {
        return AddEventListener([callback = std::move(callback)](const PlayerEvent& event) {
            if (auto* e = std::get_if<E>(&event)) {
                callback(*e);
            }
        });
    }
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Misc
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    [[nodiscard]] std::string_view GetName() const;
    [[nodiscard]] const PlayerOptions& GetOptions() const;
    [[nodiscard]] Error GetLastError() const;
    
    Result<std::string> ExecuteCommand(std::string_view command);
    
private:
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_CORE_PLAYER_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/core/event_system.hpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#pragma once

#ifndef AETHER_CORE_EVENT_SYSTEM_HPP
#define AETHER_CORE_EVENT_SYSTEM_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"
#include "aether/core/error.hpp"

#include <variant>
#include <string>
#include <functional>
#include <memory>

namespace aether {

// Forward declarations
struct MediaInfo;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Event Types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct EventBase {
    TimePoint timestamp = Clock::now();
    std::string source;
};

struct StateChangedEvent : EventBase {
    PlaybackState old_state = PlaybackState::Idle;
    PlaybackState new_state = PlaybackState::Idle;
};

struct MediaOpeningEvent : EventBase {
    std::string url;
};

struct MediaOpenedEvent : EventBase {
    std::string url;
    // MediaInfo is too large to include directly, use shared_ptr
    Shared<MediaInfo> media_info;
};

struct MediaClosedEvent : EventBase {
    std::string url;
};

struct MediaEndedEvent : EventBase {
    std::string url;
    bool natural_end = true;
};

struct PositionChangedEvent : EventBase {
    Timestamp position;
    Timestamp duration;
    f64 progress = 0;
};

struct SeekingEvent : EventBase {
    Timestamp from;
    Timestamp to;
};

struct SeekedEvent : EventBase {
    Timestamp position;
    bool success = true;
};

struct BufferingStartEvent : EventBase {
    f64 buffer_percent = 0;
};

struct BufferingProgressEvent : EventBase {
    f64 buffer_percent = 0;
    Timestamp buffered_duration;
};

struct BufferingEndEvent : EventBase {
    f64 buffer_percent = 100;
};

struct VolumeChangedEvent : EventBase {
    f32 old_volume = 0;
    f32 new_volume = 0;
    bool muted = false;
};

struct SpeedChangedEvent : EventBase {
    f32 old_speed = 1.0f;
    f32 new_speed = 1.0f;
};

struct TrackChangedEvent : EventBase {
    MediaType track_type = MediaType::Unknown;
    i32 old_index = -1;
    i32 new_index = -1;
};

struct ChapterChangedEvent : EventBase {
    i32 old_chapter = -1;
    i32 new_chapter = -1;
    std::string chapter_title;
};

struct SubtitleTextEvent : EventBase {
    std::string text;
    Timestamp start;
    Timestamp end;
    i32 track_index = -1;
};

struct ErrorEvent : EventBase {
    Error error;
    bool fatal = false;
};

struct WarningEvent : EventBase {
    std::string message;
    i32 code = 0;
};

struct VideoSizeChangedEvent : EventBase {
    Size old_size;
    Size new_size;
};

struct AudioDeviceChangedEvent : EventBase {
    std::string old_device;
    std::string new_device;
};

struct PlaylistChangedEvent : EventBase {
    enum class Action { Added, Removed, Cleared, Shuffled, IndexChanged };
    Action action = Action::Added;
    i32 index = -1;
    std::string url;
};

struct RecordingStartedEvent : EventBase {
    std::string path;
};

struct RecordingStoppedEvent : EventBase {
    std::string path;
    Timestamp duration;
    i64 file_size = 0;
};

struct SnapshotTakenEvent : EventBase {
    std::string path;
};

struct MetadataUpdatedEvent : EventBase {
    std::string key;
    std::string value;
};

struct NetworkEvent : EventBase {
    enum class Type { Connected, Disconnected, SpeedChanged, Error };
    Type type = Type::Connected;
    std::string url;
    f64 download_speed = 0;
    Error error;
};

struct AIProcessingEvent : EventBase {
    enum class Type { Started, Progress, Completed, Error };
    Type type = Type::Started;
    std::string processor;
    f64 progress = 0;
    Duration processing_time;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Event Variant
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

using PlayerEvent = std::variant<
    StateChangedEvent,
    MediaOpeningEvent,
    MediaOpenedEvent,
    MediaClosedEvent,
    MediaEndedEvent,
    PositionChangedEvent,
    SeekingEvent,
    SeekedEvent,
    BufferingStartEvent,
    BufferingProgressEvent,
    BufferingEndEvent,
    VolumeChangedEvent,
    SpeedChangedEvent,
    TrackChangedEvent,
    ChapterChangedEvent,
    SubtitleTextEvent,
    ErrorEvent,
    WarningEvent,
    VideoSizeChangedEvent,
    AudioDeviceChangedEvent,
    PlaylistChangedEvent,
    RecordingStartedEvent,
    RecordingStoppedEvent,
    SnapshotTakenEvent,
    MetadataUpdatedEvent,
    NetworkEvent,
    AIProcessingEvent
>;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Event Dispatcher
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API EventDispatcher {
public:
    using Callback = std::function<void(const PlayerEvent&)>;
    
    EventDispatcher();
    ~EventDispatcher();
    
    u64 Subscribe(Callback callback);
    
    template<typename E>
    u64 Subscribe(std::function<void(const E&)> callback) {
        return Subscribe([callback = std::move(callback)](const PlayerEvent& event) {
            if (auto* e = std::get_if<E>(&event)) {
                callback(*e);
            }
        });
    }
    
    void Unsubscribe(u64 id);
    void Clear();
    
    template<typename E, typename... Args>
    void Dispatch(Args&&... args) {
        E event{std::forward<Args>(args)...};
        event.timestamp = Clock::now();
        DispatchEvent(event);
    }
    
    void DispatchEvent(const PlayerEvent& event);
    void DispatchAsync(PlayerEvent event);
    
    void ProcessPending();
    
    [[nodiscard]] usize SubscriberCount() const;
    
private:
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Event Utility Functions
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

[[nodiscard]] AETHER_API std::string_view GetEventName(const PlayerEvent& event);

} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_CORE_EVENT_SYSTEM_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: src/core/player/player.cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#include "aether/core/player.hpp"
#include "aether/core/engine.hpp"
#include "aether/utils/logging.hpp"
#include "aether/utils/threading.hpp"

#include <mutex>
#include <atomic>
#include <thread>
#include <condition_variable>
#include <queue>

// FFmpeg headers
extern "C" {
#include <libavcodec/avcodec.h>
#include <libavformat/avformat.h>
#include <libavutil/avutil.h>
#include <libavutil/imgutils.h>
#include <libavutil/time.h>
#include <libswscale/swscale.h>
#include <libswresample/swresample.h>
}

namespace aether {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Player Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class Player::Impl {
public:
    explicit Impl(std::string name) : name_(std::move(name)) {}
    
    ~Impl() {
        Shutdown();
    }
    
    Result<void> Initialize(const PlayerOptions& options) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        if (initialized_) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::AlreadyInitialized, "Player already initialized");
        }
        
        options_ = options;
        volume_ = options.initial_volume;
        speed_ = options.initial_speed;
        loop_mode_ = options.loop_mode;
        
        dispatcher_ = MakeUnique<EventDispatcher>();
        
        initialized_ = true;
        SetState(PlaybackState::Idle);
        
        GetLogger().Debug("Player '{}' initialized", name_);
        
        return {};
    }
    
    void Shutdown() {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        if (!initialized_) {
            return;
        }
        
        Close();
        
        dispatcher_.reset();
        initialized_ = false;
        
        GetLogger().Debug("Player '{}' shutdown", name_);
    }
    
    Result<void> Open(std::string_view url, const PlayerOptions& options) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        if (!initialized_) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::NotInitialized, "Player not initialized");
        }
        
        // Close any existing media
        CloseInternal();
        
        options_ = options;
        current_url_ = std::string(url);
        
        SetState(PlaybackState::Opening);
        DispatchEvent(MediaOpeningEvent{.url = current_url_});
        
        GetLogger().Info("Opening media: {}", url);
        
        // Open format context
        AVFormatContext* fmt_ctx = nullptr;
        AVDictionary* format_opts = nullptr;
        
        // Set options
        if (!options.user_agent.empty()) {
            av_dict_set(&format_opts, "user_agent", options.user_agent.c_str(), 0);
        }
        
        for (const auto& [key, value] : options.http_headers) {
            std::string header = key + ": " + value;
            av_dict_set(&format_opts, "headers", header.c_str(), AV_DICT_APPEND);
        }
        
        // Network timeout
        av_dict_set(&format_opts, "timeout", "30000000", 0);  // 30 seconds in microseconds
        
        int ret = avformat_open_input(&fmt_ctx, current_url_.c_str(), nullptr, &format_opts);
        av_dict_free(&format_opts);
        
        if (ret < 0) {
            char err_buf[AV_ERROR_MAX_STRING_SIZE];
            av_strerror(ret, err_buf, sizeof(err_buf));
            
            SetState(PlaybackState::Error);
            last_error_ = Error::Make(ErrorCode::DemuxerError, "Failed to open: {}", err_buf);
            DispatchEvent(ErrorEvent{.error = last_error_, .fatal = true});
            
            return last_error_;
        }
        
        format_ctx_ = fmt_ctx;
        
        // Find stream info
        ret = avformat_find_stream_info(format_ctx_, nullptr);
        if (ret < 0) {
            CloseInternal();
            SetState(PlaybackState::Error);
            last_error_ = Error::Make(ErrorCode::DemuxerError, "Failed to find stream info");
            return last_error_;
        }
        
        // Build media info
        if (auto result = BuildMediaInfo(); !result) {
            CloseInternal();
            return result.error();
        }
        
        // Find best streams
        video_stream_index_ = av_find_best_stream(format_ctx_, AVMEDIA_TYPE_VIDEO, -1, -1, nullptr, 0);
        audio_stream_index_ = av_find_best_stream(format_ctx_, AVMEDIA_TYPE_AUDIO, -1, -1, nullptr, 0);
        subtitle_stream_index_ = -1;
        
        if (options.auto_select_subtitle) {
            subtitle_stream_index_ = av_find_best_stream(format_ctx_, AVMEDIA_TYPE_SUBTITLE, -1, -1, nullptr, 0);
        }
        
        // Open decoders
        if (video_stream_index_ >= 0) {
            if (auto result = OpenVideoDecoder(video_stream_index_); !result) {
                GetLogger().Warn("Failed to open video decoder: {}", result.error().Message());
            }
        }
        
        if (audio_stream_index_ >= 0) {
            if (auto result = OpenAudioDecoder(audio_stream_index_); !result) {
                GetLogger().Warn("Failed to open audio decoder: {}", result.error().Message());
            }
        }
        
        // Initialize timing
        if (format_ctx_->duration != AV_NOPTS_VALUE) {
            duration_ = Timestamp::FromSeconds(
                static_cast<f64>(format_ctx_->duration) / AV_TIME_BASE
            );
        }
        position_ = Timestamp{};
        
        // Start threads
        running_ = true;
        demux_thread_ = std::thread(&Impl::DemuxThread, this);
        decode_video_thread_ = std::thread(&Impl::DecodeVideoThread, this);
        decode_audio_thread_ = std::thread(&Impl::DecodeAudioThread, this);
        
        SetState(PlaybackState::Ready);
        
        auto media_info_ptr = MakeShared<MediaInfo>(media_info_);
        DispatchEvent(MediaOpenedEvent{
            .url = current_url_,
            .media_info = media_info_ptr
        });
        
        GetLogger().Info("Media opened: {} ({:.2f}s)", 
            media_info_.title.empty() ? current_url_ : media_info_.title,
            duration_.ToSeconds());
        
        // Auto-play if enabled
        if (options.auto_play) {
            return Play();
        }
        
        return {};
    }
    
    void Close() {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        CloseInternal();
    }
    
    void CloseInternal() {
        if (state_ == PlaybackState::Idle) {
            return;
        }
        
        GetLogger().Debug("Closing media");
        
        // Stop threads
        running_ = false;
        packet_queue_cv_.notify_all();
        video_frame_cv_.notify_all();
        audio_frame_cv_.notify_all();
        
        if (demux_thread_.joinable()) {
            demux_thread_.join();
        }
        if (decode_video_thread_.joinable()) {
            decode_video_thread_.join();
        }
        if (decode_audio_thread_.joinable()) {
            decode_audio_thread_.join();
        }
        
        // Clear queues
        ClearQueues();
        
        // Close decoders
        if (video_codec_ctx_) {
            avcodec_free_context(&video_codec_ctx_);
        }
        if (audio_codec_ctx_) {
            avcodec_free_context(&audio_codec_ctx_);
        }
        
        // Close format context
        if (format_ctx_) {
            avformat_close_input(&format_ctx_);
        }
        
        // Reset state
        video_stream_index_ = -1;
        audio_stream_index_ = -1;
        subtitle_stream_index_ = -1;
        position_ = Timestamp{};
        duration_ = Timestamp{};
        media_info_ = MediaInfo{};
        
        std::string url = current_url_;
        current_url_.clear();
        
        SetState(PlaybackState::Idle);
        DispatchEvent(MediaClosedEvent{.url = url});
    }
    
    Result<void> Play() {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        if (state_ == PlaybackState::Idle) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "No media loaded");
        }
        
        if (state_ == PlaybackState::Playing) {
            return {};
        }
        
        if (state_ == PlaybackState::Error) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "Player in error state");
        }
        
        paused_ = false;
        
        // Update start time for synchronization
        if (state_ == PlaybackState::Paused || state_ == PlaybackState::Ready) {
            pause_time_ = Clock::now();
            start_time_ = pause_time_ - std::chrono::microseconds(
                static_cast<i64>(position_.ToMicroseconds() / speed_)
            );
        }
        
        SetState(PlaybackState::Playing);
        
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<void> Pause() {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        if (state_ != PlaybackState::Playing) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "Not playing");
        }
        
        paused_ = true;
        pause_time_ = Clock::now();
        
        SetState(PlaybackState::Paused);
        
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<void> TogglePlayPause() {
        if (state_ == PlaybackState::Playing) {
            return Pause();
        } else {
            return Play();
        }
    }
    
    Result<void> Stop() {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        if (state_ == PlaybackState::Idle) {
            return {};
        }
        
        // Seek to beginning
        SeekInternal(Timestamp{});
        
        paused_ = true;
        SetState(PlaybackState::Stopped);
        
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<void> Seek(Timestamp position) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        return SeekInternal(position);
    }
    
    Result<void> SeekInternal(Timestamp target) {
        if (state_ == PlaybackState::Idle) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "No media loaded");
        }
        
        if (!media_info_.is_seekable) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::NotSupported, "Media is not seekable");
        }
        
        // Clamp to valid range
        f64 target_sec = std::clamp(target.ToSeconds(), 0.0, duration_.ToSeconds());
        target = Timestamp::FromSeconds(target_sec);
        
        PlaybackState prev_state = state_;
        SetState(PlaybackState::Seeking);
        
        DispatchEvent(SeekingEvent{
            .from = position_,
            .to = target
        });
        
        seeking_ = true;
        
        // Seek in format context
        i64 seek_target = static_cast<i64>(target.ToSeconds() * AV_TIME_BASE);
        int flags = AVSEEK_FLAG_BACKWARD;
        
        if (options_.seek_mode == SeekMode::Fast || options_.seek_mode == SeekMode::Keyframe) {
            // Seek to keyframe
        } else {
            // Precise seek - seek before target then decode forward
            flags |= AVSEEK_FLAG_ANY;
        }
        
        int ret = av_seek_frame(format_ctx_, -1, seek_target, flags);
        
        if (ret < 0) {
            seeking_ = false;
            SetState(prev_state);
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::SeekError, "Seek failed");
        }
        
        // Flush decoder buffers
        if (video_codec_ctx_) {
            avcodec_flush_buffers(video_codec_ctx_);
        }
        if (audio_codec_ctx_) {
            avcodec_flush_buffers(audio_codec_ctx_);
        }
        
        // Clear queues
        ClearQueues();
        
        position_ = target;
        
        // Update start time
        start_time_ = Clock::now() - std::chrono::microseconds(
            static_cast<i64>(position_.ToMicroseconds() / speed_)
        );
        
        seeking_ = false;
        
        SetState(prev_state == PlaybackState::Seeking ? PlaybackState::Playing : prev_state);
        
        DispatchEvent(SeekedEvent{
            .position = position_,
            .success = true
        });
        
        return {};
    }
    
    PlaybackState GetState() const {
        return state_.load();
    }
    
    PlaybackStatus GetStatus() const {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        PlaybackStatus status;
        status.state = state_.load();
        status.position = position_;
        status.duration = duration_;
        status.progress = duration_.pts > 0 
            ? static_cast<f64>(position_.pts) / duration_.pts 
            : 0.0;
        status.volume = volume_;
        status.is_muted = muted_;
        status.speed = speed_;
        status.video_track = video_stream_index_;
        status.audio_track = audio_stream_index_;
        status.subtitle_track = subtitle_stream_index_;
        status.is_buffering = buffering_;
        status.last_error = last_error_;
        status.statistics = statistics_;
        
        if (video_codec_ctx_) {
            status.video_size = Size{
                static_cast<i32>(video_codec_ctx_->width),
                static_cast<i32>(video_codec_ctx_->height)
            };
            
            if (video_stream_index_ >= 0) {
                AVStream* stream = format_ctx_->streams[video_stream_index_];
                if (stream->avg_frame_rate.den > 0) {
                    status.video_fps = av_q2d(stream->avg_frame_rate);
                }
            }
        }
        
        if (audio_codec_ctx_) {
            status.audio_sample_rate = audio_codec_ctx_->sample_rate;
            status.audio_channels = audio_codec_ctx_->ch_layout.nb_channels;
        }
        
        return status;
    }
    
    PlayerStatistics GetStatistics() const {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(stats_mutex_);
        return statistics_;
    }
    
    std::optional<MediaInfo> GetMediaInfo() const {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        
        if (state_ == PlaybackState::Idle) {
            return std::nullopt;
        }
        
        return media_info_;
    }
    
    Timestamp GetPosition() const {
        return position_;
    }
    
    Timestamp GetDuration() const {
        return duration_;
    }
    
    // Volume control
    Result<void> SetVolume(f32 volume) {
        volume_ = std::clamp(volume, 0.0f, 2.0f);
        
        f32 old_volume = volume_.exchange(volume);
        
        if (old_volume != volume) {
            DispatchEvent(VolumeChangedEvent{
                .old_volume = old_volume,
                .new_volume = volume,
                .muted = muted_.load()
            });
        }
        
        return {};
    }
    
    f32 GetVolume() const {
        return volume_.load();
    }
    
    Result<void> SetMuted(bool muted) {
        bool old_muted = muted_.exchange(muted);
        
        if (old_muted != muted) {
            DispatchEvent(VolumeChangedEvent{
                .old_volume = volume_.load(),
                .new_volume = volume_.load(),
                .muted = muted
            });
        }
        
        return {};
    }
    
    bool IsMuted() const {
        return muted_.load();
    }
    
    // Speed control
    Result<void> SetSpeed(f32 speed) {
        speed = std::clamp(speed, 0.25f, 4.0f);
        
        f32 old_speed = speed_;
        speed_ = speed;
        
        // Adjust start time to maintain position
        if (state_ == PlaybackState::Playing) {
            start_time_ = Clock::now() - std::chrono::microseconds(
                static_cast<i64>(position_.ToMicroseconds() / speed_)
            );
        }
        
        if (old_speed != speed) {
            DispatchEvent(SpeedChangedEvent{
                .old_speed = old_speed,
                .new_speed = speed
            });
        }
        
        return {};
    }
    
    f32 GetSpeed() const {
        return speed_;
    }
    
    // Event handling
    u64 AddEventListener(EventDispatcher::Callback callback) {
        if (dispatcher_) {
            return dispatcher_->Subscribe(std::move(callback));
        }
        return 0;
    }
    
    void RemoveEventListener(u64 id) {
        if (dispatcher_) {
            dispatcher_->Unsubscribe(id);
        }
    }
    
    const std::string& GetName() const {
        return name_;
    }
    
    const PlayerOptions& GetOptions() const {
        return options_;
    }
    
    Error GetLastError() const {
        return last_error_;
    }
    
private:
    void SetState(PlaybackState state) {
        PlaybackState old_state = state_.exchange(state);
        
        if (old_state != state) {
            DispatchEvent(StateChangedEvent{
                .old_state = old_state,
                .new_state = state
            });
        }
    }
    
    template<typename E>
    void DispatchEvent(E&& event) {
        if (dispatcher_) {
            event.source = name_;
            dispatcher_->DispatchEvent(std::forward<E>(event));
        }
    }
    
    Result<void> BuildMediaInfo() {
        media_info_.url = current_url_;
        
        if (format_ctx_->iformat) {
            media_info_.format_name = format_ctx_->iformat->name ? format_ctx_->iformat->name : "";
        }
        
        if (format_ctx_->duration != AV_NOPTS_VALUE) {
            media_info_.duration = Timestamp::FromSeconds(
                static_cast<f64>(format_ctx_->duration) / AV_TIME_BASE
            );
        }
        
        media_info_.bitrate = format_ctx_->bit_rate;
        
        // Parse streams (similar to ProbeMedia in Engine)
        for (u32 i = 0; i < format_ctx_->nb_streams; ++i) {
            AVStream* stream = format_ctx_->streams[i];
            AVCodecParameters* par = stream->codecpar;
            
            switch (par->codec_type) {
                case AVMEDIA_TYPE_VIDEO: {
                    VideoTrackInfo track;
                    track.index = static_cast<i32>(i);
                    track.width = par->width;
                    track.height = par->height;
                    track.bitrate = par->bit_rate;
                    
                    if (stream->avg_frame_rate.den > 0) {
                        track.avg_frame_rate = av_q2d(stream->avg_frame_rate);
                    }
                    
                    media_info_.video_tracks.push_back(track);
                    media_info_.has_video = true;
                    break;
                }
                
                case AVMEDIA_TYPE_AUDIO: {
                    AudioTrackInfo track;
                    track.index = static_cast<i32>(i);
                    track.sample_rate = par->sample_rate;
                    track.channels = par->ch_layout.nb_channels;
                    track.bitrate = par->bit_rate;
                    
                    media_info_.audio_tracks.push_back(track);
                    media_info_.has_audio = true;
                    break;
                }
                
                case AVMEDIA_TYPE_SUBTITLE: {
                    SubtitleTrackInfo track;
                    track.index = static_cast<i32>(i);
                    
                    media_info_.subtitle_tracks.push_back(track);
                    media_info_.has_subtitles = true;
                    break;
                }
                
                default:
                    break;
            }
        }
        
        // Check if seekable
        media_info_.is_seekable = format_ctx_->pb && format_ctx_->pb->seekable;
        media_info_.is_live = format_ctx_->duration == AV_NOPTS_VALUE;
        
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<void> OpenVideoDecoder(int stream_index) {
        AVStream* stream = format_ctx_->streams[stream_index];
        const AVCodec* codec = nullptr;
        
        // Try hardware decoder first
        if (options_.enable_hardware_decoding) {
            // Find hardware decoder
            AVHWDeviceType hw_type = AV_HWDEVICE_TYPE_NONE;
            
            #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
            hw_type = AV_HWDEVICE_TYPE_D3D11VA;
            #elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE)
            hw_type = AV_HWDEVICE_TYPE_VIDEOTOOLBOX;
            #elif defined(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
            hw_type = AV_HWDEVICE_TYPE_VAAPI;
            #endif
            
            if (hw_type != AV_HWDEVICE_TYPE_NONE) {
                // Hardware decoder initialization would go here
            }
        }
        
        // Fallback to software decoder
        if (!codec) {
            codec = avcodec_find_decoder(stream->codecpar->codec_id);
        }
        
        if (!codec) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::UnsupportedCodec, "No decoder found for video stream");
        }
        
        video_codec_ctx_ = avcodec_alloc_context3(codec);
        if (!video_codec_ctx_) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::OutOfMemory, "Failed to allocate video decoder context");
        }
        
        int ret = avcodec_parameters_to_context(video_codec_ctx_, stream->codecpar);
        if (ret < 0) {
            avcodec_free_context(&video_codec_ctx_);
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::DecoderError, "Failed to copy codec parameters");
        }
        
        // Set threading
        video_codec_ctx_->thread_count = 0;  // Auto
        video_codec_ctx_->thread_type = FF_THREAD_FRAME | FF_THREAD_SLICE;
        
        ret = avcodec_open2(video_codec_ctx_, codec, nullptr);
        if (ret < 0) {
            avcodec_free_context(&video_codec_ctx_);
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::DecoderError, "Failed to open video decoder");
        }
        
        GetLogger().Debug("Opened video decoder: {} ({}x{})", 
            codec->name, video_codec_ctx_->width, video_codec_ctx_->height);
        
        return {};
    }
    
    Result<void> OpenAudioDecoder(int stream_index) {
        AVStream* stream = format_ctx_->streams[stream_index];
        const AVCodec* codec = avcodec_find_decoder(stream->codecpar->codec_id);
        
        if (!codec) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::UnsupportedCodec, "No decoder found for audio stream");
        }
        
        audio_codec_ctx_ = avcodec_alloc_context3(codec);
        if (!audio_codec_ctx_) {
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::OutOfMemory, "Failed to allocate audio decoder context");
        }
        
        int ret = avcodec_parameters_to_context(audio_codec_ctx_, stream->codecpar);
        if (ret < 0) {
            avcodec_free_context(&audio_codec_ctx_);
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::DecoderError, "Failed to copy codec parameters");
        }
        
        ret = avcodec_open2(audio_codec_ctx_, codec, nullptr);
        if (ret < 0) {
            avcodec_free_context(&audio_codec_ctx_);
            return Error::Make(ErrorCode::DecoderError, "Failed to open audio decoder");
        }
        
        GetLogger().Debug("Opened audio decoder: {} ({}Hz, {} ch)", 
            codec->name, audio_codec_ctx_->sample_rate, 
            audio_codec_ctx_->ch_layout.nb_channels);
        
        return {};
    }
    
    void ClearQueues() {
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(packet_mutex_);
            while (!video_packet_queue_.empty()) {
                AVPacket* pkt = video_packet_queue_.front();
                video_packet_queue_.pop();
                av_packet_free(&pkt);
            }
            while (!audio_packet_queue_.empty()) {
                AVPacket* pkt = audio_packet_queue_.front();
                audio_packet_queue_.pop();
                av_packet_free(&pkt);
            }
        }
        
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(frame_mutex_);
            while (!video_frame_queue_.empty()) {
                AVFrame* frame = video_frame_queue_.front();
                video_frame_queue_.pop();
                av_frame_free(&frame);
            }
            while (!audio_frame_queue_.empty()) {
                AVFrame* frame = audio_frame_queue_.front();
                audio_frame_queue_.pop();
                av_frame_free(&frame);
            }
        }
    }
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Demuxing Thread
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    void DemuxThread() {
        SetThreadName("AetherDemux");
        
        AVPacket* packet = av_packet_alloc();
        
        while (running_) {
            // Wait if queues are full
            {
                std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(packet_mutex_);
                packet_queue_cv_.wait(lock, [this] {
                    return !running_ || 
                           (video_packet_queue_.size() < MAX_PACKET_QUEUE_SIZE &&
                            audio_packet_queue_.size() < MAX_PACKET_QUEUE_SIZE);
                });
            }
            
            if (!running_) break;
            if (paused_ && !seeking_) {
                std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(10));
                continue;
            }
            
            int ret = av_read_frame(format_ctx_, packet);
            
            if (ret < 0) {
                if (ret == AVERROR_EOF) {
                    // End of file
                    eof_reached_ = true;
                    
                    if (loop_mode_ == LoopMode::Single) {
                        // Seek to beginning and continue
                        av_seek_frame(format_ctx_, -1, 0, AVSEEK_FLAG_BACKWARD);
                        eof_reached_ = false;
                        continue;
                    }
                    
                    break;
                }
                
                // Error
                GetLogger().Error("Read frame error: {}", ret);
                continue;
            }
            
            // Queue packet
            AVPacket* pkt_copy = av_packet_clone(packet);
            av_packet_unref(packet);
            
            {
                std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(packet_mutex_);
                
                if (packet->stream_index == video_stream_index_) {
                    video_packet_queue_.push(pkt_copy);
                    video_frame_cv_.notify_one();
                } else if (packet->stream_index == audio_stream_index_) {
                    audio_packet_queue_.push(pkt_copy);
                    audio_frame_cv_.notify_one();
                } else {
                    av_packet_free(&pkt_copy);
                }
            }
        }
        
        av_packet_free(&packet);
        
        GetLogger().Debug("Demux thread finished");
    }
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Video Decode Thread
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    void DecodeVideoThread() {
        SetThreadName("AetherVideoDec");
        
        if (!video_codec_ctx_) {
            return;
        }
        
        AVFrame* frame = av_frame_alloc();
        
        while (running_) {
            AVPacket* packet = nullptr;
            
            {
                std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(packet_mutex_);
                video_frame_cv_.wait(lock, [this] {
                    return !running_ || !video_packet_queue_.empty();
                });
                
                if (!running_) break;
                
                if (!video_packet_queue_.empty()) {
                    packet = video_packet_queue_.front();
                    video_packet_queue_.pop();
                    packet_queue_cv_.notify_one();
                }
            }
            
            if (!packet) continue;
            
            // Send packet to decoder
            int ret = avcodec_send_packet(video_codec_ctx_, packet);
            av_packet_free(&packet);
            
            if (ret < 0) {
                continue;
            }
            
            // Receive frames
            while (ret >= 0) {
                ret = avcodec_receive_frame(video_codec_ctx_, frame);
                
                if (ret == AVERROR(EAGAIN) || ret == AVERROR_EOF) {
                    break;
                }
                
                if (ret < 0) {
                    break;
                }
                
                // Update statistics
                {
                    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(stats_mutex_);
                    statistics_.frames_decoded++;
                }
                
                // Queue frame
                AVFrame* frame_copy = av_frame_clone(frame);
                av_frame_unref(frame);
                
                {
                    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(frame_mutex_);
                    
                    // Limit queue size
                    while (video_frame_queue_.size() >= MAX_FRAME_QUEUE_SIZE) {
                        AVFrame* old = video_frame_queue_.front();
                        video_frame_queue_.pop();
                        av_frame_free(&old);
                        
                        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock2(stats_mutex_);
                        statistics_.frames_dropped++;
                    }
                    
                    video_frame_queue_.push(frame_copy);
                }
                
                // Update position
                if (frame_copy->pts != AV_NOPTS_VALUE) {
                    AVStream* stream = format_ctx_->streams[video_stream_index_];
                    f64 pts_seconds = frame_copy->pts * av_q2d(stream->time_base);
                    position_ = Timestamp::FromSeconds(pts_seconds);
                    
                    // Dispatch position event (throttled)
                    static auto last_event_time = Clock::now();
                    auto now = Clock::now();
                    if (now - last_event_time > std::chrono::milliseconds(100)) {
                        DispatchEvent(PositionChangedEvent{
                            .position = position_,
                            .duration = duration_,
                            .progress = duration_.pts > 0 
                                ? static_cast<f64>(position_.pts) / duration_.pts 
                                : 0.0
                        });
                        last_event_time = now;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        
        av_frame_free(&frame);
        
        GetLogger().Debug("Video decode thread finished");
    }
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Audio Decode Thread
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    void DecodeAudioThread() {
        SetThreadName("AetherAudioDec");
        
        if (!audio_codec_ctx_) {
            return;
        }
        
        AVFrame* frame = av_frame_alloc();
        
        while (running_) {
            AVPacket* packet = nullptr;
            
            {
                std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(packet_mutex_);
                audio_frame_cv_.wait(lock, [this] {
                    return !running_ || !audio_packet_queue_.empty();
                });
                
                if (!running_) break;
                
                if (!audio_packet_queue_.empty()) {
                    packet = audio_packet_queue_.front();
                    audio_packet_queue_.pop();
                    packet_queue_cv_.notify_one();
                }
            }
            
            if (!packet) continue;
            
            int ret = avcodec_send_packet(audio_codec_ctx_, packet);
            av_packet_free(&packet);
            
            if (ret < 0) {
                continue;
            }
            
            while (ret >= 0) {
                ret = avcodec_receive_frame(audio_codec_ctx_, frame);
                
                if (ret == AVERROR(EAGAIN) || ret == AVERROR_EOF) {
                    break;
                }
                
                if (ret < 0) {
                    break;
                }
                
                // Update statistics
                {
                    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(stats_mutex_);
                    statistics_.samples_decoded += frame->nb_samples;
                }
                
                // Queue frame
                AVFrame* frame_copy = av_frame_clone(frame);
                av_frame_unref(frame);
                
                {
                    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(frame_mutex_);
                    
                    while (audio_frame_queue_.size() >= MAX_FRAME_QUEUE_SIZE) {
                        AVFrame* old = audio_frame_queue_.front();
                        audio_frame_queue_.pop();
                        av_frame_free(&old);
                    }
                    
                    audio_frame_queue_.push(frame_copy);
                }
            }
        }
        
        av_frame_free(&frame);
        
        GetLogger().Debug("Audio decode thread finished");
    }
    
private:
    static constexpr usize MAX_PACKET_QUEUE_SIZE = 100;
    static constexpr usize MAX_FRAME_QUEUE_SIZE = 16;
    
    std::string name_;
    bool initialized_ = false;
    
    mutable std::mutex mutex_;
    mutable std::mutex stats_mutex_;
    
    PlayerOptions options_;
    std::string current_url_;
    MediaInfo media_info_;
    
    // FFmpeg contexts
    AVFormatContext* format_ctx_ = nullptr;
    AVCodecContext* video_codec_ctx_ = nullptr;
    AVCodecContext* audio_codec_ctx_ = nullptr;
    
    int video_stream_index_ = -1;
    int audio_stream_index_ = -1;
    int subtitle_stream_index_ = -1;
    
    // State
    std::atomic<PlaybackState> state_{PlaybackState::Idle};
    std::atomic<bool> paused_{false};
    std::atomic<bool> seeking_{false};
    std::atomic<bool> buffering_{false};
    std::atomic<bool> eof_reached_{false};
    
    Timestamp position_;
    Timestamp duration_;
    
    std::atomic<f32> volume_{1.0f};
    std::atomic<bool> muted_{false};
    f32 speed_ = 1.0f;
    LoopMode loop_mode_ = LoopMode::None;
    i32 audio_delay_ms_ = 0;
    i32 subtitle_delay_ms_ = 0;
    
    // Timing
    TimePoint start_time_;
    TimePoint pause_time_;
    
    // Threads
    std::atomic<bool> running_{false};
    std::thread demux_thread_;
    std::thread decode_video_thread_;
    std::thread decode_audio_thread_;
    
    // Packet queues
    std::mutex packet_mutex_;
    std::condition_variable packet_queue_cv_;
    std::queue<AVPacket*> video_packet_queue_;
    std::queue<AVPacket*> audio_packet_queue_;
    
    // Frame queues
    std::mutex frame_mutex_;
    std::condition_variable video_frame_cv_;
    std::condition_variable audio_frame_cv_;
    std::queue<AVFrame*> video_frame_queue_;
    std::queue<AVFrame*> audio_frame_queue_;
    
    // Event dispatcher
    Unique<EventDispatcher> dispatcher_;
    
    // Statistics
    PlayerStatistics statistics_;
    
    // Error handling
    Error last_error_;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Player Implementation Wrappers
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Player::Player(std::string name) : impl_(MakeUnique<Impl>(std::move(name))) {}
Player::~Player() = default;

Result<void> Player::Initialize(const PlayerOptions& options) {
    return impl_->Initialize(options);
}

void Player::Shutdown() {
    impl_->Shutdown();
}

Result<void> Player::Open(std::string_view url) {
    return impl_->Open(url, impl_->GetOptions());
}

Result<void> Player::Open(std::string_view url, const PlayerOptions& options) {
    return impl_->Open(url, options);
}

void Player::Close() {
    impl_->Close();
}

Result<void> Player::Play() {
    return impl_->Play();
}

Result<void> Player::Pause() {
    return impl_->Pause();
}

Result<void> Player::TogglePlayPause() {
    return impl_->TogglePlayPause();
}

Result<void> Player::Stop() {
    return impl_->Stop();
}

Result<void> Player::Seek(Timestamp position) {
    return impl_->Seek(position);
}

Result<void> Player::SeekTo(f64 seconds) {
    return impl_->Seek(Timestamp::FromSeconds(seconds));
}

Result<void> Player::SeekToPercent(f64 percent) {
    auto duration = impl_->GetDuration();
    f64 target = duration.ToSeconds() * std::clamp(percent, 0.0, 1.0);
    return SeekTo(target);
}

Result<void> Player::SeekRelative(f64 seconds) {
    auto position = impl_->GetPosition();
    return SeekTo(position.ToSeconds() + seconds);
}

PlaybackState Player::GetState() const {
    return impl_->GetState();
}

PlaybackStatus Player::GetStatus() const {
    return impl_->GetStatus();
}

PlayerStatistics Player::GetStatistics() const {
    return impl_->GetStatistics();
}

std::optional<MediaInfo> Player::GetMediaInfo() const {
    return impl_->GetMediaInfo();
}

Timestamp Player::GetPosition() const {
    return impl_->GetPosition();
}

Timestamp Player::GetDuration() const {
    return impl_->GetDuration();
}

f64 Player::GetPositionSeconds() const {
    return impl_->GetPosition().ToSeconds();
}

f64 Player::GetDurationSeconds() const {
    return impl_->GetDuration().ToSeconds();
}

f64 Player::GetProgress() const {
    auto duration = impl_->GetDuration();
    if (duration.pts == 0) return 0.0;
    return static_cast<f64>(impl_->GetPosition().pts) / duration.pts;
}

bool Player::IsOpened() const {
    auto state = impl_->GetState();
    return state != PlaybackState::Idle && state != PlaybackState::Error;
}

bool Player::IsPlaying() const {
    return impl_->GetState() == PlaybackState::Playing;
}

bool Player::IsPaused() const {
    return impl_->GetState() == PlaybackState::Paused;
}

bool Player::IsStopped() const {
    return impl_->GetState() == PlaybackState::Stopped;
}

bool Player::IsSeeking() const {
    return impl_->GetState() == PlaybackState::Seeking;
}

bool Player::IsBuffering() const {
    return impl_->GetState() == PlaybackState::Buffering;
}

Result<void> Player::SetVolume(f32 volume) {
    return impl_->SetVolume(volume);
}

f32 Player::GetVolume() const {
    return impl_->GetVolume();
}

Result<void> Player::SetMuted(bool muted) {
    return impl_->SetMuted(muted);
}

bool Player::IsMuted() const {
    return impl_->IsMuted();
}

Result<void> Player::ToggleMute() {
    return impl_->SetMuted(!impl_->IsMuted());
}

Result<void> Player::SetSpeed(f32 speed) {
    return impl_->SetSpeed(speed);
}

f32 Player::GetSpeed() const {
    return impl_->GetSpeed();
}

u64 Player::AddEventListener(EventCallback callback) {
    return impl_->AddEventListener(std::move(callback));
}

void Player::RemoveEventListener(u64 id) {
    impl_->RemoveEventListener(id);
}

std::string_view Player::GetName() const {
    return impl_->GetName();
}

const PlayerOptions& Player::GetOptions() const {
    return impl_->GetOptions();
}

Error Player::GetLastError() const {
    return impl_->GetLastError();
}

} // namespace aether
```

## Part 8: Event System Implementation

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: src/core/event_system.cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#include "aether/core/event_system.hpp"
#include "aether/utils/threading.hpp"

#include <mutex>
#include <vector>
#include <queue>
#include <unordered_map>

namespace aether {

class EventDispatcher::Impl {
public:
    u64 Subscribe(Callback callback) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        u64 id = next_id_++;
        subscribers_[id] = std::move(callback);
        return id;
    }
    
    void Unsubscribe(u64 id) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        subscribers_.erase(id);
    }
    
    void Clear() {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        subscribers_.clear();
    }
    
    void DispatchEvent(const PlayerEvent& event) {
        std::vector<Callback> callbacks;
        
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
            callbacks.reserve(subscribers_.size());
            for (const auto& [id, callback] : subscribers_) {
                callbacks.push_back(callback);
            }
        }
        
        for (const auto& callback : callbacks) {
            try {
                callback(event);
            } catch (...) {
                // Swallow exceptions from callbacks
            }
        }
    }
    
    void DispatchAsync(PlayerEvent event) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(queue_mutex_);
        pending_events_.push(std::move(event));
    }
    
    void ProcessPending() {
        std::queue<PlayerEvent> events;
        
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(queue_mutex_);
            events.swap(pending_events_);
        }
        
        while (!events.empty()) {
            DispatchEvent(events.front());
            events.pop();
        }
    }
    
    usize SubscriberCount() const {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        return subscribers_.size();
    }
    
private:
    mutable std::mutex mutex_;
    std::mutex queue_mutex_;
    
    std::unordered_map<u64, Callback> subscribers_;
    u64 next_id_ = 1;
    
    std::queue<PlayerEvent> pending_events_;
};

EventDispatcher::EventDispatcher() : impl_(MakeUnique<Impl>()) {}
EventDispatcher::~EventDispatcher() = default;

u64 EventDispatcher::Subscribe(Callback callback) {
    return impl_->Subscribe(std::move(callback));
}

void EventDispatcher::Unsubscribe(u64 id) {
    impl_->Unsubscribe(id);
}

void EventDispatcher::Clear() {
    impl_->Clear();
}

void EventDispatcher::DispatchEvent(const PlayerEvent& event) {
    impl_->DispatchEvent(event);
}

void EventDispatcher::DispatchAsync(PlayerEvent event) {
    impl_->DispatchAsync(std::move(event));
}

void EventDispatcher::ProcessPending() {
    impl_->ProcessPending();
}

usize EventDispatcher::SubscriberCount() const {
    return impl_->SubscriberCount();
}

std::string_view GetEventName(const PlayerEvent& event) {
    return std::visit([](const auto& e) -> std::string_view {
        using T = std::decay_t<decltype(e)>;
        
        if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, StateChangedEvent>) return "StateChanged";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, MediaOpeningEvent>) return "MediaOpening";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, MediaOpenedEvent>) return "MediaOpened";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, MediaClosedEvent>) return "MediaClosed";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, MediaEndedEvent>) return "MediaEnded";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, PositionChangedEvent>) return "PositionChanged";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, SeekingEvent>) return "Seeking";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, SeekedEvent>) return "Seeked";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, BufferingStartEvent>) return "BufferingStart";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, BufferingProgressEvent>) return "BufferingProgress";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, BufferingEndEvent>) return "BufferingEnd";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, VolumeChangedEvent>) return "VolumeChanged";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, SpeedChangedEvent>) return "SpeedChanged";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, TrackChangedEvent>) return "TrackChanged";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, ChapterChangedEvent>) return "ChapterChanged";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, SubtitleTextEvent>) return "SubtitleText";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, ErrorEvent>) return "Error";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, WarningEvent>) return "Warning";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, VideoSizeChangedEvent>) return "VideoSizeChanged";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, AudioDeviceChangedEvent>) return "AudioDeviceChanged";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, PlaylistChangedEvent>) return "PlaylistChanged";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, RecordingStartedEvent>) return "RecordingStarted";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, RecordingStoppedEvent>) return "RecordingStopped";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, SnapshotTakenEvent>) return "SnapshotTaken";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, MetadataUpdatedEvent>) return "MetadataUpdated";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, NetworkEvent>) return "Network";
        else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, AIProcessingEvent>) return "AIProcessing";
        else return "Unknown";
    }, event);
}

} // namespace aether
```

## Part 9: Simple Desktop Application

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: apps/desktop/src/main.cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#include <aether/aether.hpp>
#include <iostream>
#include <string>
#include <thread>
#include <atomic>
#include <csignal>

#ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
#include <windows.h>
#endif

using namespace aether;

std::atomic<bool> g_running{true};

void SignalHandler(int signal) {
    g_running = false;
}

void PrintUsage(const char* program) {
    std::cout << "AETHER Media Player - Command Line\n";
    std::cout << "Usage: " << program << " <media_file_or_url>\n";
    std::cout << "\nControls:\n";
    std::cout << "  Space    - Play/Pause\n";
    std::cout << "  Left     - Seek backward 10 seconds\n";
    std::cout << "  Right    - Seek forward 10 seconds\n";
    std::cout << "  Up       - Volume up\n";
    std::cout << "  Down     - Volume down\n";
    std::cout << "  M        - Mute/Unmute\n";
    std::cout << "  Q/Escape - Quit\n";
}

void PrintMediaInfo(const MediaInfo& info) {
    std::cout << "\n╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n";
    std::cout << "║                        MEDIA INFO                            ║\n";
    std::cout << "╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣\n";
    
    if (!info.title.empty()) {
        std::cout << "║ Title: " << info.title << "\n";
    }
    
    std::cout << "║ Duration: " << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) 
              << info.duration.ToSeconds() << " seconds\n";
    
    if (info.bitrate > 0) {
        std::cout << "║ Bitrate: " << info.bitrate / 1000 << " kbps\n";
    }
    
    // Video tracks
    for (const auto& track : info.video_tracks) {
        std::cout << "║ Video: " << track.width << "x" << track.height;
        if (track.avg_frame_rate > 0) {
            std::cout << " @ " << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) 
                      << track.avg_frame_rate << " fps";
        }
        std::cout << "\n";
    }
    
    // Audio tracks
    for (const auto& track : info.audio_tracks) {
        std::cout << "║ Audio: " << track.channels << " channels";
        if (track.sample_rate > 0) {
            std::cout << " @ " << track.sample_rate << " Hz";
        }
        if (!track.language.empty()) {
            std::cout << " (" << track.language << ")";
        }
        std::cout << "\n";
    }
    
    // Subtitle tracks
    for (const auto& track : info.subtitle_tracks) {
        std::cout << "║ Subtitle: ";
        if (!track.language.empty()) {
            std::cout << track.language;
        } else {
            std::cout << "Track " << track.index;
        }
        std::cout << "\n";
    }
    
    std::cout << "╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝\n\n";
}

void PrintProgress(const PlaybackStatus& status) {
    f64 pos = status.position.ToSeconds();
    f64 dur = status.duration.ToSeconds();
    f64 progress = dur > 0 ? pos / dur : 0;
    
    // Progress bar
    constexpr int BAR_WIDTH = 50;
    int filled = static_cast<int>(progress * BAR_WIDTH);
    
    std::cout << "\r[";
    for (int i = 0; i < BAR_WIDTH; ++i) {
        if (i < filled) std::cout << "█";
        else if (i == filled) std::cout << "▓";
        else std::cout << "░";
    }
    std::cout << "] ";
    
    // Time
    int pos_min = static_cast<int>(pos) / 60;
    int pos_sec = static_cast<int>(pos) % 60;
    int dur_min = static_cast<int>(dur) / 60;
    int dur_sec = static_cast<int>(dur) % 60;
    
    std::cout << std::setfill('0') << std::setw(2) << pos_min << ":"
              << std::setfill('0') << std::setw(2) << pos_sec << " / "
              << std::setfill('0') << std::setw(2) << dur_min << ":"
              << std::setfill('0') << std::setw(2) << dur_sec;
    
    // Volume
    std::cout << " | Vol: " << static_cast<int>(status.volume * 100) << "%";
    if (status.is_muted) {
        std::cout << " (Muted)";
    }
    
    // State
    std::cout << " | " << ToString(status.state);
    
    std::cout << std::flush;
}

int main(int argc, char* argv[]) {
    // Handle signals
    std::signal(SIGINT, SignalHandler);
    std::signal(SIGTERM, SignalHandler);
    
    #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
    // Enable ANSI escape codes on Windows
    HANDLE hOut = GetStdHandle(STD_OUTPUT_HANDLE);
    DWORD dwMode = 0;
    GetConsoleMode(hOut, &dwMode);
    SetConsoleMode(hOut, dwMode | ENABLE_VIRTUAL_TERMINAL_PROCESSING);
    #endif
    
    std::cout << "╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n";
    std::cout << "║           AETHER Media Engine v" << Engine::GetVersion() << "                       ║\n";
    std::cout << "║                    \"" << CODENAME << "\"                              ║\n";
    std::cout << "╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝\n\n";
    
    if (argc < 2) {
        PrintUsage(argv[0]);
        return 1;
    }
    
    // Initialize engine
    Config config = Config::Default();
    config.log.level = LogConfig::Level::Info;
    config.log.log_to_console = true;
    config.video.enable_hardware_decoding = true;
    
    if (!Initialize(config)) {
        std::cerr << "Failed to initialize AETHER Media Engine\n";
        return 1;
    }
    
    auto& engine = Engine::Instance();
    
    // Create player
    auto player_result = engine.CreatePlayer("MainPlayer");
    if (!player_result) {
        std::cerr << "Failed to create player: " << player_result.error().Message() << "\n";
        Shutdown();
        return 1;
    }
    
    auto player = *player_result;
    
    // Set up event handlers
    player->On<MediaOpenedEvent>([](const MediaOpenedEvent& e) {
        std::cout << "\n✓ Media opened: " << e.url << "\n";
        if (e.media_info) {
            PrintMediaInfo(*e.media_info);
        }
    });
    
    player->On<ErrorEvent>([](const ErrorEvent& e) {
        std::cerr << "\n✗ Error: " << e.error.Message() << "\n";
    });
    
    player->On<MediaEndedEvent>([](const MediaEndedEvent& e) {
        std::cout << "\n✓ Playback finished\n";
        g_running = false;
    });
    
    player->On<StateChangedEvent>([](const StateChangedEvent& e) {
        // Clear line and print state change
        std::cout << "\r" << std::string(80, ' ') << "\r";
        std::cout << "State: " << ToString(e.old_```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: apps/desktop/src/main.cpp (continued)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    player->On<StateChangedEvent>([](const StateChangedEvent& e) {
        // Clear line and print state change
        std::cout << "\r" << std::string(80, ' ') << "\r";
        std::cout << "State: " << ToString(e.old_state) << " -> " << ToString(e.new_state) << "\n";
    });
    
    player->On<BufferingStartEvent>([](const BufferingStartEvent& e) {
        std::cout << "\r" << std::string(80, ' ') << "\r";
        std::cout << "⏳ Buffering..." << std::flush;
    });
    
    player->On<BufferingEndEvent>([](const BufferingEndEvent& e) {
        std::cout << "\r" << std::string(80, ' ') << "\r";
    });
    
    // Open media
    std::string media_path = argv[1];
    std::cout << "Opening: " << media_path << "\n";
    
    PlayerOptions options;
    options.auto_play = true;
    options.enable_hardware_decoding = true;
    
    auto open_result = player->Open(media_path, options);
    if (!open_result) {
        std::cerr << "Failed to open media: " << open_result.error().Message() << "\n";
        Shutdown();
        return 1;
    }
    
    // Main loop
    std::cout << "\nPlayback started. Press 'q' to quit, 'h' for help.\n\n";
    
    auto last_update = Clock::now();
    
    while (g_running) {
        // Update progress display
        auto now = Clock::now();
        if (now - last_update > std::chrono::milliseconds(250)) {
            auto status = player->GetStatus();
            if (status.state == PlaybackState::Playing || 
                status.state == PlaybackState::Paused) {
                PrintProgress(status);
            }
            last_update = now;
        }
        
        // Check for input (platform-specific)
        #ifdef AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS
        if (_kbhit()) {
            int ch = _getch();
            
            switch (ch) {
                case 'q':
                case 'Q':
                case 27:  // Escape
                    g_running = false;
                    break;
                    
                case ' ':
                    player->TogglePlayPause();
                    break;
                    
                case 'h':
                case 'H':
                    PrintUsage(argv[0]);
                    break;
                    
                case 'm':
                case 'M':
                    player->ToggleMute();
                    break;
                    
                case 224:  // Arrow key prefix
                    ch = _getch();
                    switch (ch) {
                        case 75:  // Left arrow
                            player->SeekRelative(-10.0);
                            break;
                        case 77:  // Right arrow
                            player->SeekRelative(10.0);
                            break;
                        case 72:  // Up arrow
                            player->SetVolume(std::min(player->GetVolume() + 0.1f, 1.0f));
                            break;
                        case 80:  // Down arrow
                            player->SetVolume(std::max(player->GetVolume() - 0.1f, 0.0f));
                            break;
                    }
                    break;
                    
                case '+':
                case '=':
                    player->SetSpeed(std::min(player->GetSpeed() + 0.25f, 4.0f));
                    std::cout << "\nSpeed: " << player->GetSpeed() << "x\n";
                    break;
                    
                case '-':
                case '_':
                    player->SetSpeed(std::max(player->GetSpeed() - 0.25f, 0.25f));
                    std::cout << "\nSpeed: " << player->GetSpeed() << "x\n";
                    break;
                    
                case 'i':
                case 'I':
                    if (auto info = player->GetMediaInfo()) {
                        PrintMediaInfo(*info);
                    }
                    break;
            }
        }
        #else
        // Unix/Linux/macOS terminal input handling
        // Would use termios for non-blocking input
        #endif
        
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(10));
    }
    
    std::cout << "\n\nShutting down...\n";
    
    // Clean up
    player->Stop();
    player->Close();
    engine.DestroyPlayer(player);
    
    Shutdown();
    
    std::cout << "Goodbye!\n";
    
    return 0;
}
