## Part 10: Video Frame and Rendering

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/video/video_frame.hpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#pragma once

#ifndef AETHER_VIDEO_VIDEO_FRAME_HPP
#define AETHER_VIDEO_VIDEO_FRAME_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"
#include "aether/utils/memory.hpp"

#include <array>
#include <memory>

namespace aether {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Video Frame
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API VideoFrame {
public:
    static constexpr usize MAX_PLANES = 4;
    
    VideoFrame();
    VideoFrame(u32 width, u32 height, PixelFormat format);
    ~VideoFrame();
    
    VideoFrame(const VideoFrame& other);
    VideoFrame& operator=(const VideoFrame& other);
    VideoFrame(VideoFrame&& other) noexcept;
    VideoFrame& operator=(VideoFrame&& other) noexcept;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Properties
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    [[nodiscard]] u32 Width() const noexcept { return width_; }
    [[nodiscard]] u32 Height() const noexcept { return height_; }
    [[nodiscard]] Size GetSize() const noexcept { return {static_cast<i32>(width_), static_cast<i32>(height_)}; }
    [[nodiscard]] PixelFormat Format() const noexcept { return format_; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsValid() const noexcept { return width_ > 0 && height_ > 0 && data_[0] != nullptr; }
    
    [[nodiscard]] f64 AspectRatio() const noexcept {
        return height_ > 0 ? static_cast<f64>(width_) / height_ : 0.0;
    }
    
    // Timing
    [[nodiscard]] Timestamp GetTimestamp() const noexcept { return timestamp_; }
    void SetTimestamp(Timestamp ts) { timestamp_ = ts; }
    
    [[nodiscard]] i64 GetPTS() const noexcept { return timestamp_.pts; }
    void SetPTS(i64 pts) { timestamp_.pts = pts; }
    
    [[nodiscard]] Timestamp GetDuration() const noexcept { return duration_; }
    void SetDuration(Timestamp dur) { duration_ = dur; }
    
    // Color info
    [[nodiscard]] ColorSpace GetColorSpace() const noexcept { return color_space_; }
    void SetColorSpace(ColorSpace cs) { color_space_ = cs; }
    
    [[nodiscard]] ColorRange GetColorRange() const noexcept { return color_range_; }
    void SetColorRange(ColorRange cr) { color_range_ = cr; }
    
    [[nodiscard]] HDRType GetHDRType() const noexcept { return hdr_type_; }
    void SetHDRType(HDRType hdr) { hdr_type_ = hdr; }
    
    // Frame type
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsKeyFrame() const noexcept { return key_frame_; }
    void SetKeyFrame(bool kf) { key_frame_ = kf; }
    
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsInterlaced() const noexcept { return interlaced_; }
    void SetInterlaced(bool il) { interlaced_ = il; }
    
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsTopFieldFirst() const noexcept { return top_field_first_; }
    void SetTopFieldFirst(bool tff) { top_field_first_ = tff; }
    
    [[nodiscard]] i32 GetRotation() const noexcept { return rotation_; }
    void SetRotation(i32 deg) { rotation_ = deg; }
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Data Access
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    [[nodiscard]] u32 PlaneCount() const noexcept;
    
    [[nodiscard]] u8* Data(u32 plane = 0) noexcept {
        return plane < MAX_PLANES ? data_[plane] : nullptr;
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] const u8* Data(u32 plane = 0) const noexcept {
        return plane < MAX_PLANES ? data_[plane] : nullptr;
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] u32 Stride(u32 plane = 0) const noexcept {
        return plane < MAX_PLANES ? stride_[plane] : 0;
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] u32 PlaneWidth(u32 plane) const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] u32 PlaneHeight(u32 plane) const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] usize PlaneSize(u32 plane) const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] usize TotalSize() const noexcept;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Operations
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    Result<void> Allocate(u32 width, u32 height, PixelFormat format);
    void Release();
    void Clear();
    
    // Copy from external data
    Result<void> CopyFrom(const u8* const* data, const u32* strides, 
                          u32 width, u32 height, PixelFormat format);
    
    // Wrap external data (no copy, no ownership)
    Result<void> WrapExternal(u8* const* data, const u32* strides,
                              u32 width, u32 height, PixelFormat format);
    
    // Convert to different format
    Result<VideoFrame> ConvertTo(PixelFormat target_format) const;
    
    // Scale to different size
    Result<VideoFrame> ScaleTo(u32 width, u32 height) const;
    
    // Clone this frame
    [[nodiscard]] VideoFrame Clone() const;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Factory Methods
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    static VideoFrame CreateBlack(u32 width, u32 height, PixelFormat format = PixelFormat::YUV420P);
    static VideoFrame CreateTestPattern(u32 width, u32 height, PixelFormat format = PixelFormat::RGBA32);
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Hardware Frames
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsHardwareFrame() const noexcept { return hw_frames_ctx_ != nullptr; }
    [[nodiscard]] void* GetHWFramesContext() const noexcept { return hw_frames_ctx_; }
    [[nodiscard]] void* GetHWData() const noexcept { return hw_data_; }
    
    void SetHWFramesContext(void* ctx) { hw_frames_ctx_ = ctx; }
    void SetHWData(void* data) { hw_data_ = data; }
    
    // Transfer hardware frame to system memory
    Result<VideoFrame> TransferToHost() const;
    
private:
    u32 width_ = 0;
    u32 height_ = 0;
    PixelFormat format_ = PixelFormat::Unknown;
    
    std::array<u8*, MAX_PLANES> data_{};
    std::array<u32, MAX_PLANES> stride_{};
    
    bool owns_data_ = false;
    RefBuffer buffer_;  // For reference-counted storage
    
    Timestamp timestamp_;
    Timestamp duration_;
    
    ColorSpace color_space_ = ColorSpace::Unknown;
    ColorRange color_range_ = ColorRange::Unknown;
    HDRType hdr_type_ = HDRType::None;
    
    bool key_frame_ = false;
    bool interlaced_ = false;
    bool top_field_first_ = false;
    i32 rotation_ = 0;
    
    // Hardware acceleration
    void* hw_frames_ctx_ = nullptr;
    void* hw_data_ = nullptr;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Video Frame Pool
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API VideoFramePool {
public:
    VideoFramePool(u32 width, u32 height, PixelFormat format, u32 pool_size = 8);
    ~VideoFramePool();
    
    VideoFramePool(const VideoFramePool&) = delete;
    VideoFramePool& operator=(const VideoFramePool&) = delete;
    
    // Acquire a frame from the pool
    [[nodiscard]] Shared<VideoFrame> Acquire();
    
    // Return frame to pool (called automatically by shared_ptr deleter)
    void Release(VideoFrame* frame);
    
    // Pool info
    [[nodiscard]] u32 Width() const noexcept { return width_; }
    [[nodiscard]] u32 Height() const noexcept { return height_; }
    [[nodiscard]] PixelFormat Format() const noexcept { return format_; }
    [[nodiscard]] u32 PoolSize() const noexcept { return pool_size_; }
    [[nodiscard]] u32 AvailableCount() const;
    [[nodiscard]] u32 InUseCount() const;
    
    // Resize pool
    void Resize(u32 new_size);
    void Clear();
    
private:
    u32 width_;
    u32 height_;
    PixelFormat format_;
    u32 pool_size_;
    
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_VIDEO_VIDEO_FRAME_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: src/video/video_frame.cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#include "aether/video/video_frame.hpp"
#include "aether/utils/logging.hpp"

#include <cstring>
#include <algorithm>
#include <mutex>
#include <queue>

extern "C" {
#include <libavutil/imgutils.h>
#include <libswscale/swscale.h>
}

namespace aether {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Pixel Format Helpers
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

static AVPixelFormat ToFFmpegFormat(PixelFormat fmt) {
    switch (fmt) {
        case PixelFormat::YUV420P: return AV_PIX_FMT_YUV420P;
        case PixelFormat::YUV422P: return AV_PIX_FMT_YUV422P;
        case PixelFormat::YUV444P: return AV_PIX_FMT_YUV444P;
        case PixelFormat::YUV420P10: return AV_PIX_FMT_YUV420P10LE;
        case PixelFormat::NV12: return AV_PIX_FMT_NV12;
        case PixelFormat::NV21: return AV_PIX_FMT_NV21;
        case PixelFormat::P010: return AV_PIX_FMT_P010LE;
        case PixelFormat::RGB24: return AV_PIX_FMT_RGB24;
        case PixelFormat::BGR24: return AV_PIX_FMT_BGR24;
        case PixelFormat::RGBA32: return AV_PIX_FMT_RGBA;
        case PixelFormat::BGRA32: return AV_PIX_FMT_BGRA;
        case PixelFormat::ARGB32: return AV_PIX_FMT_ARGB;
        case PixelFormat::Gray8: return AV_PIX_FMT_GRAY8;
        case PixelFormat::Gray16: return AV_PIX_FMT_GRAY16LE;
        default: return AV_PIX_FMT_NONE;
    }
}

static u32 GetPlaneCount(PixelFormat fmt) {
    switch (fmt) {
        case PixelFormat::YUV420P:
        case PixelFormat::YUV422P:
        case PixelFormat::YUV444P:
        case PixelFormat::YUV420P10:
            return 3;
        case PixelFormat::NV12:
        case PixelFormat::NV21:
        case PixelFormat::P010:
            return 2;
        case PixelFormat::RGB24:
        case PixelFormat::BGR24:
        case PixelFormat::RGBA32:
        case PixelFormat::BGRA32:
        case PixelFormat::ARGB32:
        case PixelFormat::Gray8:
        case PixelFormat::Gray16:
            return 1;
        default:
            return 0;
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// VideoFrame Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

VideoFrame::VideoFrame() = default;

VideoFrame::VideoFrame(u32 width, u32 height, PixelFormat format) {
    Allocate(width, height, format);
}

VideoFrame::~VideoFrame() {
    Release();
}

VideoFrame::VideoFrame(const VideoFrame& other) {
    *this = other;
}

VideoFrame& VideoFrame::operator=(const VideoFrame& other) {
    if (this != &other) {
        Release();
        
        width_ = other.width_;
        height_ = other.height_;
        format_ = other.format_;
        timestamp_ = other.timestamp_;
        duration_ = other.duration_;
        color_space_ = other.color_space_;
        color_range_ = other.color_range_;
        hdr_type_ = other.hdr_type_;
        key_frame_ = other.key_frame_;
        interlaced_ = other.interlaced_;
        top_field_first_ = other.top_field_first_;
        rotation_ = other.rotation_;
        
        if (other.owns_data_ && other.IsValid()) {
            // Deep copy
            Allocate(width_, height_, format_);
            
            for (u32 p = 0; p < PlaneCount(); ++p) {
                if (data_[p] && other.data_[p]) {
                    for (u32 y = 0; y < PlaneHeight(p); ++y) {
                        std::memcpy(
                            data_[p] + y * stride_[p],
                            other.data_[p] + y * other.stride_[p],
                            PlaneWidth(p) * (format_ == PixelFormat::YUV420P10 || 
                                            format_ == PixelFormat::P010 ? 2 : 1)
                        );
                    }
                }
            }
        } else if (!other.owns_data_) {
            // Shallow copy for wrapped data
            data_ = other.data_;
            stride_ = other.stride_;
            owns_data_ = false;
        }
    }
    return *this;
}

VideoFrame::VideoFrame(VideoFrame&& other) noexcept {
    *this = std::move(other);
}

VideoFrame& VideoFrame::operator=(VideoFrame&& other) noexcept {
    if (this != &other) {
        Release();
        
        width_ = other.width_;
        height_ = other.height_;
        format_ = other.format_;
        data_ = other.data_;
        stride_ = other.stride_;
        owns_data_ = other.owns_data_;
        buffer_ = std::move(other.buffer_);
        timestamp_ = other.timestamp_;
        duration_ = other.duration_;
        color_space_ = other.color_space_;
        color_range_ = other.color_range_;
        hdr_type_ = other.hdr_type_;
        key_frame_ = other.key_frame_;
        interlaced_ = other.interlaced_;
        top_field_first_ = other.top_field_first_;
        rotation_ = other.rotation_;
        hw_frames_ctx_ = other.hw_frames_ctx_;
        hw_data_ = other.hw_data_;
        
        // Reset other
        other.width_ = 0;
        other.height_ = 0;
        other.format_ = PixelFormat::Unknown;
        other.data_.fill(nullptr);
        other.stride_.fill(0);
        other.owns_data_ = false;
        other.hw_frames_ctx_ = nullptr;
        other.hw_data_ = nullptr;
    }
    return *this;
}

u32 VideoFrame::PlaneCount() const noexcept {
    return GetPlaneCount(format_);
}

u32 VideoFrame::PlaneWidth(u32 plane) const noexcept {
    if (plane >= PlaneCount()) return 0;
    
    switch (format_) {
        case PixelFormat::YUV420P:
        case PixelFormat::YUV420P10:
        case PixelFormat::NV12:
        case PixelFormat::NV21:
        case PixelFormat::P010:
            return plane == 0 ? width_ : width_ / 2;
        case PixelFormat::YUV422P:
            return plane == 0 ? width_ : width_ / 2;
        default:
            return width_;
    }
}

u32 VideoFrame::PlaneHeight(u32 plane) const noexcept {
    if (plane >= PlaneCount()) return 0;
    
    switch (format_) {
        case PixelFormat::YUV420P:
        case PixelFormat::YUV420P10:
        case PixelFormat::NV12:
        case PixelFormat::NV21:
        case PixelFormat::P010:
            return plane == 0 ? height_ : height_ / 2;
        default:
            return height_;
    }
}

usize VideoFrame::PlaneSize(u32 plane) const noexcept {
    if (plane >= PlaneCount()) return 0;
    return static_cast<usize>(stride_[plane]) * PlaneHeight(plane);
}

usize VideoFrame::TotalSize() const noexcept {
    usize total = 0;
    for (u32 p = 0; p < PlaneCount(); ++p) {
        total += PlaneSize(p);
    }
    return total;
}

Result<void> VideoFrame::Allocate(u32 width, u32 height, PixelFormat format) {
    if (width == 0 || height == 0) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidArgument, "Invalid frame dimensions");
    }
    
    AVPixelFormat av_fmt = ToFFmpegFormat(format);
    if (av_fmt == AV_PIX_FMT_NONE) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::UnsupportedPixelFormat, "Unsupported pixel format");
    }
    
    Release();
    
    width_ = width;
    height_ = height;
    format_ = format;
    
    // Calculate required buffer size
    int linesize[4] = {0};
    int ret = av_image_fill_linesizes(linesize, av_fmt, width);
    if (ret < 0) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::OutOfMemory, "Failed to calculate line sizes");
    }
    
    // Align strides to 64 bytes for SIMD
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
        stride_[i] = (linesize[i] + 63) & ~63;
    }
    
    // Calculate total size
    usize total_size = 0;
    u32 plane_count = GetPlaneCount(format);
    for (u32 p = 0; p < plane_count; ++p) {
        total_size += static_cast<usize>(stride_[p]) * PlaneHeight(p);
    }
    
    // Allocate aligned memory
    buffer_ = RefBuffer(total_size);
    if (buffer_.IsEmpty()) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::OutOfMemory, "Failed to allocate frame buffer");
    }
    
    // Set plane pointers
    u8* ptr = buffer_.Data();
    for (u32 p = 0; p < plane_count; ++p) {
        data_[p] = ptr;
        ptr += static_cast<usize>(stride_[p]) * PlaneHeight(p);
    }
    
    owns_data_ = true;
    
    return {};
}

void VideoFrame::Release() {
    if (owns_data_) {
        buffer_.Reset();
    }
    
    width_ = 0;
    height_ = 0;
    format_ = PixelFormat::Unknown;
    data_.fill(nullptr);
    stride_.fill(0);
    owns_data_ = false;
    hw_frames_ctx_ = nullptr;
    hw_data_ = nullptr;
}

void VideoFrame::Clear() {
    for (u32 p = 0; p < PlaneCount(); ++p) {
        if (data_[p]) {
            std::memset(data_[p], 0, PlaneSize(p));
        }
    }
}

Result<void> VideoFrame::CopyFrom(const u8* const* data, const u32* strides,
                                   u32 width, u32 height, PixelFormat format) {
    if (width_ != width || height_ != height || format_ != format) {
        auto result = Allocate(width, height, format);
        if (!result) return result;
    }
    
    for (u32 p = 0; p < PlaneCount(); ++p) {
        if (!data[p] || !data_[p]) continue;
        
        u32 copy_width = PlaneWidth(p);
        u32 copy_height = PlaneHeight(p);
        u32 src_stride = strides[p];
        u32 dst_stride = stride_[p];
        
        // Get bytes per pixel for this plane
        u32 bytes_per_pixel = 1;
        if (format == PixelFormat::YUV420P10 || format == PixelFormat::P010) {
            bytes_per_pixel = 2;
        } else if (format == PixelFormat::RGBA32 || format == PixelFormat::BGRA32) {
            bytes_per_pixel = 4;
        } else if (format == PixelFormat::RGB24 || format == PixelFormat::BGR24) {
            bytes_per_pixel = 3;
        }
        
        usize row_bytes = static_cast<usize>(copy_width) * bytes_per_pixel;
        
        if (src_stride == dst_stride && src_stride == row_bytes) {
            // Fast path: contiguous memory
            std::memcpy(data_[p], data[p], row_bytes * copy_height);
        } else {
            // Copy row by row
            for (u32 y = 0; y < copy_height; ++y) {
                std::memcpy(
                    data_[p] + y * dst_stride,
                    data[p] + y * src_stride,
                    row_bytes
                );
            }
        }
    }
    
    return {};
}

Result<void> VideoFrame::WrapExternal(u8* const* data, const u32* strides,
                                       u32 width, u32 height, PixelFormat format) {
    Release();
    
    width_ = width;
    height_ = height;
    format_ = format;
    owns_data_ = false;
    
    u32 plane_count = GetPlaneCount(format);
    for (u32 p = 0; p < plane_count; ++p) {
        data_[p] = data[p];
        stride_[p] = strides[p];
    }
    
    return {};
}

Result<VideoFrame> VideoFrame::ConvertTo(PixelFormat target_format) const {
    if (!IsValid()) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "Invalid source frame");
    }
    
    if (format_ == target_format) {
        return Clone();
    }
    
    AVPixelFormat src_fmt = ToFFmpegFormat(format_);
    AVPixelFormat dst_fmt = ToFFmpegFormat(target_format);
    
    if (src_fmt == AV_PIX_FMT_NONE || dst_fmt == AV_PIX_FMT_NONE) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::UnsupportedPixelFormat, "Unsupported pixel format");
    }
    
    VideoFrame result;
    auto alloc_result = result.Allocate(width_, height_, target_format);
    if (!alloc_result) {
        return alloc_result.error();
    }
    
    // Create swscale context
    SwsContext* sws_ctx = sws_getContext(
        width_, height_, src_fmt,
        width_, height_, dst_fmt,
        SWS_BICUBIC, nullptr, nullptr, nullptr
    );
    
    if (!sws_ctx) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::FilterError, "Failed to create swscale context");
    }
    
    // Convert
    const u8* src_data[4] = {data_[0], data_[1], data_[2], data_[3]};
    int src_stride[4] = {
        static_cast<int>(stride_[0]),
        static_cast<int>(stride_[1]),
        static_cast<int>(stride_[2]),
        static_cast<int>(stride_[3])
    };
    
    u8* dst_data[4] = {result.data_[0], result.data_[1], result.data_[2], result.data_[3]};
    int dst_stride[4] = {
        static_cast<int>(result.stride_[0]),
        static_cast<int>(result.stride_[1]),
        static_cast<int>(result.stride_[2]),
        static_cast<int>(result.stride_[3])
    };
    
    sws_scale(sws_ctx, src_data, src_stride, 0, height_, dst_data, dst_stride);
    
    sws_freeContext(sws_ctx);
    
    // Copy metadata
    result.timestamp_ = timestamp_;
    result.duration_ = duration_;
    result.color_space_ = color_space_;
    result.color_range_ = color_range_;
    result.key_frame_ = key_frame_;
    result.rotation_ = rotation_;
    
    return result;
}

Result<VideoFrame> VideoFrame::ScaleTo(u32 width, u32 height) const {
    if (!IsValid()) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::InvalidState, "Invalid source frame");
    }
    
    if (width_ == width && height_ == height) {
        return Clone();
    }
    
    AVPixelFormat av_fmt = ToFFmpegFormat(format_);
    if (av_fmt == AV_PIX_FMT_NONE) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::UnsupportedPixelFormat, "Unsupported pixel format");
    }
    
    VideoFrame result;
    auto alloc_result = result.Allocate(width, height, format_);
    if (!alloc_result) {
        return alloc_result.error();
    }
    
    // Create swscale context
    SwsContext* sws_ctx = sws_getContext(
        width_, height_, av_fmt,
        width, height, av_fmt,
        SWS_BICUBIC, nullptr, nullptr, nullptr
    );
    
    if (!sws_ctx) {
        return Error::Make(ErrorCode::FilterError, "Failed to create swscale context");
    }
    
    // Scale
    const u8* src_data[4] = {data_[0], data_[1], data_[2], data_[3]};
    int src_stride[4] = {
        static_cast<int>(stride_[0]),
        static_cast<int>(stride_[1]),
        static_cast<int>(stride_[2]),
        static_cast<int>(stride_[3])
    };
    
    u8* dst_data[4] = {result.data_[0], result.data_[1], result.data_[2], result.data_[3]};
    int dst_stride[4] = {
        static_cast<int>(result.stride_[0]),
        static_cast<int>(result.stride_[1]),
        static_cast<int>(result.stride_[2]),
        static_cast<int>(result.stride_[3])
    };
    
    sws_scale(sws_ctx, src_data, src_stride, 0, height_, dst_data, dst_stride);
    
    sws_freeContext(sws_ctx);
    
    // Copy metadata
    result.timestamp_ = timestamp_;
    result.duration_ = duration_;
    result.color_space_ = color_space_;
    result.color_range_ = color_range_;
    result.key_frame_ = key_frame_;
    result.rotation_ = rotation_;
    
    return result;
}

VideoFrame VideoFrame::Clone() const {
    VideoFrame result;
    
    if (!IsValid()) {
        return result;
    }
    
    const u8* data[4] = {data_[0], data_[1], data_[2], data_[3]};
    u32 strides[4] = {stride_[0], stride_[1], stride_[2], stride_[3]};
    
    result.CopyFrom(data, strides, width_, height_, format_);
    
    result.timestamp_ = timestamp_;
    result.duration_ = duration_;
    result.color_space_ = color_space_;
    result.color_range_ = color_range_;
    result.hdr_type_ = hdr_type_;
    result.key_frame_ = key_frame_;
    result.interlaced_ = interlaced_;
    result.top_field_first_ = top_field_first_;
    result.rotation_ = rotation_;
    
    return result;
}

VideoFrame VideoFrame::CreateBlack(u32 width, u32 height, PixelFormat format) {
    VideoFrame frame;
    frame.Allocate(width, height, format);
    
    // Fill with black (Y=16 for limited range, UV=128)
    if (format == PixelFormat::YUV420P || format == PixelFormat::YUV422P || format == PixelFormat::YUV444P) {
        std::memset(frame.Data(0), 16, frame.PlaneSize(0));   // Y
        std::memset(frame.Data(1), 128, frame.PlaneSize(1));  // U
        std::memset(frame.Data(2), 128, frame.PlaneSize(2));  // V
    } else {
        frame.Clear();
    }
    
    return frame;
}

VideoFrame VideoFrame::CreateTestPattern(u32 width, u32 height, PixelFormat format) {
    VideoFrame frame;
    frame.Allocate(width, height, format);
    
    if (format == PixelFormat::RGBA32) {
        // Create color bars
        u32* pixels = reinterpret_cast<u32*>(frame.Data(0));
        u32 stride = frame.Stride(0) / 4;
        
        // SMPTE color bars
        u32 colors[] = {
            0xFFFFFFFF,  // White
            0xFFFFFF00,  // Yellow
            0xFF00FFFF,  // Cyan
            0xFF00FF00,  // Green
            0xFFFF00FF,  // Magenta
            0xFFFF0000,  // Red
            0xFF0000FF,  // Blue
            0xFF000000   // Black
        };
        
        u32 bar_width = width / 8;
        
        for (u32 y = 0; y < height; ++y) {
            for (u32 x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
                u32 bar = std::min(x / bar_width, 7u);
                pixels[y * stride + x] = colors[bar];
            }
        }
    }
    
    return frame;
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// VideoFramePool Implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class VideoFramePool::Impl {
public:
    std::mutex mutex;
    std::queue<VideoFrame*> available;
    std::vector<Unique<VideoFrame>> all_frames;
    u32 in_use = 0;
};

VideoFramePool::VideoFramePool(u32 width, u32 height, PixelFormat format, u32 pool_size)
    : width_(width)
    , height_(height)
    , format_(format)
    , pool_size_(pool_size)
    , impl_(MakeUnique<Impl>()) {
    
    // Pre-allocate frames
    for (u32 i = 0; i < pool_size_; ++i) {
        auto frame = MakeUnique<VideoFrame>(width_, height_, format_);
        impl_->available.push(frame.get());
        impl_->all_frames.push_back(std::move(frame));
    }
}

VideoFramePool::~VideoFramePool() {
    Clear();
}

Shared<VideoFrame> VideoFramePool::Acquire() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    
    VideoFrame* frame = nullptr;
    
    if (!impl_->available.empty()) {
        frame = impl_->available.front();
        impl_->available.pop();
    } else {
        // Pool exhausted, allocate new frame
        auto new_frame = MakeUnique<VideoFrame>(width_, height_, format_);
        frame = new_frame.get();
        impl_->all_frames.push_back(std::move(new_frame));
    }
    
    impl_->in_use++;
    
    // Create shared_ptr with custom deleter that returns frame to pool
    return Shared<VideoFrame>(frame, [this](VideoFrame* f) {
        Release(f);
    });
}

void VideoFramePool::Release(VideoFrame* frame) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    
    impl_->available.push(frame);
    impl_->in_use--;
}

u32 VideoFramePool::AvailableCount() const {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    return static_cast<u32>(impl_->available.size());
}

u32 VideoFramePool::InUseCount() const {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    return impl_->in_use;
}

void VideoFramePool::Resize(u32 new_size) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    
    while (impl_->all_frames.size() < new_size) {
        auto frame = MakeUnique<VideoFrame>(width_, height_, format_);
        impl_->available.push(frame.get());
        impl_->all_frames.push_back(std::move(frame));
    }
    
    pool_size_ = new_size;
}

void VideoFramePool::Clear() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(impl_->mutex);
    
    while (!impl_->available.empty()) {
        impl_->available.pop();
    }
    
    impl_->all_frames.clear();
    impl_->in_use = 0;
}

} // namespace aether
```

## Part 11: Video Renderer Interface

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/video/video_renderer.hpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#pragma once

#ifndef AETHER_VIDEO_VIDEO_RENDERER_HPP
#define AETHER_VIDEO_VIDEO_RENDERER_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"
#include "aether/core/error.hpp"
#include "aether/video/video_frame.hpp"

#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <functional>

namespace aether {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Renderer Types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

enum class RendererType : u8 {
    Auto = 0,
    Vulkan,
    Metal,
    D3D12,
    D3D11,
    OpenGL,
    OpenGLES,
    Software
};

enum class PresentMode : u8 {
    Immediate = 0,    // No sync, tearing possible
    VSync,            // Wait for vsync
    Mailbox,          // Triple buffering
    FIFO              // Double buffering with vsync
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Renderer Configuration
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct VideoRendererConfig {
    void* window_handle = nullptr;
    u32 width = 0;
    u32 height = 0;
    
    RendererType type = RendererType::Auto;
    PresentMode present_mode = PresentMode::VSync;
    
    bool enable_hdr = true;
    bool enable_10bit = true;
    ColorSpace output_color_space = ColorSpace::Unknown;
    
    u32 swap_chain_size = 3;
    
    // Scaling
    ScaleMode scale_mode = ScaleMode::Fit;
    std::string scale_filter = "lanczos";
    
    // Color adjustments
    f32 brightness = 0.0f;   // -1.0 to 1.0
    f32 contrast = 1.0f;     // 0.0 to 2.0
    f32 saturation = 1.0f;   // 0.0 to 2.0
    f32 hue = 0.0f;          // -180 to 180
    f32 gamma = 1.0f;        // 0.1 to 10.0
    
    // Overlay
    bool show_osd = true;
    Color background_color = Color::Black();
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Render Statistics
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct RenderStatistics {
    u64 frames_rendered = 0;
    u64 frames_dropped = 0;
    f64 average_render_time_ms = 0;
    f64 average_present_time_ms = 0;
    f64 current_fps = 0;
    i64 video_memory_used = 0;
    u32 swap_chain_latency = 0;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Subtitle Overlay
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct SubtitleOverlay {
    std::string text;
    Timestamp start;
    Timestamp end;
    
    // Styling
    std::string font_family;
    u32 font_size = 48;
    Color text_color = Color::White();
    Color background_color = Color{0, 0, 0, 0.7f};
    Color outline_color = Color::Black();
    f32 outline_width = 2.0f;
    
    // Position
    f32 x = 0.5f;  // 0.0 to 1.0, centered
    f32 y = 0.9f;  // 0.0 to 1.0, near bottom
    i32 alignment = 2;  // 0=left, 1=center, 2=bottom-center
    
    // Image-based subtitle
    bool is_bitmap = false;
    VideoFrame bitmap;
    Rectangle bitmap_rect;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// OSD (On-Screen Display)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct OSDMessage {
    std::string text;
    TimePoint expire_time;
    Color color = Color::White();
    f32 font_size = 24.0f;
    f32 x = 0.05f;
    f32 y = 0.05f;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Video Renderer Interface
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API IVideoRenderer {
public:
    virtual ~IVideoRenderer() = default;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Lifecycle
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    virtual Result<void> Initialize(const VideoRendererConfig& config) = 0;
    virtual void Shutdown() = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool IsInitialized() const = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Rendering
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    virtual Result<void> RenderFrame(const VideoFrame& frame) = 0;
    virtual Result<void> RenderFrame(Shared<VideoFrame> frame) = 0;
    
    virtual Result<void> Present() = 0;
    virtual Result<void> Clear(Color color = Color::Black()) = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Window/Surface
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    virtual Result<void> SetWindowHandle(void* handle) = 0;
    virtual Result<void> Resize(u32 width, u32 height) = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual Size GetSize() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual Size GetVideoSize() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual Rectangle GetVideoRect() const = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Configuration
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    virtual Result<void> SetScaleMode(ScaleMode mode) = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual ScaleMode GetScaleMode() const = 0;
    
    virtual Result<void> SetCustomAspectRatio(f64 ratio) = 0;
    virtual void ResetAspectRatio() = 0;
    
    virtual Result<void> SetCrop(Rectangle rect) = 0;
    virtual void ResetCrop() = 0;
    
    virtual Result<void> SetRotation(i32 degrees) = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual i32 GetRotation() const = 0;
    
    virtual Result<void> SetFlip(bool horizontal, bool vertical) = 0;
    
    virtual Result<void> SetPresentMode(PresentMode mode) = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual PresentMode GetPresentMode() const = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Color Adjustments
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    virtual Result<void> SetBrightness(f32 value) = 0;
    virtual Result<void> SetContrast(f32 value) = 0;
    virtual Result<void> SetSaturation(f32 value) = 0;
    virtual Result<void> SetHue(f32 value) = 0;
    virtual Result<void> SetGamma(f32 value) = 0;
    virtual void ResetColorAdjustments() = 0;
    
    [[nodiscard]] virtual f32 GetBrightness() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual f32 GetContrast() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual f32 GetSaturation() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual f32 GetHue() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual f32 GetGamma() const = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // HDR
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    virtual Result<void> SetHDREnabled(bool enabled) = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool IsHDREnabled() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool IsHDRSupported() const = 0;
    
    virtual Result<void> SetOutputColorSpace(ColorSpace cs) = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual ColorSpace GetOutputColorSpace() const = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Subtitles & OSD
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    virtual Result<void> SetSubtitle(const SubtitleOverlay& subtitle) = 0;
    virtual void ClearSubtitle() = 0;
    
    virtual void ShowOSD(const OSDMessage& message) = 0;
    virtual void HideOSD() = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Screenshots
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    virtual Result<VideoFrame> CaptureFrame() = 0;
    virtual Result<std::vector<u8>> CaptureFrameEncoded(std::string_view format = "png") = 0;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Info & Statistics
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    [[nodiscard]] virtual RendererType GetType() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::string GetName() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::string GetInfo() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual RenderStatistics GetStatistics() const = 0;
    virtual void ResetStatistics() = 0;
    
protected:
    IVideoRenderer() = default;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Renderer Factory
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

AETHER_API Unique<IVideoRenderer> CreateVideoRenderer(RendererType type = RendererType::Auto);
```

## Part 12: Audio System

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/audio/audio_frame.hpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#pragma once

#ifndef AETHER_AUDIO_AUDIO_FRAME_HPP
#define AETHER_AUDIO_AUDIO_FRAME_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"
#include "aether/utils/memory.hpp"

#include <vector>

namespace aether {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Audio Format
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct AudioFormat {
    u32 sample_rate = 48000;
    u32 channels = 2;
    SampleFormat sample_format = SampleFormat::F32;
    ChannelLayout channel_layout = ChannelLayout::Stereo;
    
    [[nodiscard]] u32 BytesPerSample() const noexcept {
        return GetBytesPerSample(sample_format);
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] u32 BytesPerFrame() const noexcept {
        return BytesPerSample() * channels;
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] u32 BytesPerSecond() const noexcept {
        return BytesPerFrame() * sample_rate;
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsPlanar() const noexcept {
        return IsPlanarFormat(sample_format);
    }
    
    bool operator==(const AudioFormat& other) const {
        return sample_rate == other.sample_rate &&
               channels == other.channels &&
               sample_format == other.sample_format;
    }
    
    bool operator!=(const AudioFormat& other) const {
        return !(*this == other);
    }
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Audio Frame
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API AudioFrame {
public:
    static constexpr usize MAX_CHANNELS = 32;
    
    AudioFrame();
    AudioFrame(u32 samples, const AudioFormat& format);
    ~AudioFrame();
    
    AudioFrame(const AudioFrame& other);
    AudioFrame& operator=(const AudioFrame& other);
    AudioFrame(AudioFrame&& other) noexcept;
    AudioFrame& operator=(AudioFrame&& other) noexcept;
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Properties
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    [[nodiscard]] u32 SampleCount() const noexcept { return sample_count_; }
    [[nodiscard]] const AudioFormat& Format() const noexcept { return format_; }
    [[nodiscard]] u32 Channels() const noexcept { return format_.channels; }
    [[nodiscard]] u32 SampleRate() const noexcept { return format_.sample_rate; }
    [[nodiscard]] SampleFormat SampleFmt() const noexcept { return format_.sample_format; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsValid() const noexcept { return sample_count_ > 0 && data_[0] != nullptr; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsPlanar() const noexcept { return format_.IsPlanar(); }
    
    // Duration in seconds
    [[nodiscard]] f64 DurationSeconds() const noexcept {
        return format_.sample_rate > 0 
            ? static_cast<f64>(sample_count_) / format_.sample_rate 
            : 0.0;
    }
    
    // Total bytes
    [[nodiscard]] usize TotalBytes() const noexcept {
        if (IsPlanar()) {
            return static_cast<usize>(sample_count_) * format_.BytesPerSample() * format_.channels;
        } else {
            return static_cast<usize>(sample_count_) * format_.BytesPerFrame();
        }
    }
    
    // Timing
    [[nodiscard]] Timestamp GetTimestamp() const noexcept { return timestamp_; }
    void SetTimestamp(Timestamp ts) { timestamp_ = ts; }
    
    [[nodiscard]] i64 GetPTS() const noexcept { return timestamp_.pts; }
    void SetPTS(i64 pts) { timestamp_.pts = pts; }
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Data Access
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    // For interleaved formats, channel is ignored
    [[nodiscard]] u8* Data(u32 channel = 0) noexcept {
        return channel < MAX_CHANNELS ? data_[channel] : nullptr;
    }
    
    [[nodiscard]] const u8* Data(u32 channel = 0) const noexcept {
        return channel < MAX_CHANNELS ? data_[channel] : nullptr;
    }
    
    // Typed access
    template<typename T>
    [[nodiscard]] T* DataAs(u32 channel = 0) noexcept {
        return reinterpret_cast<T*>(Data(channel));
    }
    
    template<typename T>
    [[nodiscard]] const T* DataAs(u32 channel = 0) const noexcept {
        return reinterpret_cast<const T*>(Data(channel));
    }
    
    // Float samples (regardless of actual format)
    [[nodiscard]] f32 GetSample(u32 sample, u32 channel = 0) const;
    void SetSample(u32 sample, u32 channel, f32 value);
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Operations
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    Result<void> Allocate(u32 samples, const AudioFormat& format);
    void Release();
    void Clear();
    
    // Copy from external data
    Result<void> CopyFrom(const u8* const* data, u32 samples, const AudioFormat& format);
    Result<void> CopyFromInterleaved(const void* data, u32 samples, const AudioFormat& format);
    
    // Convert to different format
    Result<AudioFrame> ConvertTo(const AudioFormat& target_format) const;
    
    // Resample to different sample rate
    Result<AudioFrame> Resample(u32 target_sample_rate) const;
    
    // Clone
    [[nodiscard]] AudioFrame Clone() const;
    
    // Mix with another frame
    Result<void> MixWith(const AudioFrame& other, f32 other_gain = 1.0f);
    
    // Apply gain
    void ApplyGain(f32 gain);
    void ApplyGainPerChannel(const std::vector<f32>& gains);
    
    // Normalize
    void Normalize(f32 target_peak = 1.0f);
    
    // Get peak level (0.0 to 1.0+)
    [[nodiscard]] f32 GetPeakLevel() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<f32> GetPeakLevels() const;  // Per channel
    
    // Get RMS level
    [[nodiscard]] f32 GetRMSLevel() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<f32> GetRMSLevels() const;  // Per channel
    
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Factory Methods
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    static AudioFrame CreateSilence(u32 samples, const AudioFormat& format);
    static AudioFrame CreateSineWave(u32 samples, const AudioFormat& format, 
                                      f32 frequency, f32 amplitude = 1.0f);
    static AudioFrame CreateWhiteNoise(u32 samples, const AudioFormat& format,
                                        f32 amplitude = 1.0f);
    
private:
    u32 sample_count_ = 0;
    AudioFormat format_;
    
    std::array<u8*, MAX_CHANNELS> data_{};
    
    bool owns_data_ = false;
    Buffer buffer_;
    
    Timestamp timestamp_;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Audio Frame Queue
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API AudioFrameQueue {
public:
    explicit AudioFrameQueue(usize max_frames = 64);
    ~AudioFrameQueue();
    
    AudioFrameQueue(const AudioFrameQueue&) = delete;
    AudioFrameQueue& operator=(const AudioFrameQueue&) = delete;
    
    void Push(AudioFrame frame);
    bool TryPush(AudioFrame frame);
    
    AudioFrame Pop();
    std::optional<AudioFrame> TryPop();
    AudioFrame Peek() const;
    
    void Clear();
    void Close();
    
    [[nodiscard]] usize Size() const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsEmpty() const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool IsClosed() const;
    [[nodiscard]] f64 DurationSeconds() const;
    
private:
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_AUDIO_AUDIO_FRAME_HPP
```

```cpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FILE: include/aether/audio/audio_device.hpp
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#pragma once

#ifndef AETHER_AUDIO_AUDIO_DEVICE_HPP
#define AETHER_AUDIO_AUDIO_DEVICE_HPP

#include "aether/export.hpp"
#include "aether/core/types.hpp"
#include "aether/core/error.hpp"
#include "aether/audio/audio_frame.hpp"

#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <functional>

namespace aether {

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Audio Backend
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

enum class AudioBackend : u8 {
    Auto = 0,
    WASAPI,         // Windows
    DirectSound,    // Windows (legacy)
    CoreAudio,      // macOS/iOS
    ALSA,           // Linux
    PulseAudio,     // Linux
    PipeWire,       // Linux (modern)
    AAudio,         // Android
    OpenSL,         // Android (legacy)
    WebAudio,       // Web
    SDL,            // Cross-platform fallback
    Null            // No audio
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Audio Device Info
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct AudioDeviceInfo {
    std::string id;
    std::string name;
    std::string description;
    
    bool is_input = false;
    bool is_output = true;
    bool is_default = false;
    bool is_loopback = false;
    
    u32 max_channels = 0;
    std::vector<u32> supported_sample_rates;
    std::vector<SampleFormat> supported_formats;
    
    u32 default_sample_rate = 0;
    SampleFormat default_format = SampleFormat::Unknown;
    
    // Exclusive mode
    bool supports_exclusive = false;
    u32 exclusive_min_buffer_frames = 0;
    u32 exclusive_max_buffer_frames = 0;
    
    // Latency
    u32 min_latency_frames = 0;
    u32 max_latency_frames = 0;
    u32 default_latency_frames = 0;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Audio Device Configuration
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct AudioDeviceConfig {
    std::string device_id;  // Empty for default device
    
    AudioFormat format;
    
    bool exclusive_mode = false;
    u32 buffer_frames = 0;  // 0 for auto
    u32 period_frames = 0;  // 0 for auto
    
    f32 volume = 1.0f;
    bool muted = false;
    
    // Callbacks
    std::function<void(const AudioDeviceInfo&)> on_device_changed;
    std::function<void(Error)> on_error;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Audio Output
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API IAudioOutput {
public:
    virtual ~IAudioOutput() = default;
    
    // Lifecycle
    virtual Result<void> Initialize(const AudioDeviceConfig& config) = 0;
    virtual void Shutdown() = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool IsInitialized() const = 0;
    
    // Playback control
    virtual Result<void> Start() = 0;
    virtual Result<void> Stop() = 0;
    virtual Result<void> Pause() = 0;
    virtual Result<void> Resume() = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool IsRunning() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool IsPaused() const = 0;
    
    // Write audio
    virtual Result<void> Write(const AudioFrame& frame) = 0;
    virtual Result<u32> Write(const f32* samples, u32 sample_count) = 0;
    virtual Result<void> Flush() = 0;
    virtual Result<void> Drain() = 0;
    
    // Volume
    virtual Result<void> SetVolume(f32 volume) = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual f32 GetVolume() const = 0;
    virtual Result<void> SetMuted(bool muted) = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool IsMuted() const = 0;
    
    // Device
    virtual Result<void> SetDevice(std::string_view device_id) = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::string GetDeviceId() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual AudioDeviceInfo GetDeviceInfo() const = 0;
    
    // Format
    [[nodiscard]] virtual AudioFormat GetFormat() const = 0;
    virtual Result<void> SetFormat(const AudioFormat& format) = 0;
    
    // Latency
    [[nodiscard]] virtual u32 GetLatencyFrames() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual f64 GetLatencySeconds() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual u32 GetBufferedFrames() const = 0;
    
    // Info
    [[nodiscard]] virtual AudioBackend GetBackend() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::string GetBackendName() const = 0;
    
protected:
    IAudioOutput() = default;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Audio Device Manager
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AETHER_API AudioDeviceManager {
public:
    static AudioDeviceManager& Instance();
    
    // Initialization
    Result<void> Initialize();
    void Shutdown();
    
    // Device enumeration
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<AudioDeviceInfo> GetOutputDevices() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<AudioDeviceInfo> GetInputDevices() const;
    [[nodiscard]] AudioDeviceInfo GetDefaultOutputDevice() const;
    [[nodiscard]] AudioDeviceInfo GetDefaultInputDevice() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::optional<AudioDeviceInfo> GetDevice(std::string_view id) const;
    
    // Device change notification
    using DeviceChangeCallback = std::function<void()>;
    u64 AddDeviceChangeListener(DeviceChangeCallback callback);
    void RemoveDeviceChangeListener(u64 id);
    
    // Create output
    Result<Unique<IAudioOutput>> CreateOutput(AudioBackend backend = AudioBackend::Auto);
    
    // Backend info
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<AudioBackend> GetAvailableBackends() const;
    [[nodiscard]] AudioBackend GetDefaultBackend() const;
    [[nodiscard]] static std::string_view GetBackendName(AudioBackend backend);
    
private:
    AudioDeviceManager();
    ~AudioDeviceManager();
    
    class Impl;
    Unique<Impl> impl_;
};

// Convenience function
inline AudioDeviceManager& GetAudioDeviceManager() {
    return AudioDeviceManager::Instance();
}

} // namespace aether

#endif // AETHER_AUDIO_AUDIO_DEVICE_HPP
```

## Part 13: CMake Build System

```cmake
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# FILE: CMakeLists.txt
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.20)

# Prevent in-source builds
if(CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR STREQUAL CMAKE_BINARY_DIR)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "In-source builds are not allowed. Please use a build directory.")
endif()

# Version
file(READ "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/VERSION" AETHER_VERSION_CONTENT)
string(STRIP "${AETHER_VERSION_CONTENT}" AETHER_VERSION)

project(AetherMediaEngine
    VERSION ${AETHER_VERSION}
    DESCRIPTION "Enterprise-grade AI-powered cross-platform media player"
    HOMEPAGE_URL "https://github.com/aether-engine/aether-media-engine"
    LANGUAGES C CXX
)

# C++ Standard
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
set(CMAKE_CXX_EXTENSIONS OFF)

# C Standard
set(CMAKE_C_STANDARD 17)
set(CMAKE_C_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

# Export compile commands
set(CMAKE_EXPORT_COMPILE_COMMANDS ON)

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Options
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

option(AETHER_BUILD_SHARED "Build shared library" ON)
option(AETHER_BUILD_STATIC "Build static library" OFF)
option(AETHER_BUILD_APPS "Build applications" ON)
option(AETHER_BUILD_EXAMPLES "Build examples" ON)
option(AETHER_BUILD_TESTS "Build tests" ON)
option(AETHER_BUILD_DOCS "Build documentation" OFF)

# Features
option(AETHER_ENABLE_AI "Enable AI/ML features" ON)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_VULKAN "Enable Vulkan renderer" ON)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_OPENGL "Enable OpenGL renderer" ON)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_HWACCEL "Enable hardware acceleration" ON)

# Debug
option(AETHER_ENABLE_ASAN "Enable AddressSanitizer" OFF)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_UBSAN "Enable UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer" OFF)
option(AETHER_ENABLE_TSAN "Enable ThreadSanitizer" OFF)

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Platform Detection
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

if(WIN32)
    set(AETHER_PLATFORM "Windows")
    set(AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS TRUE)
    add_compile_definitions(AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS=1)
elseif(APPLE)
    if(IOS)
        set(AETHER_PLATFORM "iOS")
        set(AETHER_PLATFORM_IOS TRUE)
        add_compile_definitions(AETHER_PLATFORM_IOS=1)
    else()
        set(AETHER_PLATFORM "macOS")
        set(AETHER_PLATFORM_MACOS TRUE)
        add_compile_definitions(AETHER_PLATFORM_MACOS=1)
    endif()
    set(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE TRUE)
    add_compile_definitions(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE=1)
elseif(ANDROID)
    set(AETHER_PLATFORM "Android")
    set(AETHER_PLATFORM_ANDROID TRUE)
    add_compile_definitions(AETHER_PLATFORM_ANDROID=1)
elseif(UNIX)
    set(AETHER_PLATFORM "Linux")
    set(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX TRUE)
    add_compile_definitions(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX=1)
endif()

message(STATUS "")
message(STATUS "╔═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗")
message(STATUS "║           AETHER Media Engine v${PROJECT_VERSION}                      ║")
message(STATUS "╠═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣")
message(STATUS "║ Platform:     ${AETHER_PLATFORM}")
message(STATUS "║ Build Type:   ${CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE}")
message(STATUS "║ Compiler:     ${CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER_ID} ${CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER_VERSION}")
message(STATUS "╚═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝")
message(STATUS "")

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Module Path
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

list(APPEND CMAKE_MODULE_PATH "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake/modules")

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Git Info
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

find_package(Git QUIET)
if(GIT_FOUND)
    execute_process(
        COMMAND ${GIT_EXECUTABLE} rev-parse --short HEAD
        WORKING_DIRECTORY ${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}
        OUTPUT_VARIABLE AETHER_GIT_COMMIT
        OUTPUT_STRIP_TRAILING_WHITESPACE
        ERROR_QUIET
    )
    execute_process(
        COMMAND ${GIT_EXECUTABLE} rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD
        WORKING_DIRECTORY ${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}
        OUTPUT_VARIABLE AETHER_GIT_BRANCH
        OUTPUT_STRIP_TRAILING_WHITESPACE
        ERROR_QUIET
    )
else()
    set(AETHER_GIT_COMMIT "unknown")
    set(AETHER_GIT_BRANCH "unknown")
endif()

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Configure File
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

string(TIMESTAMP BUILD_DATE "%Y-%m-%d")
string(TIMESTAMP BUILD_TIME "%H:%M:%S")

configure_file(
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake/version.cpp.in"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/generated/version.cpp"
    @ONLY
)

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Dependencies
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

find_package(PkgConfig QUIET)

# FFmpeg (required)
find_package(FFmpeg REQUIRED COMPONENTS avcodec avformat avutil swscale swresample)

# JSON (required)
find_package(nlohmann_json REQUIRED)

# Threads
find_package(Threads REQUIRED)

# Vulkan (optional)
if(AETHER_ENABLE_VULKAN)
    find_package(Vulkan)
    if(Vulkan_FOUND)
        set(AETHER_HAS_VULKAN TRUE)
        add_compile_definitions(AETHER_HAS_VULKAN=1)
        message(STATUS "Vulkan: Found")
    else()
        message(STATUS "Vulkan: Not found")
    endif()
endif()

# OpenGL (optional)
if(AETHER_ENABLE_OPENGL)
    find_package(OpenGL)
    if(OpenGL_FOUND)
        set(AETHER_HAS_OPENGL TRUE)
        add_compile_definitions(AETHER_HAS_OPENGL=1)
        message(STATUS "OpenGL: Found")
    endif()
endif()

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Source Files
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

set(AETHER_SOURCES
    # Generated
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/generated/version.cpp
    
    # Core
    src/core/error.cpp
    src/core/engine/engine.cpp
    src/core/player/player.cpp
    src/core/event_system.cpp
    
    # Config
    src/config.cpp
    
    # Utils
    src/utils/logging.cpp
    src/utils/memory.cpp
    src/utils/threading.cpp
    
    # Video
    src/video/video_frame.cpp
    
    # Audio
    src/audio/audio_frame.cpp
)

# Platform-specific sources
if(AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS)
    list(APPEND AETHER_SOURCES
        src/platform/windows/platform_windows.cpp
        src/audio/backend/wasapi_output.cpp
    )
elseif(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE)
    list(APPEND AETHER_SOURCES
        src/platform/apple/platform_apple.mm
        src/audio/backend/coreaudio_output.cpp
    )
elseif(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
    list(APPEND AETHER_SOURCES
        src/platform/linux/platform_linux.cpp
        src/audio/backend/alsa_output.cpp
        src/audio/backend/pulseaudio_output.cpp
    )
endif()

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Library
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

if(AETHER_BUILD_SHARED)
    add_library(aether SHARED ${AETHER_SOURCES})
    target_compile_definitions(aether PRIVATE AETHER_BUILD_SHARED AETHER_EXPORT)
    set_target_properties(aether PROPERTIES
        VERSION ${PROJECT_VERSION}
        SOVERSION ${PROJECT_VERSION_MAJOR}
    )
endif()

if(AETHER_BUILD_STATIC)
    add_library(aether_static STATIC ${AETHER_SOURCES})
    target_compile_definitions(aether_static PRIVATE AETHER_BUILD_STATIC)
    set_target_properties(aether_static PROPERTIES OUTPUT_NAME aether)
endif()

# Common target for both
if(AETHER_BUILD_SHARED)
    set(AETHER_MAIN_TARGET aether)
else()
    set(AETHER_MAIN_TARGET aether_static)
endif()

add_library(Aether::aether ALIAS ${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET})

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Include Directories
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

target_include_directories(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET}
    PUBLIC
        $<BUILD_INTERFACE:${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/include>
        $<BUILD_INTERFACE:${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/generated>
        $<INSTALL_INTERFACE:include>
    PRIVATE
        ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src
)

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Link Libraries
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

target_link_libraries(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET}
    PUBLIC
        Threads::Threads
    PRIVATE
        FFmpeg::avcodec
        FFmpeg::avformat
        FFmpeg::avutil
        FFmpeg::swscale
        FFmpeg::swresample
        nlohmann_json::nlohmann_json
)

# Vulkan
if(AETHER_HAS_VULKAN)
    target_link_libraries(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE Vulkan::Vulkan)
endif()

# OpenGL
if(AETHER_HAS_OPENGL)
    target_link_libraries(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE OpenGL::GL)
endif()

# Platform-specific
if(AETHER_PLATFORM_WINDOWS)
    target_link_libraries(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE
        ole32 oleaut32 uuid winmm ws2_32
    )
elseif(AETHER_PLATFORM_APPLE)
    find_library(FOUNDATION_FRAMEWORK Foundation REQUIRED)
    find_library(COREFOUNDATION_FRAMEWORK CoreFoundation REQUIRED)
    find_library(COREMEDIA_FRAMEWORK CoreMedia REQUIRED)
    find_library(COREVIDEO_FRAMEWORK CoreVideo REQUIRED)
    find_library(COREAUDIO_FRAMEWORK CoreAudio REQUIRED)
    find_library(AUDIOTOOLBOX_FRAMEWORK AudioToolbox REQUIRED)
    find_library(VIDEOTOOLBOX_FRAMEWORK VideoToolbox REQUIRED)
    
    target_link_libraries(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE
        ${FOUNDATION_FRAMEWORK}
        ${COREFOUNDATION_FRAMEWORK}
        ${COREMEDIA_FRAMEWORK}
        ${COREVIDEO_FRAMEWORK}
        ${COREAUDIO_FRAMEWORK}
        ${AUDIOTOOLBOX_FRAMEWORK}
        ${VIDEOTOOLBOX_FRAMEWORK}
    )
elseif(AETHER_PLATFORM_LINUX)
    pkg_check_modules(ALSA REQUIRED alsa)
    target_link_libraries(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE ${ALSA_LIBRARIES})
    target_include_directories(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE ${ALSA_INCLUDE_DIRS})
    
    pkg_check_modules(PULSE libpulse)
    if(PULSE_FOUND)
        target_link_libraries(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE ${PULSE_LIBRARIES})
        target_include_directories(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE ${PULSE_INCLUDE_DIRS})
        target_compile_definitions(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE AETHER_HAS_PULSEAUDIO=1)
    endif()
endif()

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Compiler Flags
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

if(CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER_ID MATCHES "GNU|Clang")
    target_compile_options(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE
        -Wall -Wextra -Wpedantic
        -Wno-unused-parameter
        -fvisibility=hidden
    )
    
    if(NOT CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE STREQUAL "Debug")
        target_compile_options(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE -O3)
    endif()
    
    # Sanitizers
    if(AETHER_ENABLE_ASAN)
        target_compile_options(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE -fsanitize=address)
        target_link_options(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE -fsanitize=address)
    endif()
    
    if(AETHER_ENABLE_UBSAN)
        target_compile_options(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE -fsanitize=undefined)
        target_link_options(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE -fsanitize=undefined)
    endif()
    
    if(AETHER_ENABLE_TSAN)
        target_compile_options(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE -fsanitize=thread)
        target_link_options(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE -fsanitize=thread)
    endif()
    
elseif(MSVC)
    target_compile_options(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE
        /W4 /WX-
        /wd4100  # unreferenced parameter
        /wd4458  # hides class member
        /utf-8
    )
    
    target_compile_definitions(${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET} PRIVATE
        WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
        NOMINMAX
        _CRT_SECURE_NO_WARNINGS
        UNICODE
        _UNICODE
    )
endif()

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Applications
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

if(AETHER_BUILD_APPS)
    add_subdirectory(apps)
endif()

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Examples
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

if(AETHER_BUILD_EXAMPLES)
    add_subdirectory(examples)
endif()

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Tests
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

if(AETHER_BUILD_TESTS)
    enable_testing()
    add_subdirectory(tests)
endif()

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Installation
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

include(GNUInstallDirs)

install(TARGETS ${AETHER_MAIN_TARGET}
    EXPORT AetherTargets
    LIBRARY DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}
    ARCHIVE DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}
    RUNTIME DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_BINDIR}
)

install(DIRECTORY include/aether
    DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_INCLUDEDIR}
)

install(EXPORT AetherTargets
    FILE AetherTargets.cmake
    NAMESPACE Aether::
    DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}/cmake/Aether
)

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Summary
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

message(STATUS "")
message(STATUS "Configuration Summary:")
message(STATUS "  Shared library:  ${AETHER_BUILD_SHARED}")
message(STATUS "  Static library:  ${AETHER_BUILD_STATIC}")
message(STATUS "  Applications:    ${AETHER_BUILD_APPS}")
message(STATUS "  Tests:           ${AETHER_BUILD_TESTS}")
message(STATUS "  Vulkan:          ${AETHER_HAS_VULKAN}")
message(STATUS "  OpenGL:          ${AETHER_HAS_OPENGL}")
message(STATUS "")
```

```cmake
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```
```cmake
# FILE: cmake/modules/FindFFmpeg.cmake
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

# Find FFmpeg libraries
#
# This module defines:
#   FFmpeg_FOUND
#   FFmpeg_INCLUDE_DIRS
#   FFmpeg_LIBRARIES
#
# For each component (avcodec, avformat, avutil, swscale, swresample, avfilter):
#   FFmpeg_<component>_FOUND
#   FFmpeg_<component>_INCLUDE_DIR
#   FFmpeg_<component>_LIBRARY
#   FFmpeg::<component> (imported target)

include(FindPackageHandleStandardArgs)

set(FFmpeg_COMPONENTS avcodec avformat avutil swscale swresample avfilter)

foreach(component ${FFmpeg_FIND_COMPONENTS})
    string(TOUPPER ${component} COMPONENT_UPPER)
    
    # Find include directory
    find_path(FFmpeg_${component}_INCLUDE_DIR
        NAMES lib${component}/${component}.h
        HINTS
            ENV FFMPEG_DIR
            /usr/local/include
            /usr/include
            /opt/homebrew/include
        PATH_SUFFIXES ffmpeg
    )
    
    # Find library
    find_library(FFmpeg_${component}_LIBRARY
        NAMES ${component} lib${component}
        HINTS
            ENV FFMPEG_DIR
            /usr/local/lib
            /usr/lib
            /opt/homebrew/lib
    )
    
    # Create imported target
    if(FFmpeg_${component}_INCLUDE_DIR AND FFmpeg_${component}_LIBRARY)
        set(FFmpeg_${component}_FOUND TRUE)
        
        if(NOT TARGET FFmpeg::${component})
            add_library(FFmpeg::${component} UNKNOWN IMPORTED)
            set_target_properties(FFmpeg::${component} PROPERTIES
                IMPORTED_LOCATION "${FFmpeg_${component}_LIBRARY}"
                INTERFACE_INCLUDE_DIRECTORIES "${FFmpeg_${component}_INCLUDE_DIR}"
            )
        endif()
        
        list(APPEND FFmpeg_INCLUDE_DIRS ${FFmpeg_${component}_INCLUDE_DIR})
        list(APPEND FFmpeg_LIBRARIES ${FFmpeg_${component}_LIBRARY})
    endif()
    
    mark_as_advanced(FFmpeg_${component}_INCLUDE_DIR FFmpeg_${component}_LIBRARY)
endforeach()

if(FFmpeg_INCLUDE_DIRS)
    list(REMOVE_DUPLICATES FFmpeg_INCLUDE_DIRS)
endif()

find_package_handle_standard_args(FFmpeg
    REQUIRED_VARS FFmpeg_INCLUDE_DIRS FFmpeg_LIBRARIES
    HANDLE_COMPONENTS
)
```

---

## Summary

This implementation covers:

### ✅ Completed Parts (1-13):

1. **Core Types & Error Handling** - Complete type system with error codes and Result type
2. **Configuration System** - Full config with JSON/TOML support
3. **Logging System** - Console and file logging with colors
4. **Memory Management** - Aligned allocation, pools, ring buffers
5. **Threading Utilities** - Thread pool, work queues, synchronization primitives
6. **Engine Core** - FFmpeg integration, capability detection
7. **Player Implementation** - Full playback control with demuxing/decoding threads
8. **Event System** - Type-safe events with dispatcher
9. **Desktop Application** - Command-line player
10. **Video Frame** - Frame class with format conversion
11. **Video Renderer Interface** - Renderer abstraction
12. **Audio System** - Audio frame and device interfaces
13. **CMake Build System** - Cross-platform build

### 📋 Additional components that would be needed for a complete implementation:

- Vulkan/Metal/D3D12 renderer implementations
- Network streaming (HLS/DASH parsers)
- AI/ML inference integration
- Plugin system
- DRM support
- GUI desktop application (Qt/GTK)
- Mobile platform support (Android JNI, iOS)
- WebAssembly support
